```go
// m3drServer stands in for the controller's API for host h1: it plans,
// applies and confirms any firewall action, answers the first plan of every
// action with the refusal named by first, and counts what it was asked.
type m3drServer struct {
	mu      sync.Mutex
	first   string // "out of sync": a failing firewall.lockout guard; "locked": 423 lock.held
	plans   map[string]int
	applies map[string]int
}

func (s *m3drServer) ServeHTTP(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	parts := strings.Split(strings.Trim(r.URL.Path, "/"), "/")
	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	switch {
	case r.Method == http.MethodPost && strings.HasSuffix(r.URL.Path, "/plan"):
		action := parts[len(parts)-3]
		s.plans[action]++
		if s.plans[action] == 1 {
			switch s.first {
			case "out of sync":
				_, _ = w.Write([]byte(`{"planId":"p-` + action + `","guards":[{"id":"firewall.lockout","ok":false,` +
					`"message":"the host is out of sync with UFW's configuration (v6 filter/INPUT)"}]}`))
				return
			case "locked":
				w.WriteHeader(http.StatusLocked)
				_, _ = w.Write([]byte(`{"code":"lock.held","detail":"lock firewall is held by change 01M6E7FXQ4D8N2R5T7V9W1Y3Z6"}`))
				return
			}
		}
		_, _ = w.Write([]byte(`{"planId":"p-` + action + `","guards":[{"id":"firewall.lockout","ok":true,"message":""}]}`))
	case r.Method == http.MethodPost && strings.HasSuffix(r.URL.Path, "/apply"):
		action := parts[len(parts)-3]
		s.applies[action]++
		_, _ = w.Write([]byte(`{"id":"c-` + action + `","state":"pending"}`))
	case r.Method == http.MethodPost && strings.HasSuffix(r.URL.Path, "/confirm"):
		_, _ = w.Write([]byte(`{"id":"` + parts[len(parts)-2] + `","state":"confirmed"}`))
	case r.Method == http.MethodGet && strings.HasSuffix(r.URL.Path, "/resources/firewall/docker"):
		_, _ = w.Write([]byte(`{"rules":[{"id":"01J00000000000000000000001","binding":{"selector":"m3d-n1","hostPort":18202,"proto":"tcp"},"kind":"allow"}]}`))
	default:
		http.NotFound(w, r)
	}
}

// TestM3drUnitTrafficApply: the allow, deny and remove steps of the traffic
// scenarios try the plan again when a background reconcile makes the host
// read out of sync or holds the firewall lock, and then apply and confirm
// once.
func TestM3drUnitTrafficApply(t *testing.T) {
	steps := []struct {
		name, action string
		run          func(ctx context.Context, t *testing.T, f *fleet)
	}{
		{"allow", "firewall.container.allow", func(ctx context.Context, t *testing.T, f *fleet) {
			m3dTrafficAllow(ctx, t, f, "m3d-n1", 18202, "")
		}},
		{"deny", "firewall.container.deny", func(ctx context.Context, t *testing.T, f *fleet) {
			m3dTrafficDeny(ctx, t, f, "m3d-n1", 18202, "198.18.0.20/32")
		}},
		{"remove", "firewall.container.remove", func(ctx context.Context, t *testing.T, f *fleet) {
			m3dTrafficRemove(ctx, t, f, "m3d-n1", 18202, "allow", "")
		}},
	}
	for _, refusal := range []string{"out of sync", "locked"} {
		for _, s := range steps {
			t.Run(refusal+"/"+s.name, func(t *testing.T) {
				srv := &m3drServer{first: refusal, plans: map[string]int{}, applies: map[string]int{}}
				ts := httptest.NewServer(srv)
				t.Cleanup(ts.Close)
				f := &fleet{api: m4TestAPI(t, ts), agentID: "h1"}
				ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 30*time.Second)
				defer cancel()
				s.run(ctx, t, f)
				srv.mu.Lock()
				defer srv.mu.Unlock()
				if srv.plans[s.action] != 2 || srv.applies[s.action] != 1 {
					t.Errorf("%s: %d plans and %d applies, want 2 plans (the first refused) and 1 apply", s.action, srv.plans[s.action], srv.applies[s.action])
				}
			})
		}
	}
}
```

### Task 3: lifecycle: fix the RunTask fence test race

**Files:**
- Modify: `internal/core/lifecycle/uninstall_test.go` (`TestRunTaskFenceRefusesWhileUninstalling`, and the `sync` import)

`internal/core/lifecycle/taskproc_test.go` is read, not edited: its `TestRunTaskFence` shows the pattern (`f.units.set(TaskUnitName(...), true)` before a `RunTask` call).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `newTaskFixture`, `taskFixture.taskApplyStart`, `taskArmed`, `taskRowState`, the fake `taskUnits` (`set`, `mu`, `onState`), `tskHost.mu`, `FakeClock.Advance`/`Pending`, `TaskUnitName`, `TaskPoll`, `BeginUninstall`, `AbortUninstall`, `Engine.RunTask` — all existing.
- Produces: nothing; a test fix.

**Items:** 302

**Depends on:** none
**Implementer:** dforge:impl-sonnet-low
**Evaluation:** files 0 - spec 1 - coupling 0 - risk 1 = 2

Why: the test starts a task change, begins an uninstall, calls `RunTask` (which refuses and leaves the row `starting`), aborts the uninstall and calls `RunTask` again, expecting the row `succeeded`. The fake unit for the task is never marked active, so it reads `not-found`, which the engine's follower (`followTask` in `taskunit.go`) treats as an inactive unit. If the follower's poll reads the row after the second `RunTask` has claimed it (`running`) and before it records its result, the follower concludes the process died and records the row `failed`; the result update then matches no row and the test sees `failed`. It happened three times in CI run 41455502877. A real task process has an active unit for as long as it runs, so the fix is in the test: mark the unit active before the second `RunTask`. The product code is correct and is not changed.

The test below forces the losing interleaving instead of waiting for it. It holds the fake task handler (`tskHost.mu`, taken first in `Task`) so the claimed row stays `running`, advances the fake clock by one `TaskPoll` so the follower polls in that window, and waits for the follower's decision before it lets the task finish. Without the active unit it fails every time; with it, it passes every time.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

In `internal/core/lifecycle/uninstall_test.go` add `"sync"` to the import block (between `"strings"` and `"testing"`), and replace the whole function `TestRunTaskFenceRefusesWhileUninstalling` with:

```go
func TestRunTaskFenceRefusesWhileUninstalling(t *testing.T) {
	f := newTaskFixture(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	v := f.taskApplyStart(refTskInstall, `{"name":"a","value":"1"}`)
	if err := BeginUninstall(ctx, f.db, true); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := f.eng.RunTask(ctx, v.ID, core.TaskOpApply, 1); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("RunTask while uninstalling = %v, want nil", err)
	}
	f.tsk.mu.Lock()
	ran := len(f.tsk.envs)
	f.tsk.mu.Unlock()
	if got := f.taskRowState(v.ID, core.TaskOpApply, 1); got != taskRowStarting || ran != 0 {
		t.Errorf("row %s, Task ran %d times; want the row starting and no Task", got, ran)
	}
	if err := AbortUninstall(ctx, f.db, "local:root@h1"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	f.taskArmed()
	polled := make(chan struct{}, 16)
	f.units.mu.Lock()
	f.units.onState = func(int) {
		select {
		case polled <- struct{}{}:
		default:
		}
	}
	f.units.mu.Unlock()
	f.tsk.mu.Lock()
	release := sync.OnceFunc(f.tsk.mu.Unlock)
	defer release()
	done := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() { done <- f.eng.RunTask(ctx, v.ID, core.TaskOpApply, 1) }()
	until := func(what string, cond func() bool) {
		t.Helper()
		deadline := time.Now().Add(10 * time.Second)
		for !cond() {
			if time.Now().After(deadline) {
				t.Fatalf("timed out waiting for %s", what)
			}
			time.Sleep(time.Millisecond)
		}
	}
	until("the second RunTask to claim the row", func() bool {
		return f.taskRowState(v.ID, core.TaskOpApply, 1) == taskRowRunning
	})
	f.clock.Advance(TaskPoll)
	select {
	case <-polled:
	case <-time.After(10 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("the task follower did not poll")
	}
	until("the follower's decision on the running row", func() bool {
		return f.taskRowState(v.ID, core.TaskOpApply, 1) == taskRowFailed || f.clock.Pending() > 0
	})
	release()
	if err := <-done; err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("second RunTask = %v", err)
	}
	if got := f.taskRowState(v.ID, core.TaskOpApply, 1); got != taskRowSucceeded {
		t.Errorf("row after the abort = %s, want succeeded: a poll while the task process runs must find its unit active", got)
	}
}
```

Create `internal/modules/docker/logsecrets_test.go`:

```go
package docker

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"reflect"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/platform/dockerapi"
)

// lsSource answers secrets and err and appends the container ids it is asked about to asked.
func lsSource(asked *[]string, secrets [][]byte, err error) SecretSource {
	return func(_ context.Context, _ core.Env, id string) ([][]byte, error) {
		*asked = append(*asked, id)
		return secrets, err
	}
}

func lsOwnedBy(module string) func(*dockerapi.ContainerJSON) {
	return func(c *dockerapi.ContainerJSON) { c.Config.Labels[core.LabelModule] = module }
}

func lsNamed(name string) func(*dockerapi.ContainerJSON) {
	return func(c *dockerapi.ContainerJSON) { c.ID, c.Name = dkID(name), "/"+name }
}

// lsHost holds web (unlabelled), proxy (owned by npm) and tunnel (owned by cloudflare).
func lsHost(t *testing.T) *dkHost {
	t.Helper()
	h := newDKHost(t)
	h.docker.put(rdWeb())
	h.docker.put(rdWeb(rdOwned, lsNamed("proxy")))
	h.docker.put(rdWeb(lsOwnedBy("cloudflare"), lsNamed("tunnel")))
	return h
}

// lsPanics runs do and returns the text of the panic it raised; no panic fails the test.
func lsPanics(t *testing.T, do func()) (msg string) {
	t.Helper()
	defer func() {
		r := recover()
		if r == nil {
			t.Error("no panic")
			return
		}
		msg = fmt.Sprint(r)
	}()
	do()
	return ""
}

func TestLsRegistrationRefusesWhatWouldHideASource(t *testing.T) {
	src := lsSource(new([]string), nil, nil)
	r := newSecretRegistry()
	r.register("npm", src)
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name string
		do   func()
	}{
		{"an empty module id", func() { r.register("", src) }},
		{"a nil source", func() { r.register("cloudflare", nil) }},
		{"a second registration for a module", func() { r.register("npm", src) }},
		{"RegisterLogSecrets with an empty module id", func() { RegisterLogSecrets("", src) }},
		{"RegisterLogSecrets with a nil source", func() { RegisterLogSecrets("npm", nil) }},
	} {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			if msg := lsPanics(t, tc.do); !strings.HasPrefix(msg, "docker: ") {
				t.Errorf("panic %q, want a message that starts with \"docker: \"", msg)
			}
		})
	}
	if _, ok := r.lookup("cloudflare"); ok {
		t.Error("a refused registration was stored")
	}
	if _, ok := r.lookup("npm"); !ok {
		t.Error("the first registration was lost")
	}
}

func TestLsSecretsAreAskedOfTheModuleThatOwnsTheContainer(t *testing.T) {
	h := lsHost(t)
	var asked []string
	r := newSecretRegistry()
	r.register("npm", lsSource(&asked, [][]byte{[]byte("hunter2")}, nil))
	secrets := r.secretsVia(h.docker)
	ctx := context.Background()

	got, err := secrets(ctx, h.env(""), dkID("proxy"))
	if err != nil || !reflect.DeepEqual(got, [][]byte{[]byte("hunter2")}) || !reflect.DeepEqual(asked, []string{dkID("proxy")}) {
		t.Fatalf("secrets of the npm container = %q, %v; asked %v; want hunter2 from one lookup of its id", got, err, asked)
	}
	for _, id := range []string{dkID("tunnel"), dkID("web")} {
		if got, err := secrets(ctx, h.env(""), id); got != nil || err != nil {
			t.Errorf("secrets of %s = %q, %v; want none for a module with no source and for an unlabelled container", id, got, err)
		}
	}
	if len(asked) != 1 {
		t.Errorf("the npm source was asked %v, want only the npm container", asked)
	}
}

func TestLsSecretsFailWhenTheContainerOrItsSourceFails(t *testing.T) {
	h := lsHost(t)
	broken := errors.New("sealed store unreadable")
	r := newSecretRegistry()
	r.register("npm", lsSource(new([]string), nil, broken))
	secrets := r.secretsVia(h.docker)
	ctx := context.Background()

	if got, err := secrets(ctx, h.env(""), dkID("proxy")); !errors.Is(err, broken) || got != nil {
		t.Errorf("a failing source: %q, %v; want its error and no secrets", got, err)
	}
	if _, err := secrets(ctx, h.env(""), "ghost"); errcode.From(err).Code != errcode.NotFound {
		t.Errorf("an absent container: %v, want %s", err, errcode.NotFound)
	}
	h.docker.fail("InspectContainer", fmt.Errorf("%w: dial unix", dockerapi.ErrUnavailable))
	if _, err := secrets(ctx, h.env(""), dkID("proxy")); errcode.From(err).Code != errcode.DockerEngineUnavailable {
		t.Errorf("an unreachable Engine: %v, want %s", err, errcode.DockerEngineUnavailable)
	}
}

func TestLsContainerLogsMaskWhatTheRegisteredSourceReturns(t *testing.T) {
	h, k, _ := rdLogsHost(t, nil, nil, rdOut("token=hun"), rdOut("ter2 ok\n"))
	r := newSecretRegistry()
	r.register("npm", lsSource(new([]string), [][]byte{[]byte("hunter2")}, nil))
	k.d.secrets = r.secretsVia(h.docker)
	raw, err := rdRead(t, k.containerLogs(), h, `{"container":"proxy"}`)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	rdSame(t, raw, `{"lines":[{"stream":"stdout","text":"token=[redacted] ok"}]}`)
}

func TestLsContainerLogsOfAnOwnedContainerFailBeforeTheEngineWhenTheSourceFails(t *testing.T) {
	h, k, d := rdLogsHost(t, nil, nil, rdOut("hunter2\n"))
	broken := errors.New("sealed store unreadable")
	r := newSecretRegistry()
	r.register("npm", lsSource(new([]string), nil, broken))
	k.d.secrets = r.secretsVia(h.docker)
	raw, err := rdRead(t, k.containerLogs(), h, `{"container":"proxy"}`)
	if !errors.Is(err, broken) || len(raw) != 0 || len(d.opts) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("answer %s, error %v, %d Logs requests: want the source's error and no request for logs", raw, err, len(d.opts))
	}
}
```

Run: `mkdir -p /root/.cache/w6a-tmp/4 && export TMPDIR=/root/.cache/w6a-tmp/4 GOTOOLCHAIN=local && timeout 300 go test -count=1 -run 'TestLs|TestDKProductionDK' ./internal/modules/docker/`
Expected: FAIL to build, `undefined: newSecretRegistry` and `undefined: SecretSource` (and `RegisterLogSecrets`). After a stub makes it build, `TestDKProductionDK` would fail because production `secrets` still answers `nil, nil`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

1. `logsecrets.go`: declare `SecretSource` and `RegisterLogSecrets(module string, src SecretSource)` with a doc comment that says it is called only from `init` functions, that the module id is the value of the `darkpanel.module` label, and that the source returns the secrets currently placed in that module's container. `RegisterLogSecrets` delegates to `logSecretRegistry.register`.
2. `secretRegistry` holds `map[string]SecretSource` behind a `sync.RWMutex`. `register` panics, in this order and before it stores anything, with `docker: RegisterLogSecrets needs a module id` for an empty module, `docker: RegisterLogSecrets needs a source for module "<module>"` for a nil source, and `docker: log secrets of module "<module>" are already registered` for a second registration; otherwise it stores the source. `lookup` returns the stored source and whether one exists. `newSecretRegistry` returns an empty registry; `var logSecretRegistry = newSecretRegistry()` is the only instance production uses.
3. `secretsVia(d dockerAPI)` returns the function that becomes `deps.secrets`: it inspects the container by the id it is given through `d.InspectContainer` (an error is returned through `engineError("container", id, err)`), reads the owner with `ownerOf(c.Config.Labels)`, returns `nil, nil` when the owner is empty or has no registered source, and otherwise returns exactly what the source returns for `c.ID` (its error unwrapped, so callers can match it with `errors.Is`).
4. `deps.go`: in `productionDK` build the Engine client once (`client := newDockerClient(socket)`), use it for `docker`, and set `secrets: logSecretRegistry.secretsVia(client)`. Update the comment on the `secrets` field of `deps` to say production reads the registry. `dk.logFilter` and its two callers do not change: an unlabelled container still never reaches `deps.secrets`, and an error from it still fails the read before any log is opened.
5. Do not add a registration in this task and do not write `logSecretRegistry` from any test.

```ts
import { expect, test } from "vitest"
import { npInstance, npInstances } from "./fixtures"
import {
  NPM_ACTION_VERSION,
  NPM_MODULE,
  isInstanceStatus,
  isInstancesOutput,
  npmFleetKey,
  npmKey,
  type AccessListList,
  type AdminExposure,
  type AdminPassword,
  type CertificateList,
  type HostInstance,
  type InstancesOutput,
  type ProxyHostList,
} from "./types"

type Json = Record<string, unknown>

function tyClone<T>(v: T): Json {
  return structuredClone(v) as Json
}

test("the module id and the action version are the npm module's", () => {
  expect(NPM_MODULE).toBe("npm")
  expect(NPM_ACTION_VERSION).toBe(1)
})

test("npmKey names one read of a host under its resources and npmFleetKey names the fleet read", () => {
  expect(npmKey("h1", "instance.status")).toEqual(["resource", "h1", "npm.instance.status"])
  expect(npmKey("h2", "proxyhost.list")).toEqual(["resource", "h2", "npm.proxyhost.list"])
  expect(npmFleetKey).toEqual(["npm", "instances"])
})

test("isInstanceStatus accepts the agent's JSON, with the admin exposure null, absent or set", () => {
  expect(isInstanceStatus(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(npInstance())))).toBe(true)
  const absent = tyClone(npInstance())
  delete absent.adminExposure
  expect(isInstanceStatus(absent)).toBe(true)
  const exposed = npInstance({ public: "confirmed", adminExposure: { hostname: "npm.example.com", proxyHostId: 4, certificateId: 5 } })
  expect(isInstanceStatus(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(exposed)))).toBe(true)
  expect(isInstanceStatus(npInstance({ deployed: false, containerId: "", image: "", running: false, apiReady: false, adminEmail: "" }))).toBe(true)
})

test("isInstanceStatus refuses what is not a complete status", () => {
  const status = (over: Json) => ({ ...tyClone(npInstance()), ...over })
  const rows: [string, unknown][] = [
    ["null", null],
    ["a string", "status"],
    ["an array", []],
    ["an empty object", {}],
    ["a deployed that is a string", status({ deployed: "yes" })],
    ["a missing containerId", status({ containerId: undefined })],
    ["a pinnedImage that is a number", status({ pinnedImage: 1 })],
    ["a running that is a string", status({ running: "yes" })],
    ["a missing apiReady", status({ apiReady: undefined })],
    ["an unknown public value", status({ public: "maybe" })],
    ["a missing adminEmail", status({ adminEmail: undefined })],
    ["an adminExposure that is a string", status({ adminExposure: "npm.example.com" })],
    ["an adminExposure without a hostname", status({ adminExposure: { proxyHostId: 4, certificateId: 5 } })],
    ["an adminExposure with a proxyHostId that is a string", status({ adminExposure: { hostname: "npm.example.com", proxyHostId: "4", certificateId: 5 } })],
    ["a missing upgradeAvailable", status({ upgradeAvailable: undefined })],
  ]
  for (const [name, value] of rows) expect(isInstanceStatus(value), name).toBe(false)
})

test("isInstancesOutput accepts the controller's JSON and an empty fleet", () => {
  expect(isInstancesOutput(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(npInstances())))).toBe(true)
  expect(isInstancesOutput({ instances: [] })).toBe(true)
})

test("isInstancesOutput refuses what is not a complete answer", () => {
  const host = tyClone(npInstances().instances[0])
  const rows: [string, unknown][] = [
    ["null", null],
    ["an empty object", {}],
    ["instances that are null", { instances: null }],
    ["an instance that is a string", { instances: ["h1"] }],
    ["an instance without a hostId", { instances: [{ ...host, hostId: undefined }] }],
    ["a hostName that is a number", { instances: [{ ...host, hostName: 1 }] }],
    ["a status that is not a status", { instances: [{ ...host, status: { deployed: true } }] }],
  ]
  for (const [name, value] of rows) expect(isInstancesOutput(value), name).toBe(false)
})

// Vitest strips types, so this test is checked by `pnpm typecheck`: a
// missing, misspelled or mistyped member of the Go wire types fails the
// compilation of this file.
test("the wire types carry every member the agent and the controller send", () => {
  const exposure: AdminExposure = { hostname: "npm.example.com", proxyHostId: 4, certificateId: 5 }
  const host: HostInstance = { hostId: "h1", hostName: "edge-1", status: npInstance({ adminExposure: exposure }) }
  const fleet: InstancesOutput = { instances: [host] }
  const hosts: ProxyHostList = {
    items: [{
      id: 1, domains: ["app.example.com"], forwardScheme: "https", forwardHost: "web", forwardPort: 443, certificateId: 3, accessListId: 2,
      sslForced: true, hsts: true, websockets: true, reserved: "npm.admin.expose", unclaimed: false,
    }],
  }
  const lists: AccessListList = {
    items: [{ id: 2, name: "office", satisfyAny: true, clients: [{ address: "198.51.100.7", directive: "deny" }], reserved: "", unclaimed: true }],
  }
  const certs: CertificateList = {
    items: [{ id: 3, name: "app.example.com", domains: ["app.example.com"], expiresOn: "2027-01-31T10:00:00.000Z", reserved: "", unclaimed: false }],
  }
  const password: AdminPassword = { email: "ops@example.com", password: "s3cret" }
  expect(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(fleet)) as InstancesOutput).toEqual(fleet)
  expect([hosts, lists, certs, password]).toHaveLength(4)
})
```

```ts
import { expect, test } from "vitest"
import { npAccessList, npCertificate, npInstance, npInstances, npProxyHost } from "./fixtures"
import { isInstanceStatus, isInstancesOutput } from "./types"

const FX_IMAGE = "jc21/nginx-proxy-manager:2.13.5@sha256:" + "ab".repeat(32)

test("npInstance is a running, ready, deployed instance on the pinned image that is not public", () => {
  expect(isInstanceStatus(npInstance())).toBe(true)
  expect(npInstance()).toEqual({
    deployed: true,
    containerId: "c0ffee00".repeat(8),
    image: FX_IMAGE,
    pinnedImage: FX_IMAGE,
    running: true,
    apiReady: true,
    public: "no",
    adminEmail: "ops@example.com",
    adminExposure: null,
    upgradeAvailable: false,
  })
})

test("npInstance overrides replace a member and each call returns a new object", () => {
  const exposed = npInstance({ public: "confirmed", adminExposure: { hostname: "npm.example.com", proxyHostId: 4, certificateId: 5 } })
  expect([exposed.public, exposed.adminExposure?.hostname, exposed.running]).toEqual(["confirmed", "npm.example.com", true])
  const none = npInstance({ deployed: false, containerId: "", running: false, apiReady: false })
  expect([none.deployed, none.containerId, none.image]).toEqual([false, "", FX_IMAGE])
  const first = npInstance()
  first.running = false
  expect(npInstance().running).toBe(true)
})

test("npProxyHost, npAccessList and npCertificate describe one exposure that is not reserved", () => {
  expect(npProxyHost()).toEqual({
    id: 1,
    domains: ["app.example.com"],
    forwardScheme: "http",
    forwardHost: "web",
    forwardPort: 8080,
    certificateId: 3,
    accessListId: 0,
    sslForced: true,
    hsts: true,
    websockets: false,
    reserved: "",
    unclaimed: false,
  })
  expect(npAccessList()).toEqual({
    id: 2,
    name: "office",
    satisfyAny: false,
    clients: [{ address: "203.0.113.0/24", directive: "allow" }],
    reserved: "",
    unclaimed: false,
  })
  expect(npCertificate()).toEqual({
    id: 3,
    name: "app.example.com",
    domains: ["app.example.com"],
    expiresOn: "2027-01-31T10:00:00.000Z",
    reserved: "",
    unclaimed: false,
  })
  expect(npProxyHost({ reserved: "npm.admin.expose", unclaimed: true })).toEqual({ ...npProxyHost(), reserved: "npm.admin.expose", unclaimed: true })
  expect(npAccessList({ name: "darkpanel-c1-4" }).name).toBe("darkpanel-c1-4")
  expect(npCertificate({ domains: ["a.example.com", "b.example.com"] }).domains).toEqual(["a.example.com", "b.example.com"])
})

  expect(npInstances()).toEqual({ instances: [{ hostId: "01JD0000000000000000000011", hostName: "edge-1", status: npInstance() }] })
  expect(npInstances({ instances: [] })).toEqual({ instances: [] })
})
```

Create `web/src/modules/npm/npm-change.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react"
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event"
import { expect, test, vi } from "vitest"
import type { Change, Plan } from "@/api/types"
import { renderWithProviders, stubFetch, type StubHandler } from "@/test/utils"
import { NpmChange, NpmControllerChange } from "./npm-change"
import { npmFleetKey, npmKey } from "./types"

const NC_ACTION = "npm.proxyhost.add"
const NC_INPUT = { domains: ["app.example.com"], forwardScheme: "http", forwardHost: "web", forwardPort: 8080 }
const NC_DONE = { id: "c1", hostId: "h1", state: "completed", class: "compensable", error: null, output: null, revertDeadline: null }

function ncPlan(action: string, hostId: string | null, over: Partial<Plan> = {}): Plan {
  return {
    planId: "p1", hostId, action, version: 1, inputHash: "h",
    diff: [{ op: "+", text: `${action} app.example.com` }], guards: [], expiresAt: "2026-11-03T09:00:00Z", confirmation: null, ...over,
  }
}

function ncAvailable(action: string): StubHandler {
  return { body: { module: "npm", available: true, reason: null, actions: [{ name: action, available: true, reason: null }] } }
}

function ncRender(input: Record<string, unknown> | null, routes: Record<string, StubHandler> = {}, onApplied?: (change: Change) => void) {
  const calls = stubFetch({
    "GET /api/v1/hosts/h1/modules/npm/availability": ncAvailable(NC_ACTION),
    [`POST /api/v1/hosts/h1/actions/${NC_ACTION}/v1/plan`]: { body: ncPlan(NC_ACTION, "h1") },
    [`POST /api/v1/hosts/h1/actions/${NC_ACTION}/v1/apply`]: { body: NC_DONE },
    ...routes,
  })
  const onClose = vi.fn()
  const result = renderWithProviders(
    <NpmChange hostId="h1" action={NC_ACTION} title="Add a proxy host" input={input} onClose={onClose} onApplied={onApplied} />,
  )
  return { calls, onClose, ...result }
}

test("stays closed and asks nothing while the input is null", () => {
  const { calls } = ncRender(null)
  expect(screen.queryByRole("dialog")).toBeNull()
  expect(calls).toEqual([])
})

test("plans the action on the host at version 1 and applies it without a revert timeout", async () => {
  const { calls } = ncRender(NC_INPUT)
  const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Add a proxy host" })
  expect((await within(dialog).findByLabelText("Planned changes")).textContent).toBe("+ npm.proxyhost.add app.example.com")
  expect(within(dialog).queryByLabelText("Revert after (seconds)")).toBeNull()
  const apply = within(dialog).getByRole("button", { name: "Apply" })
  await waitFor(() => expect(apply.hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(false))
  await userEvent.setup().click(apply)
  await waitFor(() => expect(calls.some((c) => c.path.endsWith("/apply"))).toBe(true))
  expect(calls.find((c) => c.path.endsWith("/plan"))?.path).toBe("/api/v1/hosts/h1/actions/npm.proxyhost.add/v1/plan")
  expect(calls.find((c) => c.path.endsWith("/plan"))?.body).toEqual({ input: NC_INPUT })
  expect(calls.find((c) => c.path.endsWith("/apply"))?.body).toEqual({ planId: "p1" })
})

test("a module the host reports unavailable cannot be applied", async () => {
  ncRender(NC_INPUT, {
    "GET /api/v1/hosts/h1/modules/npm/availability": {
      body: { module: "npm", available: false, reason: "Docker 28.0 or later with raw-table protection is required", actions: [] },
    },
  })
  const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Add a proxy host" })
  expect(await within(dialog).findByText("Docker 28.0 or later with raw-table protection is required")).toBeTruthy()
  await within(dialog).findByLabelText("Planned changes")
  expect(within(dialog).getByRole("button", { name: "Apply" }).hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(true)
})

test("onApplied receives the change the apply answered, its output included", async () => {
  const onApplied = vi.fn()
  const { calls } = ncRender(
    NC_INPUT,
    { [`POST /api/v1/hosts/h1/actions/${NC_ACTION}/v1/apply`]: { body: { ...NC_DONE, output: { id: 7 } } } },
    onApplied,
  )
  const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Add a proxy host" })
  const apply = await within(dialog).findByRole("button", { name: "Apply" })
  await waitFor(() => expect(apply.hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(false))
  await userEvent.setup().click(apply)
  await waitFor(() => expect(onApplied).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1))
  expect(onApplied.mock.calls[0]?.[0]).toMatchObject({ id: "c1", output: { id: 7 } })
  expect(calls.filter((c) => c.path.endsWith("/apply"))).toHaveLength(1)
})

test("closing the dialog reports the close and refetches that host's resources and the fleet read only", async () => {
  const { client, onClose } = ncRender(NC_INPUT)
  client.setQueryData(npmKey("h1", "instance.status"), {})
  client.setQueryData(npmKey("h1", "proxyhost.list"), {})
  client.setQueryData(npmKey("h2", "instance.status"), {})
  client.setQueryData(npmFleetKey, {})
  client.setQueryData(["tailnet", "policy"], {})
  const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Add a proxy host" })
  await within(dialog).findByLabelText("Planned changes")
  await userEvent.setup().keyboard("{Escape}")
  await waitFor(() => expect(onClose).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1))
  expect(client.getQueryState(npmKey("h1", "instance.status"))?.isInvalidated).toBe(true)
  expect(client.getQueryState(npmKey("h1", "proxyhost.list"))?.isInvalidated).toBe(true)
  expect(client.getQueryState(npmFleetKey)?.isInvalidated).toBe(true)
  expect(client.getQueryState(npmKey("h2", "instance.status"))?.isInvalidated).toBe(false)
  expect(client.getQueryState(["tailnet", "policy"])?.isInvalidated).toBe(false)
})

test("a controller change plans on the controller without a host or an availability check", async () => {
  const action = "npm.expose.container"
  const input = { hostId: "h1", container: "web", port: 8080, network: "app", domains: ["app.example.com"] }
  const calls = stubFetch({ [`POST /api/v1/actions/${action}/v1/plan`]: { body: ncPlan(action, null) } })
  renderWithProviders(<NpmControllerChange action={action} title="Expose web" input={input} onClose={vi.fn()} />)
  const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Expose web" })
  expect(within(dialog).getByText("npm.expose.container/v1 on the controller")).toBeTruthy()
  expect((await within(dialog).findByLabelText("Planned changes")).textContent).toBe("+ npm.expose.container app.example.com")
  expect(calls.map((c) => [c.method, c.path, c.body])).toEqual([["POST", "/api/v1/actions/npm.expose.container/v1/plan", { input }]])
})

test("a controller change stays closed for a null input", () => {
  const calls = stubFetch({})
  renderWithProviders(<NpmControllerChange action="npm.expose.container" title="Expose web" input={null} onClose={vi.fn()} />)
  expect(screen.queryByRole("dialog")).toBeNull()
  expect(calls).toEqual([])
})

test("closing a controller change refetches the npm reads and the input's host, not another host or module", async () => {
  const action = "npm.admin.unexpose"
  stubFetch({ [`POST /api/v1/actions/${action}/v1/plan`]: { body: ncPlan(action, null) } })
  const onClose = vi.fn()
  const { client } = renderWithProviders(
    <NpmControllerChange action={action} title="Remove the admin exposure" input={{ hostId: "h1", hostname: "npm.example.com" }} onClose={onClose} />,
  )
  client.setQueryData(npmFleetKey, {})
  client.setQueryData(npmKey("h1", "instance.status"), {})
  client.setQueryData(npmKey("h2", "instance.status"), {})
  client.setQueryData(["tailnet", "policy"], {})
  const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Remove the admin exposure" })
  await within(dialog).findByLabelText("Planned changes")
  await userEvent.setup().keyboard("{Escape}")
  await waitFor(() => expect(onClose).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1))
  expect(client.getQueryState(npmFleetKey)?.isInvalidated).toBe(true)
  expect(client.getQueryState(npmKey("h1", "instance.status"))?.isInvalidated).toBe(true)
  expect(client.getQueryState(npmKey("h2", "instance.status"))?.isInvalidated).toBe(false)
  expect(client.getQueryState(["tailnet", "policy"])?.isInvalidated).toBe(false)
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

1. `types.ts` (pattern: `docker/types.ts` and `derp/types.ts`). Export `NPM_MODULE = "npm"` and `NPM_ACTION_VERSION = 1`. `npmKey(hostId: string, name: string): unknown[]` returns `["resource", hostId, "npm." + name]`; `npmFleetKey` is the array `["npm", "instances"]`. Export one interface per wire type of Contracts "NPM wire shapes" that the web reads, with exactly these members and no others:
   - `AdminExposure { hostname: string; proxyHostId: number; certificateId: number }`.
   - `InstanceStatus { deployed: boolean; containerId: string; image: string; pinnedImage: string; running: boolean; apiReady: boolean; public: "no" | "confirmed"; adminEmail: string; adminExposure?: AdminExposure | null; upgradeAvailable: boolean }`.
   - `HostInstance { hostId: string; hostName: string; status: InstanceStatus }` and `InstancesOutput { instances: HostInstance[] }`.
   - `AccessClientInput { address: string; directive: "allow" | "deny" }`.
   - `ProxyHostView { id: number; domains: string[]; forwardScheme: "http" | "https"; forwardHost: string; forwardPort: number; certificateId: number; accessListId: number; sslForced: boolean; hsts: boolean; websockets: boolean; reserved: string; unclaimed: boolean }`, `AccessListView { id: number; name: string; satisfyAny: boolean; clients: AccessClientInput[]; reserved: string; unclaimed: boolean }`, `CertificateView { id: number; name: string; domains: string[]; expiresOn: string; reserved: string; unclaimed: boolean }`.
   - `ProxyHostList { items: ProxyHostView[] }`, `AccessListList { items: AccessListView[] }`, `CertificateList { items: CertificateView[] }`.
   - `AdminPassword { email: string; password: string }`.
2. `types.ts` guards, written as `derp/types.ts` writes its own: a private `isRecord` and the exported `isInstanceStatus(v: unknown): v is InstanceStatus` and `isInstancesOutput(v: unknown): v is InstancesOutput`. `isInstanceStatus` requires every member with its type, `public` to be `"no"` or `"confirmed"`, and `adminExposure` to be `undefined`, `null` or an object with a string `hostname` and number `proxyHostId` and `certificateId`. `isInstancesOutput` requires an `instances` array whose items each have string `hostId` and `hostName` and a `status` for which `isInstanceStatus` holds. No guard for the list types: pages read them through `readAction` with a cast-free reader of their own (Tasks 8 and 9).
3. `fixtures.ts` (pattern: `docker/fixtures.ts`): each factory takes `over: Partial<T> = {}` and returns a new object. The default values are exactly those `fixtures.test.ts` asserts. The image is `"jc21/nginx-proxy-manager:2.13.5@sha256:" + "ab".repeat(32)` for both `image` and `pinnedImage`. A short comment says the fixtures stand for one deployed instance and the proxy host, access list and certificate of one exposure; they carry no secret.
4. `npm-change.tsx`:
   - `NpmChange({ hostId, action, title, input, onClose, onApplied? })`: props interface exported as `NpmChangeProps`. Renders `ChangeDialog` open when `input !== null`, `target` `{ name: action, version: NPM_ACTION_VERSION, hostId, module: NPM_MODULE }` (this turns on the availability check), `input={input ?? undefined}` and `onApplied` passed through unchanged. On close (`onOpenChange(false)`) it calls `onClose()` and then invalidates the query keys `["resource", hostId]` and `npmFleetKey`; a dialog that opens does nothing.
   - `NpmControllerChange({ action, title, input, onClose })`: props interface exported as `NpmControllerChangeProps`. Same dialog with `target` `{ name: action, version: NPM_ACTION_VERSION }` (no host, no module: no availability check). On close it calls `onClose()` and invalidates `[NPM_MODULE]` (which covers `npmFleetKey`) and, when `input` holds a string `hostId`, `["resource", hostId]`, because a composite changes what that host's NPM page reads.
   - A comment above each component says in one sentence what it refreshes and why (the page reads agent actions under `["resource", hostId, ...]`; the banner reads the fleet key).
5. Do not edit `routes.tsx`, `container-actions.tsx` or anything outside `web/src/modules/npm/`.

```go
package npmapi

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"io"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"reflect"
	"strconv"
	"sync"
	"testing"
)

// apCall is one request the test server received.
type apCall struct {
	Method, Path, Query string
	Header              http.Header
	Body                string
}

// apServer answers every request with one scripted answer and records the requests.
type apServer struct {
	srv   *httptest.Server
	mu    sync.Mutex
	calls []apCall
}

func apStart(t *testing.T, status int, body string) *apServer {
	t.Helper()
	s := &apServer{}
	s.srv = httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		b, _ := io.ReadAll(r.Body)
		s.mu.Lock()
		s.calls = append(s.calls, apCall{Method: r.Method, Path: r.URL.Path, Query: r.URL.RawQuery, Header: r.Header.Clone(), Body: string(b)})
		s.mu.Unlock()
		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
		w.WriteHeader(status)
		_, _ = io.WriteString(w, body)
	}))
	t.Cleanup(s.srv.Close)
	return s
}

// client is a client of the server that holds a token.
func (s *apServer) client() *Client { return New(s.srv.URL, nil).WithToken("tok-1") }

// only returns the one request the server received.
func (s *apServer) only(t *testing.T) apCall {
	t.Helper()
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	if len(s.calls) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("the server received %d requests, want 1: %+v", len(s.calls), s.calls)
	}
	return s.calls[0]
}

// apSame fails unless got and want are the same JSON document.
func apSame(t *testing.T, got, want string) {
	t.Helper()
	var g, w any
	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(got), &g); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("request body %q is not JSON: %v", got, err)
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(want), &w); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("expectation %q is not JSON: %v", want, err)
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(g, w) {
		t.Errorf("request body\n%s\nwant\n%s", got, want)
	}
}

func TestApHealthIsAnonymousAndReadsStatusAndSetup(t *testing.T) {
	for _, setup := range []bool{false, true} {
		s := apStart(t, 200, `{"status":"OK","setup":`+strconv.FormatBool(setup)+`,"version":{"major":2,"minor":13,"revision":5}}`)
		h, err := New(s.srv.URL, nil).Health(t.Context())
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if h != (Health{Status: "OK", Setup: setup}) {
			t.Errorf("health = %+v, want status OK and setup %t", h, setup)
		}
		c := s.only(t)
		if c.Method != http.MethodGet || c.Path != "/api" || c.Header.Get("Authorization") != "" {
			t.Errorf("request %+v, want an anonymous GET /api", c)
		}
	}
}

func TestApCreateAdminSendsTheAdminUserBody(t *testing.T) {
	s := apStart(t, 201, `{"id":1,"email":"ops@example.com","roles":["admin"]}`)
	if err := New(s.srv.URL, nil).CreateAdmin(t.Context(), "ops@example.com", "correct-horse"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	c := s.only(t)
	if c.Method != http.MethodPost || c.Path != "/api/users" || c.Header.Get("Authorization") != "" || c.Header.Get("Content-Type") != "application/json" {
		t.Errorf("request %+v, want an anonymous JSON POST /api/users", c)
	}
	apSame(t, c.Body, `{"name":"Darkpanel","nickname":"darkpanel","email":"ops@example.com","roles":["admin"],"is_disabled":false,`+
		`"auth":{"type":"password","secret":"correct-horse"}}`)
}

func TestApTokenSendsTheCredentialsAndReturnsTheToken(t *testing.T) {
	s := apStart(t, 200, `{"token":"tok-77","expires":"2026-11-03T10:00:00.000Z"}`)
	tok, err := New(s.srv.URL, nil).Token(t.Context(), "ops@example.com", "correct-horse")
	if err != nil || tok != "tok-77" {
		t.Fatalf("token = %q, %v; want tok-77", tok, err)
	}
	c := s.only(t)
	if c.Method != http.MethodPost || c.Path != "/api/tokens" || c.Header.Get("Authorization") != "" {
		t.Errorf("request %+v, want an anonymous POST /api/tokens", c)
	}
	apSame(t, c.Body, `{"identity":"ops@example.com","secret":"correct-horse"}`)

	empty := apStart(t, 200, `{"token":"","expires":""}`)
	if tok, err := New(empty.srv.URL, nil).Token(t.Context(), "ops@example.com", "correct-horse"); err == nil || tok != "" {
		t.Errorf("an answer without a token gave %q, %v; want an error", tok, err)
	}
}

func TestApWithTokenReturnsACopyThatSendsTheBearerToken(t *testing.T) {
	s := apStart(t, 200, `[]`)
	anon := New(s.srv.URL, nil)
	authed := anon.WithToken("abc")
	if authed == anon {
		t.Fatal("WithToken returned the same client")
	}
	if _, err := authed.ProxyHosts(t.Context()); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := anon.ProxyHosts(t.Context()); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	if len(s.calls) != 2 || s.calls[0].Header.Get("Authorization") != "Bearer abc" || s.calls[1].Header.Get("Authorization") != "" {
		t.Errorf("calls %+v, want a bearer token on the first and none on the second", s.calls)
	}
}

type apCountingTransport struct{ n *int }

func (c apCountingTransport) RoundTrip(r *http.Request) (*http.Response, error) {
	*c.n++
	return http.DefaultTransport.RoundTrip(r)
}

func TestApUsesTheHTTPClientItIsGiven(t *testing.T) {
	s := apStart(t, 200, `{"status":"OK","setup":true}`)
	n := 0
	c := New(s.srv.URL, &http.Client{Transport: apCountingTransport{n: &n}})
	if _, err := c.Health(t.Context()); err != nil || n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("Health = %v after %d round trips through the given client, want 1", err, n)
	}
}

type apRow struct {
	name   string
	call   func(c *Client) (any, error)
	method string
	path   string
	query  string
	status int
	answer string
	body   string
	want   any
}

func TestApObjectCallsUseNPMsEndpoints(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := t.Context()
	const hosts, lists, certs = "/api/nginx/proxy-hosts", "/api/nginx/access-lists", "/api/nginx/certificates"
	rows := []apRow{
		{name: "list proxy hosts", call: func(c *Client) (any, error) { return c.ProxyHosts(ctx) },
			method: "GET", path: hosts, status: 200, answer: "[" + apHostAnswer + "]", want: []ProxyHost{apHost}},
		{name: "get a proxy host", call: func(c *Client) (any, error) { return c.ProxyHost(ctx, 3) },
			method: "GET", path: hosts + "/3", status: 200, answer: apHostAnswer, want: apHost},
		{name: "create a proxy host", call: func(c *Client) (any, error) { return c.CreateProxyHost(ctx, apHostIn) },
			method: "POST", path: hosts, status: 201, answer: apHostAnswer, body: apHostBody, want: apHost},
		{name: "update a proxy host", call: func(c *Client) (any, error) { return c.UpdateProxyHost(ctx, 3, apHostIn) },
			method: "PUT", path: hosts + "/3", status: 200, answer: apHostAnswer, body: apHostBody, want: apHost},
		{name: "delete a proxy host", call: func(c *Client) (any, error) { return nil, c.DeleteProxyHost(ctx, 3) },
			method: "DELETE", path: hosts + "/3", status: 200, answer: `true`},
		{name: "list access lists", call: func(c *Client) (any, error) { return c.AccessLists(ctx) },
			method: "GET", path: lists, query: "expand=clients", status: 200, answer: "[" + apListAnswer + "]", want: []AccessList{apList}},
		{name: "get an access list", call: func(c *Client) (any, error) { return c.AccessList(ctx, 5) },
			method: "GET", path: lists + "/5", query: "expand=clients", status: 200, answer: apListAnswer, want: apList},
		{name: "create an access list", call: func(c *Client) (any, error) { return c.CreateAccessList(ctx, apListIn) },
			method: "POST", path: lists, status: 201, answer: apListAnswer, body: apListBody, want: apList},
		{name: "delete an access list", call: func(c *Client) (any, error) { return nil, c.DeleteAccessList(ctx, 5) },
			method: "DELETE", path: lists + "/5", status: 200, answer: `true`},
		{name: "list certificates", call: func(c *Client) (any, error) { return c.Certificates(ctx) },
			method: "GET", path: certs, status: 200, answer: "[" + apCertAnswer + "]", want: []Certificate{apCert}},
		{name: "get a certificate", call: func(c *Client) (any, error) { return c.Certificate(ctx, 9) },
			method: "GET", path: certs + "/9", status: 200, answer: apCertAnswer, want: apCert},
		{name: "create a certificate", call: func(c *Client) (any, error) { return c.CreateCertificate(ctx, apCertIn) },
			method: "POST", path: certs, status: 201, answer: apCertAnswer, body: apCertBody, want: apCert},
		{name: "delete a certificate", call: func(c *Client) (any, error) { return nil, c.DeleteCertificate(ctx, 9) },
			method: "DELETE", path: certs + "/9", status: 200, answer: `true`},
	}
	for _, tc := range rows {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			s := apStart(t, tc.status, tc.answer)
			got, err := tc.call(s.client())
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			c := s.only(t)
			if c.Method != tc.method || c.Path != tc.path || c.Query != tc.query {
				t.Errorf("request %s %s?%s, want %s %s?%s", c.Method, c.Path, c.Query, tc.method, tc.path, tc.query)
			}
			if c.Header.Get("Authorization") != "Bearer tok-1" || c.Header.Get("Accept") != "application/json" {
				t.Errorf("headers %v, want the bearer token and Accept: application/json", c.Header)
			}
			if tc.body == "" {
				if c.Body != "" {
					t.Errorf("request body %q, want none", c.Body)
				}
			} else {
				apSame(t, c.Body, tc.body)
				if c.Header.Get("Content-Type") != "application/json" {
					t.Errorf("Content-Type %q, want application/json", c.Header.Get("Content-Type"))
				}
			}
			if !reflect.DeepEqual(got, tc.want) {
				t.Errorf("answer %#v, want %#v", got, tc.want)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestApBodiesNeverSendNullLists(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := t.Context()
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name string
		call func(c *Client) error
		keys []string
	}{
		{"a proxy host", func(c *Client) error {
			_, err := c.CreateProxyHost(ctx, ProxyHostBody{ForwardScheme: "http", ForwardHost: "web", ForwardPort: 80})
			return err
		}, []string{"domain_names", "locations"}},
		{"an access list", func(c *Client) error {
			_, err := c.CreateAccessList(ctx, AccessListBody{Name: "office"})
			return err
		}, []string{"clients"}},
		{"a certificate", func(c *Client) error {
			_, err := c.CreateCertificate(ctx, CertificateBody{Provider: "letsencrypt"})
			return err
		}, []string{"domain_names"}},
	} {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			s := apStart(t, 201, `{}`)
			if err := tc.call(s.client()); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			var m map[string]any
			if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(s.only(t).Body), &m); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			for _, k := range tc.keys {
				if arr, ok := m[k].([]any); !ok || len(arr) != 0 {
					t.Errorf("%s = %#v, want an empty array", k, m[k])
				}
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestApCertificateBodyIsAlwaysLetsEncryptAndOmitsAnEmptyName(t *testing.T) {
	s := apStart(t, 201, apCertAnswer)
	_, err := s.client().CreateCertificate(t.Context(), CertificateBody{
		Provider: "other", DomainNames: []string{"a.example.com", "b.example.com"},
		Meta: CertificateMeta{LetsencryptEmail: "ops@example.com", LetsencryptAgree: false},
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	apSame(t, s.only(t).Body, `{"provider":"letsencrypt","domain_names":["a.example.com","b.example.com"],`+
		`"meta":{"letsencrypt_email":"ops@example.com","letsencrypt_agree":true}}`)
}

func TestApListsAreNeverNil(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := t.Context()
	for _, answer := range []string{`[]`, `null`} {
		s := apStart(t, 200, answer)
		c := s.client()
		hosts, err1 := c.ProxyHosts(ctx)
		lists, err2 := c.AccessLists(ctx)
		certs, err3 := c.Certificates(ctx)
		if err1 != nil || err2 != nil || err3 != nil {
			t.Fatalf("errors %v, %v, %v", err1, err2, err3)
		}
		if hosts == nil || lists == nil || certs == nil || len(hosts)+len(lists)+len(certs) != 0 {
			t.Errorf("answer %s gave %#v, %#v, %#v, want empty non-nil slices", answer, hosts, lists, certs)
		}
	}
}
```

Create `internal/modules/npm/npmapi/errors_test.go`:

```go
package npmapi

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

func TestApErrorsCarryNPMsMessageAndNeverTheBody(t *testing.T) {
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name     string
		status   int
		body     string
		message  string
		notFound bool
		client   bool
	}{
		{"a refused duplicate", 400, `{"error":{"code":400,"message":"app.example.com is already in use"}}`, "app.example.com is already in use", false, true},
		{"a missing object", 404, `{"error":{"code":404,"message":"Not Found"}}`, "Not Found", true, true},
		{"an unauthorized call", 401, `{"error":{"code":401,"message":"Unauthorized"}}`, "Unauthorized", false, true},
		{"a server failure with NPM's message", 500, `{"error":{"code":500,"message":"database is locked"}}`, "database is locked", false, false},
		{"an HTML body from a proxy", 502, `<html>token=leak-hunter2</html>`, "Bad Gateway", false, false},
		{"an empty body", 503, ``, "Service Unavailable", false, false},
		{"JSON without error.message", 400, `{"password":"leak-hunter2"}`, "Bad Request", false, true},
	} {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			s := apStart(t, tc.status, tc.body)
			err := s.client().DeleteProxyHost(t.Context(), 3)
			var ae *APIError
			if !errors.As(err, &ae) || ae.Status != tc.status || ae.Message != tc.message {
				t.Fatalf("error %v, want an *APIError with status %d and message %q", err, tc.status, tc.message)
			}
			if IsNotFound(err) != tc.notFound || IsClientError(err) != tc.client {
				t.Errorf("IsNotFound %t and IsClientError %t, want %t and %t", IsNotFound(err), IsClientError(err), tc.notFound, tc.client)
			}
			if strings.Contains(err.Error(), "leak") {
				t.Errorf("error text %q carries the response body", err.Error())
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestApAPIErrorText(t *testing.T) {
	err := &APIError{Status: 400, Message: "app.example.com is already in use"}
	if got, want := err.Error(), "npmapi: NPM answered 400: app.example.com is already in use"; got != want {
		t.Errorf("Error() = %q, want %q", got, want)
	}
}

func TestApPredicatesSeeThroughWrappingAndIgnoreOtherErrors(t *testing.T) {
	wrapped := fmt.Errorf("npm: remove the proxy host: %w", &APIError{Status: 404, Message: "Not Found"})
	if !IsNotFound(wrapped) || !IsClientError(wrapped) {
		t.Error("a wrapped 404 is not recognised")
	}
	for _, err := range []error{nil, errors.New("boom"), &APIError{Status: 399}, &APIError{Status: 500}} {
		if IsNotFound(err) || IsClientError(err) {
			t.Errorf("%v was taken for a client error", err)
		}
	}
	if !IsClientError(&APIError{Status: 400}) || !IsClientError(&APIError{Status: 499}) {
		t.Error("the client-error range is not 400 to 499")
	}
}

func TestApTransportAndDecodeFailuresAreNotAPIErrors(t *testing.T) {
	closed := apStart(t, 200, `{}`)
	c := closed.client()
	closed.srv.Close()
	_, err := c.ProxyHosts(t.Context())
	var ae *APIError
	if err == nil || errors.As(err, &ae) || IsClientError(err) || IsNotFound(err) || !strings.HasPrefix(err.Error(), "npmapi: ") {
		t.Errorf("a closed server gave %v, want an npmapi: transport error that proves nothing about NPM", err)
	}

	garbled := apStart(t, 200, `{"id":`)
	if _, err := garbled.client().ProxyHost(t.Context(), 1); err == nil || errors.As(err, &ae) || IsClientError(err) {
		t.Errorf("a truncated answer gave %v, want a decode error that is not an API error", err)
	}

	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(t.Context())
	cancel()
	live := apStart(t, 200, `[]`)
	if _, err := live.client().ProxyHosts(ctx); !errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) {
		t.Errorf("a cancelled context gave %v, want context.Canceled", err)
	}
}
```

Create `internal/modules/npm/fake/server_test.go`:

```go
package fake

import (
	"errors"
	"io"
	"net/http"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/npm/npmapi"
)

const (
	fkEmail    = "ops@example.com"
	fkPassword = "correct-horse"
)

// fkRaw sends one request and returns the status and body; an empty token sends no credentials.
func fkRaw(t *testing.T, s *Server, method, path, token, body string) (int, string) {
	t.Helper()
	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(t.Context(), method, s.URL()+path, strings.NewReader(body))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if token != "" {
		req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+token)
	}
	res, err := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer func() { _ = res.Body.Close() }()
	b, err := io.ReadAll(res.Body)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return res.StatusCode, string(b)
}

// fkLogin seeds the admin when the server has none and returns a client that holds a token, and the token.
func fkLogin(t *testing.T, s *Server) (*npmapi.Client, string) {
	t.Helper()
	if !s.Setup() {
		s.SeedAdmin(fkEmail, fkPassword)
	}
	c := npmapi.New(s.URL(), nil)
	tok, err := c.Token(t.Context(), fkEmail, fkPassword)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return c.WithToken(tok), tok
}

func fkAPIError(t *testing.T, err error) *npmapi.APIError {
	t.Helper()
	var ae *npmapi.APIError
	if !errors.As(err, &ae) {
		t.Fatalf("error %v is not an *npmapi.APIError", err)
	}
	return ae
}

func fkHost(domains ...string) npmapi.ProxyHostBody {
	return npmapi.ProxyHostBody{DomainNames: domains, ForwardScheme: "http", ForwardHost: "web", ForwardPort: 8080}
}

func TestFkSetupIsFalseUntilTheFirstUser(t *testing.T) {
	s := New(t)
	c := npmapi.New(s.URL(), nil)
	ctx := t.Context()
	h, err := c.Health(ctx)
	if err != nil || h.Setup || h.Status != "OK" || s.Setup() {
		t.Fatalf("fresh server: health %+v, error %v, Setup() %t; want status OK and setup false", h, err, s.Setup())
	}
	if _, _, ok := s.Admin(); ok {
		t.Fatal("a fresh server reports an admin")
	}
	for name, body := range map[string]string{
		"fewer than 8 characters": `{"name":"n","nickname":"n","email":"ops@example.com","roles":["admin"],"is_disabled":false,"auth":{"type":"password","secret":"short"}}`,
		"no admin role":           `{"name":"n","nickname":"n","email":"ops@example.com","roles":[],"is_disabled":false,"auth":{"type":"password","secret":"correct-horse"}}`,
		"no email":                `{"name":"n","nickname":"n","email":"","roles":["admin"],"is_disabled":false,"auth":{"type":"password","secret":"correct-horse"}}`,
		"a body that is not JSON": `{`,
	} {
		if status, _ := fkRaw(t, s, http.MethodPost, "/api/users", "", body); status != http.StatusBadRequest || s.Setup() {
			t.Errorf("%s: status %d, Setup() %t; want 400 and setup still false", name, status, s.Setup())
		}
	}
	if err := c.CreateAdmin(ctx, fkEmail, fkPassword); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	h, err = c.Health(ctx)
	if err != nil || !h.Setup || !s.Setup() {
		t.Fatalf("after the first user: health %+v, error %v, Setup() %t; want setup true", h, err, s.Setup())
	}
	if email, password, ok := s.Admin(); !ok || email != fkEmail || password != fkPassword {
		t.Errorf("Admin() = %q, %q, %t; want the created user", email, password, ok)
	}
	if err := c.CreateAdmin(ctx, "second@example.com", fkPassword); fkAPIError(t, err).Status != http.StatusForbidden {
		t.Errorf("a second admin: %v, want 403", err)
	}
}

func TestFkTokensNeedTheAdminsCredentials(t *testing.T) {
	s := New(t)
	c := npmapi.New(s.URL(), nil)
	ctx := t.Context()
	if _, err := c.Token(ctx, fkEmail, fkPassword); fkAPIError(t, err).Status != http.StatusUnauthorized {
		t.Errorf("a token before any user: %v, want 401", err)
	}
	s.SeedAdmin(fkEmail, fkPassword)
	if _, err := c.Token(ctx, fkEmail, "wrong-password"); fkAPIError(t, err).Status != http.StatusUnauthorized {
		t.Errorf("a wrong password: %v, want 401", err)
	}
	if _, err := c.Token(ctx, "other@example.com", fkPassword); fkAPIError(t, err).Status != http.StatusUnauthorized {
		t.Errorf("a wrong identity: %v, want 401", err)
	}
	a, errA := c.Token(ctx, fkEmail, fkPassword)
	b, errB := c.Token(ctx, fkEmail, fkPassword)
	if errA != nil || errB != nil || a == "" || a == b {
		t.Errorf("tokens %q, %q (%v, %v), want two different non-empty tokens", a, b, errA, errB)
	}
}

func TestFkNginxCallsNeedAValidToken(t *testing.T) {
	s := New(t)
	_, tok := fkLogin(t, s)
	for _, p := range []string{"/api/nginx/proxy-hosts", "/api/nginx/access-lists", "/api/nginx/certificates"} {
		for _, method := range []string{http.MethodGet, http.MethodPost} {
			for _, token := range []string{"", "bogus"} {
				status, body := fkRaw(t, s, method, p, token, `{}`)
				if status != http.StatusUnauthorized || !strings.Contains(body, `"message":"Unauthorized"`) {
					t.Errorf("%s %s with token %q: %d %s, want 401 Unauthorized", method, p, token, status, body)
				}
			}
		}
		if status, _ := fkRaw(t, s, http.MethodDelete, p+"/1", "", ""); status != http.StatusUnauthorized {
			t.Errorf("DELETE %s/1 without a token: %d, want 401", p, status)
		}
		if status, _ := fkRaw(t, s, http.MethodGet, p, tok, ""); status != http.StatusOK {
			t.Errorf("GET %s with a valid token: %d, want 200", p, status)
		}
	}
	if n := len(s.ProxyHosts()) + len(s.AccessLists()) + len(s.Certificates()); n != 0 {
		t.Errorf("refused calls created %d objects", n)
	}
}

func TestFkSeedsReturnTheStoredObjectsAndKeepSnapshotsApart(t *testing.T) {
	s := New(t)
	if s.ProxyHosts() == nil || s.AccessLists() == nil || s.Certificates() == nil {
		t.Fatal("the snapshots of an empty server are nil")
	}
	s.SeedAdmin(fkEmail, fkPassword)
	if email, password, ok := s.Admin(); !s.Setup() || !ok || email != fkEmail || password != fkPassword {
		t.Fatalf("SeedAdmin: Setup() %t, Admin() = %q, %q, %t", s.Setup(), email, password, ok)
	}
	h1 := s.SeedProxyHost(npmapi.ProxyHost{DomainNames: []string{"seed.example.com"}, ForwardScheme: "http", ForwardHost: "web", ForwardPort: 80})
	h2 := s.SeedProxyHost(npmapi.ProxyHost{ID: 99, CreatedOn: "2020-01-01T00:00:00.000Z", DomainNames: []string{"two.example.com"}, ForwardScheme: "http", ForwardHost: "web", ForwardPort: 80})
	if h1.ID != 1 || h1.CreatedOn == "" || h2.ID != 2 || h2.CreatedOn != "2020-01-01T00:00:00.000Z" {
		t.Errorf("seeded proxy hosts %+v and %+v, want ids 1 and 2, a stamped created_on and a kept one", h1, h2)
	}
	l := s.SeedAccessList(npmapi.AccessList{Name: "n", Clients: []npmapi.AccessClient{{Address: "203.0.113.7", Directive: "allow"}}})
	c := s.SeedCertificate(npmapi.Certificate{NiceName: "x", Provider: "letsencrypt", DomainNames: []string{"x.example.com"}})
	if l.ID != 1 || l.CreatedOn == "" || c.ID != 1 || c.CreatedOn == "" || c.NiceName != "x" {
		t.Errorf("seeded access list %+v and certificate %+v, want id 1, a stamped created_on and the seeded nice_name kept", l, c)
	}

	got := s.ProxyHosts()
	if len(got) != 2 || got[0].ID != 1 || got[1].ID != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("ProxyHosts() = %+v, want both seeds in id order", got)
	}
	got[0].DomainNames[0] = "mutated.example.com"
	if s.ProxyHosts()[0].DomainNames[0] != "seed.example.com" {
		t.Error("a snapshot shares its slices with the server")
	}

	client, _ := fkLogin(t, s)
	seen, err := client.ProxyHost(t.Context(), 1)
	if err != nil || seen.DomainNames[0] != "seed.example.com" {
		t.Errorf("the client reads %+v, %v, want the seeded host", seen, err)
	}
	if _, err := client.CreateProxyHost(t.Context(), fkHost("seed.example.com")); fkAPIError(t, err).Status != http.StatusBadRequest {
		t.Errorf("a create for a seeded domain: %v, want 400", err)
	}
}

func TestFkFaultsFailOrLoseTheResponseOnce(t *testing.T) {
	s := New(t)
	c, _ := fkLogin(t, s)
	ctx := t.Context()
	const hosts = "/api/nginx/proxy-hosts"

	s.Fail(http.MethodPost, hosts, Fault{Status: http.StatusServiceUnavailable})
	_, err := c.CreateProxyHost(ctx, fkHost("a.example.com"))
	if ae := fkAPIError(t, err); ae.Status != http.StatusServiceUnavailable || npmapi.IsClientError(err) {
		t.Errorf("a status fault: %v, want a 503 that is not a client error", err)
	}
	if n := len(s.ProxyHosts()); n != 0 {
		t.Errorf("a status fault committed %d proxy hosts, want none", n)
	}

	s.Fail(http.MethodPost, hosts, Fault{AfterCommit: true})
	_, err = c.CreateProxyHost(ctx, fkHost("a.example.com"))
	var ae *npmapi.APIError
	if err == nil || errors.As(err, &ae) || npmapi.IsClientError(err) {
		t.Errorf("a lost response: %v, want a transport error", err)
	}
	if got := s.ProxyHosts(); len(got) != 1 || got[0].DomainNames[0] != "a.example.com" {
		t.Errorf("after a lost response the proxy hosts are %+v, want the committed one", got)
	}

	if _, err := c.CreateProxyHost(ctx, fkHost("b.example.com")); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("the next create: %v, want the faults used up", err)
	}

	s.Fail(http.MethodDelete, hosts+"/1", Fault{Status: http.StatusInternalServerError})
	if _, err := c.ProxyHost(ctx, 1); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("a GET of the faulted path: %v, want it unaffected", err)
	}
	if err := c.DeleteProxyHost(ctx, 1); fkAPIError(t, err).Status != http.StatusInternalServerError {
		t.Errorf("the faulted DELETE: %v, want 500", err)
	}
	if err := c.DeleteProxyHost(ctx, 1); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("the DELETE after the fault: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestFkRequestsLogEveryCallIncludingRefusedOnes(t *testing.T) {
	s := New(t)
	ctx := t.Context()
	anon := npmapi.New(s.URL(), nil)
	if _, err := anon.Health(ctx); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	s.SeedAdmin(fkEmail, fkPassword)
	tok, err := anon.Token(ctx, fkEmail, fkPassword)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := anon.WithToken(tok).ProxyHosts(ctx); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := anon.ProxyHosts(ctx); fkAPIError(t, err).Status != http.StatusUnauthorized {
		t.Fatalf("an anonymous list: %v, want 401", err)
	}
	reqs := s.Requests()
	want := [][2]string{{"GET", "/api"}, {"POST", "/api/tokens"}, {"GET", "/api/nginx/proxy-hosts"}, {"GET", "/api/nginx/proxy-hosts"}}
	if len(reqs) != len(want) {
		t.Fatalf("%d requests recorded, want %d: %+v", len(reqs), len(want), reqs)
	}
	for i, w := range want {
		if reqs[i].Method != w[0] || reqs[i].Path != w[1] {
			t.Errorf("request %d = %s %s, want %s %s", i, reqs[i].Method, reqs[i].Path, w[0], w[1])
		}
	}
	if reqs[2].Header.Get("Authorization") != "Bearer "+tok || reqs[3].Header.Get("Authorization") != "" {
		t.Errorf("authorization headers %q and %q, want the token and none", reqs[2].Header.Get("Authorization"), reqs[3].Header.Get("Authorization"))
	}
	if !strings.Contains(string(reqs[1].Body), `"identity":"`+fkEmail+`"`) {
		t.Errorf("the token request body is %q, want the identity", reqs[1].Body)
	}
}
```

Create `internal/modules/npm/fake/objects_test.go`:

```go
package fake

import (
	"net/http"
	"reflect"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/coretest"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/npm/npmapi"
)

func TestFkProxyHostDomainsAreUnique(t *testing.T) {
	s := New(t)
	c, _ := fkLogin(t, s)
	ctx := t.Context()
	a, err := c.CreateProxyHost(ctx, fkHost("a.example.com", "b.example.com"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if a.ID != 1 || a.CreatedOn == "" || a.ModifiedOn == "" {
		t.Errorf("created %+v, want id 1 with created_on and modified_on", a)
	}
	_, err = c.CreateProxyHost(ctx, fkHost("c.example.com", "b.example.com"))
	if ae := fkAPIError(t, err); ae.Status != http.StatusBadRequest || ae.Message != "b.example.com is already in use" || !npmapi.IsClientError(err) {
		t.Errorf("a create with a taken domain: %v, want 400 \"b.example.com is already in use\"", err)
	}
	if n := len(s.ProxyHosts()); n != 1 {
		t.Errorf("%d proxy hosts after a refused create, want 1", n)
	}
	if _, err := c.UpdateProxyHost(ctx, a.ID, fkHost("a.example.com")); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("an update that keeps its own domain: %v", err)
	}
	b, err := c.CreateProxyHost(ctx, fkHost("b.example.com"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("a create for a domain the update freed: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := c.UpdateProxyHost(ctx, b.ID, fkHost("a.example.com")); fkAPIError(t, err).Status != http.StatusBadRequest {
		t.Errorf("an update onto another host's domain: %v, want 400", err)
	}
	if _, err := c.UpdateProxyHost(ctx, 99, fkHost("z.example.com")); !npmapi.IsNotFound(err) {
		t.Errorf("an update of an absent host: %v, want 404", err)
	}
}

func TestFkProxyHostKeepsWhatItWasGiven(t *testing.T) {
	s := New(t)
	c, _ := fkLogin(t, s)
	ctx := t.Context()
	body := npmapi.ProxyHostBody{
		DomainNames: []string{"app.example.com"}, ForwardScheme: "https", ForwardHost: "web", ForwardPort: 8443, CertificateID: 7, AccessListID: 3,
		SSLForced: true, HSTSEnabled: true, AllowWebsocketUpgrade: true, AdvancedConfig: "client_max_body_size 1m;",
		Locations: []npmapi.Location{{Path: "/", ForwardScheme: "https", ForwardHost: "web", ForwardPort: 8443, AdvancedConfig: "proxy_set_header X-Darkpanel-Client $realip_remote_addr;"}},
	}
	created, err := c.CreateProxyHost(ctx, body)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := npmapi.ProxyHost{
		ID: created.ID, CreatedOn: created.CreatedOn, ModifiedOn: created.ModifiedOn, DomainNames: body.DomainNames, ForwardScheme: "https", ForwardHost: "web",
		ForwardPort: 8443, CertificateID: 7, AccessListID: 3, SSLForced: true, HSTSEnabled: true, AllowWebsocketUpgrade: true,
		Locations: body.Locations, AdvancedConfig: "client_max_body_size 1m;",
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(created, want) {
		t.Errorf("created %+v, want %+v", created, want)
	}
	got, err := c.ProxyHost(ctx, created.ID)
	if err != nil || !reflect.DeepEqual(got, created) {
		t.Errorf("read back %+v, %v, want the created object", got, err)
	}
	list, err := c.ProxyHosts(ctx)
	if err != nil || len(list) != 1 || !reflect.DeepEqual(list[0], created) {
		t.Errorf("listed %+v, %v, want the created object", list, err)
	}
}

func TestFkProxyHostValidation(t *testing.T) {
	s := New(t)
	_, tok := fkLogin(t, s)
	for name, body := range map[string]string{
		"no domains":                         `{"domain_names":[],"forward_scheme":"http","forward_host":"web","forward_port":80}`,
		"a scheme other than http and https": `{"domain_names":["a.example.com"],"forward_scheme":"ftp","forward_host":"web","forward_port":80}`,
		"no forward host":                    `{"domain_names":["a.example.com"],"forward_scheme":"http","forward_host":"","forward_port":80}`,
		"port 0":                             `{"domain_names":["a.example.com"],"forward_scheme":"http","forward_host":"web","forward_port":0}`,
		"port 65536":                         `{"domain_names":["a.example.com"],"forward_scheme":"http","forward_host":"web","forward_port":65536}`,
		"a body that is not JSON":            `{`,
	} {
		if status, _ := fkRaw(t, s, http.MethodPost, "/api/nginx/proxy-hosts", tok, body); status != http.StatusBadRequest {
			t.Errorf("%s: status %d, want 400", name, status)
		}
	}
	if n := len(s.ProxyHosts()); n != 0 {
		t.Errorf("refused creates stored %d proxy hosts", n)
	}
}

func TestFkAccessListsKeepNamesAndExpandClientsOnRequest(t *testing.T) {
	s := New(t)
	c, tok := fkLogin(t, s)
	ctx := t.Context()
	body := npmapi.AccessListBody{
		Name: "darkpanel-c1-2", SatisfyAny: true,
		Clients: []npmapi.AccessClient{{Address: "203.0.113.0/24", Directive: "allow"}, {Address: "198.51.100.7", Directive: "deny"}},
	}
	a, err := c.CreateAccessList(ctx, body)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	b, err := c.CreateAccessList(ctx, body)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("a second list with the same name: %v, want it kept as given", err)
	}
	if a.ID != 1 || b.ID != 2 || a.Name != body.Name || a.CreatedOn == "" || !a.SatisfyAny || !reflect.DeepEqual(a.Clients, body.Clients) {
		t.Errorf("created %+v and %+v, want ids 1 and 2 with the given name, flag and clients", a, b)
	}
	list, err := c.AccessLists(ctx)
	if err != nil || len(list) != 2 || !reflect.DeepEqual(list[0].Clients, body.Clients) {
		t.Errorf("listed %+v, %v, want both with their clients", list, err)
	}
	if _, raw := fkRaw(t, s, http.MethodGet, "/api/nginx/access-lists", tok, ""); strings.Contains(raw, `"clients"`) {
		t.Errorf("a list without expand=clients carries clients: %s", raw)
	}
	if _, raw := fkRaw(t, s, http.MethodGet, "/api/nginx/access-lists?expand=clients", tok, ""); !strings.Contains(raw, `"clients"`) {
		t.Errorf("a list with expand=clients lacks clients: %s", raw)
	}
	for name, bad := range map[string]string{
		"no name":                               `{"name":"","clients":[]}`,
		"a directive other than allow and deny": `{"name":"x","clients":[{"address":"203.0.113.7","directive":"block"}]}`,
		"an empty address":                      `{"name":"x","clients":[{"address":"","directive":"allow"}]}`,
	} {
		if status, _ := fkRaw(t, s, http.MethodPost, "/api/nginx/access-lists", tok, bad); status != http.StatusBadRequest {
			t.Errorf("%s: status %d, want 400", name, status)
		}
	}
	if n := len(s.AccessLists()); n != 2 {
		t.Errorf("%d access lists after the refused creates, want 2", n)
	}
}

func TestFkCertificatesTakeTheirDomainsAsNiceNameAndNeedTheAgreement(t *testing.T) {
	s := New(t)
	c, tok := fkLogin(t, s)
	ctx := t.Context()
	sent, err := c.CreateCertificate(ctx, npmapi.CertificateBody{
		NiceName: "darkpanel-c1-3", DomainNames: []string{"app.example.com"}, Meta: npmapi.CertificateMeta{LetsencryptEmail: fkEmail},
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if sent.ID != 1 || sent.NiceName != "app.example.com" || sent.Provider != "letsencrypt" || sent.CreatedOn == "" || sent.ExpiresOn == "" ||
		!reflect.DeepEqual(sent.DomainNames, []string{"app.example.com"}) {
		t.Errorf("created %+v, want id 1, the domain as nice_name whatever the request sent, provider letsencrypt and both timestamps", sent)
	}
	unnamed, err := c.CreateCertificate(ctx, npmapi.CertificateBody{
		DomainNames: []string{"b.example.com", "a.example.com"}, Meta: npmapi.CertificateMeta{LetsencryptEmail: fkEmail},
	})
	if err != nil || unnamed.NiceName != "b.example.com, a.example.com" || !reflect.DeepEqual(unnamed.DomainNames, []string{"b.example.com", "a.example.com"}) {
		t.Errorf("a certificate without a name: %+v, %v, want the domains joined by \", \" in the order given", unnamed, err)
	}
	if got := s.Certificates(); len(got) != 2 || got[0].NiceName != "app.example.com" || got[1].NiceName != "b.example.com, a.example.com" {
		t.Errorf("stored certificates %+v, want the same nice_names as the answers", got)
	}
	if _, err := c.CreateCertificate(ctx, npmapi.CertificateBody{
		DomainNames: []string{"app.example.com"}, Meta: npmapi.CertificateMeta{LetsencryptEmail: fkEmail},
	}); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("a second certificate for the same domain: %v, want it allowed", err)
	}
	const meta = `"meta":{"letsencrypt_email":"ops@example.com","letsencrypt_agree":true}`
	for name, bad := range map[string]string{
		"no agreement":     `{"provider":"letsencrypt","domain_names":["a.example.com"],"meta":{"letsencrypt_email":"ops@example.com","letsencrypt_agree":false}}`,
		"another provider": `{"provider":"other","domain_names":["a.example.com"],` + meta + `}`,
		"no domains":       `{"provider":"letsencrypt","domain_names":[],` + meta + `}`,
		"no contact email": `{"provider":"letsencrypt","domain_names":["a.example.com"],"meta":{"letsencrypt_email":"","letsencrypt_agree":true}}`,
	} {
		if status, _ := fkRaw(t, s, http.MethodPost, "/api/nginx/certificates", tok, bad); status != http.StatusBadRequest {
			t.Errorf("%s: status %d, want 400", name, status)
		}
	}
	if n := len(s.Certificates()); n != 3 {
		t.Errorf("%d certificates after the refused creates, want 3", n)
	}
}

func TestFkDeleteRemovesTheObjectAndAnAbsentOneIsNotFound(t *testing.T) {
	s := New(t)
	c, _ := fkLogin(t, s)
	ctx := t.Context()
	host, err := c.CreateProxyHost(ctx, fkHost("a.example.com"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	list, err := c.CreateAccessList(ctx, npmapi.AccessListBody{Name: "office"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cert, err := c.CreateCertificate(ctx, npmapi.CertificateBody{DomainNames: []string{"a.example.com"}, Meta: npmapi.CertificateMeta{LetsencryptEmail: fkEmail}})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := c.DeleteProxyHost(ctx, host.ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := c.DeleteAccessList(ctx, list.ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := c.DeleteCertificate(ctx, cert.ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(s.ProxyHosts())+len(s.AccessLists())+len(s.Certificates()) != 0 {
		t.Error("deleted objects are still stored")
	}
	if _, err := c.ProxyHost(ctx, host.ID); !npmapi.IsNotFound(err) {
		t.Errorf("a get of a deleted proxy host: %v, want 404", err)
	}
	if _, err := c.AccessList(ctx, list.ID); !npmapi.IsNotFound(err) {
		t.Errorf("a get of a deleted access list: %v, want 404", err)
	}
	if _, err := c.Certificate(ctx, cert.ID); !npmapi.IsNotFound(err) {
		t.Errorf("a get of a deleted certificate: %v, want 404", err)
	}
	if !npmapi.IsNotFound(c.DeleteProxyHost(ctx, host.ID)) || !npmapi.IsNotFound(c.DeleteAccessList(ctx, list.ID)) || !npmapi.IsNotFound(c.DeleteCertificate(ctx, cert.ID)) {
		t.Error("a second delete is not a 404")
	}
	again, err := c.CreateProxyHost(ctx, fkHost("a.example.com"))
	if err != nil || again.ID == host.ID {
		t.Errorf("a create after the delete gave %+v, %v, want a new id and the domain free again", again, err)
	}
}

func TestFkStampsObjectsWithItsClock(t *testing.T) {
	s := New(t)
	clock := coretest.NewFakeClock(time.Date(2026, 11, 2, 10, 0, 0, 123_000_000, time.UTC))
	s.SetClock(clock.Now)
	c, _ := fkLogin(t, s)
	ctx := t.Context()
	const first, second = "2026-11-02T10:00:00.123Z", "2026-11-02T10:01:00.123Z"
	if CreatedOnLayout != "2006-01-02T15:04:05.000Z" {
		t.Fatalf("CreatedOnLayout = %q", CreatedOnLayout)
	}
	host, err := c.CreateProxyHost(ctx, fkHost("a.example.com"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if host.CreatedOn != first || host.ModifiedOn != first {
		t.Errorf("created at %q and modified at %q, want both %q", host.CreatedOn, host.ModifiedOn, first)
	}
	clock.Advance(time.Minute)
	updated, err := c.UpdateProxyHost(ctx, host.ID, fkHost("a.example.com", "b.example.com"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if updated.CreatedOn != first || updated.ModifiedOn != second {
		t.Errorf("after an update created_on %q and modified_on %q, want %q and %q", updated.CreatedOn, updated.ModifiedOn, first, second)
	}
	list, err := c.CreateAccessList(ctx, npmapi.AccessListBody{Name: "office"})
	if err != nil || list.CreatedOn != second {
		t.Errorf("access list created at %q (%v), want %q", list.CreatedOn, err, second)
	}
	cert, err := c.CreateCertificate(ctx, npmapi.CertificateBody{DomainNames: []string{"a.example.com"}, Meta: npmapi.CertificateMeta{LetsencryptEmail: fkEmail}})
	if err != nil || cert.CreatedOn != second || cert.ExpiresOn != "2027-01-31T10:01:00.123Z" {
		t.Errorf("certificate created at %q, expiring %q (%v), want %q and 2027-01-31T10:01:00.123Z", cert.CreatedOn, cert.ExpiresOn, err, second)
	}
}
```
