Expected: both packages FAIL to build, `undefined: New` / `undefined: Client` / `undefined: ProxyHost` in `npmapi` and `undefined: New` / `undefined: Server` / `undefined: Fault` in `fake` (the packages have no non-test file yet).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Before writing code, read Task 1's record `docs/dforge/notes/v1-w6a-npm-spike.md` for the API facts it confirms (the field names below, the admin-creation body, the `created_on` format). Implement the names below as written; if the record contradicts one, stop and report it rather than changing it. Its `Certificate identity:` line does not change the fake: the fake always sets a Let's Encrypt certificate's `nice_name` as Contracts "NPM API client" states.

`npmapi` (hand-written over `net/http`, standard library only):

1. `types.go`: `Health{Status string \`json:"status"\`; Setup bool \`json:"setup"\`}`. The JSON names of the other types are NPM's, in snake case, with these tags and no `omitempty` unless stated:
   - `ProxyHost`: `ID` `id`, `CreatedOn` `created_on`, `ModifiedOn` `modified_on`, `DomainNames` `domain_names`, `ForwardScheme` `forward_scheme`, `ForwardHost` `forward_host`, `ForwardPort` `forward_port`, `CertificateID` `certificate_id`, `AccessListID` `access_list_id`, `SSLForced` `ssl_forced`, `HSTSEnabled` `hsts_enabled`, `AllowWebsocketUpgrade` `allow_websocket_upgrade`, `Locations` `locations`, `AdvancedConfig` `advanced_config`. `ProxyHostBody` has the same members and tags without `ID`, `CreatedOn` and `ModifiedOn`.
   - `Location`: `path`, `forward_scheme`, `forward_host`, `forward_port`, `advanced_config`.
   - `AccessList`: `id`, `created_on`, `name`, `satisfy_any`, `pass_auth`, `clients`; `AccessListBody`: `name`, `satisfy_any`, `pass_auth`, `clients`; `AccessClient`: `address`, `directive`.
   - `Certificate`: `id`, `created_on`, `nice_name`, `provider`, `domain_names`, `expires_on`; `CertificateBody`: `provider`, `nice_name` (with `omitempty`), `domain_names`, `meta`; `CertificateMeta`: `letsencrypt_email`, `letsencrypt_agree`.
   Unknown members of an answer (`enabled`, `meta`, `owner_user_id`) are ignored.
2. `errors.go`: `type APIError struct{ Status int; Message string }` with a pointer-receiver `Error()` that returns `npmapi: NPM answered <Status>: <Message>`; `IsNotFound(err)` (an `*APIError` found with `errors.As` whose status is 404) and `IsClientError(err)` (an `*APIError` with status 400 to 499); both are false for `nil`, for any other error and for 5xx.
3. `client.go`: `Client` holds the base URL, the `*http.Client` and a token. `New(baseURL, hc)` uses `&http.Client{}` when `hc` is nil (no client timeout: a certificate request waits for the ACME order, and the caller's context bounds every call). `WithToken` returns a copy with the token. One private method sends every request: it builds the request with the context, sets `Accept: application/json`, `Content-Type: application/json` when there is a body and `Authorization: Bearer <token>` when the client holds a token; a 2xx answer is decoded into the result when one is wanted (the answer to a delete is read and dropped); any other status becomes an `*APIError` whose `Message` is `error.message` of a JSON answer of the form `{"error":{"message":...}}` and otherwise `http.StatusText(status)`, never any part of the body (read at most 64 KiB of it); a transport failure is returned as `fmt.Errorf("npmapi: %w", err)` (so a cancelled context still satisfies `errors.Is(err, context.Canceled)`) and a decode failure as `npmapi: decode <path> answer: <error>`; neither is an `*APIError`.
4. The calls and their endpoints are those of Contracts "NPM API client": `Health` is `GET /api`; `CreateAdmin` is `POST /api/users` with the body `{"name":"Darkpanel","nickname":"darkpanel","email":<email>,"roles":["admin"],"is_disabled":false,"auth":{"type":"password","secret":<password>}}` and no token; `Token` is `POST /api/tokens` with `{"identity","secret"}`, no token, and an error `npmapi: the token answer carries no token` when the answer's `token` is empty; proxy hosts use `/api/nginx/proxy-hosts` (`POST` creates, `PUT .../<id>` updates, `DELETE .../<id>` deletes); access lists use `/api/nginx/access-lists` with the query `expand=clients` on the two reads; certificates use `/api/nginx/certificates`. `CreateCertificate` always sends `provider: "letsencrypt"` and `meta.letsencrypt_agree: true`, whatever the body holds. A body never encodes a nil list as `null`: `DomainNames`, `Locations` and `Clients` go out as `[]` when nil. The three list reads return a non-nil, possibly empty, slice, also for an answer of `null`.

`fake` (built on `internal/controller/cloud/cloudfake`, as `internal/modules/cloudflare/fake` is; `New(tb)` starts the cloudfake server, closed by `tb.Cleanup`; the `Server` is safe for concurrent use):

5. `server.go`: the `Server`, `New`, `URL`, `SetClock`, `CreatedOnLayout`, `Request`, `Fault`, `Fail`, `Requests`, `Admin`, `Setup`, `SeedAdmin`, the guard and the routes for `/api`, `/api/users` and `/api/tokens`. `Fail(method, path, f)` queues a fault for the next request that matches the method and the exact URL path (a queue per method and path, oldest first, each fault used by one request): `Status` answers that status with the body `{"error":{"code":<status>,"message":"<http.StatusText>"}}` without running the handler; `AfterCommit` runs the handler, so the write commits, and then closes the connection without an answer (`AfterCommit` wins when both are set); a fault with neither set makes `Fail` call `tb.Fatalf`. Every request is recorded, faulted and refused ones included.
6. The rules of the endpoints, all answers JSON, errors as `{"error":{"code":<status>,"message":<text>}}`:
   - `GET /api` answers `{"status":"OK","setup":<bool>,"version":{"major":2,"minor":13,"revision":5}}` with no credentials; `setup` is false until a user exists.
   - `POST /api/users` needs no credentials. While no user exists it requires `email` non-empty, `roles` containing `admin` and `auth.secret` of 8 to 100 characters, else 400; on success it stores the one admin, sets `setup` true and answers 201 with `id`, `name`, `nickname`, `email`, `roles`, `is_disabled`, never the secret. Once a user exists it answers 403 `Setup is already complete`.
   - `POST /api/tokens` with `{"identity","secret"}` answers 200 `{"token","expires"}` with a new token (`fake-token-<n>`) when they match the admin, else 401 `Invalid email or password`; a body that is not JSON is a 400.
   - Every `/api/nginx/*` route answers 401 `Unauthorized` unless `Authorization` holds `Bearer <a token the fake issued>`.
   - Ids are per kind, start at 1, increase and are never reused. Create answers 201, the others 200; `DELETE` answers `true`; an absent id answers 404 `Not Found`; a body that is not JSON is a 400. `created_on`, `modified_on` and `expires_on` come from the clock in `CreatedOnLayout`, in UTC.
7. `objects.go`: the three kinds.
   - Proxy hosts: create and update require at least one domain, `forward_scheme` `http` or `https`, a non-empty `forward_host` and `forward_port` from 1 to 65535, else 400; a domain already held by another proxy host answers 400 `<domain> is already in use` (the first such domain in the order given; a host does not conflict with itself on update); update replaces every member of `ProxyHostBody`, keeps `id` and `created_on` and sets `modified_on`; `locations` and `advanced_config` are stored and returned as given.
   - Access lists: `name` non-empty, every client with a non-empty `address` and `directive` `allow` or `deny`, else 400; names may repeat and are kept as given; the answers carry `clients` only when the request has `expand=clients` (a create answers with them).
   - Certificates: `provider` must be `letsencrypt`, at least one domain, `meta.letsencrypt_email` non-empty and `meta.letsencrypt_agree` true, else 400; `nice_name` is set to the `domain_names` joined by `, ` in the order given, whatever the request sent (as NPM does for `letsencrypt`); the certificate is issued at once with `expires_on` 90 days after `created_on`; certificates may share domains.
8. Accessors and seeds: `ProxyHosts()`, `AccessLists()` and `Certificates()` return deep copies in id order, never nil. `SeedAdmin(email, password)` stores the admin (replacing any) and sets `setup` true. `SeedProxyHost`, `SeedAccessList` and `SeedCertificate` store the object with the next id of its kind (the given `ID` is ignored), a `created_on` from the clock when it has none (a given one is kept), no validation (`SeedCertificate` keeps the `nice_name` it is given), and return the stored copy. A seeded domain blocks a create like any other.

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/modules/npm/proxy/location.go`
- Create: `internal/modules/npm/proxy/upstream.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `npmapi.Location` (Task 6, Contracts "NPM API client"), `errcode.New`, `errcode.NPMReserved`, `errcode.NPMUnsupportedTarget` (`internal/core/errcode/errcode.go`), Task 1's record of the template lines (`docs/dforge/notes/v1-w6a-npm-spike.md`).
- Produces: Contracts "Proxy rules": `func Location(forwardScheme, forwardHost string, forwardPort int, panel bool) npmapi.Location`, `type Reserved struct{ NPMAddrs []netip.Addr; PanelNetwork netip.Prefix; PanelGateway netip.Addr }`, `func CheckUpstream(r Reserved, host string, port int, parent string) error`, `type Endpoint struct{ Driver string; UserDefined bool }`, `func Target(endpoints map[string]Endpoint, network string) error`, `func FirstTargetNetwork(endpoints map[string]Endpoint) (string, bool)`.

**Depends on:** Task 1, Task 6

The package is pure: no I/O, no Docker, no database. `Location` returns `npmapi.Location`, which Task 6 creates. Its callers (Tasks 16 and 20 for the upstream rule, Task 15 for the target rule) supply the facts.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `internal/modules/npm/proxy/location_test.go`:

```go
package proxy

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/npm/npmapi"
)

const pxClientLine = "proxy_set_header X-Darkpanel-Client $realip_remote_addr;"

func TestPxLocationAddsOnlyTheClientHeader(t *testing.T) {
	got := Location("http", "web", 8080, false)
	want := npmapi.Location{Path: "/", ForwardScheme: "http", ForwardHost: "web", ForwardPort: 8080, AdvancedConfig: pxClientLine}
	if got != want {
		t.Errorf("Location = %+v, want %+v", got, want)
	}
}

func TestPxLocationForThePanelAddsTheReadTimeoutOnASecondLine(t *testing.T) {
	got := Location("https", "172.31.255.1", 9443, true)
	want := npmapi.Location{
		Path: "/", ForwardScheme: "https", ForwardHost: "172.31.255.1", ForwardPort: 9443,
		AdvancedConfig: pxClientLine + "\nproxy_read_timeout 3600s;",
	}
	if got != want {
		t.Errorf("Location = %+v, want %+v", got, want)
	}
}

// The NPM template of a custom location already sets Host, X-Forwarded-*, X-Real-IP, Upgrade and Connection;
// repeating one would send two of them upstream.
func TestPxLocationNeverEmitsAHeaderTheTemplateSets(t *testing.T) {
	for _, panel := range []bool{false, true} {
		cfg := Location("http", "web", 80, panel).AdvancedConfig
		if strings.HasSuffix(cfg, "\n") {
			t.Errorf("panel=%t: %q ends with a newline", panel, cfg)
		}
		lines := strings.Split(cfg, "\n")
		if len(lines) != map[bool]int{false: 1, true: 2}[panel] {
			t.Errorf("panel=%t: %d lines, want %d", panel, len(lines), map[bool]int{false: 1, true: 2}[panel])
		}
		for _, line := range lines {
			f := strings.Fields(line)
			client := len(f) == 3 && f[0] == "proxy_set_header" && f[1] == "X-Darkpanel-Client"
			timeout := len(f) == 2 && f[0] == "proxy_read_timeout"
			if !client && !timeout {
				t.Errorf("panel=%t: line %q is neither the client header nor the read timeout", panel, line)
			}
		}
	}
}
```

Create `internal/modules/npm/proxy/upstream_test.go`:

```go
package proxy

import (
	"net/netip"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
)

func pxReserved() Reserved {
	return Reserved{
		NPMAddrs:     []netip.Addr{netip.MustParseAddr("172.18.0.2"), netip.MustParseAddr("fd00:18::2")},
		PanelNetwork: netip.MustParsePrefix("172.31.255.0/29"),
		PanelGateway: netip.MustParseAddr("172.31.255.1"),
	}
}

func TestPxCheckUpstream(t *testing.T) {
	const admin, panel = "npm.admin.expose", "panel.exposure.add"
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name   string
		host   string
		port   int
		parent string
		refuse bool
	}{
		{"loopback address on the admin port", "127.0.0.1", 81, "", true},
		{"loopback address on the admin port from its owner", "127.0.0.1", 81, admin, false},
		{"loopback address from the other owner", "127.0.0.1", 81, panel, true},
		{"loopback address on any port", "127.0.0.1", 8080, "", true},
		{"another address of 127.0.0.0/8", "127.1.2.3", 9000, "", true},
		{"localhost", "localhost", 81, "", true},
		{"localhost in capitals with a trailing dot", "LocalHost.", 3000, "", true},
		{"localhost from its owner", "localhost", 81, admin, false},
		{"the IPv6 loopback", "::1", 81, "", true},
		{"the bracketed IPv6 loopback", "[::1]", 81, "", true},
		{"the IPv4-mapped loopback", "::ffff:127.0.0.1", 81, "", true},
		{"the unspecified address, which Linux connects to the local host", "0.0.0.0", 81, "", true},
		{"the IPv6 unspecified address", "::", 81, "", true},
		{"an NPM address on the admin port", "172.18.0.2", 81, "", true},
		{"an NPM address on the admin port from its owner", "172.18.0.2", 81, admin, false},
		{"an NPM IPv6 address on the admin port", "fd00:18::2", 81, "", true},
		{"an NPM address on another port", "172.18.0.2", 80, "", false},
		{"the NPM container's name on the admin port", "darkpanel-npm", 81, "", true},
		{"the NPM container's name on another port", "darkpanel-npm", 8080, "", false},
		{"an address inside the panel network", "172.31.255.2", 9443, "", true},
		{"an address inside the panel network from its owner", "172.31.255.2", 9443, panel, false},
		{"an address inside the panel network from the admin exposure", "172.31.255.2", 9443, admin, true},
		{"the panel gateway", "172.31.255.1", 9443, "", true},
		{"the panel gateway from its owner", "172.31.255.1", 9443, panel, false},
		{"an address just outside the panel network", "172.31.255.8", 9443, "", false},
		{"a container name", "web", 8080, "", false},
		{"a public name from the panel exposure", "app.example.com", 443, panel, false},
		{"an unrelated private address on the admin port", "10.0.0.5", 81, "", false},
		{"a container name on the admin port", "web", 81, "", false},
	} {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			err := CheckUpstream(pxReserved(), tc.host, tc.port, tc.parent)
			if !tc.refuse {
				if err != nil {
					t.Fatalf("CheckUpstream(%q, %d, %q) = %v, want nil", tc.host, tc.port, tc.parent, err)
				}
				return
			}
			e := errcode.From(err)
			if e.Code != errcode.NPMReserved || e.Detail == "" {
				t.Fatalf("CheckUpstream(%q, %d, %q) = %v, want npm.reserved with a detail", tc.host, tc.port, tc.parent, err)
			}
			if !strings.Contains(e.Detail, tc.host) {
				t.Errorf("detail %q does not name the upstream %q", e.Detail, tc.host)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestPxCheckUpstreamWithNothingKnownRefusesOnlyTheLoopback(t *testing.T) {
	if err := CheckUpstream(Reserved{}, "172.31.255.2", 9443, ""); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("an empty Reserved refused a private address: %v", err)
	}
	if err := CheckUpstream(Reserved{}, "127.0.0.1", 81, ""); errcode.From(err).Code != errcode.NPMReserved {
		t.Errorf("an empty Reserved let the loopback through: %v", err)
	}
}
```

Create `internal/modules/npm/proxy/target_test.go`:

```go
package proxy

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
)

func pxEndpoints() map[string]Endpoint {
	return map[string]Endpoint{
		"bridge":   {Driver: "bridge", UserDefined: false},
		"app":      {Driver: "bridge", UserDefined: true},
		"host":     {Driver: "host", UserDefined: false},
		"overlay1": {Driver: "overlay", UserDefined: true},
		"lan":      {Driver: "macvlan", UserDefined: true},
	}
}

func TestPxTarget(t *testing.T) {
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name      string
		endpoints map[string]Endpoint
		network   string
		refuse    bool
	}{
		{"a user-defined bridge the container is on", pxEndpoints(), "app", false},
		{"the default bridge", pxEndpoints(), "bridge", true},
		{"the host network", pxEndpoints(), "host", true},
		{"a user-defined overlay", pxEndpoints(), "overlay1", true},
		{"a user-defined macvlan", pxEndpoints(), "lan", true},
		{"a network the container is not on", pxEndpoints(), "other", true},
		{"an empty network name", pxEndpoints(), "", true},
		{"a container on no network", nil, "app", true},
	} {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			err := Target(tc.endpoints, tc.network)
			if !tc.refuse {
				if err != nil {
					t.Fatalf("Target(%q) = %v, want nil", tc.network, err)
				}
				return
			}
			e := errcode.From(err)
			if e.Code != errcode.NPMUnsupportedTarget || e.Detail != "connect the container to a user-defined network" {
				t.Fatalf("Target(%q) = %v, want npm.unsupported_target with the reason \"connect the container to a user-defined network\"", tc.network, err)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestPxFirstTargetNetwork(t *testing.T) {
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name      string
		endpoints map[string]Endpoint
		want      string
		ok        bool
	}{
		{"the first supported network in name order", map[string]Endpoint{
			"zeta": {Driver: "bridge", UserDefined: true}, "alpha": {Driver: "bridge", UserDefined: true}, "bridge": {Driver: "bridge"},
		}, "alpha", true},
		{"unsupported networks sort first but are skipped", map[string]Endpoint{
			"0-overlay": {Driver: "overlay", UserDefined: true}, "1-host": {Driver: "host"}, "b-net": {Driver: "bridge", UserDefined: true}, "a-bridge": {Driver: "bridge"},
		}, "b-net", true},
		{"only the default bridge", map[string]Endpoint{"bridge": {Driver: "bridge"}}, "", false},
		{"no endpoints", map[string]Endpoint{}, "", false},
		{"a nil map", nil, "", false},
	} {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			got, ok := FirstTargetNetwork(tc.endpoints)
			if got != tc.want || ok != tc.ok {
				t.Errorf("FirstTargetNetwork = %q, %t; want %q, %t", got, ok, tc.want, tc.ok)
			}
			if ok && Target(tc.endpoints, got) != nil {
				t.Errorf("the network %q it picked is refused by Target", got)
			}
		})
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the tests to verify they fail**

Run: `mkdir -p /root/.cache/w6a-tmp/7 && export TMPDIR=/root/.cache/w6a-tmp/7 GOTOOLCHAIN=local && timeout 300 go test -count=1 ./internal/modules/npm/proxy/`
Expected: FAIL to build, `undefined: Location`, `undefined: Reserved`, `undefined: CheckUpstream`, `undefined: Endpoint`, `undefined: Target`, `undefined: FirstTargetNetwork` (the package has no non-test file yet).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

1. `location.go`: `Location` returns `npmapi.Location` with `Path` `"/"`, the given scheme, host and port, and `AdvancedConfig` the line `proxy_set_header X-Darkpanel-Client $realip_remote_addr;`, followed, only when `panel` is true, by `\n` and the line `proxy_read_timeout 3600s;`; no trailing newline. A comment explains why it is only these lines: NPM's custom-location template already sets `Host`, `X-Forwarded-*`, `X-Real-IP` and the websocket headers, so repeating one would send it twice (spec 6, Client header).
2. `upstream.go`: `Reserved` and `CheckUpstream` as the Interfaces block states. `host` is normalised by trimming one pair of square brackets and a trailing dot and lower-casing it, then parsed with `netip.ParseAddr` (an IPv4-mapped IPv6 address is unmapped). The checks, each independent:
   - Loopback: the name `localhost`, or an address that is loopback (`127.0.0.0/8`, `::1`) or unspecified (`0.0.0.0`, `::`, which Linux connects to the local host), on any port, is refused unless `parent == "npm.admin.expose"`.
   - NPM's own addresses: an address equal to one of `r.NPMAddrs`, or the name `darkpanel-npm` (the NPM container's name, a constant of this package), on port 81 only, is refused unless `parent == "npm.admin.expose"`; the same address on another port is allowed.
   - The panel: an address inside `r.PanelNetwork` or equal to `r.PanelGateway` is refused unless `parent == "panel.exposure.add"`. An unset prefix or address (the zero value) matches nothing.
   - A host name that is not an address and not one of the names above is allowed: the function resolves nothing.
   A refusal is `errcode.New(errcode.NPMReserved, <detail>)`, a plain `*errcode.Error` with no `core.Definite` wrapper (it runs in a plan, where nothing is committed). The detail names the upstream as given, for example `upstream 127.0.0.1:81 is reserved for the admin exposure` or `upstream 172.31.255.2:9443 is reserved for the panel exposure`, and carries no other data.
3. `target.go`: `Endpoint`; `Target` returns nil only when `endpoints[network]` exists with `Driver == "bridge"` and `UserDefined` true, otherwise `errcode.New(errcode.NPMUnsupportedTarget, "connect the container to a user-defined network")`; `FirstTargetNetwork` walks the network names in ascending byte order (`slices.Sorted(maps.Keys(...))`) and returns the first one for which `Target` is nil, or `"", false`.
4. Every exported identifier has a doc comment; the package doc comment says it holds the NPM exposure rules that need no I/O.

Expected: PASS.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `web/src/modules/npm/instance-panel.test.tsx` (the helper prefix is `ip`; assumes Task 5's `npInstance()` default has no `adminExposure` — `null` or absent — and every test sets the other fields it asserts):

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react"
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event"
import { expect, test } from "vitest"
import { axe } from "vitest-axe"
import { fwDocker } from "@/modules/firewall/fixtures"
import { renderWithProviders, stubFetch, type FetchCall, type StubHandler } from "@/test/utils"
import { npInstance } from "./fixtures"
import { InstancePanel } from "./instance-panel"
import type { InstanceStatus } from "./types"

const IP_HOST = "/api/v1/hosts/h1"
const IP_STATUS = `POST ${IP_HOST}/actions/npm.instance.status/v1/read`
const IP_DOCKER = `GET ${IP_HOST}/resources/firewall/docker`
const IP_REVEAL = `POST ${IP_HOST}/actions/npm.admin.reveal-password/v1/reveal`
const IP_IMAGE = "jc21/nginx-proxy-manager:2.13.5@sha256:" + "a".repeat(64)
const IP_ON = "The Docker firewall integration is on: ports 80 and 443 are opened for the NPM container."
const IP_OFF = "The Docker firewall integration is off: open ports 80 and 443 to the internet yourself, then confirm that the instance is public."

function ipPlan(call: FetchCall) {
  const parts = call.path.split("/")
  return {
    body: {
      planId: "p1",
      hostId: call.path.includes("/hosts/") ? "h1" : null,
      action: parts[parts.length - 3] ?? "",
      version: 1,
      inputHash: "h",
      diff: [{ op: "~", text: "change" }],
      guards: [],
      expiresAt: "2026-11-02T13:00:00Z",
      confirmation: null,
    },
  }
}

const IP_PLANS = [
  `POST ${IP_HOST}/actions/npm.instance.confirm-public/v1/plan`,
  `POST ${IP_HOST}/actions/npm.instance.upgrade/v1/plan`,
  `POST ${IP_HOST}/actions/npm.instance.remove/v1/plan`,
  "POST /api/v1/actions/npm.instance.deploy-public/v1/plan",
  "POST /api/v1/actions/npm.admin.expose/v1/plan",
  "POST /api/v1/actions/npm.admin.unexpose/v1/plan",
]

function ipRunning(over: Partial<InstanceStatus> = {}): InstanceStatus {
  return npInstance({
    deployed: true,
    containerId: "c0ffee",
    image: IP_IMAGE,
    pinnedImage: IP_IMAGE,
    running: true,
    apiReady: true,
    public: "confirmed",
    adminEmail: "admin@example.com",
    upgradeAvailable: false,
    ...over,
  })
}

function ipNotDeployed(): InstanceStatus {
  return npInstance({ deployed: false, containerId: "", image: "", running: false, apiReady: false, public: "no", adminEmail: "" })
}

function ipRender(status: InstanceStatus, routes: Record<string, StubHandler> = {}) {
  const calls = stubFetch({
    [IP_STATUS]: { body: { output: status } },
    [IP_DOCKER]: { body: fwDocker() },
    [`GET ${IP_HOST}/modules/npm/availability`]: { body: { module: "npm", available: true, reason: null, actions: [] } },
    ...Object.fromEntries(IP_PLANS.map((key) => [key, ipPlan])),
    ...routes,
  })
  const result = renderWithProviders(<InstancePanel hostId="h1" />)
  return { calls, ...result }
}

function ipValue(label: string): string | null {
  return screen.getByText(label).nextElementSibling?.textContent ?? null
}

function ipPlanBodies(calls: FetchCall[]): unknown[] {
  return calls.filter((c) => c.method === "POST" && c.path.endsWith("/plan")).map((c) => c.body)
}

function ipPlanPath(calls: FetchCall[]): string | undefined {
  return calls.find((c) => c.method === "POST" && c.path.endsWith("/plan"))?.path
}

function ipStatusReads(calls: FetchCall[]): number {
  return calls.filter((c) => c.method === "POST" && c.path === `${IP_HOST}/actions/npm.instance.status/v1/read`).length
}

test("shows the state of a running, public instance", async () => {
  ipRender(ipRunning())
  await screen.findByText("Admin email")
  expect(ipValue("Status")).toBe("running")
  expect(ipValue("API")).toBe("ready")
  expect(ipValue("Image")).toBe(IP_IMAGE)
  expect(ipValue("Pinned image")).toBe(IP_IMAGE)
  expect(ipValue("Admin email")).toBe("admin@example.com")
  expect(ipValue("Public")).toBe("confirmed")
  expect(ipValue("Admin interface")).toBe("not exposed")
  expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Confirm public" })).toBeNull()
  expect(screen.queryByText("Upgrade available")).toBeNull()
})

test("says when the container is stopped, the API is not ready and public access is not confirmed", async () => {
  ipRender(ipRunning({ running: false, apiReady: false, public: "no", upgradeAvailable: true }))
  await screen.findByText("Admin email")
  expect(ipValue("Status")).toBe("stopped")
  expect(ipValue("API")).toBe("not ready")
  expect(ipValue("Public")).toBe("not confirmed")
  expect(screen.getByText("Upgrade available")).toBeTruthy()
  expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Confirm public" })).toBeTruthy()
})

test.each([
  ["on", fwDocker({ enabled: true, supported: true }), true, IP_ON],
  ["off", fwDocker({ enabled: false, supported: true }), false, IP_OFF],
  ["unsupported", fwDocker({ enabled: true, supported: false, unsupported: ["rules"] }), false, IP_OFF],
])("deploys with the integration flag read from firewall.docker when it is %s", async (_, docker, integration, hint) => {
  const { calls } = ipRender(ipNotDeployed(), { [IP_DOCKER]: { body: docker } })
  const user = userEvent.setup()
  const form = await screen.findByRole("form", { name: "Deploy" })
  const plan = within(form).getByRole("button", { name: "Plan" })
  expect(plan.hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(true)
  await within(form).findByText(hint)
  expect(plan.hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(true)
  await user.type(within(form).getByLabelText("Let's Encrypt email"), " ops@example.com ")
  expect(plan.hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(false)
  await user.click(plan)
  const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Deploy Nginx Proxy Manager" })
  await within(dialog).findByLabelText("Planned changes")
  expect(ipPlanPath(calls)).toBe("/api/v1/actions/npm.instance.deploy-public/v1/plan")
  expect(ipPlanBodies(calls)).toEqual([{ input: { hostId: "h1", acmeEmail: "ops@example.com", integration } }])
})

test("keeps Plan off and shows the reason while firewall.docker cannot be read", async () => {
  ipRender(ipNotDeployed(), {
    [IP_DOCKER]: { status: 503, body: { type: "about:blank", title: "Unavailable", status: 503, detail: "the firewall module is unavailable", code: "module.unavailable" } },
  })
  const user = userEvent.setup()
  const form = await screen.findByRole("form", { name: "Deploy" })
  await user.type(within(form).getByLabelText("Let's Encrypt email"), "ops@example.com")
  expect((await within(form).findByRole("alert")).textContent).toBe("the firewall module is unavailable")
  expect(within(form).getByRole("button", { name: "Plan" }).hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(true)
})

test("Confirm public plans npm.instance.confirm-public on the host", async () => {
  const { calls } = ipRender(ipRunning({ public: "no" }))
  await userEvent.setup().click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Confirm public" }))
  const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Confirm that the instance is public" })
  await within(dialog).findByLabelText("Planned changes")
  expect(ipPlanPath(calls)).toBe(`${IP_HOST}/actions/npm.instance.confirm-public/v1/plan`)
  expect(ipPlanBodies(calls)).toEqual([{ input: {} }])
})

test.each([
  ["Upgrade", "npm.instance.upgrade", "Upgrade Nginx Proxy Manager"],
  ["Remove instance", "npm.instance.remove", "Remove Nginx Proxy Manager"],
])("%s plans %s on the host with an empty input", async (button, action, title) => {

  const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: title })
  await within(dialog).findByLabelText("Planned changes")
  expect(ipPlanPath(calls)).toBe(`${IP_HOST}/actions/${action}/v1/plan`)
  expect(ipPlanBodies(calls)).toEqual([{ input: {} }])
})

test.each([
  ["", { hostId: "h1", hostname: "npm.example.com" }],
  ["7", { hostId: "h1", hostname: "npm.example.com", accessListId: 7 }],
])("exposing the admin interface with access list %j plans npm.admin.expose on the controller", async (accessList, input) => {

test.each(["abc", "0", "-3"])("the access list ID %j keeps Plan off", async (typed) => {
  ipRender(ipRunning())
```

Create `web/src/modules/npm/npm-page.test.tsx` (prefix `pg`):

```tsx
import { screen } from "@testing-library/react"
import { Route, Routes } from "react-router"
import { expect, test } from "vitest"
import { axe } from "vitest-axe"
import { fwDocker } from "@/modules/firewall/fixtures"
import { renderWithProviders, stubFetch, type StubHandler } from "@/test/utils"
import { npInstance } from "./fixtures"
import NpmRoutes from "./routes"
import type { InstanceStatus } from "./types"

const PG_STATUS = "POST /api/v1/hosts/h1/actions/npm.instance.status/v1/read"
const PG_EXPOSED = "The NPM admin interface is published at https://npm.example.com. Remove the exposure when you no longer need it."

function pgStatus(over: Partial<InstanceStatus> = {}): InstanceStatus {
  return npInstance({ deployed: true, containerId: "c0ffee", running: true, apiReady: true, public: "confirmed", adminEmail: "admin@example.com", ...over })
}

function pgRender(path: string, status: InstanceStatus, routes: Record<string, StubHandler> = {}) {
  const calls = stubFetch({
    [PG_STATUS]: { body: { output: status } },
    "GET /api/v1/hosts/h1/resources/firewall/docker": { body: fwDocker() },
    ...routes,
  })
  const result = renderWithProviders(
    <Routes>
      <Route path="/hosts/:hostId/npm/*" element={<NpmRoutes hostId="h1" />} />
    </Routes>,
    { path },
  )
  return { calls, ...result }
}

test("opens on the Instance tab and reads the status once for the page and the panel", async () => {
  const { calls } = pgRender("/hosts/h1/npm", pgStatus())
  expect((await screen.findByText("admin@example.com")).tagName).toBe("DD")
  expect(screen.getByRole("tab", { name: "Instance", selected: true })).toBeTruthy()
  expect(calls.filter((c) => c.method === "POST" && c.path.endsWith("/npm.instance.status/v1/read"))).toHaveLength(1)
})

test("warns while the admin interface is exposed", async () => {
  pgRender("/hosts/h1/npm", pgStatus({ adminExposure: { hostname: "npm.example.com", proxyHostId: 4, certificateId: 5 } }))
  expect((await screen.findByText(PG_EXPOSED)).textContent).toBe(PG_EXPOSED)
})

test("shows no warning when the admin interface is not exposed", async () => {
  pgRender("/hosts/h1/npm", pgStatus())
  await screen.findByText("admin@example.com")
  expect(screen.queryByText(/is published at/)).toBeNull()
})

test("an unknown subpage is answered", () => {
  pgRender("/hosts/h1/npm/nope", pgStatus())
  expect(screen.getByText("Page not found").textContent).toBe("Page not found")
})

test("the page has no accessibility violations", async () => {
  const { container } = pgRender("/hosts/h1/npm", pgStatus({ adminExposure: { hostname: "npm.example.com", proxyHostId: 4, certificateId: 5 } }))
  await screen.findByText(PG_EXPOSED)
  expect(await axe(container)).toHaveNoViolations()
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

1. `status.ts`: `readInstanceStatus(hostId)` calls `readAction<unknown>("npm.instance.status", NPM_ACTION_VERSION, {}, hostId)` and returns the body when `isInstanceStatus` accepts it; otherwise it throws `new Error("The host answered with an unexpected NPM instance status.")`. `messageOf(error)` returns `errorMessage(error)` for an `ApiError`, the message of any other `Error`, and `String(error)` otherwise (the pattern of `derp-page.tsx`). The file exports only these two functions.
2. `routes.tsx`: replace the stub with the default export `NpmRoutes({hostId}: ModuleRouteProps)`, a `Routes` with `index` rendering `<NpmPage hostId={hostId} />` and `path="*"` rendering `EmptyState` titled `Page not found` with the description `The Nginx Proxy Manager module has no page at this address.` (the shape of `docker/routes.tsx`).
3. `npm-page.tsx` exports only `NpmPage({hostId})`. It reads the status with `useQuery({queryKey: npmKey(hostId, "instance.status"), queryFn: () => readInstanceStatus(hostId)})`, the same key and function as the panel, so the two share one request. It renders, in a column: first, when `status.data?.adminExposure` is neither `null` nor `undefined`, an `Alert variant="warning" role="status"` with `AlertTitle` `Admin interface exposed` and `AlertDescription` holding exactly one text node `The NPM admin interface is published at https://<hostname>. Remove the exposure when you no longer need it.`; then `Tabs defaultValue="instance"` with `TabsList aria-label="Nginx Proxy Manager"`, one `TabsTrigger value="instance"` labelled `Instance` and its `TabsContent` holding `<InstancePanel hostId={hostId} />`. The page shows no error of its own; the panel reports a failed read.
4. `instance-panel.tsx` exports only `InstancePanel({hostId})`. State: `pending` (`null` or `{scope: "host" | "controller", action, title, input}`), `dialog` (`null`, `"expose"`, `"reveal"`), `revealed` (`null` or `{email, password}`), `revealError` (`string | null`), `revealing`. Read the status with the page's key and function. While pending show `Spinner aria-label="Loading the NPM instance"`; on error a destructive `Alert role="alert"` with `messageOf(error)`.
   - A status with `deployed === false` shows the text `Nginx Proxy Manager is not deployed on this host.` and `<form aria-label="Deploy">` with `Label` `Let's Encrypt email` and its `Input`, a `Plan` button, and a hint area inside the form. The form reads `firewall.docker` with `useQuery({queryKey: dockerQueryKey(hostId), queryFn: () => readResource<DockerStatus>(hostId, FIREWALL_MODULE, "docker")})`. While that read is pending the hint area shows `Spinner aria-label="Loading the Docker integration"`; on error a destructive `Alert role="alert"` with the message; on success the hint is exactly `The Docker firewall integration is on: ports 80 and 443 are opened for the NPM container.` when `enabled && supported`, otherwise exactly `The Docker firewall integration is off: open ports 80 and 443 to the internet yourself, then confirm that the instance is public.` `Plan` is disabled until the trimmed email is not empty and the Docker read succeeded. Submitting sets `pending` to a controller change: action `npm.instance.deploy-public`, title `Deploy Nginx Proxy Manager`, input `{hostId, acmeEmail: <trimmed email>, integration: enabled && supported}`.
   - A deployed status shows a `<dl>` whose rows are a `<dt>` label followed by its `<dd>` value, in this order: `Status` (`running` or `stopped`), `API` (`ready` or `not ready`), `Image`, `Pinned image`, `Admin email`, `Public` (`confirmed` when `public === "confirmed"`, else `not confirmed`), `Admin interface` (`https://<hostname>` when `adminExposure` is set, else `not exposed`). When `public` is not `confirmed`, a `variant="warning" role="status"` alert says `Public access is not confirmed. Confirm only when ports 80 and 443 reach this host from the internet.` A row of buttons follows: `Confirm public` (only when `public` is not `confirmed`; host change `npm.instance.confirm-public`, title `Confirm that the instance is public`), `Upgrade` (host change `npm.instance.upgrade`, title `Upgrade Nginx Proxy Manager`), `Remove instance` (host change `npm.instance.remove`, title `Remove Nginx Proxy Manager`), `Reveal admin password`, and either `Expose admin interface` (no exposure) or `Remove admin exposure` (controller change `npm.admin.unexpose`, title `Remove the admin exposure at <hostname>`, input `{hostId, hostname}`). A `Badge variant="secondary"` `Upgrade available` shows beside the buttons when `upgradeAvailable`. The three host changes use input `{}`.
   - `Expose admin interface` opens a `Dialog` titled `Expose the admin interface` with the description `Publishes the NPM admin interface over HTTPS on a hostname you choose. A warning stays on every page while it is exposed.` and `<form aria-label="Admin exposure">`: `Label` `Hostname`, `Label` `Access list ID (optional)`, buttons `Cancel` and `Plan`. `Plan` is disabled while the trimmed hostname is empty or the access list text is non-empty and does not match `^[1-9][0-9]*$`. Submitting closes the dialog and sets a controller change: action `npm.admin.expose`, title `Expose the admin interface at <hostname>`, input `{hostId, hostname}` plus `accessListId: <number>` only when an access list was typed; the hostname is trimmed and lowercased.
   - `Reveal admin password` posts with `request<{value: unknown}>("POST", "/api/v1/hosts/" + encodeURIComponent(hostId) + "/actions/npm.admin.reveal-password/v1/reveal", {input: {}})` from an event handler (not a query, so nothing enters the cache), checks that `value.email` and `value.password` are strings with a local, non-exported guard, and stores them in component state. A `Dialog` titled `NPM admin credentials` with the description `Shown once. Closing this dialog hides the password.` shows a `<dl>` with `Email` and `Password` rows and a `Hide password` button that clears the state; closing the dialog any other way also clears it. A failed or malformed reveal shows a destructive `Alert role="alert"` with `messageOf(error)` and opens no dialog.
   - Render the pending change with `NpmChange` (`scope: "host"`, passing `hostId`) or `NpmControllerChange` (`scope: "controller"`). Their `onClose` sets `pending` to `null` and calls `queryClient.invalidateQueries({queryKey: ["resource", hostId]})`, so the status is read again after any change, composite ones included.
   - The file exports only the component, and the tests' copy strings above are the exact visible text.

```ts
  ["a.example.com, A.example.com", ["a.example.com"]],

  [" , ", []],
])("parseDomains(%j) is %j", (text, want) => {

})

})

    ok: true,
    clients: [
      { address: "10.0.0.0/8", directive: "allow" },
      { address: "192.0.2.1", directive: "deny" },
      { address: "2001:db8::/32", directive: "allow" },

  })
})

  ["\n  \n", "Add at least one rule."],
  ["permit 10.0.0.1", "Line 1: start with allow or deny, then an address."],
  ["allow 10.0.0.1\n\nallow", "Line 3: start with allow or deny, then an address."],
  ["allow 10.0.0.1 extra", "Line 1: start with allow or deny, then an address."],
])("parseRules(%j) is refused with %j", (text, error) => {

})
```

```tsx
import { useState } from "react"
import { screen } from "@testing-library/react"
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event"
import { expect, test } from "vitest"
import { renderWithProviders } from "@/test/utils"
import { Field, MarkBadges } from "./list-parts"

function LpField() {
  const [value, setValue] = useState("")
  return <Field id="lp-name" label="Name" value={value} onChange={setValue} help="Use letters only." invalid={value === "x"} />
}

test("a reserved object is marked with its owner", () => {
  const { container } = renderWithProviders(<MarkBadges reserved="npm.admin.expose" unclaimed={false} />)
  expect(container.textContent).toContain("Reserved: npm.admin.expose")
  expect(container.textContent).not.toContain("Unclaimed")
})

test("an unclaimed object is marked as an unclaimed Darkpanel object", () => {
  const { container } = renderWithProviders(<MarkBadges reserved="" unclaimed />)
  expect(container.textContent).toContain("Unclaimed Darkpanel object")
  expect(container.textContent).not.toContain("Reserved")
})

test("an ordinary object has no mark", () => {
  const { container } = renderWithProviders(<MarkBadges reserved="" unclaimed={false} />)
  expect(container.textContent).not.toContain("Reserved")
  expect(container.textContent).not.toContain("Unclaimed")
})

test("a field has a label, a help line that describes it and an invalid state", async () => {
  renderWithProviders(<LpField />)
  const input = screen.getByLabelText("Name")
  expect(input.getAttribute("aria-describedby")).toBe("lp-name-help")
  expect(screen.getByText("Use letters only.").getAttribute("id")).toBe("lp-name-help")
  expect(input.getAttribute("aria-invalid")).not.toBe("true")
  await userEvent.setup().type(input, "x")
  expect(input.getAttribute("aria-invalid")).toBe("true")
})
```

Create `web/src/modules/npm/proxy-hosts-tab.test.tsx` (prefix `ph`):

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react"
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event"
import { expect, test } from "vitest"
import { axe } from "vitest-axe"
import { renderWithProviders, stubFetch, type FetchCall, type StubHandler } from "@/test/utils"
import { npProxyHost } from "./fixtures"
import { ProxyHostsTab } from "./proxy-hosts-tab"
import type { ProxyHostView } from "./types"

const PH_HOST = "/api/v1/hosts/h1"
const PH_LIST = `POST ${PH_HOST}/actions/npm.proxyhost.list/v1/read`
const PH_NAMES = "app.example.com, www.example.com"

function phPlan(call: FetchCall) {
  const parts = call.path.split("/")
  return {
    body: {
      planId: "p1", hostId: "h1", action: parts[parts.length - 3] ?? "", version: 1, inputHash: "h",
      diff: [{ op: "~", text: "change" }], guards: [], expiresAt: "2026-11-02T13:00:00Z", confirmation: null,
    },
  }
}

function phHosts(): ProxyHostView[] {
  return [
    npProxyHost({ id: 3, domains: ["app.example.com", "www.example.com"], forwardScheme: "http", forwardHost: "web", forwardPort: 8080, certificateId: 5, accessListId: 2, sslForced: true, hsts: true, websockets: false, reserved: "", unclaimed: false }),
    npProxyHost({ id: 4, domains: ["npm.example.com"], forwardScheme: "http", forwardHost: "127.0.0.1", forwardPort: 81, certificateId: 6, accessListId: 0, sslForced: true, hsts: true, websockets: true, reserved: "npm.admin.expose", unclaimed: false }),
    npProxyHost({ id: 9, domains: ["old.example.com"], forwardScheme: "https", forwardHost: "10.0.0.5", forwardPort: 443, certificateId: 0, accessListId: 0, sslForced: false, hsts: false, websockets: false, reserved: "", unclaimed: true }),
  ]
}

function phRender(items: ProxyHostView[] = phHosts(), routes: Record<string, StubHandler> = {}) {
  const calls = stubFetch({
    [PH_LIST]: { body: { output: { items } } },
    [`GET ${PH_HOST}/modules/npm/availability`]: { body: { module: "npm", available: true, reason: null, actions: [] } },
    [`POST ${PH_HOST}/actions/npm.proxyhost.add/v1/plan`]: phPlan,
    [`POST ${PH_HOST}/actions/npm.proxyhost.update/v1/plan`]: phPlan,
    [`POST ${PH_HOST}/actions/npm.proxyhost.remove/v1/plan`]: phPlan,
    ...routes,
  })
  const result = renderWithProviders(<ProxyHostsTab hostId="h1" />)
  return { calls, ...result }
}

function phCells(n: number): (string | null)[] {
  const row = within(screen.getByRole("table", { name: "Proxy hosts" })).getAllByRole("row")[n]
  if (row === undefined) throw new Error(`no row ${n}`)
  return within(row).getAllByRole("cell").map((c) => c.textContent)
}

function phPlanBodies(calls: FetchCall[]): unknown[] {
  return calls.filter((c) => c.method === "POST" && c.path.endsWith("/plan")).map((c) => c.body)
}

function phPlanPath(calls: FetchCall[]): string | undefined {
  return calls.find((c) => c.method === "POST" && c.path.endsWith("/plan"))?.path
}

test("lists each proxy host with its target, certificate, options and marks", async () => {
  phRender()
  await screen.findByRole("table", { name: "Proxy hosts" })
  expect(phCells(1).slice(0, 7)).toEqual(["3", PH_NAMES, "http://web:8080", "5", "2", "force SSL, HSTS", ""])
  expect(phCells(2).slice(0, 7)).toEqual(["4", "npm.example.com", "http://127.0.0.1:81", "6", "none", "force SSL, HSTS, websockets", "Reserved: npm.admin.expose"])
  expect(phCells(3).slice(0, 7)).toEqual(["9", "old.example.com", "https://10.0.0.5:443", "none", "none", "", "Unclaimed Darkpanel object"])
})

test("says so when there are no proxy hosts", async () => {
  phRender([])
  expect((await screen.findByText("No proxy hosts")).textContent).toBe("No proxy hosts")
  expect(screen.queryByRole("table")).toBeNull()
})

test("shows the reason when the proxy hosts cannot be read", async () => {
  phRender([], {
    [PH_LIST]: { status: 409, body: { type: "about:blank", title: "Unhealthy", status: 409, detail: "Nginx Proxy Manager is not running", code: "npm.unhealthy" } },
  })
  expect((await screen.findByRole("alert")).textContent).toBe("Nginx Proxy Manager is not running")
})

test("a reserved proxy host cannot be edited or removed here", async () => {
  phRender()
  await screen.findByRole("table", { name: "Proxy hosts" })
  expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Edit npm.example.com" }).hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(true)
  expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Remove npm.example.com" }).hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(true)
  expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Edit old.example.com" }).hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(false)
  expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: `Remove ${PH_NAMES}` }).hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(false)
})

test("removing a proxy host plans npm.proxyhost.remove with its id", async () => {
  const { calls } = phRender()
  await screen.findByRole("table", { name: "Proxy hosts" })
  await userEvent.setup().click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Remove old.example.com" }))
  const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Remove proxy host old.example.com" })
  await within(dialog).findByLabelText("Planned changes")
  expect(phPlanPath(calls)).toBe(`${PH_HOST}/actions/npm.proxyhost.remove/v1/plan`)
  expect(phPlanBodies(calls)).toEqual([{ input: { id: 9 } }])
})

test("the add form needs domains, a host and a valid port, and plans npm.proxyhost.add with every field", async () => {
  const { calls } = phRender()
  const user = userEvent.setup()
  await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Add proxy host" }))
  const form = await screen.findByRole("form", { name: "Proxy host" })
  const plan = within(form).getByRole("button", { name: "Plan" })
  expect(plan.hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(true)
  await user.type(within(form).getByLabelText("Domains"), "App.Example.com, www.example.com")
  await user.type(within(form).getByLabelText("Forward host"), " web ")
  expect(plan.hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(true)
  await user.type(within(form).getByLabelText("Forward port"), "8080")
  expect(plan.hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(false)
  await user.click(within(form).getByRole("radio", { name: "https" }))
  await user.type(within(form).getByLabelText("Certificate ID (optional)"), "5")
  await user.click(within(form).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Force SSL" }))
  await user.click(within(form).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Websockets" }))
  await user.click(plan)
  const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: `Add proxy host ${PH_NAMES}` })
  await within(dialog).findByLabelText("Planned changes")
  expect(phPlanPath(calls)).toBe(`${PH_HOST}/actions/npm.proxyhost.add/v1/plan`)
  expect(phPlanBodies(calls)).toEqual([
    {
      input: {
        domains: ["app.example.com", "www.example.com"],
        forwardScheme: "https",
        forwardHost: "web",
        forwardPort: 8080,
        certificateId: 5,
        accessListId: 0,
        sslForced: true,
        hsts: false,
        websockets: true,
      },
    },
  ])
})

test.each([
  ["Forward port", "0"],
  ["Forward port", "70000"],
  ["Certificate ID (optional)", "x"],
  ["Access list ID (optional)", "-2"],
])("%s %j keeps Plan off", async (label, typed) => {

test("editing prefills the form and plans npm.proxyhost.update with the id and every field", async () => {
  const { calls } = phRender()
  const user = userEvent.setup()
  await screen.findByRole("table", { name: "Proxy hosts" })
  await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: `Edit ${PH_NAMES}` }))

  expect(value("Domains")).toBe(PH_NAMES)
  expect(value("Forward host")).toBe("web")
  expect(value("Forward port")).toBe("8080")
  expect(value("Certificate ID (optional)")).toBe("5")
  expect(value("Access list ID (optional)")).toBe("2")
  expect(within(form).getByRole("radio", { name: "http", checked: true })).toBeTruthy()
  expect(within(form).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Force SSL", checked: true })).toBeTruthy()
  expect(within(form).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "HSTS", checked: true })).toBeTruthy()
  expect(within(form).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Websockets", checked: false })).toBeTruthy()
  const port = within(form).getByLabelText("Forward port")
  await user.clear(port)
  await user.type(port, "9090")
  await user.click(within(form).getByRole("button", { name: "Plan" }))
  const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: `Update proxy host ${PH_NAMES}` })
  await within(dialog).findByLabelText("Planned changes")
  expect(phPlanPath(calls)).toBe(`${PH_HOST}/actions/npm.proxyhost.update/v1/plan`)
  expect(phPlanBodies(calls)).toEqual([
    {
      input: {
        id: 3,
        domains: ["app.example.com", "www.example.com"],
        forwardScheme: "http",
        forwardHost: "web",
        forwardPort: 9090,
        certificateId: 5,
        accessListId: 2,
        sslForced: true,
        hsts: true,
        websockets: false,
      },
    },
  ])
})

test("the add form starts empty each time it opens", async () => {
  phRender()
  const user = userEvent.setup()
  await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Add proxy host" }))
  await user.type(within(await screen.findByRole("form", { name: "Proxy host" })).getByLabelText("Domains"), "app.example.com")
  await user.keyboard("{Escape}")
  await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByRole("dialog")).toBeNull())
  await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Add proxy host" }))
  expect((within(await screen.findByRole("form", { name: "Proxy host" })).getByLabelText("Domains") as HTMLInputElement).value).toBe("")
})

test("the table has no accessibility violations", async () => {
  phRender()
  const table = await screen.findByRole("table", { name: "Proxy hosts" })
  expect(await axe(table)).toHaveNoViolations()
})
```

Create `web/src/modules/npm/access-lists-tab.test.tsx` (prefix `al`):

```tsx
import { screen, within } from "@testing-library/react"
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event"
import { expect, test } from "vitest"
import { axe } from "vitest-axe"
import { renderWithProviders, stubFetch, type FetchCall, type StubHandler } from "@/test/utils"
import { AccessListsTab } from "./access-lists-tab"
import { npAccessList } from "./fixtures"
import type { AccessListView } from "./types"

const AL_HOST = "/api/v1/hosts/h1"
const AL_LIST = `POST ${AL_HOST}/actions/npm.accesslist.list/v1/read`

function alPlan(call: FetchCall) {
  const parts = call.path.split("/")
  return {
    body: {
      planId: "p1", hostId: "h1", action: parts[parts.length - 3] ?? "", version: 1, inputHash: "h",
      diff: [{ op: "~", text: "change" }], guards: [], expiresAt: "2026-11-02T13:00:00Z", confirmation: null,
    },
  }
}

function alLists(): AccessListView[] {
  return [
    npAccessList({ id: 2, name: "darkpanel-01jd-1", satisfyAny: true, clients: [{ address: "10.0.0.0/8", directive: "allow" }, { address: "192.0.2.1", directive: "deny" }], reserved: "", unclaimed: false }),
    npAccessList({ id: 3, name: "darkpanel-01jd-2", satisfyAny: false, clients: [{ address: "203.0.113.0/24", directive: "allow" }], reserved: "npm.admin.expose", unclaimed: false }),
    npAccessList({ id: 8, name: "darkpanel-01jd-9", satisfyAny: false, clients: [{ address: "198.51.100.7", directive: "allow" }], reserved: "", unclaimed: true }),
  ]
}

function alRender(items: AccessListView[] = alLists(), routes: Record<string, StubHandler> = {}) {
  const calls = stubFetch({
    [AL_LIST]: { body: { output: { items } } },
    [`GET ${AL_HOST}/modules/npm/availability`]: { body: { module: "npm", available: true, reason: null, actions: [] } },
    [`POST ${AL_HOST}/actions/npm.accesslist.add/v1/plan`]: alPlan,
    [`POST ${AL_HOST}/actions/npm.accesslist.remove/v1/plan`]: alPlan,
    ...routes,
  })
  const result = renderWithProviders(<AccessListsTab hostId="h1" />)
  return { calls, ...result }
}

function alCells(n: number): (string | null)[] {
  const row = within(screen.getByRole("table", { name: "Access lists" })).getAllByRole("row")[n]
  if (row === undefined) throw new Error(`no row ${n}`)
  return within(row).getAllByRole("cell").map((c) => c.textContent)
}

function alPlanBodies(calls: FetchCall[]): unknown[] {
  return calls.filter((c) => c.method === "POST" && c.path.endsWith("/plan")).map((c) => c.body)
}

test("lists each access list with how it combines its rules, the rules and the marks", async () => {
  alRender()
  await screen.findByRole("table", { name: "Access lists" })
  expect(alCells(1).slice(0, 5)).toEqual(["2", "darkpanel-01jd-1", "any rule", "allow 10.0.0.0/8, deny 192.0.2.1", ""])
  expect(alCells(2).slice(0, 5)).toEqual(["3", "darkpanel-01jd-2", "all rules", "allow 203.0.113.0/24", "Reserved: npm.admin.expose"])
  expect(alCells(3).slice(0, 5)).toEqual(["8", "darkpanel-01jd-9", "all rules", "allow 198.51.100.7", "Unclaimed Darkpanel object"])
})

test("says so when there are no access lists", async () => {
  alRender([])
  expect((await screen.findByText("No access lists")).textContent).toBe("No access lists")
})

test("shows the reason when the access lists cannot be read", async () => {
  alRender([], {
    [AL_LIST]: { status: 409, body: { type: "about:blank", title: "Unhealthy", status: 409, detail: "Nginx Proxy Manager is not running", code: "npm.unhealthy" } },
  })
  expect((await screen.findByRole("alert")).textContent).toBe("Nginx Proxy Manager is not running")
})

test("a reserved access list cannot be removed here", async () => {
  alRender()
  await screen.findByRole("table", { name: "Access lists" })
  expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Remove darkpanel-01jd-2" }).hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(true)
  expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Remove darkpanel-01jd-1" }).hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(false)
})

test("removing an access list plans npm.accesslist.remove with its id", async () => {
  const { calls } = alRender()
  await screen.findByRole("table", { name: "Access lists" })
  await userEvent.setup().click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Remove darkpanel-01jd-9" }))
  const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Remove access list darkpanel-01jd-9" })
  await within(dialog).findByLabelText("Planned changes")
  expect(calls.find((c) => c.path.endsWith("/plan"))?.path).toBe(`${AL_HOST}/actions/npm.accesslist.remove/v1/plan`)
  expect(alPlanBodies(calls)).toEqual([{ input: { id: 8 } }])
})

test("the add form reads one rule per line and plans npm.accesslist.add", async () => {
  const { calls } = alRender()
  const user = userEvent.setup()
  await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Add access list" }))
  const form = await screen.findByRole("form", { name: "Access list" })
  const plan = within(form).getByRole("button", { name: "Plan" })
  expect(plan.hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(true)
  await user.type(within(form).getByLabelText("Rules"), "allow 10.0.0.0/8{Enter}{Enter}Deny 192.0.2.1")
  expect(plan.hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(false)
  await user.click(within(form).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Satisfy any rule" }))
  await user.click(plan)
  const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Add an access list with 2 rules" })
  await within(dialog).findByLabelText("Planned changes")
  expect(calls.find((c) => c.path.endsWith("/plan"))?.path).toBe(`${AL_HOST}/actions/npm.accesslist.add/v1/plan`)
  expect(alPlanBodies(calls)).toEqual([
    { input: { satisfyAny: true, clients: [{ address: "10.0.0.0/8", directive: "allow" }, { address: "192.0.2.1", directive: "deny" }] } },
  ])
})

test("a line that is not a rule is named and keeps Plan off", async () => {
  alRender()
  const user = userEvent.setup()
  await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Add access list" }))
  const form = await screen.findByRole("form", { name: "Access list" })
  await user.type(within(form).getByLabelText("Rules"), "allow 10.0.0.1{Enter}permit 10.0.0.2")
  expect(within(form).getByText("Line 2: start with allow or deny, then an address.")).toBeTruthy()
  expect(within(form).getByRole("button", { name: "Plan" }).hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(true)
})

test("the table has no accessibility violations", async () => {
  alRender()
  const table = await screen.findByRole("table", { name: "Access lists" })
  expect(await axe(table)).toHaveNoViolations()
})
```

```tsx
import { screen, within } from "@testing-library/react"
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event"
import { expect, test } from "vitest"
import { axe } from "vitest-axe"
import { renderWithProviders, stubFetch, type FetchCall, type StubHandler } from "@/test/utils"
import { CertificatesTab } from "./certificates-tab"
import { npCertificate } from "./fixtures"
import type { CertificateView } from "./types"

const CE_HOST = "/api/v1/hosts/h1"
const CE_LIST = `POST ${CE_HOST}/actions/npm.certificate.list/v1/read`

function cePlan(call: FetchCall) {
  const parts = call.path.split("/")
  return {
    body: {
      planId: "p1", hostId: "h1", action: parts[parts.length - 3] ?? "", version: 1, inputHash: "h",
      diff: [{ op: "~", text: "change" }], guards: [], expiresAt: "2026-11-02T13:00:00Z", confirmation: null,
    },
  }
}

function ceCerts(): CertificateView[] {
  return [
    npCertificate({ id: 5, name: "darkpanel-01jd-3", domains: ["app.example.com", "www.example.com"], expiresOn: "2027-01-30 10:00:00", reserved: "", unclaimed: false }),
    npCertificate({ id: 6, name: "darkpanel-01jd-4", domains: ["npm.example.com"], expiresOn: "2027-02-01 10:00:00", reserved: "npm.admin.expose", unclaimed: false }),
    npCertificate({ id: 7, name: "darkpanel-01jd-8", domains: ["old.example.com"], expiresOn: "2027-02-02 10:00:00", reserved: "", unclaimed: true }),
  ]
}

function ceRender(items: CertificateView[] = ceCerts(), routes: Record<string, StubHandler> = {}) {
  const calls = stubFetch({
    [CE_LIST]: { body: { output: { items } } },
    [`GET ${CE_HOST}/modules/npm/availability`]: { body: { module: "npm", available: true, reason: null, actions: [] } },
    [`POST ${CE_HOST}/actions/npm.certificate.request/v1/plan`]: cePlan,
    [`POST ${CE_HOST}/actions/npm.certificate.remove/v1/plan`]: cePlan,
    ...routes,
  })
  const result = renderWithProviders(<CertificatesTab hostId="h1" />)
  return { calls, ...result }
}

function ceCells(n: number): (string | null)[] {
  const row = within(screen.getByRole("table", { name: "Certificates" })).getAllByRole("row")[n]
  if (row === undefined) throw new Error(`no row ${n}`)
  return within(row).getAllByRole("cell").map((c) => c.textContent)
}

function cePlanBodies(calls: FetchCall[]): unknown[] {
  return calls.filter((c) => c.method === "POST" && c.path.endsWith("/plan")).map((c) => c.body)
}

test("lists each certificate with its domains, expiry and marks", async () => {
  ceRender()
  await screen.findByRole("table", { name: "Certificates" })
  expect(ceCells(1).slice(0, 5)).toEqual(["5", "darkpanel-01jd-3", "app.example.com, www.example.com", "2027-01-30 10:00:00", ""])
  expect(ceCells(2).slice(0, 5)).toEqual(["6", "darkpanel-01jd-4", "npm.example.com", "2027-02-01 10:00:00", "Reserved: npm.admin.expose"])
  expect(ceCells(3).slice(0, 5)).toEqual(["7", "darkpanel-01jd-8", "old.example.com", "2027-02-02 10:00:00", "Unclaimed Darkpanel object"])
})

  expect((await screen.findByText("No certificates")).textContent).toBe("No certificates")
})

  })

})

  const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Remove certificate darkpanel-01jd-8" })

  expect(calls.find((c) => c.path.endsWith("/plan"))?.path).toBe(`${CE_HOST}/actions/npm.certificate.remove/v1/plan`)

test("the request form needs a domain and plans npm.certificate.request with the domains", async () => {
  const { calls } = ceRender()
  const user = userEvent.setup()
  await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Request certificate" }))
  const form = await screen.findByRole("form", { name: "Certificate" })
  const plan = within(form).getByRole("button", { name: "Plan" })

  await user.type(within(form).getByLabelText("Domains"), "A.example.com b.example.com")
  expect(plan.hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(false)

  await within(dialog).findByLabelText("Planned changes")
  expect(calls.find((c) => c.path.endsWith("/plan"))?.path).toBe(`${CE_HOST}/actions/npm.certificate.request/v1/plan`)
  expect(cePlanBodies(calls)).toEqual([{ input: { domains: ["a.example.com", "b.example.com"] } }])
})

test("the table has no accessibility violations", async () => {
  ceRender()
  const table = await screen.findByRole("table", { name: "Certificates" })
  expect(await axe(table)).toHaveNoViolations()
})
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the tests to verify they fail**

Run: `mkdir -p /root/.cache/w6a-tmp/9 && export TMPDIR=/root/.cache/w6a-tmp/9 GOTOOLCHAIN=local && timeout 600 pnpm -C web exec vitest run src/modules/npm/parse.test.ts src/modules/npm/list-parts.test.tsx src/modules/npm/proxy-hosts-tab.test.tsx src/modules/npm/access-lists-tab.test.tsx src/modules/npm/certificates-tab.test.tsx src/modules/npm/npm-page.test.tsx`
Expected: FAIL — the five new test files with `Failed to resolve import "./parse"` (and `./list-parts`, `./proxy-hosts-tab`, `./access-lists-tab`, `./certificates-tab`); the two new `npm-page.test.tsx` tests with a tab list of `["Instance"]` where four tabs are expected. Quote the output in the report.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

1. `parse.ts` (functions only, no React): `parseDomains(text)` splits on commas and whitespace, lowercases, drops empty entries and later duplicates, and keeps the order; `parsePort(text)` returns the whole number 1 to 65535 in the trimmed text, else `null` (digits only: `80.5`, `-1`, `1e3` are `null`); `parseOptionalId(text)` returns `0` for blank text, the whole positive number for digits that are not zero, else `null`; `parseRules(text)` returns `{ok: true, clients: {address, directive}[]}` or `{ok: false, error}`. It splits on `\r?\n`, skips blank lines, and takes each remaining line as exactly two whitespace-separated tokens, a directive of `allow` or `deny` in any letter case (stored lowercase) and an address. Any other line gives `Line <n>: start with allow or deny, then an address.` where `<n>` is the 1-based line number counting blank lines; no rule at all gives `Add at least one rule.` Whether the address is an IP or CIDR is left to the action.
2. `list-parts.tsx` exports only the components `MarkBadges` and `Field`. `MarkBadges({reserved, unclaimed})` renders a `Badge variant="secondary"` with text `Reserved: <reserved>` when `reserved` is not empty and a `Badge variant="outline"` with text `Unclaimed Darkpanel object` when `unclaimed`; with neither it renders nothing. `Field` is a `Label` plus `Input` with `id`, `value`, `placeholder`, `autoComplete="off"`, `aria-invalid` set when `invalid`, and, when `help` is given, a `<p id="<id>-help">` that the input names in `aria-describedby`.
3. Each tab follows `networks-tab.tsx`: a `useQuery` with the key named in Interfaces and `readAction<{items: <View>[]}>("<list action>", NPM_ACTION_VERSION, {}, hostId)`; `Spinner` with `aria-label` `Loading proxy hosts`, `Loading access lists`, `Loading certificates` while pending; a destructive `Alert role="alert"` with `errorMessage(error)` on error; `EmptyState` titled `No proxy hosts`, `No access lists`, `No certificates` for an empty list; otherwise a `Table` whose `aria-label` is `Proxy hosts`, `Access lists`, `Certificates`. A local `pending` state `{action, title, input}` feeds `<NpmChange hostId={hostId} action=... title=... input={pending?.input ?? null} onClose={() => setPending(null)} />`; a local `dialog` state opens the form `Dialog` (`open` and `onOpenChange` like `derp/deploy-dialog.tsx`, mounted only while open so each opening starts from its initial values). A form's `Plan` button closes the dialog and sets `pending`. The Remove (and Edit) button of a row whose `reserved` is not empty is disabled.
4. `proxy-hosts-tab.tsx` (`ProxyHostsTab`): columns `ID`, `Domains`, `Forwards to`, `Certificate`, `Access list`, `Options`, `Status`, and a visually hidden `Actions`. Cells: the id; `domains.join(", ")`; `<scheme>://<host>:<port>`; the certificate id or `none` when 0; the access list id or `none` when 0; the options `force SSL`, `HSTS`, `websockets` that are set, joined by `, `; `<MarkBadges>`; the buttons `Edit <domains joined>` and `Remove <domains joined>` (visible texts `Edit`, `Remove`). A header button `Add proxy host` opens the form. The form (`<form aria-label="Proxy host">`, dialog title `Add a proxy host` or `Edit a proxy host`) has `Domains` (help `Separate several names with commas or spaces.`), a `RadioGroup aria-label="Scheme"` with radios `http` and `https` (default `http`), `Forward host`, `Forward port`, `Certificate ID (optional)`, `Access list ID (optional)`, and checkboxes `Force SSL`, `HSTS`, `Websockets`, then `Plan`. `Plan` is disabled until `parseDomains` gives at least one name, the trimmed host is not empty, `parsePort` accepts the port, and `parseOptionalId` accepts both ids (an unparseable id marks its field invalid). The input is always `{domains, forwardScheme, forwardHost: <trimmed>, forwardPort, certificateId, accessListId, sslForced, hsts, websockets}` with `0` for an empty id and `false` for an unticked box. Add: action `npm.proxyhost.add`, title `Add proxy host <domains joined by ", ">`. Edit prefills every field from the row (domains joined by `, `, ids as text with `0` shown as empty) and sends the same input plus `id`: action `npm.proxyhost.update`, title `Update proxy host <domains joined>`. Remove: action `npm.proxyhost.remove`, title `Remove proxy host <domains joined>`, input `{id}`.
5. `access-lists-tab.tsx` (`AccessListsTab`): columns `ID`, `Name`, `Satisfy` (`any rule` when `satisfyAny`, else `all rules`), `Rules` (`<directive> <address>` joined by `, `), `Status` (`MarkBadges`), `Actions`. Header button `Add access list`. The form (`<form aria-label="Access list">`, dialog title `Add an access list`) has the checkbox `Satisfy any rule`, a `Label` `Rules` with a `Textarea` (help `One rule per line: allow or deny, then an IP address or CIDR.`; when the text is not blank and `parseRules` refuses it, the error shows as the field's help line and marks it invalid) and `Plan`, disabled until `parseRules` accepts. Action `npm.accesslist.add`, input `{satisfyAny, clients}`, title `Add an access list with <n> rule` or `... <n> rules`. Row button `Remove <name>`: action `npm.accesslist.remove`, title `Remove access list <name>`, input `{id}`. There is no edit.
6. `certificates-tab.tsx` (`CertificatesTab`): columns `ID`, `Name`, `Domains`, `Expires` (the `expiresOn` text as given), `Status`, `Actions`. Header button `Request certificate`. The form (`<form aria-label="Certificate">`, dialog title `Request a certificate`) has `Domains` (same help as above) and `Plan`, disabled until `parseDomains` gives a name. Action `npm.certificate.request`, input `{domains}`, title `Request a certificate for <domains joined by ", ">`. Row button `Remove <name>` (the domains joined when the name is empty): action `npm.certificate.remove`, title `Remove certificate <name>`, input `{id}`; the typed confirmation, if the plan asks for one, is the change dialog's.
7. `npm-page.tsx`: add `TabsTrigger` and `TabsContent` for `Proxy hosts` (`ProxyHostsTab`), `Access lists` (`AccessListsTab`) and `Certificates` (`CertificatesTab`) after the Instance tab, each passing `hostId`. Do not change the warning or the Instance tab. Radix mounts only the open tab's content, so a list is read when its tab opens.
8. Component files export only components (`react-refresh/only-export-components`); the helper functions live in `parse.ts`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Run the tests to verify they pass, then the checks**

### Task 10: web: expose action and admin-exposure banner

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/modules/npm/banner.tsx`
- Modify: `web/src/modules/npm/container-actions.tsx` (the stub `ExposeAction`), `web/src/shell.tsx` (add `<AdminExposureBanner />` directly above `<Outlet />`), `web/src/shell.test.tsx` (extend `renderShell`, add one test)
- Test: `web/src/modules/npm/container-actions.test.tsx`, `web/src/modules/npm/banner.test.tsx` (both new), `web/src/shell.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Contracts "Web: npm" (Task 5): `NPM_MODULE`, `NPM_ACTION_VERSION`, `npmKey`, `npmFleetKey`, `isInstanceStatus`, `isInstancesOutput`, the types `InstanceStatus`, `HostInstance`, `InstancesOutput`, the fixture `npInstance(over)`, and `NpmControllerChange({action, title, input, onClose})` from `npm-change.tsx`. Contracts "NPM controller reads and composites": `npm.instance.list` is a controller read that answers `InstancesOutput` (`{instances: [{hostId, hostName, status}]}`); `npm.expose.container` takes `ExposeInput` (`{hostId, container, port, network, domains, websockets, accessListId}`, lowerCamelCase). Existing: `ContainerRef` from `@/modules/types`; `ContainerDetail`, `DockerNetwork`, `DOCKER_MODULE`, `DOCKER_ACTION_VERSION`, `dockerKey` from `@/modules/docker/types`; `dkDetail`, `dkNetwork` from `@/modules/docker/fixtures` (tests); `readAction`, `readResource`, `errorMessage` from `@/api/client`.
- Produces: `container-actions.tsx` exports `ExposeAction({container, onDone}: {container: ContainerRef; onDone: () => void})` (the registry already lazy-loads it as the container action `npm.expose`). `banner.tsx` exports `AdminExposureBanner()`. `shell.tsx` renders the banner on every page.
- Query keys: the status read is `npmKey(hostId, "instance.status")`; the container is `["resource", hostId, "docker.container", containerId]` (the key `container-page.tsx` uses for the same read); the networks are `dockerKey(hostId, "networks")`; the fleet is `npmFleetKey`.
