Create `web/src/modules/npm/container-actions.test.tsx` (prefix `ca`; assumes Task 5's `npInstance()` default sets no `adminExposure`; every other asserted field is set explicitly):

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react"
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event"
import { expect, test, vi } from "vitest"
import { axe } from "vitest-axe"
import { dkDetail, dkNetwork } from "@/modules/docker/fixtures"
import type { ContainerDetail, EndpointView } from "@/modules/docker/types"
import type { ContainerRef } from "@/modules/types"
import { renderWithProviders, stubFetch, type FetchCall, type StubHandler } from "@/test/utils"
import { ExposeAction } from "./container-actions"
import { npInstance } from "./fixtures"
import type { InstanceStatus } from "./types"

const CA_HOST = "/api/v1/hosts/h1"
const CA_STATUS = `POST ${CA_HOST}/actions/npm.instance.status/v1/read`
const CA_INSPECT = `POST ${CA_HOST}/actions/docker.container.inspect/v1/read`
const CA_NETWORKS = `GET ${CA_HOST}/resources/docker/networks`
const CA_PLAN = "POST /api/v1/actions/npm.expose.container/v1/plan"
const CA_UNSUPPORTED =
  "This container is not on a user-defined bridge network, so Nginx Proxy Manager cannot reach it by name: connect the container to a user-defined network."

const caContainer: ContainerRef = { hostId: "h1", containerId: "c1", name: "web", ports: [] }

function caPlan(call: FetchCall) {
  return {
    body: {
      planId: "p1", hostId: null, action: "npm.expose.container", version: 1, inputHash: "h",
      diff: [{ op: "~", text: String(call.path) }], guards: [], expiresAt: "2026-11-02T13:00:00Z", confirmation: null,
    },
  }
}

function caStatus(over: Partial<InstanceStatus> = {}): InstanceStatus {
  return npInstance({ deployed: true, containerId: "c0ffee", running: true, apiReady: true, public: "confirmed", adminEmail: "admin@example.com", ...over })
}

function caEndpoint(network: string): EndpointView {
  return { network, networkId: "id-" + network, container: "web" }
}

function caDetail(networks: string[] = ["bridge", "backend", "alpha", "mesh"]): ContainerDetail {
  return dkDetail({
    id: "c1",
    ports: [{ privatePort: 9000, proto: "tcp" }, { privatePort: 80, proto: "tcp" }],
    networks: networks.map(caEndpoint),
  })
}

function caNetworks() {
  return [
    dkNetwork({ id: "id-bridge", name: "bridge", driver: "bridge" }),
    dkNetwork({ id: "id-backend", name: "backend", driver: "bridge" }),
    dkNetwork({ id: "id-alpha", name: "alpha", driver: "bridge" }),
    dkNetwork({ id: "id-mesh", name: "mesh", driver: "overlay" }),
    dkNetwork({ id: "id-host", name: "host", driver: "host" }),
  ]
}

function caRender(routes: Record<string, StubHandler> = {}, container: ContainerRef = caContainer, onDone: () => void = () => {}) {
  const calls = stubFetch({
    [CA_STATUS]: { body: { output: caStatus() } },
    [CA_INSPECT]: { body: { output: caDetail() } },
    [CA_NETWORKS]: { body: { networks: caNetworks() } },
    [CA_PLAN]: caPlan,
    ...routes,
  })
  const result = renderWithProviders(<ExposeAction container={container} onDone={onDone} />)
  return { calls, ...result }
}

function caPlanBodies(calls: FetchCall[]): unknown[] {
  return calls.filter((c) => c.method === "POST" && c.path.endsWith("/plan")).map((c) => c.body)
}

async function caForm() {
  return screen.findByRole("form", { name: "Expose container" })
}

test("says so when the host has no deployed Nginx Proxy Manager", async () => {
  caRender({ [CA_STATUS]: { body: { output: caStatus({ deployed: false, containerId: "", running: false, apiReady: false, public: "no", adminEmail: "" }) } } })
  expect((await screen.findByText("Nginx Proxy Manager is not deployed on this host. Deploy it first.")).textContent).toBe(
    "Nginx Proxy Manager is not deployed on this host. Deploy it first.",
  )
  expect(screen.queryByRole("form")).toBeNull()
})

test("shows the reason when the NPM status cannot be read", async () => {
  caRender({ [CA_STATUS]: { status: 503, body: { type: "about:blank", title: "Unavailable", status: 503, detail: "the NPM module is unavailable", code: "module.unavailable" } } })
  expect((await screen.findByRole("alert")).textContent).toBe("the NPM module is unavailable")
  expect(screen.queryByRole("form")).toBeNull()
})

test("shows the reason when the container cannot be read", async () => {
  caRender({ [CA_INSPECT]: { status: 503, body: { type: "about:blank", title: "Unavailable", status: 503, detail: "the Docker daemon is not reachable", code: "docker.engine_unavailable" } } })
  expect((await screen.findByRole("alert")).textContent).toBe("the Docker daemon is not reachable")
})

test("offers only the user-defined bridge networks, selects the first in name order and defaults to the lowest port", async () => {
  const { calls } = caRender()
  const user = userEvent.setup()
  const form = await caForm()
  expect(within(form).getAllByRole("radio")).toHaveLength(2)
  expect(within(form).getByRole("radio", { name: "alpha", checked: true })).toBeTruthy()
  expect(within(form).getByRole("radio", { name: "backend", checked: false })).toBeTruthy()
  for (const hidden of ["bridge", "mesh", "host"]) expect(within(form).queryByRole("radio", { name: hidden })).toBeNull()
  expect((within(form).getByLabelText("Port") as HTMLInputElement).value).toBe("80")
  const plan = within(form).getByRole("button", { name: "Plan" })
  expect(plan.hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(true)
  await user.type(within(form).getByLabelText("Domains"), "App.Example.com, www.example.com")
  expect(plan.hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(false)
  await user.click(plan)
  const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Expose web at app.example.com, www.example.com" })
  await within(dialog).findByLabelText("Planned changes")
  expect(calls.find((c) => c.path.endsWith("/plan"))?.path).toBe("/api/v1/actions/npm.expose.container/v1/plan")
  expect(caPlanBodies(calls)).toEqual([
    { input: { hostId: "h1", container: "web", port: 80, network: "alpha", domains: ["app.example.com", "www.example.com"] } },
  ])
})

test("another network, websockets and an access list reach the input", async () => {
  const { calls } = caRender()
  const user = userEvent.setup()
  const form = await caForm()
  await user.click(within(form).getByRole("radio", { name: "backend" }))
  const port = within(form).getByLabelText("Port")
  await user.clear(port)
  await user.type(port, "9000")
  await user.type(within(form).getByLabelText("Domains"), "api.example.com")
  await user.click(within(form).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Websockets" }))
  await user.type(within(form).getByLabelText("Access list ID (optional)"), "3")
  await user.click(within(form).getByRole("button", { name: "Plan" }))
  await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Expose web at api.example.com" })
  await waitFor(() => expect(caPlanBodies(calls)).toHaveLength(1))
  expect(caPlanBodies(calls)).toEqual([
    { input: { hostId: "h1", container: "web", port: 9000, network: "backend", domains: ["api.example.com"], websockets: true, accessListId: 3 } },
  ])
})

test("the container name is sent without Docker's leading slash", async () => {
  const { calls } = caRender({}, { ...caContainer, name: "/web" })
  const user = userEvent.setup()
  const form = await caForm()
  await user.type(within(form).getByLabelText("Domains"), "app.example.com")
  await user.click(within(form).getByRole("button", { name: "Plan" }))
  await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Expose web at app.example.com" })
  expect((caPlanBodies(calls)[0] as { input: { container: string } }).input.container).toBe("web")
})

test.each([
  ["Port", "0"],
  ["Port", "65536"],
  ["Port", ""],
  ["Port", "http"],
  ["Access list ID (optional)", "abc"],
])("%s %j keeps Plan off", async (label, typed) => {
  caRender()
  const user = userEvent.setup()
  const form = await caForm()
  await user.type(within(form).getByLabelText("Domains"), "app.example.com")
  expect(within(form).getByRole("button", { name: "Plan" }).hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(false)
  const field = within(form).getByLabelText(label)
  await user.clear(field)
  if (typed !== "") await user.type(field, typed)
  expect(within(form).getByRole("button", { name: "Plan" }).hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(true)
})

test("a container with no user-defined network is warned about and its refusal in the preview is shown", async () => {
  const { calls } = caRender({
    [CA_INSPECT]: { body: { output: caDetail(["mesh", "bridge"]) } },
    [CA_PLAN]: {
      status: 409,
      body: {
        type: "about:blank", title: "Unsupported target", status: 409,
        detail: "web is not on a user-defined bridge network: connect the container to a user-defined network", code: "npm.unsupported_target",
      },
    },
  })
  const user = userEvent.setup()
  const form = await caForm()
  expect(within(form).getByText(CA_UNSUPPORTED)).toBeTruthy()
  expect(within(form).queryAllByRole("radio")).toHaveLength(0)
  await user.type(within(form).getByLabelText("Domains"), "app.example.com")
  await user.click(within(form).getByRole("button", { name: "Plan" }))
  const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Expose web at app.example.com" })
  expect((await within(dialog).findByRole("alert")).textContent).toBe(
    "web is not on a user-defined bridge network: connect the container to a user-defined network",
  )
  expect(caPlanBodies(calls)).toEqual([{ input: { hostId: "h1", container: "web", port: 80, network: "bridge", domains: ["app.example.com"] } }])
})

test("closing the change dialog calls onDone", async () => {
  const onDone = vi.fn()
  caRender({}, caContainer, onDone)
  const user = userEvent.setup()
  const form = await caForm()
  await user.type(within(form).getByLabelText("Domains"), "app.example.com")
  await user.click(within(form).getByRole("button", { name: "Plan" }))
  const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Expose web at app.example.com" })
  await within(dialog).findByLabelText("Planned changes")
  expect(onDone).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
  await user.keyboard("{Escape}")
  await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByRole("dialog")).toBeNull())
  expect(onDone).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
})

test("the form has no accessibility violations", async () => {
  caRender()
  const form = await caForm()
  expect(await axe(form)).toHaveNoViolations()
})
```

Create `web/src/modules/npm/banner.test.tsx` (prefix `bn`):

```tsx
import { useState, type ReactElement } from "react"
import { fireEvent, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react"
import type { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { expect, test, vi } from "vitest"
import { axe } from "vitest-axe"
import { renderWithProviders, stubFetch, type FetchCall, type StubHandler } from "@/test/utils"
import { AdminExposureBanner } from "./banner"
import { npInstance } from "./fixtures"
import { npmFleetKey, type HostInstance } from "./types"

const BN_LIST = "POST /api/v1/actions/npm.instance.list/v1/read"
const BN_WEB1 = "The NPM admin interface of web-1 is exposed at https://npm.example.com."

function bnExposed(hostId: string, hostName: string, hostname: string): HostInstance {
  return { hostId, hostName, status: npInstance({ deployed: true, adminExposure: { hostname, proxyHostId: 4, certificateId: 5 } }) }
}

function bnPlain(hostId: string, hostName: string): HostInstance {
  return { hostId, hostName, status: npInstance({ deployed: true }) }
}

function bnRender(response: StubHandler, ui: ReactElement = <AdminExposureBanner />) {
  const calls = stubFetch({ [BN_LIST]: response })
  const result = renderWithProviders(ui)
  return { calls, ...result }
}

function bnReads(calls: FetchCall[]): number {
  return calls.filter((c) => c.method === "POST" && c.path === "/api/v1/actions/npm.instance.list/v1/read").length
}

async function bnSettled(client: QueryClient) {
  await waitFor(() => expect(["success", "error"]).toContain(client.getQueryState(npmFleetKey)?.status))
}

function BnToggle() {
  const [shown, setShown] = useState(true)
  return (
    <>
      <button onClick={() => setShown((s) => !s)}>Toggle</button>
      {shown && <AdminExposureBanner />}
    </>
  )
}

test("shows one warning for each host whose admin interface is exposed, linking to its NPM page", async () => {
  const output = { instances: [bnExposed("h1", "web-1", "npm.example.com"), bnPlain("h2", "web-2"), bnExposed("h3", "web-3", "admin.example.org")] }
  bnRender({ body: { output } })
  expect(await screen.findByText(BN_WEB1)).toBeTruthy()
  expect(screen.getByText("The NPM admin interface of web-3 is exposed at https://admin.example.org.")).toBeTruthy()
  expect(screen.queryByText(/web-2/)).toBeNull()
  expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "Open Nginx Proxy Manager on web-1" }).getAttribute("href")).toBe("/hosts/h1/npm")
  expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "Open Nginx Proxy Manager on web-3" }).getAttribute("href")).toBe("/hosts/h3/npm")
})

test("renders nothing when no host exposes its admin interface", async () => {
  const { client } = bnRender({ body: { output: { instances: [bnPlain("h1", "web-1")] } } })
  await bnSettled(client)
  expect(screen.queryByText(/NPM admin interface/)).toBeNull()
  expect(screen.queryByRole("link")).toBeNull()
})

test("renders nothing when the fleet read fails", async () => {
  const { client } = bnRender({ status: 503, body: { type: "about:blank", title: "Unavailable", status: 503, detail: "the database is busy", code: "unavailable" } })
  await bnSettled(client)
  expect(client.getQueryState(npmFleetKey)?.status).toBe("error")
  expect(screen.queryByText(/NPM admin interface/)).toBeNull()
  expect(screen.queryByRole("alert")).toBeNull()
})

test("renders nothing when the controller answers with something that is not an instance list", async () => {
  const { client } = bnRender({ body: { output: { instances: "none" } } })
  await bnSettled(client)
  expect(client.getQueryState(npmFleetKey)?.status).toBe("error")
  expect(screen.queryByText(/NPM admin interface/)).toBeNull()
})

test("the fleet read is kept for 60 seconds before a new mount reads it again", async () => {
  vi.useFakeTimers({ shouldAdvanceTime: true })
  try {
    const { calls } = bnRender({ body: { output: { instances: [bnExposed("h1", "web-1", "npm.example.com")] } } }, <BnToggle />)
    expect(await screen.findByText(BN_WEB1)).toBeTruthy()
    const toggle = () => fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Toggle" }))
    toggle()
    expect(screen.queryByText(BN_WEB1)).toBeNull()
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(30_000)
    toggle()
    expect(await screen.findByText(BN_WEB1)).toBeTruthy()
    expect(bnReads(calls)).toBe(1)
    toggle()
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(31_000)
    toggle()
    await waitFor(() => expect(bnReads(calls)).toBe(2))
  } finally {
    vi.useRealTimers()
  }
})

test("the banner has no accessibility violations", async () => {
  const { container } = bnRender({ body: { output: { instances: [bnExposed("h1", "web-1", "npm.example.com")] } } })
  await screen.findByText(BN_WEB1)
  expect(await axe(container)).toHaveNoViolations()
})
```

Edit `web/src/shell.test.tsx`. Add these imports with the existing ones: `import { npInstance } from "@/modules/npm/fixtures"` and `import type { InstancesOutput } from "@/modules/npm/types"`. Replace the `renderShell` function with this version, which adds a trailing `extra` parameter (existing callers are unchanged):

```tsx
function renderShell(
  listeners: StubResponse,
  path = "/",
  logout: StubResponse = { status: 204 },
  hosts: Host[] = [],
  hostList: StubResponse = { body: { output: { hosts } } },
  extra: Record<string, StubResponse> = {},
) {
  const calls = stubFetch({
    "GET /api/v1/auth/session": { body: session },
    "GET /api/v1/controller/listeners": listeners,
    "POST /api/v1/auth/logout": logout,
    "POST /api/v1/actions/core.host.list/v1/read": hostList,
    ...extra,
  })
  renderWithProviders(
    <SessionProvider>
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/login" element={<p>Login page</p>} />
        <Route element={<RequireSession><Shell /></RequireSession>}>
          <Route index element={<p>Hosts content</p>} />
          <Route path="audit" element={<p>Audit content</p>} />
          <Route path="hosts/:hostId/*" element={<p>Host content</p>} />
        </Route>
      </Routes>
    </SessionProvider>,
    { path },
  )
  return calls
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the tests to verify they fail**

Run: `mkdir -p /root/.cache/w6a-tmp/10 && export TMPDIR=/root/.cache/w6a-tmp/10 GOTOOLCHAIN=local && timeout 600 pnpm -C web exec vitest run src/modules/npm/container-actions.test.tsx src/modules/npm/banner.test.tsx src/shell.test.tsx`
Expected: FAIL — `banner.test.tsx` with `Failed to resolve import "./banner"`; `container-actions.test.tsx` because the stub `ExposeAction` renders `null`, so `findByText("Nginx Proxy Manager is not deployed on this host. Deploy it first.")` and `findByRole("form", {name: "Expose container"})` time out; the new `shell.test.tsx` test cannot find the banner text (the other shell tests still pass). Quote the output in the report.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

1. `banner.tsx` exports only `AdminExposureBanner()`. It reads `useQuery({queryKey: npmFleetKey, queryFn, staleTime: 60_000})`, where `queryFn` is `readAction<unknown>("npm.instance.list", NPM_ACTION_VERSION, {})` that throws `new Error("The controller answered with an unexpected NPM instance list.")` unless `isInstancesOutput` accepts the body. While pending, on error, and when no instance has `status.adminExposure` set (neither `null` nor `undefined`), it renders nothing (`null`); it shows no spinner and no error. Otherwise, for each such instance in the order the controller gave, it renders `Alert variant="warning" role="status" className="mb-4"` with `AlertTitle` `Admin interface exposed`, an `AlertDescription` holding exactly one text node `The NPM admin interface of <hostName> is exposed at https://<hostname>.`, and below it a react-router `Link` to `/hosts/<encodeURIComponent(hostId)>/npm` whose text is `Open Nginx Proxy Manager on <hostName>`.
2. `shell.tsx`: import `AdminExposureBanner` from `@/modules/npm/banner` and render `<AdminExposureBanner />` directly above `<Outlet />` (after the logout error alert), so every page shows it. No other change.
3. `container-actions.tsx` replaces the stub; it exports only `ExposeAction({container, onDone})`, built from three components.
   - `ExposeAction` reads the status with `useQuery({queryKey: npmKey(container.hostId, "instance.status"), queryFn})`, where `queryFn` calls `readAction<unknown>("npm.instance.status", NPM_ACTION_VERSION, {}, container.hostId)` and throws `new Error("The host answered with an unexpected NPM instance status.")` unless `isInstanceStatus` accepts it. Pending shows `Spinner aria-label="Loading Nginx Proxy Manager"`. An error shows a destructive `Alert role="alert"` with the message of an `ApiError` (`errorMessage`) or the guard's `Error.message`. `deployed === false` shows the plain text `Nginx Proxy Manager is not deployed on this host. Deploy it first.` and no form. Otherwise it renders `ExposeLoader`.
   - `ExposeLoader({container, onDone})` reads the container with key `["resource", hostId, "docker.container", containerId]` and `readAction<ContainerDetail>("docker.container.inspect", DOCKER_ACTION_VERSION, {container: containerId}, hostId)`, and the networks with key `dockerKey(hostId, "networks")` and `readResource<{networks: DockerNetwork[]}>(hostId, DOCKER_MODULE, "networks")`. While either is pending it shows `Spinner aria-label="Loading the container's networks"`; the first error shows a destructive `Alert role="alert"` with `errorMessage`. When both answered it renders `ExposeForm` with the container's endpoint names and the network list.
   - `ExposeForm` computes the supported networks: the container's endpoint network names that appear in the network list with driver `bridge` and a name other than `bridge`, sorted by name. The selected network is the one picked in the radio group, else the first supported. When none is supported the form shows `Alert variant="warning" role="status"` with exactly `This container is not on a user-defined bridge network, so Nginx Proxy Manager cannot reach it by name: connect the container to a user-defined network.`, no radios, and plans with `network` set to the first endpoint name in name order (or `""` when it has none), so that the controller's own refusal (`npm.unsupported_target`) shows in the preview; `Plan` stays enabled in that case. The form is `<form aria-label="Expose container">` with: `RadioGroup aria-label="Network"` whose radios are labelled with the network names (only when some are supported); `Port` (initial text: the lowest `privatePort` of the container's `ports`, empty when it has none); `Domains` (help `Separate several names with commas or spaces.`); a checkbox `Websockets`; `Access list ID (optional)`; and `Plan`. `Plan` is disabled until the port is a whole number from 1 to 65535, at least one domain is given (split on commas and whitespace, lowercased, duplicates dropped), and the access list text is empty or a positive whole number (digits only, not `0`). Submitting sets the pending change: action `npm.expose.container`, title `Expose <name> at <domains joined by ", ">`, input `{hostId, container: <name>, port, network, domains}` plus `websockets: true` only when ticked and `accessListId: <number>` only when given; `<name>` is `container.name` without a leading `/`. The pending change renders `NpmControllerChange` with `onClose` setting the pending change to `null` and calling `onDone()`.
   - The registry test keeps passing because every read failure renders an alert instead of throwing.

**Depends on:** Task 1, Task 6
**Implementer:** dforge:impl-opus-high
**Evaluation:** files 1 - spec 2 - coupling 2 - risk 3 = 8

Decisions this task embodies (spec 6 "Preconditions", "Deploy (R65)", "Bootstrap", "Recovery identities"; Contracts "NPM identities"):
- `Module()` leaves `npm.go` for `deps.go` because it needs the production wiring; `module` becomes `struct{ n nm }`, and `Manifest()` keeps `ID`, `Version` 1 and `DefaultEnabled`, adding `OwnedPaths: ownedPaths()` and `Migrations: {core.SideAgent: agentMigrations()}`. It declares no `Actions`: Task 27 lists them, so no schema snapshot is due before it. The migration runs on the agent database only.
- An Engine creation effect is observed in the restorable form of the container document (no `startedAt`, no `finishedAt`) in every effect state. Task 12 starts the container it created, and the engine compares an effect's observation with its recorded post-state before it undoes that effect (`runInverse`). Were the creation compared in the recorded form, the start and its stop would leave `startedAt` and `finishedAt` set, the creation would look changed, and the undo would stop with a `conflict`. A modification keeps the recorded form until it is being undone.
- `run` skips an effect not only on a 4xx answer but also when `do` returns an error already marked `core.Definite` (a local precondition that failed before anything changed), so a bookkeeping effect of Task 13 ends `skipped`, not `intended`.
- Beyond Contracts "NPM identities": an Engine creation effect `npm.endpoint:?<network>` declares the recovery value `<network>`, because the engine requires a creation's resource to be `<noun>:?<recovery value>`. Contracts' `darkpanel-npm@<network>` is the `InverseRecovery` value of an endpoint removal, which Task 15 declares on a modification. `observeEngine` reads an endpoint by the network's name and renders it through `dstate.Endpoint` with the container name `darkpanel-npm` and that network name.
- Production wiring never reaches the host from a test binary: when `testing.Testing()` is true the Docker client dials `absentSocket` and the NPM client `absentURL`, as `docker/deps.go` does.

```go
package npm

	_ core.Runner        = (*thHost)(nil)
	_ core.FileWriter    = (*thHost)(nil)
	_ core.EffectJournal = (*thJournal)(nil)
	_ dockerAPI          = (*thDocker)(nil)
	_ dockerAPI          = dockerClient{}
	_ npmAPI             = (*npmapi.Client)(nil)
```
