```go
package npm

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"net/netip"
	"reflect"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/platform/dockerapi"
)

func TestSdMigrationTables(t *testing.T) {
	h := newTHHost(t)
	rows, err := h.db.Read().Query(`SELECT name, strict FROM pragma_table_list WHERE name LIKE 'npm\_%' ESCAPE '\' ORDER BY name`)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer func() { _ = rows.Close() }()
	var got []string
	for rows.Next() {
		var name string
		var strict int
		if err := rows.Scan(&name, &strict); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if strict != 1 {
			t.Errorf("table %s is not STRICT", name)
		}
		got = append(got, name)
	}
	want := []string{"npm_attachments", "npm_instances", "npm_reserved", "npm_upgrades", "npm_watch"}
	if !slices.Equal(got, want) {
		t.Errorf("tables %v, want %v", got, want)
	}
}

func TestSdMigrationConstraints(t *testing.T) {
	h := newTHHost(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	exec := func(q string) error {
		return h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error { _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, q); return err })
	}
	const inst = `INSERT INTO npm_instances (id, container_id, image, admin_email, password_ct, public, created_at, updated_at) VALUES `
	const upg = `INSERT INTO npm_upgrades (change_id, from_image, to_image, config, phase, updated_at) VALUES `
	bad := map[string]string{
		"a second instance row":     inst + `(2, 'c', 'i', 'e', x'00', 'no', '', '')`,
		"an unknown public mark":    inst + `(1, 'c', 'i', 'e', x'00', 'maybe', '', '')`,
		"an unknown reserved kind":  `INSERT INTO npm_reserved VALUES ('server', 1, 'npm.admin.expose', 'h', 'c1')`,
		"an unknown reserved owner": `INSERT INTO npm_reserved VALUES ('proxy_host', 1, 'someone', 'h', 'c1')`,
		"a preexisting flag of 2":   `INSERT INTO npm_attachments VALUES ('n', 'c', 'c1', 2)`,
		"an unknown upgrade phase":  upg + `('c1', 'a', 'b', '{}', 'sideways', '')`,
		"a text object id":          `INSERT INTO npm_reserved VALUES ('proxy_host', 'x', 'npm.admin.expose', 'h', 'c1')`,
	}
	for name, q := range bad {
		if err := exec(q); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("%s was accepted", name)
		}
	}
	for _, q := range []string{
		inst + `(1, 'c', 'i', 'e', x'00', 'confirmed', '', '')`,
		`INSERT INTO npm_reserved VALUES ('certificate', 1, 'panel.exposure.add', 'h', 'c1')`,
		`INSERT INTO npm_attachments VALUES ('n', 'c', 'c1', 1)`,
		upg + `('c1', 'a', 'b', '{}', 'recorded', '')`,
	} {
		if err := exec(q); err != nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: %v", q, err)
		}
	}
	var archives, result string
	if err := h.db.Read().QueryRow(`SELECT archives, result FROM npm_upgrades WHERE change_id = 'c1'`).Scan(&archives, &result); err != nil || archives != "[]" || result != "" {
		t.Errorf("upgrade defaults %q and %q (error %v), want [] and an empty result", archives, result, err)
	}
}

func TestSdStoreTime(t *testing.T) {
	if storeTime(time.Time{}) != "" {
		t.Error("the zero time is not stored as an empty string")
	}
	at := time.Date(2026, 11, 2, 12, 30, 15, 123456789, time.FixedZone("x", 3600))
	text := storeTime(at)
	if text != "2026-11-02T11:30:15.123456789Z" {
		t.Errorf("storeTime = %q", text)
	}
	back, err := parseStoreTime(text)
	if err != nil || !back.Equal(at) {
		t.Errorf("parseStoreTime(%q) = %v, %v; want %v", text, back, err, at)
	}
	if zero, err := parseStoreTime(""); err != nil || !zero.IsZero() {
		t.Errorf("parseStoreTime(\"\") = %v, %v; want the zero time", zero, err)
	}
	if _, err := parseStoreTime("yesterday"); err == nil {
		t.Error("parseStoreTime accepted yesterday")
	}
}

func TestSdInstanceRow(t *testing.T) {
	h := newTHHost(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	if _, ok, err := loadInstance(ctx, h.db.Read()); err != nil || ok {
		t.Fatalf("empty store: ok %v, error %v", ok, err)
	}
	want := instanceRow{ContainerID: "c1", Image: "img1", AdminEmail: "ops@darkpanel.dev", PasswordCT: []byte{1, 2, 3}, Public: "no", CreatedAt: thStart, UpdatedAt: thStart}
	save := func(r instanceRow) {
		t.Helper()
		if err := h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error { return saveInstance(ctx, tx, r) }); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	save(want)
	got, ok, err := loadInstance(ctx, h.db.Read())
	if err != nil || !ok {
		t.Fatalf("load: ok %v, error %v", ok, err)
	}
	if got.ContainerID != "c1" || got.Image != "img1" || got.AdminEmail != "ops@darkpanel.dev" || !slices.Equal(got.PasswordCT, []byte{1, 2, 3}) ||
		got.Public != "no" || !got.CreatedAt.Equal(thStart) || !got.UpdatedAt.Equal(thStart) {
		t.Errorf("loaded %+v, want %+v", got, want)
	}
	want.Public, want.Image, want.UpdatedAt = "confirmed", "img2", thStart.Add(time.Hour)
	save(want)
	got, _, _ = loadInstance(ctx, h.db.Read())
	if got.Public != "confirmed" || got.Image != "img2" || !got.UpdatedAt.Equal(thStart.Add(time.Hour)) || !got.CreatedAt.Equal(thStart) {
		t.Errorf("after a second save %+v, want the one row updated", got)
	}
	var n int
	if err := h.db.Read().QueryRow(`SELECT count(*) FROM npm_instances`).Scan(&n); err != nil || n != 1 {
		t.Errorf("%d instance rows (error %v), want 1", n, err)
	}
	for range 2 {
		if err := h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error { return deleteInstance(ctx, tx) }); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	if _, ok, _ := loadInstance(ctx, h.db.Read()); ok {
		t.Error("the instance survived deleteInstance")
	}
}

func TestSdReservedRows(t *testing.T) {
	h := newTHHost(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	rows := []reservedRow{
		{Kind: "proxy_host", ObjectID: 7, Owner: "npm.admin.expose", Exposure: "npm.example.org", ChangeID: "c1"},
		{Kind: "certificate", ObjectID: 7, Owner: "npm.admin.expose", Exposure: "npm.example.org", ChangeID: "c1"},
		{Kind: "proxy_host", ObjectID: 8, Owner: "panel.exposure.add", Exposure: "panel.example.org", ChangeID: "c2"},
	}
	if err := h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		for _, r := range rows {
			if err := addReserved(ctx, tx, r); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		return nil
	}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, r := range rows {
		got, ok, err := reservedOf(ctx, h.db.Read(), r.Kind, r.ObjectID)
		if err != nil || !ok || got != r {
			t.Errorf("reservedOf(%s, %d) = %+v, %v, %v; want %+v", r.Kind, r.ObjectID, got, ok, err, r)
		}
	}
	if _, ok, err := reservedOf(ctx, h.db.Read(), "access_list", 7); err != nil || ok {
		t.Errorf("an unreserved object: ok %v, error %v", ok, err)
	}
	if err := h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error { return addReserved(ctx, tx, rows[0]) }); err == nil {
		t.Error("an object reserved twice was accepted")
	}
	if err := h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error { return dropReservedExposure(ctx, tx, "npm.example.org") }); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, c := range []struct {
		kind string
		id   int
		held bool
	}{{"proxy_host", 7, false}, {"certificate", 7, false}, {"proxy_host", 8, true}} {
		if _, ok, _ := reservedOf(ctx, h.db.Read(), c.kind, c.id); ok != c.held {
			t.Errorf("%s %d reserved = %v after releasing npm.example.org, want %v", c.kind, c.id, ok, c.held)
		}
	}
	if err := h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error { return dropReservedExposure(ctx, tx, "nobody.example.org") }); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("releasing an exposure with no rows: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestSdClaims(t *testing.T) {
	h := newTHHost(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	all := []claimRow{
		{Network: "net2", Claimant: "a", ChangeID: "c1"},
		{Network: "net1", Claimant: "b", ChangeID: "c2", Preexisting: true},
		{Network: "net1", Claimant: "a", ChangeID: "c3"},
	}
	if err := h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		for _, c := range all {
			if err := addClaim(ctx, tx, c); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		return nil
	}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error { return addClaim(ctx, tx, all[0]) }); err == nil {
		t.Error("a claimant claiming a network twice was accepted")
	}
	got, err := claims(ctx, h.db.Read(), "net1")
	want := []claimRow{all[2], all[1]}
	if err != nil || !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Errorf("claims(net1) = %+v, %v; want %+v by claimant", got, err, want)
	}
	got, err = claims(ctx, h.db.Read(), "")
	want = []claimRow{all[2], all[1], all[0]}
	if err != nil || !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Errorf("claims(\"\") = %+v, %v; want %+v by network and claimant", got, err, want)
	}
	if got, err := claims(ctx, h.db.Read(), "net3"); err != nil || len(got) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("claims(net3) = %+v, %v; want none", got, err)
	}
	for range 2 {
		if err := h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error { return dropClaim(ctx, tx, "net1", "a") }); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	if got, _ := claims(ctx, h.db.Read(), "net1"); len(got) != 1 || got[0].Claimant != "b" {
		t.Errorf("claims(net1) after dropping a = %+v, want only b", got)
	}
}

func TestSdUpgradeRow(t *testing.T) {
	h := newTHHost(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	cfg := containerConfig{
		Create: dockerapi.ContainerCreate{
			ContainerConfig: dockerapi.ContainerConfig{Image: "img1", Labels: map[string]string{"darkpanel.module": "npm"}, ExposedPorts: map[string]struct{}{"80/tcp": {}}},
			HostConfig: &dockerapi.HostConfig{
				NetworkMode:  "darkpanel-npm",
				PortBindings: map[string][]dockerapi.PortBinding{"81/tcp": {{HostIP: "127.0.0.1", HostPort: "81"}}},
				Mounts:       []dockerapi.Mount{{Type: "volume", Source: "darkpanel-npm-data", Target: "/data"}},
			},
		},
		Name: "darkpanel-npm",
		Networks: map[string]*dockerapi.EndpointSettings{
			"app": {IPAMConfig: &dockerapi.EndpointIPAMConfig{IPv4Address: netip.MustParseAddr("172.31.255.2")}, Aliases: []string{"npm"}},
		},
	}
	want := upgradeRow{ChangeID: "c1", FromImage: "img1", ToImage: "img2", Config: cfg, Phase: "recorded", UpdatedAt: thStart}
	if _, ok, err := loadUpgrade(ctx, h.db.Read(), "c1"); err != nil || ok {
		t.Fatalf("empty store: ok %v, error %v", ok, err)
	}
	if err := insertUpgrade(ctx, h.db, want); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := insertUpgrade(ctx, h.db, want); err == nil {
		t.Error("a second upgrade of one change was accepted")
	}
	got, ok, err := loadUpgrade(ctx, h.db.Read(), "c1")
	if err != nil || !ok {
		t.Fatalf("load: ok %v, error %v", ok, err)
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(got.Config, cfg) || got.FromImage != "img1" || got.ToImage != "img2" || got.Phase != "recorded" || got.Result != "" ||
		len(got.Archives) != 0 || !got.UpdatedAt.Equal(thStart) {
		t.Errorf("loaded %+v, want %+v", got, want)
	}

	archives := []archiveRef{{Volume: "darkpanel-npm-data", File: "c1-data.tar", SHA256: strings.Repeat("a", 64)}}
	later := thStart.Add(time.Minute)
	if err := setUpgradePhase(ctx, h.db, "c1", "archived", "", archives, later); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	got, _, _ = loadUpgrade(ctx, h.db.Read(), "c1")
	if got.Phase != "archived" || !reflect.DeepEqual(got.Archives, archives) || !got.UpdatedAt.Equal(later) {
		t.Errorf("after archiving %+v, want phase archived with the archives", got)
	}
	if err := setUpgradePhase(ctx, h.db, "c1", "failed", "the API never came up", nil, later); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	got, _, _ = loadUpgrade(ctx, h.db.Read(), "c1")
	if got.Phase != "failed" || got.Result != "the API never came up" || !reflect.DeepEqual(got.Archives, archives) {
		t.Errorf("after failing %+v, want the archives kept by a nil argument", got)
	}
	if err := setUpgradePhase(ctx, h.db, "c1", "rolled_back", "", []archiveRef{}, later); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	got, _, _ = loadUpgrade(ctx, h.db.Read(), "c1")
	if len(got.Archives) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("archives %+v after an empty replacement, want none", got.Archives)
	}
	if err := setUpgradePhase(ctx, h.db, "nobody", "done", "", nil, later); err == nil {
		t.Error("setUpgradePhase of an unknown change succeeded")
	}
	if err := setUpgradePhase(ctx, h.db, "c1", "sideways", "", nil, later); err == nil {
		t.Error("setUpgradePhase accepted an unknown phase")
	}
}

func TestSdWatch(t *testing.T) {
	h := newTHHost(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	add := func(kind, identity string, seq int) {
		t.Helper()
		if err := h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error { return addWatch(ctx, tx, kind, identity, "c1", seq) }); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	add("access_list", "darkpanel-c1-3", 3)
	add("access_list", "darkpanel-c1-3", 3)
	add("certificate", "a.example.org", 4)
	got, err := watched(ctx, h.db.Read(), "access_list")
	if err != nil || !reflect.DeepEqual(got, map[string]bool{"darkpanel-c1-3": true}) {
		t.Errorf("watched(access_list) = %v, %v", got, err)
	}
	if got, err := watched(ctx, h.db.Read(), "proxy_host"); err != nil || got == nil || len(got) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("watched(proxy_host) = %#v, %v; want an empty, non-nil map", got, err)
	}
}
```

Create `internal/modules/npm/effects_test.go`:

```go
package npm

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"reflect"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/npm/npmapi"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/platform/dockerapi"
)

func sdDoc(t *testing.T, raw json.RawMessage) map[string]any {
	t.Helper()
	var m map[string]any
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &m); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("%s: %v", raw, err)
	}
	return m
}

func sdCode(t *testing.T, err error) errcode.Code {
	t.Helper()
	var coded *errcode.Error
	if !errors.As(err, &coded) {
		t.Fatalf("error %v carries no code", err)
	}
	return coded.Code
}

func TestSdResourceAndIdentity(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct{ noun, identity, resource string }{
		{nounNetwork, "?darkpanel-npm", "npm.network:?darkpanel-npm"},
		{nounNetwork, "abc", "npm.network:abc"},
		{nounProxyHost, "?a.example.org,b.example.org", "npm.proxyhost:?a.example.org,b.example.org"},
		{nounClaim, "net|expose:a.example.org", "npm.claim:net|expose:a.example.org"},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		if got := resourceOf(tc.noun, tc.identity); got != tc.resource {
			t.Errorf("resourceOf(%q, %q) = %q, want %q", tc.noun, tc.identity, got, tc.resource)
		}
		if got := identityOf(tc.resource); got != tc.identity {
			t.Errorf("identityOf(%q) = %q, want %q", tc.resource, got, tc.identity)
		}
	}
	nouns := map[string]string{
		nounNetwork: "npm.network", nounVolume: "npm.volume", nounContainer: "npm.container", nounEndpoint: "npm.endpoint",
		nounProxyHost: "npm.proxyhost", nounAccessList: "npm.accesslist", nounCertificate: "npm.certificate",
		nounInstance: "npm.instance", nounClaim: "npm.claim",
	}
	for got, want := range nouns {
		if got != want {
			t.Errorf("noun %q, want %q", got, want)
		}
	}
}

func TestSdNpmEffect(t *testing.T) {
	create := npmEffect(core.EffectCreate, "npm.proxyhost:?a.example.org,b.example.org", json.RawMessage(`{"ignored":true}`), certCommitWait)
	want := core.Effect{
		Kind: core.EffectCreate, Resource: "npm.proxyhost:?a.example.org,b.example.org", CommitWait: certCommitWait,
		Recovery: &core.RecoveryID{Kind: "name", Value: "a.example.org,b.example.org"},
		Before:   core.StateDoc{State: json.RawMessage("null")},
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(create, want) {
		t.Errorf("creation %+v, want %+v", create, want)
	}
	modify := npmEffect(core.EffectModify, "npm.container:abc", json.RawMessage(`{"running":false}`), commitWait)
	want = core.Effect{
		Kind: core.EffectModify, Resource: "npm.container:abc", CommitWait: commitWait,
		Before: core.StateDoc{State: json.RawMessage(`{"running":false}`)},
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(modify, want) {
		t.Errorf("modification %+v, want %+v", modify, want)
	}
	if create.Local || modify.Local || create.After != nil || modify.After != nil {
		t.Error("an npm effect is not local and has no After")
	}
}

func TestSdRun(t *testing.T) {
	dockerErr := func(code int) error { return &dockerapi.APIError{StatusCode: code, Message: "no"} }
	npmErr := func(code int) error { return &npmapi.APIError{Status: code, Message: "no"} }
	creation := npmEffect(core.EffectCreate, resourceOf(nounNetwork, "?darkpanel-npm"), nil, commitWait)
	modify := npmEffect(core.EffectModify, resourceOf(nounContainer, "abc"), json.RawMessage(`{"running":false}`), commitWait)
	boom := errors.New("connection reset")
	wrapped := npmErr(422)
	tests := []struct {
		name     string
		effect   core.Effect
		resource string // what do names
		cause    error
		definite bool
		state    core.EffectState
		made     string
	}{
		{"a creation that names its object", creation, resourceOf(nounNetwork, "abc"), nil, false, core.EffectMade, "npm.network:abc"},
		{"a modification that names nothing", modify, "", nil, false, core.EffectMade, "npm.container:abc"},
		{"a creation that still names no object", creation, resourceOf(nounNetwork, "?x"), nil, false, core.EffectIntended, ""},
		{"a 409 from the Engine", creation, "", dockerErr(409), true, core.EffectSkipped, ""},
		{"a 404 from the Engine", modify, "", dockerErr(404), true, core.EffectSkipped, ""},
		{"a 500 from the Engine", creation, "", dockerErr(500), false, core.EffectIntended, ""},
		{"a 400 from NPM", creation, "", npmErr(400), true, core.EffectSkipped, ""},
		{"a wrapped 422 from NPM", creation, "", fmt.Errorf("create: %w", wrapped), true, core.EffectSkipped, ""},
		{"a 502 from NPM", creation, "", npmErr(502), false, core.EffectIntended, ""},
		{"a transport error", creation, "", boom, false, core.EffectIntended, ""},
		{"a refusal the callee marked Definite", modify, "", core.Definite(errors.New("npm: no instance")), true, core.EffectSkipped, ""},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h := newTHHost(t)
			var observed []string
			observe := func(_ context.Context, _ core.Env, e core.Effect) (core.Observation, error) {
				observed = append(observed, e.Resource+" "+string(e.State))
				return named(json.RawMessage(`{"seen":"` + e.Resource + `"}`)), nil
			}
			do := func(context.Context) (string, error) { return tc.resource, tc.cause }
			err := h.nm().run(context.Background(), h.env("c1"), tc.effect, observe, do)
			effects := h.journal.effects()
			if len(effects) != 1 {
				t.Fatalf("%d effects journaled, want 1", len(effects))
			}
			got := effects[0]
			if got.State != tc.state || got.Resource != sdFirstOf(tc.made, tc.effect.Resource) {
				t.Errorf("effect %s %s, want %s %s", got.Resource, got.State, sdFirstOf(tc.made, tc.effect.Resource), tc.state)
			}
			switch tc.state {
			case core.EffectMade:
				if err != nil {
					t.Fatal(err)
				}
				if got.Post == nil || string(got.Post.State) != `{"seen":"`+tc.made+`"}` {
					t.Errorf("post-state %+v, want what observe read from %s", got.Post, tc.made)
				}
				if !reflect.DeepEqual(observed, []string{tc.made + " intended"}) {
					t.Errorf("observe was called with %v", observed)
				}
			default:
				if err == nil {
					t.Fatal("run succeeded")
				}
				if core.IsDefinite(err) != tc.definite {
					t.Errorf("Definite = %v, want %v (error %v)", core.IsDefinite(err), tc.definite, err)
				}
				if tc.cause != nil && !errors.Is(err, tc.cause) {
					t.Errorf("error %v lost its cause %v", err, tc.cause)
				}
			}
		})
	}
}

func sdFirstOf(a, b string) string {
	if a != "" {
		return a
	}
	return b
}

func TestSdObserveEngine(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	labels := map[string]string{core.LabelModule: ID}
	observe := func(t *testing.T, h *thHost, resource string, state core.EffectState) core.Observation {
		t.Helper()
		obs, err := h.nm().observeEngine(ctx, h.env("c1"), core.Effect{Resource: resource, State: state})
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("observe %s: %v", resource, err)
		}
		return obs
	}
	absent := func(t *testing.T, obs core.Observation, matches int) {
		t.Helper()
		if obs.Matches != matches || string(obs.Current.State) != "null" {
			t.Errorf("observation %+v, want %d matches and the null state", obs, matches)
		}
	}
	netID, ctrID := thID("network:darkpanel-npm"), thID("container:darkpanel-npm")

	t.Run("a network created by name", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newTHHost(t)
		absent(t, observe(t, h, "npm.network:?darkpanel-npm", core.EffectIntended), 0)
		h.docker.putNetwork(thNetwork("darkpanel-npm-extra"))
		absent(t, observe(t, h, "npm.network:?darkpanel-npm", core.EffectIntended), 0)
		h.docker.putNetwork(thNetwork("darkpanel-npm", thNetLabels(labels)))
		obs := observe(t, h, "npm.network:?darkpanel-npm", core.EffectIntended)
		doc := sdDoc(t, obs.Current.State)
		if obs.Matches != 1 || obs.Resource != "npm.network:"+netID || !obs.Exclusive || doc["id"] != netID || doc["unused"] != true {
			t.Errorf("observation %+v (state %v), want one exclusive match, unused", obs, doc)
		}
	})
	t.Run("a network by id", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newTHHost(t)
		absent(t, observe(t, h, "npm.network:"+netID, core.EffectMade), 1)
		h.docker.putNetwork(thNetwork("darkpanel-npm"))
		if doc := sdDoc(t, observe(t, h, "npm.network:"+netID, core.EffectMade).Current.State); doc["unused"] != true {
			t.Errorf("state %v, want unused", doc)
		}
		h.docker.putContainer(thContainer("web", thEndpoint("darkpanel-npm", "172.20.0.3")))
		if doc := sdDoc(t, observe(t, h, "npm.network:"+netID, core.EffectMade).Current.State); doc["unused"] != false {
			t.Errorf("state %v, want a network in use", doc)
		}
	})
	t.Run("a volume", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newTHHost(t)
		absent(t, observe(t, h, "npm.volume:?darkpanel-npm-data", core.EffectIntended), 0)
		absent(t, observe(t, h, "npm.volume:darkpanel-npm-data", core.EffectMade), 1)
		h.docker.putVolume(dockerapi.Volume{Name: dataVolume, Labels: labels})
		obs := observe(t, h, "npm.volume:?darkpanel-npm-data", core.EffectIntended)
		if obs.Matches != 1 || obs.Resource != "npm.volume:darkpanel-npm-data" || !obs.Exclusive || sdDoc(t, obs.Current.State)["unused"] != true {
			t.Errorf("observation %+v, want one exclusive, unused match", obs)
		}
		h.docker.putContainer(thContainer("darkpanel-npm", thMounted(dataVolume, "/data")))
		if doc := sdDoc(t, observe(t, h, "npm.volume:darkpanel-npm-data", core.EffectMade).Current.State); doc["unused"] != false {
			t.Errorf("state %v, want a volume in use", doc)
		}
	})
	t.Run("a container", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newTHHost(t)
		absent(t, observe(t, h, "npm.container:?darkpanel-npm", core.EffectIntended), 0)
		absent(t, observe(t, h, "npm.container:"+ctrID, core.EffectMade), 1)
		h.docker.putContainer(thContainer("darkpanel-npm", thRunning))
		obs := observe(t, h, "npm.container:?darkpanel-npm", core.EffectIntended)
		doc := sdDoc(t, obs.Current.State)
		if obs.Matches != 1 || obs.Resource != "npm.container:"+ctrID || !obs.Exclusive || doc["id"] != ctrID || doc["running"] != true {
			t.Errorf("observation %+v (state %v), want one exclusive, running match", obs, doc)
		}
		byID := observe(t, h, "npm.container:"+ctrID, core.EffectMade)
		if _, ok := sdDoc(t, byID.Current.State)["startedAt"]; !ok || byID.Matches != 1 {
			t.Errorf("recorded state %s, want a startedAt", byID.Current.State)
		}
		for _, st := range []core.EffectState{core.EffectUndoing, core.EffectUndone} {
			if _, ok := sdDoc(t, observe(t, h, "npm.container:"+ctrID, st).Current.State)["startedAt"]; ok {
				t.Errorf("state of an %s effect holds a startedAt, want the restorable form", st)
			}
		}
		made, err := h.nm().observeEngine(ctx, h.env("c1"), core.Effect{Kind: core.EffectCreate, Resource: "npm.container:" + ctrID, State: core.EffectMade})
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if _, ok := sdDoc(t, made.Current.State)["startedAt"]; ok {
			t.Errorf("state of a made creation %s holds a startedAt, want the restorable form", made.Current.State)
		}
	})
	t.Run("an endpoint", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newTHHost(t)
		absent(t, observe(t, h, "npm.endpoint:?app", core.EffectIntended), 0)
		h.docker.putContainer(thContainer("darkpanel-npm", thRunning))
		absent(t, observe(t, h, "npm.endpoint:?app", core.EffectIntended), 0)
		absent(t, observe(t, h, "npm.endpoint:app", core.EffectMade), 1)
		h.docker.putContainer(thContainer("darkpanel-npm", thRunning, thEndpoint("app", "172.20.0.2")))
		obs := observe(t, h, "npm.endpoint:?app", core.EffectIntended)
		doc := sdDoc(t, obs.Current.State)
		if obs.Matches != 1 || obs.Resource != "npm.endpoint:app" || !obs.Exclusive || doc["present"] != true || doc["container"] != "darkpanel-npm" || doc["network"] != "app" {
			t.Errorf("observation %+v (state %v), want the container's attachment to app", obs, doc)
		}
		byName := observe(t, h, "npm.endpoint:app", core.EffectMade)
		if byName.Matches != 1 || sdDoc(t, byName.Current.State)["present"] != true {
			t.Errorf("observation by identity %+v", byName)
		}
	})
	t.Run("what is not an Engine object", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newTHHost(t)
		for _, r := range []string{"npm.proxyhost:7", "npm.network:", "npm.network:?", "npm", "docker.network:abc"} {
			if _, err := h.nm().observeEngine(ctx, h.env("c1"), core.Effect{Resource: r}); err == nil {
				t.Errorf("observeEngine(%q) succeeded", r)
			}
		}
	})
	t.Run("an Engine that is not reachable", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newTHHost(t)
		h.docker.Fail("InspectNetwork", dockerapi.ErrUnavailable)
		_, err := h.nm().observeEngine(ctx, h.env("c1"), core.Effect{Resource: "npm.network:" + netID})
		if err == nil || sdCode(t, err) != errcode.DockerEngineUnavailable {
			t.Errorf("error %v, want %s", err, errcode.DockerEngineUnavailable)
		}
	})
}

// TestSdRunSettlesThroughObserve: run records the post-state the Engine reports after the call, so the next observation of the
// object equals it.
func TestSdRunSettlesThroughObserve(t *testing.T) {
	h := newTHHost(t)
	n, ctx := h.nm(), context.Background()
	e := npmEffect(core.EffectCreate, resourceOf(nounVolume, "?"+dataVolume), nil, commitWait)
	err := n.run(ctx, h.env("c1"), e, n.observeEngine, func(ctx context.Context) (string, error) {
		v, err := n.d.docker.CreateVolume(ctx, dockerapi.VolumeCreate{Name: dataVolume, Labels: map[string]string{core.LabelModule: ID}})
		return resourceOf(nounVolume, v.Name), err
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	made := h.journal.effects()[0]
	obs, err := n.observeEngine(ctx, h.env("c1"), made)
	if err != nil || made.State != core.EffectMade || made.Resource != "npm.volume:darkpanel-npm-data" || made.Post == nil ||
		!reflect.DeepEqual(sdDoc(t, made.Post.State), sdDoc(t, obs.Current.State)) {
		t.Errorf("made effect %+v and observation %+v (error %v) disagree", made, obs, err)
	}
	if made.CommitWait != 2*time.Minute || made.Local {
		t.Errorf("effect commit wait %v, local %v; want 2m and not local", made.CommitWait, made.Local)
	}
}
```

```go
package npm

func TestSdConstants(t *testing.T) {

	want := []any{"npm", 2 * time.Minute, 10 * time.Minute, "darkpanel-npm", "darkpanel-npm", "darkpanel-npm-data", "darkpanel-npm-letsencrypt",

func TestSdOwnedPathsAndArchiveNames(t *testing.T) {
	owned := ownedPaths()
	if len(owned) != 1 || owned[0].Path != backupDir || owned[0].Kind != core.OwnedManagedDir || owned[0].Pattern != `^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9._-]{0,63}\.tar$` {
		t.Fatalf("owned paths %+v, want the backup directory as a managed-dir with the .tar pattern", owned)
	}
	pattern := regexp.MustCompile(owned[0].Pattern)
	tests := []struct{ changeID, volume, want string }{
		{"01J9ZABCDEF", dataVolume, "01j9zabcdef-data.tar"},
		{"01J9ZABCDEF", certVolume, "01j9zabcdef-letsencrypt.tar"},
		{"c1", dataVolume, "c1-data.tar"},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		got := archiveName(tc.changeID, tc.volume)
		if got != tc.want || !pattern.MatchString(got) {
			t.Errorf("archiveName(%q, %q) = %q (matches the pattern: %v), want %q", tc.changeID, tc.volume, got, pattern.MatchString(got), tc.want)
		}
	}
	for _, name := range []string{"x.tar.gz", "../x.tar", "X.tar", ".x.tar", "x/y.tar", "x.TAR"} {
		if pattern.MatchString(name) {
			t.Errorf("the pattern admits %q", name)
		}
	}
}

func TestSdInstanceCreate(t *testing.T) {
	c := instanceCreate("img@sha256:abc")
	if c.Name != "darkpanel-npm" || c.Image != "img@sha256:abc" || !reflect.DeepEqual(c.Labels, map[string]string{"darkpanel.module": "npm"}) {
		t.Errorf("name %q, image %q, labels %v", c.Name, c.Image, c.Labels)
	}
	if len(c.Env) != 0 || len(c.Cmd) != 0 || len(c.Entrypoint) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("env %v, cmd %v, entrypoint %v; the container takes none, and no secret goes in its environment", c.Env, c.Cmd, c.Entrypoint)
	}
	hc := c.HostConfig
	if hc == nil || hc.NetworkMode != "darkpanel-npm" || hc.RestartPolicy.Name != "unless-stopped" || hc.Privileged || len(hc.Binds) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("host config %+v", hc)
	}
	wantMounts := []dockerapi.Mount{{Type: "volume", Source: "darkpanel-npm-data", Target: "/data"}, {Type: "volume", Source: "darkpanel-npm-letsencrypt", Target: "/etc/letsencrypt"}}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(hc.Mounts, wantMounts) {
		t.Errorf("mounts %+v, want %+v", hc.Mounts, wantMounts)
	}
	wantPorts := map[string][]dockerapi.PortBinding{
		"80/tcp":  {{HostPort: "80"}},
		"81/tcp":  {{HostIP: "127.0.0.1", HostPort: "81"}},
		"443/tcp": {{HostPort: "443"}},
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(hc.PortBindings, wantPorts) {
		t.Errorf("port bindings %+v, want %+v", hc.PortBindings, wantPorts)
	}
	if c.NetworkingConfig == nil || len(c.NetworkingConfig.EndpointsConfig) != 1 || c.NetworkingConfig.EndpointsConfig["darkpanel-npm"] == nil {
		t.Errorf("networking config %+v, want one endpoint on darkpanel-npm", c.NetworkingConfig)
	}
}

// TestSdProductionNMReachesNothing: in a test binary the production wiring dials neither the host's Docker socket nor
// 127.0.0.1:81.
func TestSdProductionNMReachesNothing(t *testing.T) {
	n := productionNM()
	d := n.d
	if d.docker == nil || d.api == nil || d.anon == nil || d.info == nil || d.raw == nil || d.random == nil || d.readFile == nil ||
		d.readDir == nil || d.panel == nil || d.sockets == nil {
		t.Fatalf("a production dependency is nil: %+v", d)
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	if _, err := d.docker.Info(ctx); err == nil || !errors.Is(err, dockerapi.ErrUnavailable) {
		t.Errorf("Info error %v, want the Engine unavailable", err)
	}
	s, err := d.docker.PullImage(ctx, "jc21/nginx-proxy-manager:2.13.5")
	if err == nil || s != nil {
		t.Errorf("PullImage = %v, %v; want a nil stream and an error", s, err)
	}
	if _, err := d.anon().Health(ctx); err == nil {
		t.Error("the production NPM client reached something")
	}
	if b, err := d.random(32); err != nil || len(b) != 32 || reflect.DeepEqual(b, make([]byte, 32)) {
		t.Errorf("random(32) = %x, %v", b, err)
	}
}

func TestSdAPIVia(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	t.Run("the stored administrator gets a token", func(t *testing.T) {

	t.Run("no instance", func(t *testing.T) {

		_, err := h.nm().d.api(ctx, h.env(""))
		if err == nil || !core.IsDefinite(err) || sdCode(t, err) != errcode.NotFound {

		}

	t.Run("a password NPM refuses", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newTHHost(t)
		inst := h.deployed()
		h.npm.SeedAdmin(inst.Email, "another-password-entirely")
		_, err := h.nm().d.api(ctx, h.env(""))
		if err == nil || sdCode(t, err) != errcode.NPMUnhealthy {
			t.Fatalf("error %v, want %s", err, errcode.NPMUnhealthy)
		}

			t.Errorf("error %q carries a password", err)
		}
	})
	t.Run("a sealed password that cannot be opened", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newTHHost(t)
		h.deployed()
		err := h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
			_, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, `UPDATE npm_instances SET password_ct = x'0102'`)
			return err
		})
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if _, err := h.nm().d.api(ctx, h.env("")); err == nil || sdCode(t, err) != errcode.NPMUnhealthy {
			t.Errorf("error %v, want %s", err, errcode.NPMUnhealthy)
		}
	})
}

const (
	sdFilterDocker = "*filter\n:INPUT ACCEPT [0:0]\n:FORWARD DROP [0:0]\n:OUTPUT ACCEPT [0:0]\n:DOCKER-USER - [0:0]\n:DOCKER-FORWARD - [0:0]\n" +
		"-A FORWARD -j DOCKER-USER\n-A FORWARD -j DOCKER-FORWARD\nCOMMIT\n"
	sdFilterPlain = "*filter\n:INPUT ACCEPT [0:0]\n:FORWARD DROP [0:0]\n:OUTPUT ACCEPT [0:0]\nCOMMIT\n"
	sdRawEmpty    = "*raw\n:PREROUTING ACCEPT [0:0]\n:OUTPUT ACCEPT [0:0]\nCOMMIT\n"
	sdRawDrop     = "*raw\n:PREROUTING ACCEPT [0:0]\n:OUTPUT ACCEPT [0:0]\n-A PREROUTING -d 127.0.0.1/32 ! -i lo -p tcp -m tcp --dport 81 -j DROP\nCOMMIT\n"
)

// sdReader answers variant reads from save text: variant -> table -> text. A variant it holds nothing of reads as not loaded, and a table of a variant it holds that has no text is left out, as ReadVariant leaves out a legacy table that does not exist.
type sdReader struct {
	tables map[string]map[string]string
	err    error
	asked  []string
}

func (r *sdReader) read(_ context.Context, _ core.Env, f fwstate.Family, variant string, tables []string) (*netfilter.Ruleset, error) {
	r.asked = append(r.asked, variant+":"+strings.Join(tables, ","))
	if f != fwstate.V4 {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("asked for family %s", f)
	}
	if r.err != nil {
		return nil, r.err
	}
	held, ok := r.tables[variant]
	if !ok {
		return nil, nil
	}
	var text strings.Builder
	for _, name := range tables {
		text.WriteString(held[name])
	}
	if text.Len() == 0 {
		return &netfilter.Ruleset{}, nil
	}
	return netfilter.Parse(strings.NewReader(text.String()))
}

func TestSdRawVia(t *testing.T) {
	publishing := []func(*dockerapi.ContainerJSON){thRunning, thPublish("127.0.0.1:81:81/tcp")}
	boom := errors.New("nft is gone")
	tests := []struct {
		name       string
		tables     map[string]map[string]string
		containers [][]func(*dockerapi.ContainerJSON)
		readErr    error
		want       bool
		wantErr    bool
		asked      []string
	}{
		{"Docker's chains are in neither variant", map[string]map[string]string{"nft": {"filter": sdFilterPlain}, "legacy": {"filter": sdFilterPlain}}, nil, nil, false, false,
			[]string{"nft:filter", "legacy:filter"}},
		{"neither variant is loaded", nil, nil, nil, false, false, []string{"nft:filter", "legacy:filter"}},
		{"Docker's chains are in both variants", map[string]map[string]string{"nft": {"filter": sdFilterDocker}, "legacy": {"filter": sdFilterDocker}}, nil, nil, false, false,
			[]string{"nft:filter", "legacy:filter"}},
		{"an idle Docker has nothing to protect", map[string]map[string]string{"nft": {"filter": sdFilterDocker, "raw": sdRawEmpty}}, nil, nil, true, false,
			[]string{"nft:filter", "legacy:filter", "nft:raw"}},
		{"the raw table is missing", map[string]map[string]string{"nft": {"filter": sdFilterDocker}}, nil, nil, false, false,
			[]string{"nft:filter", "legacy:filter", "nft:raw"}},
		{"a loopback port without its drop rule", map[string]map[string]string{"nft": {"filter": sdFilterDocker, "raw": sdRawEmpty}}, [][]func(*dockerapi.ContainerJSON){publishing}, nil, false, false,
			[]string{"nft:filter", "legacy:filter", "nft:raw"}},
		{"a loopback port with its drop rule", map[string]map[string]string{"nft": {"filter": sdFilterDocker, "raw": sdRawDrop}}, [][]func(*dockerapi.ContainerJSON){publishing}, nil, true, false,
			[]string{"nft:filter", "legacy:filter", "nft:raw"}},
		{"the legacy variant holds the chains", map[string]map[string]string{"legacy": {"filter": sdFilterDocker, "raw": sdRawDrop}}, [][]func(*dockerapi.ContainerJSON){publishing}, nil, true, false,
			[]string{"nft:filter", "legacy:filter", "legacy:raw"}},
		{"the read fails", map[string]map[string]string{"nft": {"filter": sdFilterDocker}}, nil, boom, false, true, []string{"nft:filter"}},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h := newTHHost(t)
			for i, opts := range tc.containers {
				h.docker.putContainer(thContainer(fmt.Sprintf("darkpanel-npm%d", i), opts...))
			}
			r := &sdReader{tables: tc.tables, err: tc.readErr}
			got, err := rawVia(r.read, h.docker)(context.Background(), h.env(""))
			if (err != nil) != tc.wantErr || got != tc.want {
				t.Errorf("rawVia = %v, %v; want %v (error %v)", got, err, tc.want, tc.wantErr)
			}
			if tc.wantErr && !errors.Is(err, boom) {
				t.Errorf("error %v lost its cause", err)
			}
			if !reflect.DeepEqual(r.asked, tc.asked) {
				t.Errorf("reads %v, want %v", r.asked, tc.asked)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestSdRawViaIgnoresInternalNetworks(t *testing.T) {
	// An internal network's bridge gets no raw drop rule from Docker, so a container on one needs none.
	h := newTHHost(t)
	h.docker.putNetwork(thNetwork("private", thInternal, thSubnet("172.30.0.0/24", "172.30.0.1")))
	h.docker.putContainer(thContainer("worker", thRunning, thEndpoint("private", "172.30.0.2")))
	r := &sdReader{tables: map[string]map[string]string{"nft": {"filter": sdFilterDocker, "raw": sdRawEmpty}}}
	if got, err := rawVia(r.read, h.docker)(context.Background(), h.env("")); err != nil || !got {
		t.Errorf("rawVia = %v, %v; want protected, since the only endpoint is on an internal network", got, err)
	}
	h.docker.putNetwork(thNetwork("public", thSubnet("172.31.0.0/24", "172.31.0.1")))
	h.docker.putContainer(thContainer("worker", thRunning, thEndpoint("public", "172.31.0.2")))
	if got, err := rawVia(r.read, h.docker)(context.Background(), h.env("")); err != nil || got {
		t.Errorf("rawVia = %v, %v; want unprotected: a bridge endpoint without a drop rule", got, err)
	}
}

func TestSdReservedVia(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	t.Run("the panel network and the NPM container", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newTHHost(t)
		h.docker.putNetwork(thNetwork("darkpanel-panel", thSubnet("172.31.255.0/29", "172.31.255.1")))
		h.docker.putContainer(thContainer("darkpanel-npm", thRunning, thEndpoint("darkpanel-panel", "172.31.255.2"), thEndpoint("darkpanel-npm", "172.20.0.2")))
		got, err := reservedVia(h.docker)(ctx, h.env(""))
		want := proxy.Reserved{
			NPMAddrs:     []netip.Addr{netip.MustParseAddr("172.20.0.2"), netip.MustParseAddr("172.31.255.2")},
			PanelNetwork: netip.MustParsePrefix("172.31.255.0/29"),
			PanelGateway: netip.MustParseAddr("172.31.255.1"),
		}
		if err != nil || !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
			t.Errorf("reserved = %+v, %v; want %+v", got, err, want)
		}
	})
	t.Run("neither exists", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newTHHost(t)
		got, err := reservedVia(h.docker)(ctx, h.env(""))
		if err != nil || !reflect.DeepEqual(got, proxy.Reserved{}) {
			t.Errorf("reserved = %+v, %v; want the zero value", got, err)
		}
	})
	t.Run("the Engine is not reachable", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newTHHost(t)
		h.docker.Fail("InspectContainer", dockerapi.ErrUnavailable)
		if _, err := reservedVia(h.docker)(ctx, h.env("")); err == nil || sdCode(t, err) != errcode.DockerEngineUnavailable {
			t.Errorf("error %v, want %s", err, errcode.DockerEngineUnavailable)
		}
	})
}
```

```go
package npm

import (
	"io/fs"
	"reflect"
	"testing"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
)

func TestSdManifest(t *testing.T) {
	m := Module().Manifest()
	if m.ID != ID || m.Version != 1 || !m.DefaultEnabled {
		t.Errorf("manifest %q version %d default enabled %t, want npm 1 true", m.ID, m.Version, m.DefaultEnabled)
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(m.OwnedPaths, ownedPaths()) || len(m.OwnedPaths) != 1 || m.OwnedPaths[0].Path != backupDir {
		t.Errorf("owned paths %+v, want the backup directory", m.OwnedPaths)
	}
	if len(m.Migrations) != 1 || m.Migrations[core.SideAgent] == nil {
		t.Fatalf("migrations %v, want the agent side only", m.Migrations)
	}
	if _, err := fs.Stat(m.Migrations[core.SideAgent], "0001_npm.sql"); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("the agent migrations lack 0001_npm.sql: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := core.NewRegistry(Module()); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("the module is not accepted by the registry: %v", err)
	}
}
```

1. `migrations/agent/0001_npm.sql`: the five tables of Contracts "NPM store", all `STRICT`, with exactly the columns, defaults and `CHECK` constraints written there and nothing else (no index, no foreign key):

```sql
-- The Nginx Proxy Manager module's agent tables (spec 6), read and written
-- only through internal/modules/npm. Times are fixed-width RFC 3339 UTC text,
-- so they compare as text.

-- The one instance on this host. The admin password is sealed with the agent's
-- sealer (associated data npm_instances.password_ct.1).
CREATE TABLE npm_instances (
    id          INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 1),
    container_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    image       TEXT NOT NULL,
    admin_email TEXT NOT NULL,
    password_ct BLOB NOT NULL,
    public      TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'no' CHECK (public IN ('no', 'confirmed')),
    created_at  TEXT NOT NULL,
    updated_at  TEXT NOT NULL
) STRICT;

-- The objects an exposure of the owner's admin UI or of the panel owns.
-- exposure is the exposure's host name.
CREATE TABLE npm_reserved (
    kind      TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('proxy_host', 'access_list', 'certificate')),
    object_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    owner     TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (owner IN ('npm.admin.expose', 'panel.exposure.add')),
    exposure  TEXT NOT NULL,
    change_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    PRIMARY KEY (kind, object_id)
) STRICT;

-- Who needs the NPM container attached to a network.
CREATE TABLE npm_attachments (
    network     TEXT NOT NULL,
    claimant    TEXT NOT NULL,
    change_id   TEXT NOT NULL,
    preexisting INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (preexisting IN (0, 1)),
    PRIMARY KEY (network, claimant)
) STRICT;

-- One row per npm.instance.upgrade change: its agent-local transaction.
-- config is the original container's create configuration as JSON, and
-- archives is a JSON array of {volume, file, sha256}.
CREATE TABLE npm_upgrades (
    change_id  TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    from_image TEXT NOT NULL,
    to_image   TEXT NOT NULL,
    config     TEXT NOT NULL,
    archives   TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',
    phase      TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (phase IN ('recorded', 'stopped', 'archiving', 'archived', 'recreating', 'waiting', 'done', 'rolled_back', 'failed')),
    result     TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
) STRICT;

-- Identities whose effect was resolved skipped: an object that appears later
-- under one of them is shown as unclaimed.
CREATE TABLE npm_watch (
    kind      TEXT NOT NULL,
    identity  TEXT NOT NULL,
    change_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    seq       INTEGER NOT NULL,
    PRIMARY KEY (kind, identity)
) STRICT;
```

2. `store.go`:
   - `agentMigrations() fs.FS` returns the `migrations/agent/*.sql` files embedded in the variable `agentMigrationsFS`, rooted so that a file's name is `0001_npm.sql`.
   - `storeTimeFormat = "2006-01-02T15:04:05.000000000Z"`; `storeTime(t)` formats `t` in UTC with it; `parseStoreTime(s)` parses it and refuses any other layout. Times are fixed-width, so they compare as text.
   - `queryer` is the interface of `QueryRowContext` and `QueryContext`, satisfied by `*sql.DB` and `*sql.Tx`.
   - `instanceRow`, `loadInstance(ctx, q)` (the row with `id = 1`; `ok` false when none), `saveInstance(ctx, tx, r)` (an upsert of `id = 1`, both times written with `storeTime`), `deleteInstance(ctx, tx)` (no row is not an error). `instanceColumns` is the column list loads share.
   - `reservedRow`, `reservedOf(ctx, q, kind, id)`, `addReserved` (a second row for the same `(kind, object_id)` is an error), `dropReservedExposure(ctx, tx, exposure)` (deletes every row of the exposure; none is not an error).
   - `claimRow`, `claims(ctx, q, network)` (network `""` returns every claim ordered by network and then claimant; a name returns that network's claims ordered by claimant; never nil), `addClaim`, `dropClaim` (no row is not an error).
   - `containerConfig{Create dockerapi.ContainerCreate; Name string; Networks map[string]*dockerapi.EndpointSettings}` marshals with Go's default field names; `archiveRef{Volume, File, SHA256}` with the JSON names `volume`, `file`, `sha256`; `upgradeRow`; `insertUpgrade(ctx, db, u)` (writes through `db.Write`; the phase `recorded`, `archives` `[]`), `encodeArchives` (nil encodes `[]`), `loadUpgrade(ctx, q, changeID)`, `upgradeColumns`, `setUpgradePhase(ctx, db, changeID, phase, result, archives, at)` (a nil `archives` keeps the stored list, a non-nil one replaces it; an unknown change id is an error), `addWatch` (`ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`), `watched(ctx, q, kind)` (a non-nil map of the identities).
   - Errors start with `npm:` and wrap their cause.
3. `wire.go`: every type of Contracts "NPM wire shapes", with `json` names in lowerCamelCase exactly as `wire_test.go` pins them. Beyond the contract's list: `InstanceListInput struct{}`. Tag rules the tests pin: `InstanceStatus.AdminExposure` is `json:"adminExposure" jsonschema:"nullable"` (always emitted, `null` when none); `InstanceStatus.Public` and `PublicMark.Public` carry `enum=no,enum=confirmed`; `ProxyHostInput.ForwardScheme` `enum=http,enum=https`; ports `minimum=1,maximum=65535`; `AccessClientInput.Directive` `enum=allow,enum=deny`; `PublicResult.Via` `enum=mark,enum=ports`; `DeployInput.AcmeEmail` and `DeployPublicInput.AcmeEmail` `minLength=3,maxLength=254`; `AdminPassword.Password` carries `secret:"true"`. Optional input fields use `omitempty` (`ProxyHostInput`'s `certificateId`, `accessListId`, `sslForced`, `hsts`, `websockets`; `AccessListInput.satisfyAny`; `ExposeInput`'s `websockets` and `accessListId`; `AdminExposeInput.accessListId`; `AttachInput.target`; `ProxyHostRemoveInput`'s `id` and `domain`) and `AttachInput.Address` is a `netip.Addr` with `omitzero`; the view and output types have no `omitempty`. `DetachInput.Network` is required and may be `""`. `ProxyHostUpdateInput` embeds `ProxyHostInput` so that its JSON is flat. The list outputs hold `Items`; nothing in this file builds them, so the never-null rule is for the readers (Task 19).
4. `deps.go`: the constants, `nm`, `deps` (with `sockets`), `dockerAPI`, `pullStream`, `npmAPI` and `productionNM` of Contracts "NPM deps" and "NPM shared internals", and:
   - `dockerClient` embeds `*dockerapi.Client` and overrides `PullImage` to return the `pullStream`, returning a nil interface (not a typed nil pointer) on error.
   - `productionNM` dials `dockerapi.DefaultSocket` and `apiURL`, or `absentSocket` (`/nonexistent/darkpanel/docker.sock`) and `absentURL` (`http://127.0.0.1:1`) when `testing.Testing()`; its HTTP client has a 15-minute timeout and does not follow redirects; `random` is `crypto/rand`; `readFile`, `readDir` are `os.ReadFile`, `os.ReadDir`; `sockets` is `netstat.Sockets`; `raw` is `rawVia(loaded.Reader{}.ReadVariant, docker)` and `panel` is `reservedVia(docker)`.
   - `apiVia(newClient)` builds `deps.api`: no instance row gives a `core.Definite` `not_found`; a password that does not open, a refused token (its detail names only the HTTP status) or an unreachable NPM gives `npm.unhealthy`; the password never appears in an error. It returns the client `WithToken`.
   - `rawVia(read, docker)` builds `deps.raw`: it reads the IPv4 `filter` table of the `nft` and the `legacy` variant through `read`, treats a variant as Docker's when `dockerfw.DockerChains` finds Docker's chains in it, and returns false unless exactly one variant holds them. It then builds `dockerfw.Facts` from the Engine (`dockerFacts`: the networks, with the bridge name from the `com.docker.network.bridge.name` option or `dockerfw.Bridge`, the gateway modes from the two `gateway_mode` options and the masked subnets; the running containers' published ports and endpoints, sorted; `containerFact`), drops the endpoints of internal networks (`withoutInternal`: Docker loads no raw rule for them), reads the `raw` table of that variant and returns `dockerfw.RawProtected`. A missing raw table is false. The narrowing of a bounded `ParseUint` to 16 bits carries `//nolint:gosec // G115: ParseUint bounded both to 16 bits.`.
   - `engineCoded(err)` maps `dockerapi.ErrUnavailable` to `docker.engine_unavailable` (wrapping the cause) and returns any other error unchanged.
   - `reservedVia(docker)` builds `deps.panel`: the `darkpanel-npm` container's endpoint addresses (sorted, unmapped; none when the container is absent) and the first IPv4 subnet and gateway of the `darkpanel-panel` network (none when it is absent).
   - `ownedPaths()` is one `core.OwnedPath` for `backupDir`, kind `core.OwnedManagedDir`, pattern `^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9._-]{0,63}\.tar$`; `archiveName(changeID, volume)` is the lowercased change id, `-`, the volume name without the `darkpanel-npm-` prefix, `.tar`.
   - `instanceCreate(image)` is the container of spec 6 and `TestSdInstanceCreate`: name `darkpanel-npm`, the image, the label `core.LabelModule` = `npm`, exposed ports 80, 81 and 443 (tcp), network mode `darkpanel-npm`, port 80 and 443 bound with an empty host IP and 81 to `127.0.0.1`, restart policy `unless-stopped`, two volume mounts (`darkpanel-npm-data` at `/data`, `darkpanel-npm-letsencrypt` at `/etc/letsencrypt`), one endpoint on `darkpanel-npm`, and no environment, command or entrypoint.
   - `Module()` returns `module{n: productionNM()}`.
5. `effects.go`: the nouns of Contracts "NPM shared internals", `resourceOf`, `identityOf` (the part after the first `:`), `npmEffect` (a creation: `Before` the null state and the `name` recovery cut from the `?` identity; every effect: `Local` false, no `After`), `refused(err)` (a `*dockerapi.APIError` with a 4xx status or `npmapi.IsClientError`), `(nm) run` as the decision above and `TestSdRun` pin it (it calls `observe` with a copy of the effect in state `intended` whose resource is the one `do` returned; `do` returning `""` keeps `e.Resource`; a resource that still starts with `?` is an error and leaves the effect intended), `nullState`, `named` (`Matches` 1, `Exclusive` true), `phaseOf` and `(nm) observeEngine`:
   - a network is read with `InspectNetwork` (only the inspect answer lists the attached containers) and rendered by `dstate.NetworkCreated`; a creation (`?name`) lists networks filtered by name, keeps the exact name matches sorted by id and returns `Matches` 0 with the null state, one exclusive match with `Resource` set to `npm.network:<id>`, or the candidates when several;
   - a volume is rendered by `dstate.VolumeCreated` with `unused` true when no container (running or not) mounts it; a container by `dstate.ContainerRun` in `phaseOf(e)` form, a creation found by name `darkpanel-npm` in its `npm.container:<id>` resource; an endpoint by `dstate.Endpoint` for the `darkpanel-npm` container on the network named by the identity, absent (container or attachment) being the null state;
   - an object looked up by identity that does not exist is `named(null)`; a creation that does not exist is `Matches` 0 with the null state; an Engine error goes through `engineCoded`; a resource of another noun, or without a name, is an error.
6. `npm.go`: `type module struct{ n nm }` and the manifest of the decision above; delete `Module()` from this file.

### Task 12: npm: instance.deploy with bootstrap

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/modules/npm/instance_deploy.go`
- Test: `internal/modules/npm/instance_deploy_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Contracts "NPM wire shapes" (`DeployInput`, `Deployed`); "NPM deps" (`nm` and the `deps` fields `docker`, `api`, `anon`, `info`, `raw`, `random`, `sockets`, `readFile`); "NPM shared internals" (`lockKey`, `commitWait`, `networkName`, `containerName`, `dataVolume`, `certVolume`, the nouns, `resourceOf`, `npmEffect`, `nm.run`, `nm.observeEngine`, `nullState`, `named`, `found`, `engineCoded`, `refused`, `instanceCreate`); "NPM store" (`instanceRow`, `loadInstance`, `saveInstance`, `deleteInstance`); "NPM identities" (the Engine objects and their `name` recovery); "NPM test host" (`thHost`, `newTHHost`, `h.nm()`, `h.env`, `h.deployed`, `h.docker`, `h.npm`, `h.journal`, `h.clock`, `thID`, `thRandomBytes`, `thAPIError`, `thContainer`, `thNetwork`, `thMounted`, `thStart`); `dstate.ContainerRunning`; `netstat.Socket`; the codes `module.unavailable`, `docker.engine_unavailable`, `docker.in_use`, `derp.port_in_use`, `request.invalid_input`, `npm.setup_failed`, `npm.unhealthy`.
- Produces: `func (n nm) instanceDeploy() core.ActionDef` (Contracts "NPM actions"; Task 27 lists it in `actions()`). Names this task declares in package `npm`, used by no other task: `deployHandler`, `func (n nm) deployAvailable`, `checkAcmeEmail`, `deployDERPUnit`, `deployPoll`, `deployReady`, `deployStop`, `deployMinMajor`, `deployReservedDomains`, `deploySleep`. The bookkeeping effect is a Local creation `npm.instance:?darkpanel-npm` (made as `npm.instance:darkpanel-npm`) whose state is `{"containerId", "image", "adminEmail"}` and never holds the password.

**Items:** 29

**Depends on:** Task 11
**Implementer:** dforge:impl-opus-high
**Evaluation:** files 1 - spec 2 - coupling 1 - risk 3 = 7
**Lenses:** security

Decisions this task embodies (spec 6 "Preconditions", "Deploy (R65)", "Bootstrap"; Contracts "NPM identities"; Assumptions, the ruling "Docker 28.0+ with raw protection"):
- Everything the deploy changes on the Engine is its own effect, in this order: create the network, create the data volume, create the certificate volume, create the container, start the container (a modification whose `Before` is the container stopped, in the restorable form). Each is a non-Local effect with `CommitWait` 2 minutes, run through `nm.run`, so a lost answer leaves the effect `intended` for the engine to settle as uncertain and a 4xx answer ends `skipped` and `core.Definite`.
- The administrator password (32 random bytes, base64url without padding) is generated before the first Engine call and never leaves memory except sealed. It is journaled as the secret of the bookkeeping effect `npm.instance:?darkpanel-npm` (Local, `InverseLocal`, `CommitWait` 0), which is intended after the container starts and before NPM is asked to create the administrator: a deploy cut off after NPM created the administrator keeps the password the operator needs to undo or repair. The effect is made after the instance row is written. The row holds the password sealed with `env.Sealer.Seal("npm_instances.password_ct.1", password)`.
- A failure while waiting for NPM or creating the administrator is `npm.setup_failed` and is not `core.Definite`: the container runs and the administrator may exist. The Engine effects stay `made` and the engine settles the bookkeeping effect as `skipped` (its observation finds no row), so the change ends `failed`, as spec 6 says; the operator undoes the change. The detail carries NPM's HTTP status when it answered and never the password or a response body. A host where NPM already has an administrator (`GET /api` says `setup: true` before the deploy created one) is also `npm.setup_failed`, and NPM is not sent a user.
- The acme email is the administrator's email, so it must be an address Let's Encrypt can register: a bare address (no display name), whose domain is not an IP literal, has a dot, and is not one of the reserved names in `deployReservedDomains` or under one. The standard library has no public-suffix list and no dependency may be added, so the module refuses the reserved names (RFC 2606, RFC 6761, RFC 6762, RFC 8375 and the common private suffixes) as its approximation of "a public registrable domain"; the harness uses an address under `darkpanel.dev`, which Pebble accepts. A refusal is `request.invalid_input` with the field path `/acmeEmail`, `core.Definite`.
- `Plan` and `Apply` run the same checks (the plan can be stale): the email, no instance row, no network, volume or container of the module's names on the Engine (`docker.in_use`), no listener on tcp 80 or 443 (`derp.port_in_use`, the message of the derp install), and no installed DERP server (spec 6: an installed but stopped one holds no listener, so the check reads the derp unit file `/etc/systemd/system/darkpanel-derper.service` through `n.d.readFile`; present is `core.Definite` `derp.port_in_use` with the detail `a DERP server is installed (darkpanel-derper.service)`; `fs.ErrNotExist` is free; any other read error is returned as it is). `Plan` changes nothing and does not pull the image; `Apply` pulls it when the Engine does not have it. A pull failure is `core.Definite` (nothing the module journals has changed) and journals nothing.
- The engine runs `Available` before `Plan`: Docker 28.0 or later and raw-table protection loaded (`module.unavailable` with the reason; an unreachable Engine is `docker.engine_unavailable`).
