### Task 14: npm: instance.remove

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/modules/npm/instance_remove.go`
- Test: `internal/modules/npm/instance_remove_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Contracts "NPM wire shapes" (`RemoveInput`, `Removed`); "NPM store" (`loadInstance`, `deleteInstance`, `storeTime`, the tables `npm_reserved`, `npm_attachments`, `npm_watch`); "NPM deps" (`nm`, `dockerAPI`); "NPM shared internals" (the constants `lockKey`, `commitWait`, `networkName`, `containerName`, `dataVolume`, `certVolume`; `nounContainer`, `nounVolume`, `nounNetwork`, `resourceOf`, `npmEffect`, `(nm).run`, `(nm).observeEngine`); "NPM identities" (Engine objects, `CommitWait` 2 min); "NPM test host" (`newTHHost`, `(*thHost).deployed`, `.nm`, `.env`, `.addChange`, `.docker`, `.journal`, `.db`, `thNetwork`, `thContainer`).
- Produces: `func (n nm) instanceRemove() core.ActionDef` (Contracts "NPM actions", Task 14), which Task 27 lists in `actions()`.

**Depends on:** Task 11
**Implementer:** dforge:impl-opus-high
**Evaluation:** files 1 - spec 2 - coupling 1 - risk 3 = 7

`npm.instance.remove` is irreversible and deletes data: the container, both volumes (the NPM database and the certificates in them) and the network. It refuses while any exposure still reserves an object of the instance, and removes what only the instance's own bookkeeping describes (the stored admin, the attachment claims, the watch marks) in the same call.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `internal/modules/npm/instance_remove_test.go`:

```go
package npm

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/schema"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/platform/dockerapi"
)

// rmModule registers one action, so the registry's own checks run on it.
type rmModule struct{ def core.ActionDef }

func (m rmModule) Manifest() core.Manifest {
	return core.Manifest{ID: ID, Version: 1, Actions: []core.ActionDef{m.def}}
}

var rmCountSQL = map[string]string{
	"npm_instances":   "SELECT count(*) FROM npm_instances",
	"npm_attachments": "SELECT count(*) FROM npm_attachments",
	"npm_watch":       "SELECT count(*) FROM npm_watch",
}

func rmCount(t *testing.T, h *thHost, table string) int {
	t.Helper()
	var n int
	if err := h.db.Read().QueryRowContext(context.Background(), rmCountSQL[table]).Scan(&n); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return n
}

func rmShort(id string) string { return id[:min(len(id), 12)] }

// rmRefused asserts err is a definite refusal with the given code and returns it.
func rmRefused(t *testing.T, err error, code errcode.Code) *errcode.Error {
	t.Helper()
	var coded *errcode.Error
	if !errors.As(err, &coded) || coded.Code != code {
		t.Fatalf("error %v, want code %s", err, code)
	}
	if !core.IsDefinite(err) {
		t.Errorf("error %v is not marked definite", err)
	}
	return coded
}

// rmDeployed seeds a deployed instance and makes sure its container, network
// and both volumes exist, are labelled darkpanel.module=npm and the container
// runs, as a finished deploy leaves them.
func rmDeployed(t *testing.T) (*thHost, thInstance) {
	t.Helper()
	h := newTHHost(t)
	inst := h.deployed()
	labels := map[string]string{core.LabelModule: ID}
	nw, ok := h.docker.network(networkName)
	if !ok {
		nw = thNetwork(networkName)
	}
	nw.Labels = labels
	h.docker.putNetwork(nw)
	for _, name := range []string{dataVolume, certVolume} {
		v, ok := h.docker.volume(name)
		if !ok {
			v = dockerapi.Volume{Name: name, Driver: "local"}
		}
		v.Labels = labels
		h.docker.putVolume(v)
	}
	c, ok := h.docker.container(inst.ContainerID)
	if !ok {
		t.Fatal("deployed() left no container")
	}
	if strings.TrimPrefix(c.Name, "/") != containerName {
		t.Fatalf("deployed container is named %q, want %q", c.Name, containerName)
	}
	if c.Config.Labels == nil {
		c.Config.Labels = map[string]string{}
	}
	c.Config.Labels[core.LabelModule] = ID
	c.State.Running, c.State.Status = true, "running"
	h.docker.putContainer(c)
	return h, inst
}

func rmStop(t *testing.T, h *thHost, inst thInstance) {
	t.Helper()
	c, _ := h.docker.container(inst.ContainerID)
	c.State.Running, c.State.Status = false, "exited"
	h.docker.putContainer(c)
}

func rmDropContainer(t *testing.T, h *thHost, inst thInstance) {
	t.Helper()
	err := h.docker.RemoveContainer(context.Background(), inst.ContainerID, dockerapi.RemoveContainerOptions{Force: true})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

// rmReserve records an object an exposure reserves.
func rmReserve(t *testing.T, h *thHost, _ thInstance) {
	t.Helper()
	ctx := context.Background()
	err := h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		return addReserved(ctx, tx, reservedRow{Kind: "proxy_host", ObjectID: 7, Owner: "npm.admin.expose", Exposure: "npm.example.com", ChangeID: "c0"})
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

// rmWeb puts a second container on the NPM network, mounting the data volume.
func rmWeb(onNetwork, mountsData bool) func(t *testing.T, h *thHost, inst thInstance) {
	return func(t *testing.T, h *thHost, _ thInstance) {
		t.Helper()
		nw, _ := h.docker.network(networkName)
		h.docker.putContainer(thContainer("web", func(c *dockerapi.ContainerJSON) {
			if onNetwork {
				c.NetworkSettings.Networks = map[string]*dockerapi.EndpointSettings{networkName: {NetworkID: nw.ID}}
			}
			if mountsData {
				c.Mounts = []dockerapi.MountPoint{{Type: "volume", Name: dataVolume, Destination: "/data"}}
			}
		}))
	}
}

var (
	rmVolumeDiff = []core.DiffLine{
		{Op: "-", Text: "volume darkpanel-npm-data"},
		{Op: "-", Text: "volume darkpanel-npm-letsencrypt"},
		{Op: " ", Text: "the proxy hosts, access lists, certificates and users in the volumes are deleted and cannot be restored"},
	}
	rmTailDiff = []core.DiffLine{
		{Op: "-", Text: "network darkpanel-npm"},
		{Op: "-", Text: "the stored admin password and the attachment claims of this instance"},
	}
)

func rmContainerDiff(inst thInstance, killed bool) []core.DiffLine {
	d := []core.DiffLine{{Op: "-", Text: "container darkpanel-npm (" + rmShort(inst.ContainerID) + ")"}}
	if killed {
		d = append(d, core.DiffLine{Op: " ", Text: "the running container is killed first"})
	}
	return d
}

func TestRmPlan(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	accepted := []struct {
		name  string
		setup func(t *testing.T, h *thHost, inst thInstance)
		diff  func(inst thInstance) []core.DiffLine
	}{
		{name: "running instance", diff: func(inst thInstance) []core.DiffLine {
			return slices.Concat(rmContainerDiff(inst, true), rmVolumeDiff, rmTailDiff)
		}},
		{name: "stopped container", setup: rmStop, diff: func(inst thInstance) []core.DiffLine {
			return slices.Concat(rmContainerDiff(inst, false), rmVolumeDiff, rmTailDiff)
		}},
		{name: "container already removed", setup: rmDropContainer, diff: func(thInstance) []core.DiffLine {
			return slices.Concat(rmVolumeDiff, rmTailDiff)
		}},
	}
	for _, tt := range accepted {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h, inst := rmDeployed(t)
			if tt.setup != nil {
				tt.setup(t, h, inst)
			}
			out, err := h.nm().instanceRemove().Handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), json.RawMessage(`{}`))
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if want := tt.diff(inst); !slices.Equal(out.Diff, want) {
				t.Errorf("diff %v, want %v", out.Diff, want)
			}
			if out.Confirmation != "darkpanel-npm" {
				t.Errorf("confirmation %q, want darkpanel-npm", out.Confirmation)
			}
			if out.StateHash == "" {
				t.Error("empty state hash")
			}
			for _, call := range []string{"RemoveContainer", "RemoveVolume", "RemoveNetwork"} {
				if slices.Contains(h.docker.calls(), call) {
					t.Errorf("a plan called %s", call)
				}
			}
		})
	}

	refusals := []struct {
		name  string
		fresh bool
		setup func(t *testing.T, h *thHost, inst thInstance)
		code  errcode.Code
		has   string
	}{
		{name: "no instance", fresh: true, code: errcode.NotFound, has: "no Nginx Proxy Manager instance"},
		{name: "an exposure reserves an object", setup: rmReserve, code: errcode.NPMReserved, has: "npm.example.com"},
		{name: "another container uses the network", setup: rmWeb(true, false), code: errcode.DockerInUse, has: "web"},
		{name: "another container mounts a volume", setup: rmWeb(false, true), code: errcode.DockerInUse, has: "web"},
		{name: "container not labelled", setup: func(_ *testing.T, h *thHost, inst thInstance) {
			c, _ := h.docker.container(inst.ContainerID)
			delete(c.Config.Labels, core.LabelModule)
			h.docker.putContainer(c)
		}, code: errcode.InvalidInput, has: "darkpanel.module"},
		{name: "engine unavailable", setup: func(_ *testing.T, h *thHost, _ thInstance) {
			h.docker.Fail("InspectContainer", dockerapi.ErrUnavailable)
		}, code: errcode.DockerEngineUnavailable},
	}
	for _, tt := range refusals {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			var h *thHost
			var inst thInstance
			if tt.fresh {
				h = newTHHost(t)
			} else {
				h, inst = rmDeployed(t)
			}
			if tt.setup != nil {
				tt.setup(t, h, inst)
			}
			_, err := h.nm().instanceRemove().Handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), json.RawMessage(`{}`))
			coded := rmRefused(t, err, tt.code)
			if !strings.Contains(coded.Detail, tt.has) {
				t.Errorf("detail %q does not hold %q", coded.Detail, tt.has)
			}
			if slices.Contains(h.docker.calls(), "RemoveContainer") {
				t.Error("a refused plan removed the container")
			}
		})
	}
}

// TestRmPlanStateHash: the hash is stable while the instance is, and changes
// when the container stops, so a plan made before a stop is refused.
func TestRmPlanStateHash(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, inst := rmDeployed(t)
	def := h.nm().instanceRemove()
	plan := func() string {
		t.Helper()
		out, err := def.Handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), json.RawMessage(`{}`))
		if err != nil || out.StateHash == "" {
			t.Fatalf("plan: hash %q, error %v", out.StateHash, err)
		}
		return out.StateHash
	}
	first := plan()
	if again := plan(); again != first {
		t.Errorf("a second plan hashes %s, want %s", again, first)
	}
	rmStop(t, h, inst)
	if stopped := plan(); stopped == first {
		t.Error("the hash did not change when the container stopped")
	}
}

func TestRmApply(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	apply := func(h *thHost, change string) (json.RawMessage, error) {
		h.addChange(change, "npm.instance.remove")
		return h.nm().instanceRemove().Handler.Apply(ctx, h.env(change), json.RawMessage(`{}`))
	}

	t.Run("removes the instance and its data", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, inst := rmDeployed(t)
		nw, _ := h.docker.network(networkName)
		err := h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
			if err := addClaim(ctx, tx, claimRow{Network: "appnet", Claimant: "expose:a.example.com", ChangeID: "c0"}); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return addWatch(ctx, tx, "proxy_host", "a.example.com", "c0", 1)
		})
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		raw, err := apply(h, "c1")
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		var out Removed
		if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &out); err != nil || out != (Removed{ContainerID: inst.ContainerID}) {
			t.Errorf("output %s (%v), want container %s", raw, err, inst.ContainerID)
		}
		if _, ok := h.docker.container(inst.ContainerID); ok {
			t.Error("the container is still there")
		}
		for _, v := range []string{dataVolume, certVolume} {
			if _, ok := h.docker.volume(v); ok {
				t.Errorf("volume %s is still there", v)
			}
		}
		if _, ok := h.docker.network(networkName); ok {
			t.Error("the network is still there")
		}
		for _, table := range []string{"npm_instances", "npm_attachments", "npm_watch"} {
			if n := rmCount(t, h, table); n != 0 {
				t.Errorf("%s holds %d rows, want 0", table, n)
			}
		}
		want := []string{"npm.container:" + inst.ContainerID, "npm.volume:" + dataVolume, "npm.volume:" + certVolume, "npm.network:" + nw.ID}
		effects := h.journal.effects()
		if len(effects) != len(want) {
			t.Fatalf("%d effects, want %d: %+v", len(effects), len(want), effects)
		}
		for i, e := range effects {
			if e.Kind != core.EffectDelete || e.Resource != want[i] || e.State != core.EffectMade || e.Local || e.CommitWait != commitWait {
				t.Errorf("effect %d is %+v, want a made non-local delete of %s with commit wait %s", i, e, want[i], commitWait)
			}
			if string(e.Before.State) == "null" {
				t.Errorf("effect %d journals the absent state as its before-state", i)
			}
			if e.Post == nil || string(e.Post.State) != "null" {
				t.Errorf("effect %d post %+v, want the absent state null", i, e.Post)
			}
		}
	})

	t.Run("the daemon refuses a volume removal", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, _ := rmDeployed(t)
		h.docker.Fail("RemoveVolume", &dockerapi.APIError{StatusCode: 409, Message: "volume is in use"})
		_, err := apply(h, "c1")
		if err == nil || !core.IsDefinite(err) {
			t.Fatalf("error %v, want a definite refusal", err)
		}
		effects := h.journal.effects()
		if len(effects) != 2 || effects[0].State != core.EffectMade || effects[1].State != core.EffectSkipped {
			t.Fatalf("effects %+v, want the container made and the volume skipped", effects)
		}
		if n := rmCount(t, h, "npm_instances"); n != 1 {
			t.Errorf("npm_instances holds %d rows, want the instance kept", n)
		}
	})

	t.Run("the daemon fails on the network", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, _ := rmDeployed(t)
		h.docker.Fail("RemoveNetwork", &dockerapi.APIError{StatusCode: 500, Message: "boom"})
		_, err := apply(h, "c1")
		if err == nil || core.IsDefinite(err) {
			t.Fatalf("error %v, want an uncertain failure", err)
		}
		effects := h.journal.effects()
		if len(effects) != 4 || effects[3].State != core.EffectIntended {
			t.Fatalf("effects %+v, want four with the network left intended", effects)
		}
		if n := rmCount(t, h, "npm_instances"); n != 1 {
			t.Errorf("npm_instances holds %d rows, want the instance kept", n)
		}
	})

	t.Run("refused again at apply", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, inst := rmDeployed(t)
		rmReserve(t, h, inst)
		_, err := apply(h, "c1")
		_ = rmRefused(t, err, errcode.NPMReserved)
		if len(h.journal.effects()) != 0 || slices.Contains(h.docker.calls(), "RemoveContainer") {
			t.Error("apply journaled an effect or removed the container")
		}
	})

	t.Run("resumes after the container is gone", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, inst := rmDeployed(t)
		rmDropContainer(t, h, inst)
		raw, err := apply(h, "c1")
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		var out Removed
		if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &out); err != nil || out.ContainerID != inst.ContainerID {
			t.Errorf("output %s (%v), want the stored container %s", raw, err, inst.ContainerID)
		}
		if n := len(h.journal.effects()); n != 3 {
			t.Errorf("%d effects, want 3 (two volumes and the network)", n)
		}
		if n := rmCount(t, h, "npm_instances"); n != 0 {
			t.Errorf("npm_instances holds %d rows, want 0", n)
		}
	})
}

func TestRmVerify(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, _ := rmDeployed(t)
	def := h.nm().instanceRemove()
	verify := func() error {
		return def.Handler.Verify(ctx, h.env("c1"), json.RawMessage(`{}`), json.RawMessage(`{}`))
	}
	if verify() == nil {
		t.Error("verify passed while the instance is deployed")
	}
	h.addChange("c1", "npm.instance.remove")
	if _, err := def.Handler.Apply(ctx, h.env("c1"), json.RawMessage(`{}`)); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := verify(); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("verify after the removal: %v", err)
	}
	h.docker.putVolume(dockerapi.Volume{Name: dataVolume, Driver: "local"})
	if verify() == nil {
		t.Error("verify passed with a volume of the instance back")
	}
}

// TestRmObserve: a settle finds the container's before-state while it is there
// and the absent state once it is removed.
func TestRmObserve(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, _ := rmDeployed(t)
	def := h.nm().instanceRemove()
	h.addChange("c1", "npm.instance.remove")
	h.docker.Fail("RemoveContainer", &dockerapi.APIError{StatusCode: 500, Message: "boom"})
	if _, err := def.Handler.Apply(ctx, h.env("c1"), json.RawMessage(`{}`)); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("apply succeeded")
	}
	e := h.journal.effects()[0]
	obs, err := def.Handler.Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), e)
	if err != nil || string(obs.Current.State) != string(e.Before.State) {
		t.Errorf("observation %s (%v), want the journaled before-state %s", obs.Current.State, err, e.Before.State)
	}
	h.addChange("c2", "npm.instance.remove")
	if _, err := def.Handler.Apply(ctx, h.env("c2"), json.RawMessage(`{}`)); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	obs, err = def.Handler.Observe(ctx, h.env("c2"), e)
	if err != nil || string(obs.Current.State) != "null" {
		t.Errorf("observation %s (%v), want null", obs.Current.State, err)
	}
}

func TestRmDefinition(t *testing.T) {
	h := newTHHost(t)
	def := h.nm().instanceRemove()
	s := def.Spec
	if s.Name != "npm.instance.remove" || s.Version != 1 || s.Class != core.ClassIrreversible || s.Side != core.SideAgent ||
		!slices.Equal(s.LockKeys, []string{"npm"}) {
		t.Errorf("spec %+v", s)
	}
	if def.Handler.Inverse != nil || def.Handler.Observe == nil {
		t.Errorf("inverse %v observe %v, want no inverse and an observer", def.Handler.Inverse != nil, def.Handler.Observe != nil)
	}
	if _, err := core.NewRegistry(rmModule{def}); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("the registry refuses the action: %v", err)
	}
	v := schema.NewValidator()
	for input, field := range map[string]string{`{}`: "", `{"x":1}`: "/x"} {
		err := v.ValidateInput(def, json.RawMessage(input))
		var coded *errcode.Error
		switch {
		case field == "" && err != nil:
			t.Errorf("input %s: %v, want it accepted", input, err)
		case field != "" && (!errors.As(err, &coded) || !slices.ContainsFunc(coded.Fields, func(f errcode.FieldError) bool { return f.Path == field })):
			t.Errorf("input %s: %v, want a refusal naming %s", input, err, field)
		}
	}
}
```

Expected: FAIL to build, `h.nm().instanceRemove undefined (type nm has no field or method instanceRemove)`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `internal/modules/npm/instance_remove.go` in package `npm`.

1. `func (n nm) instanceRemove() core.ActionDef` is `core.DefineMutating[RemoveInput, Removed]` with the spec: name `npm.instance.remove`, summary "Remove the Nginx Proxy Manager instance and its data.", version 1, class `core.ClassIrreversible`, side `core.SideAgent`, `LockKeys: []string{lockKey}`, the default apply timeout. The handler `instanceRemoveHandler{n nm}` implements Plan, Apply, Verify and Observe, and has no Inverse.
2. `target(ctx, env)` is the one function Plan and Apply both call; it reads and decides, changes nothing, and marks every failure `core.Definite`. In this order:
   - `loadInstance(ctx, env.DB.Read())`; no row is `errcode.NotFound` with the detail "no Nginx Proxy Manager instance is deployed on this host".
   - `SELECT DISTINCT exposure FROM npm_reserved ORDER BY exposure`; any row is `errcode.NPMReserved` whose detail names every exposure hostname (sorted, comma-separated) and says to remove them first.
   - `InspectContainer(ctx, containerName)` (the first Engine call; `dockerapi.IsNotFound` means the container is absent), `InspectNetwork(ctx, networkName)` and `InspectVolume` of `dataVolume` and `certVolume`, each absent when not found. An object that exists but does not carry the label `darkpanel.module=npm` (`core.LabelModule`, `ID`) is `errcode.InvalidInput` with the detail "<kind> <name> is not labelled darkpanel.module=npm; Darkpanel does not remove it".
   - One `ListContainers` with `All: true`. A container other than the NPM container (compared by id) that is attached to the network (its summary's `NetworkSettings.Networks` has the network's name as key or an endpoint with the network's id; the summary's `NetworkSettings` may be nil) is `errcode.DockerInUse` ("network darkpanel-npm is used by container <name>"); one that has a `Mounts` entry named after either volume is `errcode.DockerInUse` ("volume <volume> is used by container <name>"). A container's name is its first `Names` entry without the leading slash, or its id when it has none.
   - A failure of any Engine call is returned `core.Definite`; `dockerapi.ErrUnavailable` is first wrapped as `errcode.DockerEngineUnavailable` with the detail "the Docker Engine is not reachable".
   Use a file-local helper for the unavailable mapping, named with the prefix `instanceRemove` (no other task may meet the name).
3. Plan returns the diff lines in exactly this order, each only when its object exists: `-` "container darkpanel-npm (<first 12 characters of the id>)"; then ` ` "the running container is killed first" when it runs; then one `-` "volume <name>" per existing volume (data first), followed, when at least one volume exists, by ` ` "the proxy hosts, access lists, certificates and users in the volumes are deleted and cannot be restored"; then `-` "network darkpanel-npm" when it exists; always last `-` "the stored admin password and the attachment claims of this instance". `Confirmation` is `containerName`. `Guards` is the empty non-nil slice. `StateHash` is the hex SHA-256 of the instance row's `UpdatedAt` (`storeTime`), the container id and running flag, the names of the existing volumes and the network id, so a plan goes stale when any of them changes.
4. Apply runs `target` again, then removes in this order, skipping an object that is absent: the container, the data volume, the certificate volume, the network. Each removal is one effect of kind `core.EffectDelete` built by `npmEffect(core.EffectDelete, resourceOf(<noun>, <identity>), before, commitWait)` with the identity the container id, the volume name or the network id. `before` is the `State` of `n.observeEngine` called on an `intended` effect with the same resource, read just before the call, so it matches what a later settlement observes. Each effect goes through `n.run(ctx, env, e, n.observeEngine, do)`, where `do` calls `RemoveContainer(ctx, id, dockerapi.RemoveContainerOptions{Force: true})`, `RemoveVolume(ctx, name, false)` or `RemoveNetwork(ctx, id)` and returns the effect's resource. When the last removal has succeeded, one `env.DB.Write` transaction calls `deleteInstance` and deletes every row of `npm_attachments` and of `npm_watch`, since they describe objects of the removed instance. The output is `Removed{ContainerID}` with the id of the container removed, or the stored id when the container was already gone. A failed removal returns `run`'s error and leaves the instance row, so running the action again continues where it stopped.
5. Verify reads from the handler alone (recovery passes the zero output): the container `darkpanel-npm`, both volumes and the network must be absent and `npm_instances` must hold no row; otherwise it returns an error naming what is left. Observe returns `n.observeEngine(ctx, env, e)`.

### Task 15: npm: network.attach and network.detach claims

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/modules/npm/network_claims.go`
- Test: `internal/modules/npm/network_claims_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Contracts "NPM wire shapes" (`AttachInput`, `Attachment`, `DetachInput`); "NPM store" (`loadInstance`, `claims`, `addClaim`, `dropClaim`, `claimRow`, the table `npm_attachments`); "NPM deps"; "NPM shared internals" (`lockKey`, `commitWait`, `containerName`, `nounEndpoint`, `nounClaim`, `resourceOf`, `identityOf`, `npmEffect`, `(nm).run`, `(nm).observeEngine`, `nullState`, `named`); "NPM identities" (`npm.endpoint:<network>`, bookkeeping `npm.claim:<network>|<claimant>`); "Proxy rules" (`proxy.Target`, `proxy.Endpoint`); "NPM test host".
- Produces: `func (n nm) networkAttach() core.ActionDef` and `func (n nm) networkDetach() core.ActionDef` (Contracts "NPM actions", Task 15), which Task 27 lists in `actions()`; the claim semantics Tasks 25 and 26 and wave 8 rely on: a claim per `(network, claimant)`, `Preexisting` shared by all claims of a network, `Network: ""` in `DetachInput` detaching every claim of the claimant.

**Depends on:** Task 11
**Implementer:** dforge:impl-opus-high
**Evaluation:** files 1 - spec 2 - coupling 2 - risk 2 = 7

`npm.network.attach` joins the NPM container to a network so it can reach a target by name, and records who needs the attachment. `npm.network.detach` drops a claim and leaves the network only when no claim is left and NPM was not there before. Both are compensable with an undo; detach also declares `Converges` (it is a step of the forward composites).

Decisions this task embodies:
- The Engine endpoint is a `modify` effect of `npm.endpoint:<network name>` (its before-state is read with `observeEngine`), not a creation, because its identity is known before the call. The endpoint effect of a detach declares `InverseRecovery` `{name, darkpanel-npm@<network>}`, since its undo creates the endpoint again; the attach's endpoint effect declares none. The undo of an endpoint effect tells the two apart by that field.
- A claim is one `npm.claim:<network>|<claimant>` effect with `Local: true` and `InverseLocal: true`: a create for attach (`Resource` `npm.claim:?<network>|<claimant>`, recovery `name` the same value, `After` the claim document) and a delete for detach (`Before` the claim document, `After` the null state). The claim document is `{"network", "claimant", "preexisting", "changeId"}`; the state `null` means no row.
- `Preexisting` is true when no claim existed on the network and NPM was already attached; a claim added to a network that has claims copies the `Preexisting` of the existing ones.
- The undo of a detach reconnects NPM with a dynamically assigned address; it does not restore a static address.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `internal/modules/npm/network_claims_test.go`:

```go
package npm

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"net/netip"
	"reflect"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/schema"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/platform/dockerapi"
)

const (
	nwNet      = "appnet"
	nwNet2     = "appnet2"
	nwClaimant = "expose:app.example.com"
	nwOther    = "expose:other.example.com"
)

// nwModule registers the actions, so the registry's own checks run on them.
type nwModule struct{ defs []core.ActionDef }

func (m nwModule) Manifest() core.Manifest {
	return core.Manifest{ID: ID, Version: 1, Actions: m.defs}
}

type nwSetupFunc func(t *testing.T, h *thHost, user dockerapi.Network)

// nwRefused asserts err is a definite refusal with the given code and returns it.
func nwRefused(t *testing.T, err error, code errcode.Code) *errcode.Error {
	t.Helper()
	var coded *errcode.Error
	if !errors.As(err, &coded) || coded.Code != code {
		t.Fatalf("error %v, want code %s", err, code)
	}
	if !core.IsDefinite(err) {
		t.Errorf("error %v is not marked definite", err)
	}
	return coded
}

func nwJSON(t *testing.T, v any) json.RawMessage {
	t.Helper()
	b, err := json.Marshal(v)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return b
}

// nwBridge is a user-defined bridge network.
func nwBridge(name string) dockerapi.Network {
	return thNetwork(name, func(n *dockerapi.Network) { n.Driver = "bridge" })
}

// nwSetup deploys the instance and adds the user-defined network appnet with a
// container web on it.
func nwSetup(t *testing.T) (*thHost, dockerapi.Network) {
	t.Helper()
	h := newTHHost(t)
	h.deployed()
	user := nwBridge(nwNet)
	h.docker.putNetwork(user)
	h.docker.putContainer(thContainer("web", func(c *dockerapi.ContainerJSON) {
		c.NetworkSettings.Networks = map[string]*dockerapi.EndpointSettings{nwNet: {NetworkID: user.ID}}
	}))
	return h, user
}

// nwConnect puts the NPM container on network, as if it had been there.
func nwConnect(t *testing.T, h *thHost, network dockerapi.Network, ep *dockerapi.EndpointSettings) {
	t.Helper()
	if err := h.docker.ConnectNetwork(context.Background(), network.ID, containerName, ep); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

func nwStatic(addr string) *dockerapi.EndpointSettings {
	return &dockerapi.EndpointSettings{IPAMConfig: &dockerapi.EndpointIPAMConfig{IPv4Address: netip.MustParseAddr(addr)}}
}

// nwEndpoint is the NPM container's endpoint on the network with the id, nil when it is not attached.
func nwEndpoint(t *testing.T, h *thHost, networkID string) *dockerapi.EndpointSettings {
	t.Helper()
	c, ok := h.docker.container(containerName)
	if !ok {
		t.Fatal("the NPM container is missing")
	}
	for _, ep := range c.NetworkSettings.Networks {
		if ep != nil && ep.NetworkID == networkID {
			return ep
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func nwClaim(t *testing.T, h *thHost, network, claimant, change string, preexisting bool) {
	t.Helper()
	ctx := context.Background()
	err := h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		return addClaim(ctx, tx, claimRow{Network: network, Claimant: claimant, ChangeID: change, Preexisting: preexisting})
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

func nwClaims(t *testing.T, h *thHost, network string) []claimRow {
	t.Helper()
	got, err := claims(context.Background(), h.db.Read(), network)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return got
}

func nwCalls(h *thHost, method string) int {
	n := 0
	for _, c := range h.docker.calls() {
		if c == method {
			n++
		}
	}
	return n
}

func nwApplyAttach(t *testing.T, h *thHost, change string, in AttachInput) (Attachment, error) {
	t.Helper()
	h.addChange(change, "npm.network.attach")
	raw, err := h.nm().networkAttach().Handler.Apply(context.Background(), h.env(change), nwJSON(t, in))
	if err != nil {
		return Attachment{}, err
	}
	var out Attachment
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &out); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return out, nil
}

func nwApplyDetach(t *testing.T, h *thHost, change string, in DetachInput) (Attachment, error) {
	t.Helper()
	h.addChange(change, "npm.network.detach")
	raw, err := h.nm().networkDetach().Handler.Apply(context.Background(), h.env(change), nwJSON(t, in))
	if err != nil {
		return Attachment{}, err
	}
	var out Attachment
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &out); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return out, nil
}

func nwBridgeOnly(_ *testing.T, h *thHost, _ dockerapi.Network) {
	br := thNetwork("bridge", func(n *dockerapi.Network) { n.Driver = "bridge" })
	h.docker.putNetwork(br)
	h.docker.putContainer(thContainer("legacy", func(c *dockerapi.ContainerJSON) {
		c.NetworkSettings.Networks = map[string]*dockerapi.EndpointSettings{"bridge": {NetworkID: br.ID}}
	}))
}

func nwOverlay(_ *testing.T, h *thHost, _ dockerapi.Network) {
	ov := thNetwork("overlaynet", func(n *dockerapi.Network) { n.Driver = "overlay" })
	h.docker.putNetwork(ov)
	h.docker.putContainer(thContainer("ov", func(c *dockerapi.ContainerJSON) {
		c.NetworkSettings.Networks = map[string]*dockerapi.EndpointSettings{"overlaynet": {NetworkID: ov.ID}}
	}))
}

func nwOtherNet(_ *testing.T, h *thHost, _ dockerapi.Network) {
	h.docker.putNetwork(nwBridge("other"))
}

func TestNwAttachPlan(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	base := AttachInput{Network: nwNet, Claimant: nwClaimant, Target: "web"}
	join := core.DiffLine{Op: "+", Text: "container darkpanel-npm joins network appnet"}
	claim := core.DiffLine{Op: "+", Text: "claim of expose:app.example.com on network appnet"}
	tests := []struct {
		name  string
		setup nwSetupFunc
		mod   func(in *AttachInput)
		diff  []core.DiffLine
	}{
		{name: "first claim connects", diff: []core.DiffLine{join, claim}},
		{name: "without a target", mod: func(in *AttachInput) { in.Target = "" }, diff: []core.DiffLine{join, claim}},
		{name: "static address", mod: func(in *AttachInput) { in.Address = netip.MustParseAddr("10.9.0.2") },
			diff: []core.DiffLine{join, {Op: "+", Text: "ipv4 10.9.0.2"}, claim}},
		{name: "attachment already there", setup: func(t *testing.T, h *thHost, user dockerapi.Network) {
			nwConnect(t, h, user, nil)
		}, diff: []core.DiffLine{claim, {Op: " ", Text: "darkpanel-npm is already on network appnet; detaching leaves it there"}}},
		{name: "second claimant", setup: func(t *testing.T, h *thHost, user dockerapi.Network) {
			nwConnect(t, h, user, nil)
			nwClaim(t, h, nwNet, nwOther, "c0", false)
		}, diff: []core.DiffLine{claim}},
		{name: "same claimant again", setup: func(t *testing.T, h *thHost, user dockerapi.Network) {
			nwConnect(t, h, user, nil)
			nwClaim(t, h, nwNet, nwClaimant, "c0", false)
		}, diff: nil},
		{name: "claim whose attachment is gone", setup: func(t *testing.T, h *thHost, _ dockerapi.Network) {
			nwClaim(t, h, nwNet, nwClaimant, "c0", false)
		}, diff: []core.DiffLine{join}},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h, user := nwSetup(t)
			if tt.setup != nil {
				tt.setup(t, h, user)
			}
			in := base
			if tt.mod != nil {
				tt.mod(&in)
			}
			connects := nwCalls(h, "ConnectNetwork")
			out, err := h.nm().networkAttach().Handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), nwJSON(t, in))
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if !slices.Equal(out.Diff, tt.diff) {
				t.Errorf("diff %v, want %v", out.Diff, tt.diff)
			}
			if out.StateHash == "" {
				t.Error("empty state hash")
			}
			if nwCalls(h, "ConnectNetwork") != connects {
				t.Error("a plan connected a network")
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestNwAttachPlanStateHash(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, user := nwSetup(t)
	def := h.nm().networkAttach()
	in := nwJSON(t, AttachInput{Network: nwNet, Claimant: nwClaimant, Target: "web"})
	plan := func() string {
		t.Helper()
		out, err := def.Handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), in)
		if err != nil || out.StateHash == "" {
			t.Fatalf("plan: hash %q, error %v", out.StateHash, err)
		}
		return out.StateHash
	}
	first := plan()
	if again := plan(); again != first {
		t.Errorf("a second plan hashes %s, want %s", again, first)
	}
	nwConnect(t, h, user, nil)
	if attached := plan(); attached == first {
		t.Error("the hash did not change when NPM joined the network")
	}
}

func TestNwAttachPlanRefusals(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	base := AttachInput{Network: nwNet, Claimant: nwClaimant, Target: "web"}
	tests := []struct {
		name  string
		setup nwSetupFunc
		mod   func(in *AttachInput)
		code  errcode.Code
		field string
		has   string
	}{
		{name: "unknown network", mod: func(in *AttachInput) { in.Network = "ghost" }, code: errcode.NotFound},
		{name: "unknown target", mod: func(in *AttachInput) { in.Target = "ghost" }, code: errcode.NotFound},
		{name: "target only on the default bridge", setup: nwBridgeOnly,
			mod:  func(in *AttachInput) { in.Network, in.Target = "bridge", "legacy" },
			code: errcode.NPMUnsupportedTarget, has: "connect the container to a user-defined network"},
		{name: "target on a non-bridge driver", setup: nwOverlay,
			mod:  func(in *AttachInput) { in.Network, in.Target = "overlaynet", "ov" },
			code: errcode.NPMUnsupportedTarget, has: "connect the container to a user-defined network"},
		{name: "target not on the network", setup: nwOtherNet,
			mod:  func(in *AttachInput) { in.Network = "other" },
			code: errcode.NPMUnsupportedTarget, has: "connect the container to a user-defined network"},
		{name: "the default bridge without a target", setup: nwBridgeOnly,
			mod:  func(in *AttachInput) { in.Network, in.Target = "bridge", "" },
			code: errcode.NPMUnsupportedTarget},
		{name: "empty claimant", mod: func(in *AttachInput) { in.Claimant = "" }, code: errcode.InvalidInput, field: "/claimant"},
		{name: "claimant with a space", mod: func(in *AttachInput) { in.Claimant = "expose: x" }, code: errcode.InvalidInput, field: "/claimant"},
		{name: "network name with a space", mod: func(in *AttachInput) { in.Network = "a b" }, code: errcode.InvalidInput, field: "/network"},
		{name: "ipv6 address", mod: func(in *AttachInput) { in.Address = netip.MustParseAddr("fd00::2") },
			code: errcode.InvalidInput, field: "/address"},
		{name: "address differs from the existing endpoint", setup: func(t *testing.T, h *thHost, user dockerapi.Network) {
			nwConnect(t, h, user, nwStatic("10.9.0.5"))
		}, mod: func(in *AttachInput) { in.Address = netip.MustParseAddr("10.9.0.2") }, code: errcode.InvalidInput, field: "/address"},
		{name: "NPM container missing", setup: func(t *testing.T, h *thHost, _ dockerapi.Network) {
			err := h.docker.RemoveContainer(ctx, containerName, dockerapi.RemoveContainerOptions{Force: true})
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
		}, code: errcode.NPMUnhealthy},
		{name: "engine unavailable", setup: func(_ *testing.T, h *thHost, _ dockerapi.Network) {
			h.docker.Fail("InspectContainer", dockerapi.ErrUnavailable)
		}, code: errcode.DockerEngineUnavailable},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h, user := nwSetup(t)
			if tt.setup != nil {
				tt.setup(t, h, user)
			}
			in := base
			if tt.mod != nil {
				tt.mod(&in)
			}
			_, err := h.nm().networkAttach().Handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), nwJSON(t, in))
			coded := nwRefused(t, err, tt.code)
			if tt.field != "" && !slices.ContainsFunc(coded.Fields, func(f errcode.FieldError) bool { return f.Path == tt.field }) {
				t.Errorf("fields %v, want one at %s", coded.Fields, tt.field)
			}
			if !strings.Contains(coded.Detail, tt.has) {
				t.Errorf("detail %q does not hold %q", coded.Detail, tt.has)
			}
			if nwCalls(h, "ConnectNetwork") != 0 {
				t.Error("a refused plan connected a network")
			}
		})
	}
	t.Run("no instance", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newTHHost(t)
		_, err := h.nm().networkAttach().Handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), nwJSON(t, base))
		_ = nwRefused(t, err, errcode.NotFound)
	})
}

func TestNwAttachApply(t *testing.T) {
	in := AttachInput{Network: nwNet, Claimant: nwClaimant, Target: "web"}

	t.Run("connects and claims", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, user := nwSetup(t)
		out, err := nwApplyAttach(t, h, "c1", in)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if want := (Attachment{Network: nwNet, Claimant: nwClaimant, Attached: true}); out != want {
			t.Errorf("output %+v, want %+v", out, want)
		}
		if nwEndpoint(t, h, user.ID) == nil {
			t.Error("NPM is not on the network")
		}
		want := []claimRow{{Network: nwNet, Claimant: nwClaimant, ChangeID: "c1"}}
		if got := nwClaims(t, h, nwNet); !slices.Equal(got, want) {
			t.Errorf("claims %+v, want %+v", got, want)
		}
		effects := h.journal.effects()
		if len(effects) != 2 {
			t.Fatalf("%d effects, want 2: %+v", len(effects), effects)
		}
		ep, cl := effects[0], effects[1]
		if ep.Kind != core.EffectModify || ep.Resource != "npm.endpoint:appnet" || ep.State != core.EffectMade || ep.Local ||
			ep.CommitWait != commitWait || ep.InverseRecovery != nil {
			t.Errorf("endpoint effect %+v, want a made non-local modify of npm.endpoint:appnet with commit wait %s", ep, commitWait)
		}
		if cl.Kind != core.EffectCreate || cl.Resource != "npm.claim:appnet|expose:app.example.com" || cl.State != core.EffectMade || !cl.Local {
			t.Errorf("claim effect %+v, want a made local create of the claim", cl)
		}
		var doc struct {
			Network     string `json:"network"`
			Claimant    string `json:"claimant"`
			Preexisting bool   `json:"preexisting"`
			ChangeID    string `json:"changeId"`
		}
		if cl.Post == nil || json.Unmarshal(cl.Post.State, &doc) != nil || doc.Network != nwNet || doc.Claimant != nwClaimant || doc.Preexisting || doc.ChangeID != "c1" {
			t.Errorf("claim post %+v (%+v), want the claim of change c1", cl.Post, doc)
		}
	})

	t.Run("static address", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, user := nwSetup(t)
		withAddr := in
		withAddr.Address = netip.MustParseAddr("10.9.0.2")
		if _, err := nwApplyAttach(t, h, "c1", withAddr); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		ep := nwEndpoint(t, h, user.ID)
		if ep == nil || ep.IPAMConfig == nil || ep.IPAMConfig.IPv4Address != withAddr.Address {
			t.Errorf("endpoint %+v, want the static address %s", ep, withAddr.Address)
		}
	})

	t.Run("second claimant adds only a claim", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, user := nwSetup(t)
		nwConnect(t, h, user, nil)
		nwClaim(t, h, nwNet, nwOther, "c0", false)
		connects := nwCalls(h, "ConnectNetwork")
		out, err := nwApplyAttach(t, h, "c1", in)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if nwCalls(h, "ConnectNetwork") != connects {
			t.Error("apply connected a network that NPM is on")
		}
		if out.Preexisting || !out.Attached {
			t.Errorf("output %+v, want a claim that is not preexisting and attached", out)
		}
		if effects := h.journal.effects(); len(effects) != 1 || effects[0].Kind != core.EffectCreate {
			t.Errorf("effects %+v, want only the claim creation", effects)
		}
		if got := nwClaims(t, h, nwNet); len(got) != 2 {
			t.Errorf("claims %+v, want two", got)
		}
	})

	t.Run("attachment that was there before", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, user := nwSetup(t)
		nwConnect(t, h, user, nil)
		out, err := nwApplyAttach(t, h, "c1", in)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if !out.Preexisting || !out.Attached {
			t.Errorf("output %+v, want a preexisting attachment", out)
		}
		want := []claimRow{{Network: nwNet, Claimant: nwClaimant, ChangeID: "c1", Preexisting: true}}
		if got := nwClaims(t, h, nwNet); !slices.Equal(got, want) {
			t.Errorf("claims %+v, want %+v", got, want)
		}
	})

	t.Run("a later claim copies preexisting", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, user := nwSetup(t)
		nwConnect(t, h, user, nil)
		nwClaim(t, h, nwNet, nwOther, "c0", true)
		out, err := nwApplyAttach(t, h, "c1", in)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if !out.Preexisting {
			t.Errorf("output %+v, want preexisting copied from the existing claim", out)
		}
	})

	t.Run("same claimant again changes nothing", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, user := nwSetup(t)
		nwConnect(t, h, user, nil)
		nwClaim(t, h, nwNet, nwClaimant, "c0", false)
		out, err := nwApplyAttach(t, h, "c1", in)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if !out.Attached || len(h.journal.effects()) != 0 {
			t.Errorf("output %+v with %d effects, want attached and none", out, len(h.journal.effects()))
		}
	})

	t.Run("claim whose attachment is gone reconnects only", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, user := nwSetup(t)
		nwClaim(t, h, nwNet, nwClaimant, "c0", false)
		if _, err := nwApplyAttach(t, h, "c1", in); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if nwEndpoint(t, h, user.ID) == nil {
			t.Error("NPM is not on the network")
		}
		if effects := h.journal.effects(); len(effects) != 1 || effects[0].Kind != core.EffectModify {
			t.Errorf("effects %+v, want only the endpoint modify", effects)
		}
		if got := nwClaims(t, h, nwNet); len(got) != 1 || got[0].ChangeID != "c0" {
			t.Errorf("claims %+v, want the original claim untouched", got)
		}
	})

	t.Run("the daemon refuses the connect", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, _ := nwSetup(t)
		h.docker.Fail("ConnectNetwork", &dockerapi.APIError{StatusCode: 403, Message: "forbidden"})
		_, err := nwApplyAttach(t, h, "c1", in)
		if err == nil || !core.IsDefinite(err) {
			t.Fatalf("error %v, want a definite refusal", err)
		}
		if effects := h.journal.effects(); len(effects) != 1 || effects[0].State != core.EffectSkipped {
			t.Errorf("effects %+v, want one skipped", effects)
		}
		if got := nwClaims(t, h, nwNet); len(got) != 0 {
			t.Errorf("claims %+v, want none", got)
		}
	})

	t.Run("the daemon fails", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, _ := nwSetup(t)
		h.docker.Fail("ConnectNetwork", &dockerapi.APIError{StatusCode: 500, Message: "boom"})
		_, err := nwApplyAttach(t, h, "c1", in)
		if err == nil || core.IsDefinite(err) {
			t.Fatalf("error %v, want an uncertain failure", err)
		}
		if effects := h.journal.effects(); len(effects) != 1 || effects[0].State != core.EffectIntended {
			t.Errorf("effects %+v, want one left intended", effects)
		}
		if got := nwClaims(t, h, nwNet); len(got) != 0 {
			t.Errorf("claims %+v, want none", got)
		}
	})
}

func TestNwAttachVerify(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, _ := nwSetup(t)
	def := h.nm().networkAttach()
	raw := nwJSON(t, AttachInput{Network: nwNet, Claimant: nwClaimant, Target: "web"})
	if def.Handler.Verify(ctx, h.env("c1"), raw, json.RawMessage(`{}`)) == nil {
		t.Error("verify passed before the attach")
	}
	if _, err := nwApplyAttach(t, h, "c1", AttachInput{Network: nwNet, Claimant: nwClaimant, Target: "web"}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := def.Handler.Verify(ctx, h.env("c1"), raw, json.RawMessage(`{}`)); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("verify after the attach: %v", err)
	}
}

// nwSeedAttached puts NPM on the network with a claim, as an earlier attach left it.
func nwSeedAttached(t *testing.T, h *thHost, network dockerapi.Network, claimant string, preexisting bool) {
	t.Helper()
	nwConnect(t, h, network, nil)
	nwClaim(t, h, network.Name, claimant, "c0", preexisting)
}

func TestNwDetachPlan(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	del := func(network string) core.DiffLine {
		return core.DiffLine{Op: "-", Text: "claim of expose:app.example.com on network " + network}
	}
	leave := func(network string) core.DiffLine {
		return core.DiffLine{Op: "-", Text: "container darkpanel-npm leaves network " + network}
	}
	tests := []struct {
		name  string
		setup nwSetupFunc
		in    DetachInput
		diff  []core.DiffLine
	}{
		{name: "last claim leaves the network", setup: func(t *testing.T, h *thHost, user dockerapi.Network) {
			nwSeedAttached(t, h, user, nwClaimant, false)
		}, in: DetachInput{Network: nwNet, Claimant: nwClaimant}, diff: []core.DiffLine{del(nwNet), leave(nwNet)}},
		{name: "another claim keeps NPM there", setup: func(t *testing.T, h *thHost, user dockerapi.Network) {
			nwSeedAttached(t, h, user, nwClaimant, false)
			nwClaim(t, h, nwNet, nwOther, "c0", false)
		}, in: DetachInput{Network: nwNet, Claimant: nwClaimant}, diff: []core.DiffLine{del(nwNet),
			{Op: " ", Text: "darkpanel-npm stays on network appnet: claimed by expose:other.example.com"}}},
		{name: "attachment that was there before", setup: func(t *testing.T, h *thHost, user dockerapi.Network) {
			nwSeedAttached(t, h, user, nwClaimant, true)
		}, in: DetachInput{Network: nwNet, Claimant: nwClaimant}, diff: []core.DiffLine{del(nwNet),
			{Op: " ", Text: "darkpanel-npm stays on network appnet: it was there before"}}},
		{name: "no claim", setup: func(t *testing.T, h *thHost, user dockerapi.Network) {
			nwConnect(t, h, user, nil)
		}, in: DetachInput{Network: nwNet, Claimant: nwClaimant}, diff: nil},
		{name: "no claim on any network", in: DetachInput{Claimant: nwClaimant}, diff: nil},
		{name: "every network of the claimant", setup: func(t *testing.T, h *thHost, user dockerapi.Network) {
			second := nwBridge(nwNet2)
			h.docker.putNetwork(second)
			nwSeedAttached(t, h, user, nwClaimant, false)
			nwSeedAttached(t, h, second, nwClaimant, false)
		}, in: DetachInput{Claimant: nwClaimant}, diff: []core.DiffLine{del(nwNet), leave(nwNet), del(nwNet2), leave(nwNet2)}},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h, user := nwSetup(t)
			if tt.setup != nil {
				tt.setup(t, h, user)
			}
			disconnects := nwCalls(h, "DisconnectNetwork")
			out, err := h.nm().networkDetach().Handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), nwJSON(t, tt.in))
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if !slices.Equal(out.Diff, tt.diff) {
				t.Errorf("diff %v, want %v", out.Diff, tt.diff)
			}
			if nwCalls(h, "DisconnectNetwork") != disconnects {
				t.Error("a plan disconnected a network")
			}
		})
	}
	t.Run("claimant is required", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, _ := nwSetup(t)
		_, err := h.nm().networkDetach().Handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), nwJSON(t, DetachInput{Network: nwNet}))
		_ = nwRefused(t, err, errcode.InvalidInput)
	})
}

func TestNwDetachApply(t *testing.T) {
	t.Run("last claim disconnects", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, user := nwSetup(t)
		nwSeedAttached(t, h, user, nwClaimant, false)
		out, err := nwApplyDetach(t, h, "c2", DetachInput{Network: nwNet, Claimant: nwClaimant})
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if want := (Attachment{Network: nwNet, Claimant: nwClaimant}); out != want {
			t.Errorf("output %+v, want %+v", out, want)
		}
		if nwEndpoint(t, h, user.ID) != nil {
			t.Error("NPM is still on the network")
		}
		if got := nwClaims(t, h, nwNet); len(got) != 0 {
			t.Errorf("claims %+v, want none", got)
		}
		effects := h.journal.effects()
		if len(effects) != 2 {
			t.Fatalf("%d effects, want 2: %+v", len(effects), effects)
		}
		ep, cl := effects[0], effects[1]
		if ep.Kind != core.EffectModify || ep.Resource != "npm.endpoint:appnet" || ep.State != core.EffectMade || ep.Local ||
			ep.CommitWait != commitWait || ep.InverseRecovery == nil || ep.InverseRecovery.Value != "darkpanel-npm@appnet" {
			t.Errorf("endpoint effect %+v, want a made non-local modify declaring the inverse recovery darkpanel-npm@appnet", ep)
		}
		if cl.Kind != core.EffectDelete || cl.Resource != "npm.claim:appnet|expose:app.example.com" || cl.State != core.EffectMade ||
			!cl.Local || cl.Post == nil || string(cl.Post.State) != "null" {
			t.Errorf("claim effect %+v, want a made local delete with the null post-state", cl)
		}
	})

	t.Run("another claim keeps NPM there", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, user := nwSetup(t)
		nwSeedAttached(t, h, user, nwClaimant, false)
		nwClaim(t, h, nwNet, nwOther, "c0", false)
		out, err := nwApplyDetach(t, h, "c2", DetachInput{Network: nwNet, Claimant: nwClaimant})
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if !out.Attached {
			t.Errorf("output %+v, want NPM still attached", out)
		}
		if nwCalls(h, "DisconnectNetwork") != 0 || nwEndpoint(t, h, user.ID) == nil {
			t.Error("NPM left a network another claim needs")
		}
		if got := nwClaims(t, h, nwNet); len(got) != 1 || got[0].Claimant != nwOther {
			t.Errorf("claims %+v, want only %s", got, nwOther)
		}
		if effects := h.journal.effects(); len(effects) != 1 || effects[0].Kind != core.EffectDelete {
			t.Errorf("effects %+v, want only the claim deletion", effects)
		}
	})

	t.Run("attachment that was there before stays", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, user := nwSetup(t)
		nwSeedAttached(t, h, user, nwClaimant, true)
		out, err := nwApplyDetach(t, h, "c2", DetachInput{Network: nwNet, Claimant: nwClaimant})
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if !out.Preexisting || !out.Attached || nwEndpoint(t, h, user.ID) == nil {
			t.Errorf("output %+v, want a preexisting attachment left in place", out)
		}
	})

	t.Run("every network of the claimant", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, user := nwSetup(t)
		second := nwBridge(nwNet2)
		h.docker.putNetwork(second)
		nwSeedAttached(t, h, user, nwClaimant, false)
		nwSeedAttached(t, h, second, nwClaimant, false)
		nwClaim(t, h, nwNet, nwOther, "c0", false)
		if _, err := nwApplyDetach(t, h, "c2", DetachInput{Claimant: nwClaimant}); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if nwEndpoint(t, h, user.ID) == nil {
			t.Error("NPM left appnet, which the other claimant still needs")
		}
		if nwEndpoint(t, h, second.ID) != nil {
			t.Error("NPM is still on appnet2")
		}
		if got := nwClaims(t, h, nwNet2); len(got) != 0 {
			t.Errorf("appnet2 claims %+v, want none", got)
		}
		if got := nwClaims(t, h, nwNet); len(got) != 1 || got[0].Claimant != nwOther {
			t.Errorf("appnet claims %+v, want only %s", got, nwOther)
		}
	})

	t.Run("no claim changes nothing", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, user := nwSetup(t)
		nwConnect(t, h, user, nil)
		if _, err := nwApplyDetach(t, h, "c2", DetachInput{Network: nwNet, Claimant: nwClaimant}); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if len(h.journal.effects()) != 0 || nwCalls(h, "DisconnectNetwork") != 0 {
			t.Error("a detach without a claim journaled an effect or disconnected")
		}
	})

	t.Run("NPM container missing", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, _ := nwSetup(t)
		nwClaim(t, h, nwNet, nwClaimant, "c0", false)
		if err := h.docker.RemoveContainer(context.Background(), containerName, dockerapi.RemoveContainerOptions{Force: true}); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if _, err := nwApplyDetach(t, h, "c2", DetachInput{Network: nwNet, Claimant: nwClaimant}); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if nwCalls(h, "DisconnectNetwork") != 0 || len(nwClaims(t, h, nwNet)) != 0 {
			t.Error("detach disconnected a missing container or kept the claim")
		}
	})

	t.Run("the daemon refuses the disconnect", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, user := nwSetup(t)
		nwSeedAttached(t, h, user, nwClaimant, false)
		h.docker.Fail("DisconnectNetwork", &dockerapi.APIError{StatusCode: 403, Message: "forbidden"})
		_, err := nwApplyDetach(t, h, "c2", DetachInput{Network: nwNet, Claimant: nwClaimant})
		if err == nil || !core.IsDefinite(err) {
			t.Fatalf("error %v, want a definite refusal", err)
		}
		if got := nwClaims(t, h, nwNet); len(got) != 1 {
			t.Errorf("claims %+v, want the claim kept so a repeat can finish", got)
		}
		if effects := h.journal.effects(); len(effects) != 1 || effects[0].State != core.EffectSkipped {
			t.Errorf("effects %+v, want one skipped", effects)
		}
	})
}

func TestNwUndo(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()

	t.Run("attach", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, user := nwSetup(t)
		if _, err := nwApplyAttach(t, h, "c1", AttachInput{Network: nwNet, Claimant: nwClaimant, Target: "web"}); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		def := h.nm().networkAttach()
		effects := h.journal.effects()
		for i := len(effects) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
			if _, err := def.Handler.Inverse(ctx, h.env("u1"), effects[i]); err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("inverse of effect %d: %v", i, err)
			}
		}
		if nwEndpoint(t, h, user.ID) != nil {
			t.Error("NPM is still on the network after the undo")
		}
		if got := nwClaims(t, h, nwNet); len(got) != 0 {
			t.Errorf("claims %+v, want none after the undo", got)
		}
	})

	t.Run("detach", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, user := nwSetup(t)
		nwSeedAttached(t, h, user, nwClaimant, false)
		if _, err := nwApplyDetach(t, h, "c2", DetachInput{Network: nwNet, Claimant: nwClaimant}); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		def := h.nm().networkDetach()
		effects := h.journal.effects()
		for i := len(effects) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
			res, err := def.Handler.Inverse(ctx, h.env("u1"), effects[i])
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("inverse of effect %d: %v", i, err)
			}
			if effects[i].Kind == core.EffectModify && res.Resource != "npm.endpoint:appnet" {
				t.Errorf("inverse of the endpoint names %q, want npm.endpoint:appnet", res.Resource)
			}
		}
		if nwEndpoint(t, h, user.ID) == nil {
			t.Error("NPM was not reconnected")
		}
		want := []claimRow{{Network: nwNet, Claimant: nwClaimant, ChangeID: "c0"}}
		if got := nwClaims(t, h, nwNet); !slices.Equal(got, want) {
			t.Errorf("claims %+v, want the original claim %+v back", got, want)
		}
	})

	t.Run("a claim of another change is not dropped", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, _ := nwSetup(t)
		nwClaim(t, h, nwNet, nwClaimant, "c9", false)
		e := core.Effect{ChangeID: "c1", Kind: core.EffectCreate, Resource: "npm.claim:appnet|expose:app.example.com", Local: true}
		_, err := h.nm().networkAttach().Handler.Inverse(ctx, h.env("u1"), e)
		_ = nwRefused(t, err, errcode.InvalidInput)
		if got := nwClaims(t, h, nwNet); len(got) != 1 {
			t.Errorf("claims %+v, want the other change's claim kept", got)
		}
	})

	t.Run("NPM stays while another change's claim remains", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, user := nwSetup(t)
		if _, err := nwApplyAttach(t, h, "c1", AttachInput{Network: nwNet, Claimant: nwClaimant, Target: "web"}); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		nwClaim(t, h, nwNet, nwOther, "c2", false)
		endpointEffect := h.journal.effects()[0]
		_, err := h.nm().networkAttach().Handler.Inverse(ctx, h.env("u1"), endpointEffect)
		_ = nwRefused(t, err, errcode.InvalidInput)
		if nwCalls(h, "DisconnectNetwork") != 0 || nwEndpoint(t, h, user.ID) == nil {
			t.Error("the undo disconnected a network another claim needs")
		}
	})
}

func TestNwObserve(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, user := nwSetup(t)
	def := h.nm().networkAttach()
	const res = "npm.claim:appnet|expose:app.example.com"
	creation := core.Effect{ChangeID: "c1", Kind: core.EffectCreate, Resource: "npm.claim:?appnet|expose:app.example.com",
		Recovery: &core.RecoveryID{Kind: "name", Value: "appnet|expose:app.example.com"}, Local: true}

	obs, err := def.Handler.Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), creation)
	if err != nil || obs.Matches != 0 || string(obs.Current.State) != "null" {
		t.Errorf("creation without a row: %+v (%v), want no match and the null state", obs, err)
	}
	nwClaim(t, h, nwNet, nwClaimant, "c1", true)
	obs, err = def.Handler.Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), creation)
	if err != nil || obs.Matches != 1 || obs.Resource != res || !obs.Exclusive {
		t.Fatalf("creation with a row: %+v (%v), want one exclusive match of %s", obs, err, res)
	}
	var doc struct {
		Network     string `json:"network"`
		Claimant    string `json:"claimant"`
		Preexisting bool   `json:"preexisting"`
		ChangeID    string `json:"changeId"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(obs.Current.State, &doc); err != nil || doc.Network != nwNet || doc.Claimant != nwClaimant || !doc.Preexisting || doc.ChangeID != "c1" {
		t.Errorf("claim document %s (%v)", obs.Current.State, err)
	}
	present := core.Effect{ChangeID: "c1", Kind: core.EffectDelete, Resource: res, Local: true}
	again, err := def.Handler.Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), present)
	if err != nil || again.Matches != 1 || string(again.Current.State) != string(obs.Current.State) {
		t.Errorf("existing claim: %+v (%v), want the same document", again, err)
	}
	if err := h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error { return dropClaim(ctx, tx, nwNet, nwClaimant) }); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	gone, err := def.Handler.Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), present)
	if err != nil || gone.Matches != 1 || string(gone.Current.State) != "null" {
		t.Errorf("dropped claim: %+v (%v), want the null state", gone, err)
	}

	endpoint := core.Effect{ChangeID: "c1", Kind: core.EffectModify, Resource: "npm.endpoint:appnet"}
	nwConnect(t, h, user, nil)
	got, err := def.Handler.Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), endpoint)
	want, wantErr := h.nm().observeEngine(ctx, h.env("c1"), endpoint)
	if err != nil || wantErr != nil || !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Errorf("endpoint observation %+v (%v), want the Engine observation %+v (%v)", got, err, want, wantErr)
	}
}

func TestNwDefinition(t *testing.T) {
	h := newTHHost(t)
	n := h.nm()
	attach, detach := n.networkAttach(), n.networkDetach()
	for _, tt := range []struct {
		def       core.ActionDef
		name      string
		converges bool
	}{{attach, "npm.network.attach", false}, {detach, "npm.network.detach", true}} {
		s := tt.def.Spec
		if s.Name != tt.name || s.Version != 1 || s.Class != core.ClassCompensable || s.Side != core.SideAgent ||
			!slices.Equal(s.LockKeys, []string{"npm"}) || s.Converges != tt.converges {
			t.Errorf("%s spec %+v", tt.name, s)
		}
		if tt.def.Handler.Inverse == nil || tt.def.Handler.Observe == nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: inverse %v observe %v, want both", tt.name, tt.def.Handler.Inverse != nil, tt.def.Handler.Observe != nil)
		}
	}
	if _, err := core.NewRegistry(nwModule{defs: []core.ActionDef{attach, detach}}); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("the registry refuses the actions: %v", err)
	}
	v := schema.NewValidator()
	for _, tt := range []struct {
		def   core.ActionDef
		input string
		field string
	}{
		{attach, `{"network":"appnet","claimant":"c","target":"web","address":"10.9.0.2"}`, ""},
		{attach, `{"network":"appnet","claimant":"c","x":1}`, "/x"},
		{detach, `{"network":"","claimant":"c"}`, ""},
		{detach, `{"claimant":"c","x":1}`, "/x"},
	} {
		err := v.ValidateInput(tt.def, json.RawMessage(tt.input))
		var coded *errcode.Error
		switch {
		case tt.field == "" && err != nil:
			t.Errorf("input %s: %v, want it accepted", tt.input, err)
		case tt.field != "" && (!errors.As(err, &coded) || !slices.ContainsFunc(coded.Fields, func(f errcode.FieldError) bool { return f.Path == tt.field })):
			t.Errorf("input %s: %v, want a refusal naming %s", tt.input, err, tt.field)
		}
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `internal/modules/npm/network_claims.go` in package `npm`. Name every file-local identifier with the prefix `network` or `claim` (the constant `networkName` already exists in `deps.go` and must not be redeclared).

1. `func (n nm) networkAttach() core.ActionDef` is `core.DefineMutating[AttachInput, Attachment]`: name `npm.network.attach`, summary "Attach Nginx Proxy Manager to a network.", version 1, `core.ClassCompensable`, `core.SideAgent`, `LockKeys: []string{lockKey}`. `func (n nm) networkDetach() core.ActionDef` is `core.DefineMutating[DetachInput, Attachment]` with the name `npm.network.detach`, summary "Release an attachment claim.", the same class, side and lock, and `Converges: true`. Both handlers embed one struct `networkClaims{n nm}` that supplies the shared `Observe` and `Inverse` methods (the registry finds them on the handler value), so there is one implementation of each. Each handler adds Plan, Apply and Verify.
2. Attach `check(ctx, env, in)` decides everything and changes nothing; every failure is `core.Definite`. In this order:
   - Input shape, each with a `Fields` entry: `network` matches `^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,127}$` (`/network`); `claimant` is 1 to 255 bytes with no whitespace or control character (`/claimant`); a non-empty `target` matches the same pattern as `network` (`/target`); a set `address` must be IPv4 (`/address`).
   - `loadInstance`; no row is `errcode.NotFound` "no Nginx Proxy Manager instance is deployed on this host".
   - `InspectContainer(ctx, containerName)` (the first Engine call): absent is `errcode.NPMUnhealthy` "the NPM container darkpanel-npm does not exist"; `dockerapi.ErrUnavailable` is `errcode.DockerEngineUnavailable` "the Docker Engine is not reachable". Use a file-local helper for that mapping, named with the prefix `network`.
   - `InspectNetwork(ctx, in.Network)`; absent is `errcode.NotFound`. Use the inspected `Name` as the network name from here on.
   - The supported-target rule through `proxy.Target(endpoints, network.Name)`, its error returned unchanged. With a `target`: inspect it (absent is `errcode.NotFound`), call `ListNetworks` once with no filter, and build `endpoints` with one `proxy.Endpoint` per network name in the target's `NetworkSettings.Networks`, `Driver` the listed network's driver (`""` when the endpoint's `NetworkID` is not listed) and `UserDefined` true unless the name is `bridge`, `host` or `none`. Without a `target`: `endpoints` holds only the inspected network, built the same way.
   - The NPM container's endpoint on the network is the one whose `NetworkID` equals the network's id. When `address` is set and the endpoint exists, its IPv4 (`IPAMConfig.IPv4Address` when valid, otherwise `IPAddress`) must equal `address`, else `errcode.InvalidInput` at `/address` naming the address NPM has.
   - The network's claims from `claims(ctx, env.DB.Read(), network.Name)`. The result says whether to connect (the endpoint is absent), whether to add a claim (this claimant has none), and the new claim's `Preexisting`: true when the network has no claim and the endpoint exists; the value shared by the existing claims when there are any; false otherwise.
3. Attach Plan: diff lines in this order, only those that apply: `+` "container darkpanel-npm joins network <network>", `+` "ipv4 <address>" when an address is set and NPM connects, `+` "claim of <claimant> on network <network>" when a claim is added, then, when a claim is added with `Preexisting` true and no claim existed, ` ` "darkpanel-npm is already on network <network>; detaching leaves it there". Nothing to do gives an empty diff. `Guards` is the empty non-nil slice. `StateHash` is the hex SHA-256 of a JSON document holding the network id, the endpoint's id or `""`, the target's id or `""` and the network's claims sorted by claimant (claimant, change id, preexisting).
4. Attach Apply runs `check` again, then, in this order and each only when the check says so:
   - Connect: an effect built by `npmEffect(core.EffectModify, resourceOf(nounEndpoint, network.Name), before, commitWait)` with `before` the `State` of `n.observeEngine` on an `intended` effect of the same resource, run with `n.run(ctx, env, e, n.observeEngine, do)`; `do` calls `ConnectNetwork(ctx, network.ID, container.ID, settings)` where `settings` is nil without an address and an `EndpointSettings` whose `IPAMConfig.IPv4Address` is the address with one, and returns the effect's resource.
   - Claim: an effect built by `npmEffect(core.EffectCreate, resourceOf(nounClaim, "?"+network+"|"+claimant), nullState().State, 0)` with `Local` and `InverseLocal` set to true and `After` the claim document, run with `n.run(ctx, env, e, h.Observe, do)`; `do` adds the row with `addClaim` (change id `env.ChangeID`) in one `env.DB.Write` and returns `npm.claim:<network>|<claimant>`.
   The output is `Attachment{Network, Claimant, Preexisting, Attached: true}`, with the claim's own `Preexisting` (the existing claim's when the claimant already had one).
5. Attach Verify, from `in` alone: NPM's container is on the network and a claim of the claimant exists on it; otherwise an error naming what is missing.
6. Detach `check` validates `claimant` as above (`/claimant`) and `network` when it is not empty, loads the claims to remove (the claimant's claim on `network`, or with `network == ""` every claim of the claimant, read with a direct `SELECT network, claimant, change_id, preexisting FROM npm_attachments WHERE claimant = ? ORDER BY network`), and for each decides: the other claims of that network (different claimant) are `others`; NPM is disconnected exactly when `others` is empty, the removed claim's `Preexisting` is false and the NPM container is currently on the network. A missing NPM container, or a network that no longer exists, counts as "not on the network"; it is not an error. No claim to remove is an empty plan, not an error.
7. Detach Plan: per claim in network-name order, `-` "claim of <claimant> on network <network>", then `-` "container darkpanel-npm leaves network <network>" when NPM is disconnected, else ` ` "darkpanel-npm stays on network <network>: claimed by <other claimants sorted, joined by \", \">" when `others` is not empty, else ` ` "darkpanel-npm stays on network <network>: it was there before" when the claim is preexisting. `Guards` is the empty non-nil slice; `StateHash` hashes the same document as attach over the networks involved.
8. Detach Apply runs `check` again and handles the claims in network-name order, the disconnect before the claim removal (so a failed disconnect leaves the claim and a repeat finishes the work). The disconnect is the endpoint effect: `npmEffect(core.EffectModify, ...)` for `npm.endpoint:<network>` with `before` read by `n.observeEngine` and `InverseRecovery` set to `&core.RecoveryID{Kind: "name", Value: "darkpanel-npm@" + network}`, `do` calling `DisconnectNetwork(ctx, network.ID, container.ID, false)`. The claim removal is an effect `npmEffect(core.EffectDelete, resourceOf(nounClaim, network+"|"+claimant), claimDocument, 0)` with `Local` and `InverseLocal` true and `After` the null state, `do` deleting the row with `dropClaim`. The output is `Attachment{Network: in.Network, Claimant, Preexisting: true when any removed claim was preexisting, Attached: true when NPM is still on any network the call touched}`; an empty plan returns `Attachment{Network: in.Network, Claimant}`. Detach Verify, from `in` alone: no claim of the claimant remains (on `in.Network`, or anywhere when it is empty).
9. Shared `Observe(ctx, env, e)` switches on the noun of `e.Resource`. For `nounEndpoint` it returns `n.observeEngine(ctx, env, e)`. For `nounClaim` it takes the identity (cut the leading `?` of a creation), splits at the first `|` into network and claimant, reads the claim with `claims`, and returns: the claim document as `Current.State` (`null` when there is no row); for a creation `Matches` 1 with `Resource` `npm.claim:<network>|<claimant>` when the row exists and `Matches` 0 when it does not; for a non-creation `Matches` 1 either way; `Exclusive` true. Any other noun is an error.
10. Shared `Inverse(ctx, env, e)` switches on the noun. Claim, created by `e.ChangeID`: delete the row; no row is done; a row whose `ChangeID` differs is `core.Definite(errcode.InvalidInput ...)` and nothing is deleted. Claim, deleted: re-add the row from `e.Before.State` with its original change id and `Preexisting`; an equal row already there is done, a different one is a Definite `errcode.InvalidInput`. Endpoint effect without `InverseRecovery` (the undo of a connect): refuse with a Definite `errcode.InvalidInput` when any claim on the network belongs to a change other than `e.ChangeID`; otherwise `DisconnectNetwork(ctx, network, containerName, false)`, a not-found answer being done and any other 4xx answer `core.Definite`. Endpoint effect with `InverseRecovery` (the undo of a disconnect): `ConnectNetwork(ctx, network, containerName, nil)` and return `core.InverseResult{Resource: resourceOf(nounEndpoint, network)}`, a 4xx answer being `core.Definite`. The network is the identity of `e.Resource`.
