### Task 16: npm: proxyhost.add with reservation rules

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/modules/npm/proxyhost_add.go`
- Test: `internal/modules/npm/proxyhost_add_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Contracts "NPM wire shapes" (`ProxyHostInput`, `ProxyHostView`); "NPM API client" (`npmapi.ProxyHost`, `npmapi.ProxyHostBody`, `npmapi.Location`, `npmapi.APIError`, `IsNotFound`, `IsClientError`, the client methods through `npmAPI`); "Proxy rules" (`proxy.Location`, `proxy.Reserved`, `proxy.CheckUpstream`); "NPM store" (`reservedOf`, `addReserved`, `addWatch`, `watched`, `reservedRow`, the table `npm_reserved`); "NPM deps" (`deps.api`, `deps.panel`); "NPM shared internals" (`lockKey`, `commitWait`, `containerName`, `nounProxyHost`, `resourceOf`, `identityOf`, `npmEffect`, `(nm).run`, `nullState`, `named`); "NPM identities" (`npm.proxyhost:<id>`, `Observe`, the `Resolver`); "NPM controller reads and composites" (the `ParentAction` rules); "NPM test host" and the fake NPM server of Contracts "NPM API client" (`fake.Server`, `fake.Fault`, the `Seed*` helpers).
- Produces: `func (n nm) proxyhostAdd() core.ActionDef` (Contracts "NPM actions", Task 16) and the helpers Contracts "NPM shared internals" names for this task, with exactly these signatures: `func proxyHostBody(in ProxyHostInput, parent string) npmapi.ProxyHostBody`, `func proxyHostState(h npmapi.ProxyHost) json.RawMessage`, `func validateProxyHostInput(in ProxyHostInput) error`, `func (n nm) checkUpstream(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in ProxyHostInput) error`; Task 20 reuses them. Task 16 also declares `func proxyHostCreatedOn(s string) (time.Time, error)`, which parses NPM's `created_on` (RFC 3339, or `2006-01-02 15:04:05` read as UTC), and the handler type `proxyhostAddHandler`.

**Items:** 30

**Depends on:** Task 7, Task 11
**Implementer:** dforge:impl-opus-high
**Evaluation:** files 1 - spec 2 - coupling 2 - risk 3 = 8
**Lenses:** security

`npm.proxyhost.add` creates a proxy host through NPM's API. It is where the reservation rules of spec 6 are enforced for creation: an upstream NPM must never be pointed at on a user's say-so (NPM's own admin port, loopback, the panel network and gateway) is refused with `npm.reserved` unless the change is a child of the composite that owns it, read from `Env.Call.ParentAction`. When the parent is `npm.admin.expose` or `panel.exposure.add`, the action also records what it created in `npm_reserved`.

Decisions this task embodies:
- Domains must be lowercase. Domain sets are compared and journaled sorted, joined by `,`.
- The identity of a creation is `npm.proxyhost:?<sorted domains joined by ",">` with recovery `name` of that value, `CommitWait` `commitWait`. `Observe` finds a creation by the exact domain set and a `created_on` not before the intent; the intent time is `CommitBy` minus `CommitWait` of the effect, truncated to the second, because NPM's `created_on` is.
- A `ForwardHost` that is a name is not resolved; only IP literals and `localhost` can match a reserved address.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `internal/modules/npm/proxyhost_add_test.go`:

```go
package npm

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"net/netip"
	"reflect"
	"slices"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/schema"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/npm/fake"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/npm/npmapi"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/npm/proxy"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/platform/dockerapi"
)

const (
	paAdmin = "npm.admin.expose"
	paPanel = "panel.exposure.add"
)

// paModule registers one action, so the registry's own checks run on it.
type paModule struct{ def core.ActionDef }

func (m paModule) Manifest() core.Manifest {
	return core.Manifest{ID: ID, Version: 1, Actions: []core.ActionDef{m.def}}
}

// paRefused asserts err is a definite refusal with the given code and returns it.
func paRefused(t *testing.T, err error, code errcode.Code) *errcode.Error {
	t.Helper()
	var coded *errcode.Error
	if !errors.As(err, &coded) || coded.Code != code {
		t.Fatalf("error %v, want code %s", err, code)
	}
	if !core.IsDefinite(err) {
		t.Errorf("error %v is not marked definite", err)
	}
	return coded
}

func paJSON(t *testing.T, v any) json.RawMessage {
	t.Helper()
	b, err := json.Marshal(v)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return b
}

// paHost is a deployed instance whose container has the address 172.18.0.2
// and a module whose panel network is 172.31.255.0/29 with gateway .1.
func paHost(t *testing.T) (*thHost, nm) {
	t.Helper()
	h := newTHHost(t)
	h.deployed()
	c, ok := h.docker.container(containerName)
	if !ok {
		t.Fatal("deployed() left no container")
	}
	c.NetworkSettings.Networks = map[string]*dockerapi.EndpointSettings{
		networkName: {NetworkID: "n1", IPAddress: netip.MustParseAddr("172.18.0.2")},
	}
	h.docker.putContainer(c)
	n := h.nm()
	n.d.panel = func(context.Context, core.Env) (proxy.Reserved, error) {
		return proxy.Reserved{
			PanelNetwork: netip.MustParsePrefix("172.31.255.0/29"),
			PanelGateway: netip.MustParseAddr("172.31.255.1"),
		}, nil
	}
	return h, n
}

func paEnv(h *thHost, change, parent string) core.Env {
	if parent == "" {
		return h.env(change)
	}
	return h.child(change, parent)
}

func paInput(domains ...string) ProxyHostInput {
	return ProxyHostInput{Domains: domains, ForwardScheme: "http", ForwardHost: "web", ForwardPort: 8080}
}

func paApply(t *testing.T, h *thHost, n nm, change, parent string, in ProxyHostInput) (ProxyHostView, error) {
	t.Helper()
	h.addChange(change, "npm.proxyhost.add")
	raw, err := n.proxyhostAdd().Handler.Apply(context.Background(), paEnv(h, change, parent), paJSON(t, in))
	if err != nil {
		return ProxyHostView{}, err
	}
	var out ProxyHostView
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &out); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return out, nil
}

func TestPaValidate(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name   string
		mutate func(in *ProxyHostInput)
		field  string // "" accepted, "*" refused at any field
	}{
		{name: "valid"},
		{name: "wildcard domain", mutate: func(in *ProxyHostInput) { in.Domains = []string{"*.example.com"} }},
		{name: "https with certificate and forced ssl", mutate: func(in *ProxyHostInput) {
			in.ForwardScheme, in.CertificateID, in.SSLForced, in.HSTS = "https", 3, true, true
		}},
		{name: "no domains", mutate: func(in *ProxyHostInput) { in.Domains = nil }, field: "/domains"},
		{name: "uppercase domain", mutate: func(in *ProxyHostInput) { in.Domains = []string{"A.example.com"} }, field: "/domains/0"},
		{name: "domain with a path", mutate: func(in *ProxyHostInput) { in.Domains = []string{"a.example.com/x"} }, field: "/domains/0"},
		{name: "single label domain", mutate: func(in *ProxyHostInput) { in.Domains = []string{"localhost"} }, field: "/domains/0"},
		{name: "duplicate domain", mutate: func(in *ProxyHostInput) { in.Domains = []string{"a.example.com", "a.example.com"} }, field: "/domains/1"},
		{name: "scheme", mutate: func(in *ProxyHostInput) { in.ForwardScheme = "ftp" }, field: "/forwardScheme"},
		{name: "empty host", mutate: func(in *ProxyHostInput) { in.ForwardHost = "" }, field: "/forwardHost"},
		{name: "host with a scheme", mutate: func(in *ProxyHostInput) { in.ForwardHost = "http://web" }, field: "/forwardHost"},
		{name: "port zero", mutate: func(in *ProxyHostInput) { in.ForwardPort = 0 }, field: "/forwardPort"},
		{name: "port too high", mutate: func(in *ProxyHostInput) { in.ForwardPort = 65536 }, field: "/forwardPort"},
		{name: "forced ssl without a certificate", mutate: func(in *ProxyHostInput) { in.SSLForced = true }, field: "*"},
		{name: "hsts without forced ssl", mutate: func(in *ProxyHostInput) { in.CertificateID, in.HSTS = 3, true }, field: "*"},
		{name: "negative access list", mutate: func(in *ProxyHostInput) { in.AccessListID = -1 }, field: "*"},
		{name: "negative certificate", mutate: func(in *ProxyHostInput) { in.CertificateID = -1 }, field: "*"},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			in := paInput("a.example.com")
			if tt.mutate != nil {
				tt.mutate(&in)
			}
			err := validateProxyHostInput(in)
			if tt.field == "" {
				if err != nil {
					t.Fatalf("refused: %v", err)
				}
				return
			}
			coded := paRefused(t, err, errcode.InvalidInput)
			if tt.field != "*" && !slices.ContainsFunc(coded.Fields, func(f errcode.FieldError) bool { return f.Path == tt.field }) {
				t.Errorf("fields %v, want one at %s", coded.Fields, tt.field)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestPaBody(t *testing.T) {
	in := ProxyHostInput{
		Domains: []string{"b.example.com", "a.example.com"}, ForwardScheme: "https", ForwardHost: "172.31.255.1", ForwardPort: 9443,
		CertificateID: 3, AccessListID: 2, SSLForced: true, HSTS: true, Websockets: true,
	}
	for _, tt := range []struct {
		parent string
		panel  bool
	}{{"", false}, {paAdmin, false}, {paPanel, true}} {
		want := npmapi.ProxyHostBody{
			DomainNames: []string{"a.example.com", "b.example.com"}, ForwardScheme: "https", ForwardHost: "172.31.255.1", ForwardPort: 9443,
			CertificateID: 3, AccessListID: 2, SSLForced: true, HSTSEnabled: true, AllowWebsocketUpgrade: true,
			Locations: []npmapi.Location{proxy.Location("https", "172.31.255.1", 9443, tt.panel)},
		}
		if got := proxyHostBody(in, tt.parent); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
			t.Errorf("parent %q: body %+v, want %+v", tt.parent, got, want)
		}
	}
	if got := proxyHostBody(in, paPanel).Locations[0].AdvancedConfig; !strings.Contains(got, "proxy_read_timeout 3600s;") {
		t.Errorf("panel location %q lacks the read timeout", got)
	}
	if got := proxyHostBody(in, "").Locations[0].AdvancedConfig; strings.Contains(got, "proxy_read_timeout") {
		t.Errorf("ordinary location %q has a read timeout", got)
	}
}

func TestPaState(t *testing.T) {
	host := npmapi.ProxyHost{
		ID: 9, CreatedOn: "2026-11-02T10:00:00.000Z", ModifiedOn: "2026-11-02T10:00:01.000Z",
		DomainNames: []string{"b.example.com", "a.example.com"}, ForwardScheme: "http", ForwardHost: "web", ForwardPort: 8080,
		CertificateID: 3, AccessListID: 2, SSLForced: true, HSTSEnabled: true,
		Locations: []npmapi.Location{{Path: "/", ForwardScheme: "http", ForwardHost: "web", ForwardPort: 8080, AdvancedConfig: "x"}},
	}
	var got map[string]any
	if err := json.Unmarshal(proxyHostState(host), &got); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := map[string]any{
		"domains": []any{"a.example.com", "b.example.com"}, "forwardScheme": "http", "forwardHost": "web", "forwardPort": float64(8080),
		"certificateId": float64(3), "accessListId": float64(2), "sslForced": true, "hsts": true, "websockets": false,
		"locations": []any{map[string]any{
			"path": "/", "forwardScheme": "http", "forwardHost": "web", "forwardPort": float64(8080), "advancedConfig": "x",
		}},
		"advancedConfig": "",
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Errorf("state %v, want %v", got, want)
	}
	host.Locations = nil
	if err := json.Unmarshal(proxyHostState(host), &got); err != nil || !reflect.DeepEqual(got["locations"], []any{}) {
		t.Errorf("a host without locations: locations %v (%v), want an empty array", got["locations"], err)
	}
}

func TestPaCreatedOn(t *testing.T) {
	want := time.Date(2026, 11, 2, 10, 0, 1, 0, time.UTC)
	for _, s := range []string{"2026-11-02T10:00:01Z", "2026-11-02T10:00:01.000Z", "2026-11-02 10:00:01"} {
		if got, err := proxyHostCreatedOn(s); err != nil || !got.Equal(want) {
			t.Errorf("%q parses to %v (%v), want %v", s, got, err, want)
		}
	}
	if _, err := proxyHostCreatedOn("yesterday"); err == nil {
		t.Error("an unparseable time was accepted")
	}
}

func TestPaPlan(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	t.Run("diff", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, n := paHost(t)
		cert := h.npm.SeedCertificate(npmapi.Certificate{NiceName: "c", Provider: "letsencrypt", DomainNames: []string{"a.example.com"}})
		list := h.npm.SeedAccessList(npmapi.AccessList{Name: "al"})
		plain, err := n.proxyhostAdd().Handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), paJSON(t, paInput("b.example.com", "a.example.com")))
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		wantPlain := []core.DiffLine{
			{Op: "+", Text: "proxy host a.example.com, b.example.com"},
			{Op: " ", Text: "forward http://web:8080"},
		}
		if !slices.Equal(plain.Diff, wantPlain) || plain.StateHash == "" {
			t.Errorf("diff %v hash %q, want %v and a hash", plain.Diff, plain.StateHash, wantPlain)
		}
		full := ProxyHostInput{
			Domains: []string{"a.example.com"}, ForwardScheme: "https", ForwardHost: "web", ForwardPort: 8443,
			CertificateID: cert.ID, AccessListID: list.ID, SSLForced: true, HSTS: true, Websockets: true,
		}
		out, err := n.proxyhostAdd().Handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), paJSON(t, full))
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		wantFull := []core.DiffLine{
			{Op: "+", Text: "proxy host a.example.com"},
			{Op: " ", Text: "forward https://web:8443"},
			{Op: " ", Text: "certificate " + strconv.Itoa(cert.ID)},
			{Op: " ", Text: "access list " + strconv.Itoa(list.ID)},
			{Op: " ", Text: "force SSL"},
			{Op: " ", Text: "HSTS"},
			{Op: " ", Text: "websockets"},
		}
		if !slices.Equal(out.Diff, wantFull) {
			t.Errorf("diff %v, want %v", out.Diff, wantFull)
		}
		if len(h.npm.ProxyHosts()) != 0 {
			t.Error("a plan created a proxy host")
		}
		again, err := n.proxyhostAdd().Handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), paJSON(t, paInput("b.example.com", "a.example.com")))
		if err != nil || again.StateHash != plain.StateHash {
			t.Errorf("a second plan hashes %q (%v), want %q", again.StateHash, err, plain.StateHash)
		}
		other, err := n.proxyhostAdd().Handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), paJSON(t, paInput("c.example.com")))
		if err != nil || other.StateHash == plain.StateHash {
			t.Errorf("another domain hashes %q (%v), want a different hash", other.StateHash, err)
		}
	})

	t.Run("refusals", func(t *testing.T) {
		tests := []struct {
			name  string
			setup func(h *thHost)
			in    ProxyHostInput
			field string
		}{
			{name: "invalid input", in: ProxyHostInput{}, field: "/domains"},
			{name: "domain already has a proxy host", setup: func(h *thHost) {
				h.npm.SeedProxyHost(npmapi.ProxyHost{DomainNames: []string{"x.example.com", "a.example.com"}, ForwardScheme: "http", ForwardHost: "old", ForwardPort: 80})
			}, in: paInput("b.example.com", "a.example.com"), field: "/domains/1"},
			{name: "certificate missing", in: func() ProxyHostInput {
				in := paInput("a.example.com")
				in.CertificateID = 99
				return in
			}()},
			{name: "access list missing", in: func() ProxyHostInput {
				in := paInput("a.example.com")
				in.AccessListID = 99
				return in
			}()},
		}
		for _, tt := range tests {
			t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
				h, n := paHost(t)
				if tt.setup != nil {
					tt.setup(h)
				}
				_, err := n.proxyhostAdd().Handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), paJSON(t, tt.in))
				coded := paRefused(t, err, errcode.InvalidInput)
				if tt.field != "" && !slices.ContainsFunc(coded.Fields, func(f errcode.FieldError) bool { return f.Path == tt.field }) {
					t.Errorf("fields %v, want one at %s", coded.Fields, tt.field)
				}
			})
		}
	})
}

func TestPaReservedUpstreams(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n := paHost(t)
	tests := []struct {
		host    string
		port    int
		parent  string
		refused bool
	}{
		{"127.0.0.1", 81, "", true},
		{"localhost", 81, "", true},
		{"172.18.0.2", 81, "", true},
		{"172.31.255.1", 9443, "", true},
		{"172.31.255.2", 9443, "", true},
		{"127.0.0.1", 81, paAdmin, false},
		{"172.18.0.2", 81, paAdmin, false},
		{"127.0.0.1", 81, paPanel, true},
		{"172.31.255.1", 9443, paPanel, false},
		{"172.31.255.2", 9443, paPanel, false},
		{"172.31.255.1", 9443, paAdmin, true},
		{"10.0.0.5", 81, "", false},
		{"web", 8080, "", false},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(fmt.Sprintf("%s:%d as child of %q", tt.host, tt.port, tt.parent), func(t *testing.T) {
			in := paInput("x.example.com")
			in.ForwardHost, in.ForwardPort = tt.host, tt.port
			_, err := n.proxyhostAdd().Handler.Plan(ctx, paEnv(h, "", tt.parent), paJSON(t, in))
			if tt.refused {
				_ = paRefused(t, err, errcode.NPMReserved)
				return
			}
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("refused: %v", err)
			}
		})
	}
	in := paInput("x.example.com")
	in.ForwardHost, in.ForwardPort = "127.0.0.1", 81
	_, err := paApply(t, h, n, "c1", "", in)
	_ = paRefused(t, err, errcode.NPMReserved)
	if len(h.npm.ProxyHosts()) != 0 || len(h.journal.effects()) != 0 {
		t.Error("a refused apply created a proxy host or journaled an effect")
	}
}

func TestPaApply(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()

	t.Run("creates the proxy host", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, n := paHost(t)
		out, err := paApply(t, h, n, "c1", "", paInput("b.example.com", "a.example.com"))
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		hosts := h.npm.ProxyHosts()
		if len(hosts) != 1 {
			t.Fatalf("%d proxy hosts, want 1", len(hosts))
		}
		created := hosts[0]
		if !slices.Equal(created.DomainNames, []string{"a.example.com", "b.example.com"}) || created.ForwardHost != "web" || created.ForwardPort != 8080 ||
			len(created.Locations) != 1 || created.Locations[0] != proxy.Location("http", "web", 8080, false) {
			t.Errorf("created %+v, want the domains sorted, the target and the custom location", created)
		}
		if out.ID != created.ID || !slices.Equal(out.Domains, []string{"a.example.com", "b.example.com"}) || out.ForwardHost != "web" || out.Reserved != "" {
			t.Errorf("output %+v, want the created host unreserved", out)
		}
		effects := h.journal.effects()
		if len(effects) != 1 {
			t.Fatalf("%d effects, want 1", len(effects))
		}
		e := effects[0]
		if e.Kind != core.EffectCreate || e.Resource != "npm.proxyhost:"+strconv.Itoa(created.ID) || e.State != core.EffectMade || e.Local || e.CommitWait != commitWait {
			t.Errorf("effect %+v, want a made non-local create of npm.proxyhost:%d with commit wait %s", e, created.ID, commitWait)
		}
		if e.Recovery == nil || e.Recovery.Kind != "name" || e.Recovery.Value != "a.example.com,b.example.com" || string(e.Before.State) != "null" {
			t.Errorf("recovery %+v before %s, want the sorted domain set and the null state", e.Recovery, e.Before.State)
		}
		if e.Post == nil || string(e.Post.State) != string(proxyHostState(created)) {
			t.Errorf("post %+v, want the proxy host's state document", e.Post)
		}
		if rows := reservedCount(t, h); rows != 0 {
			t.Errorf("npm_reserved holds %d rows, want none for an unreserved change", rows)
		}
	})

	t.Run("child of the admin exposure records its objects", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, n := paHost(t)
		cert := h.npm.SeedCertificate(npmapi.Certificate{NiceName: "darkpanel-c0-1", Provider: "letsencrypt", DomainNames: []string{"npm.example.com"}})
		in := ProxyHostInput{
			Domains: []string{"npm.example.com"}, ForwardScheme: "http", ForwardHost: "127.0.0.1", ForwardPort: 81,
			CertificateID: cert.ID, SSLForced: true, HSTS: true,
		}
		out, err := paApply(t, h, n, "c1", paAdmin, in)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if out.Reserved != paAdmin {
			t.Errorf("output reserved %q, want %s", out.Reserved, paAdmin)
		}
		for kind, id := range map[string]int{"proxy_host": out.ID, "certificate": cert.ID} {
			row, ok, err := reservedOf(ctx, h.db.Read(), kind, id)
			want := reservedRow{Kind: kind, ObjectID: id, Owner: paAdmin, Exposure: "npm.example.com", ChangeID: "c1"}
			if err != nil || !ok || row != want {
				t.Errorf("reserved %s %d: %+v (found %v, %v), want %+v", kind, id, row, ok, err, want)
			}
		}
	})

	t.Run("child of the panel exposure sets the read timeout", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, n := paHost(t)
		in := ProxyHostInput{Domains: []string{"panel.example.com"}, ForwardScheme: "https", ForwardHost: "172.31.255.1", ForwardPort: 9443}
		out, err := paApply(t, h, n, "c1", paPanel, in)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		want := "proxy_set_header X-Darkpanel-Client $realip_remote_addr;\nproxy_read_timeout 3600s;"
		if got := h.npm.ProxyHosts()[0].Locations[0].AdvancedConfig; got != want {
			t.Errorf("location configuration %q, want %q", got, want)
		}
		row, ok, err := reservedOf(ctx, h.db.Read(), "proxy_host", out.ID)
		if err != nil || !ok || row.Owner != paPanel || row.Exposure != "panel.example.com" {
			t.Errorf("reserved row %+v (found %v, %v), want owner %s and exposure panel.example.com", row, ok, err, paPanel)
		}
	})

	t.Run("NPM refuses", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, n := paHost(t)
		h.npm.Fail("POST", "/api/nginx/proxy-hosts", fake.Fault{Status: 400})
		_, err := paApply(t, h, n, "c1", paAdmin, ProxyHostInput{Domains: []string{"npm.example.com"}, ForwardScheme: "http", ForwardHost: "127.0.0.1", ForwardPort: 81})
		_ = paRefused(t, err, errcode.InvalidInput)
		if effects := h.journal.effects(); len(effects) != 1 || effects[0].State != core.EffectSkipped {
			t.Errorf("effects %+v, want one skipped", effects)
		}
		if rows := reservedCount(t, h); rows != 0 {
			t.Errorf("npm_reserved holds %d rows after a refusal", rows)
		}
	})

	t.Run("NPM fails", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, n := paHost(t)
		h.npm.Fail("POST", "/api/nginx/proxy-hosts", fake.Fault{Status: 500})
		_, err := paApply(t, h, n, "c1", "", paInput("a.example.com"))
		if err == nil || core.IsDefinite(err) {
			t.Fatalf("error %v, want an uncertain failure", err)
		}
		if effects := h.journal.effects(); len(effects) != 1 || effects[0].State != core.EffectIntended {
			t.Errorf("effects %+v, want one left intended", effects)
		}
	})
}

func reservedCount(t *testing.T, h *thHost) int {
	t.Helper()
	var n int
	if err := h.db.Read().QueryRowContext(context.Background(), "SELECT count(*) FROM npm_reserved").Scan(&n); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return n
}

// TestPaLostResponse: NPM commits the proxy host and the answer is lost; the
// effect stays intended and Observe finds the host by its domain set.
func TestPaLostResponse(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n := paHost(t)
	h.npm.Fail("POST", "/api/nginx/proxy-hosts", fake.Fault{AfterCommit: true})
	_, err := paApply(t, h, n, "c1", "", paInput("b.example.com", "a.example.com"))
	if err == nil || core.IsDefinite(err) {
		t.Fatalf("error %v, want an uncertain failure", err)
	}
	hosts := h.npm.ProxyHosts()
	if len(hosts) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("%d proxy hosts, want the committed one", len(hosts))
	}
	e := h.journal.effects()[0]
	if e.State != core.EffectIntended || e.Resource != "npm.proxyhost:?a.example.com,b.example.com" {
		t.Fatalf("effect %+v, want one intended creation of the domain set", e)
	}
	created, err := proxyHostCreatedOn(hosts[0].CreatedOn)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	def := n.proxyhostAdd()

	e.CommitBy = created.Add(-time.Second).Add(e.CommitWait)
	obs, err := def.Handler.Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), e)
	wantResource := "npm.proxyhost:" + strconv.Itoa(hosts[0].ID)
	if err != nil || obs.Matches != 1 || obs.Resource != wantResource || !obs.Exclusive || string(obs.Current.State) != string(proxyHostState(hosts[0])) {
		t.Errorf("observation %+v (%v), want one exclusive match of %s", obs, err, wantResource)
	}

	e.CommitBy = created.Add(time.Hour).Add(e.CommitWait)
	obs, err = def.Handler.Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), e)
	if err != nil || obs.Matches != 0 || string(obs.Current.State) != "null" {
		t.Errorf("a host created before the intent: %+v (%v), want no match and the null state", obs, err)
	}

	e.CommitBy = created.Add(-time.Second).Add(e.CommitWait)
	e.Resource, e.Recovery = "npm.proxyhost:?a.example.com", &core.RecoveryID{Kind: "name", Value: "a.example.com"}
	obs, err = def.Handler.Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), e)
	if err != nil || obs.Matches != 0 {
		t.Errorf("a subset of the domains: %+v (%v), want no match, the domain set being exact", obs, err)
	}
}

func TestPaObserveMade(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n := paHost(t)
	out, err := paApply(t, h, n, "c1", "", paInput("a.example.com"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	def := n.proxyhostAdd()
	e := core.Effect{ChangeID: "c1", Kind: core.EffectCreate, Resource: "npm.proxyhost:" + strconv.Itoa(out.ID), State: core.EffectMade}
	obs, err := def.Handler.Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), e)
	if err != nil || obs.Matches != 1 || string(obs.Current.State) != string(proxyHostState(h.npm.ProxyHosts()[0])) {
		t.Errorf("observation %+v (%v), want the host's state document", obs, err)
	}
	e.Resource = "npm.proxyhost:999"
	obs, err = def.Handler.Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), e)
	if err != nil || obs.Matches != 1 || string(obs.Current.State) != "null" {
		t.Errorf("absent host: %+v (%v), want the null state", obs, err)
	}
}

func TestPaInverse(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	admin := ProxyHostInput{Domains: []string{"npm.example.com"}, ForwardScheme: "http", ForwardHost: "127.0.0.1", ForwardPort: 81}

	t.Run("deletes the host and drops its reservations", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, n := paHost(t)
		cert := h.npm.SeedCertificate(npmapi.Certificate{NiceName: "c", Provider: "letsencrypt", DomainNames: []string{"npm.example.com"}})
		in := admin
		in.CertificateID, in.SSLForced = cert.ID, true
		out, err := paApply(t, h, n, "c1", paAdmin, in)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if _, err := n.proxyhostAdd().Handler.Inverse(ctx, h.env("u1"), h.journal.effects()[0]); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if len(h.npm.ProxyHosts()) != 0 {
			t.Error("the proxy host is still there")
		}
		if _, ok, _ := reservedOf(ctx, h.db.Read(), "proxy_host", out.ID); ok {
			t.Error("the proxy host is still reserved")
		}
		if _, ok, _ := reservedOf(ctx, h.db.Read(), "certificate", cert.ID); ok {
			t.Error("the certificate is still reserved")
		}
	})

	t.Run("a host that is already gone is done", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, n := paHost(t)
		if _, err := paApply(t, h, n, "c1", "", paInput("a.example.com")); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		e := h.journal.effects()[0]
		def := n.proxyhostAdd()
		if _, err := def.Handler.Inverse(ctx, h.env("u1"), e); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if _, err := def.Handler.Inverse(ctx, h.env("u1"), e); err != nil {
			t.Errorf("a second inverse: %v", err)
		}
	})

	t.Run("NPM refuses the delete", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, n := paHost(t)
		out, err := paApply(t, h, n, "c1", "", paInput("a.example.com"))
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		h.npm.Fail("DELETE", "/api/nginx/proxy-hosts/"+strconv.Itoa(out.ID), fake.Fault{Status: 403})
		_, err = n.proxyhostAdd().Handler.Inverse(ctx, h.env("u1"), h.journal.effects()[0])
		if err == nil || !core.IsDefinite(err) {
			t.Errorf("error %v, want a definite refusal", err)
		}
		if len(h.npm.ProxyHosts()) != 1 {
			t.Error("the proxy host was deleted despite the refusal")
		}
	})

	t.Run("NPM fails", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, n := paHost(t)
		out, err := paApply(t, h, n, "c1", "", paInput("a.example.com"))
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		h.npm.Fail("DELETE", "/api/nginx/proxy-hosts/"+strconv.Itoa(out.ID), fake.Fault{Status: 500})
		_, err = n.proxyhostAdd().Handler.Inverse(ctx, h.env("u1"), h.journal.effects()[0])
		if err == nil || core.IsDefinite(err) {
			t.Errorf("error %v, want an uncertain failure", err)
		}
	})
}

func TestPaVerify(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n := paHost(t)
	def := n.proxyhostAdd()
	in := paJSON(t, paInput("a.example.com"))

		t.Fatal(err)

}

func TestPaResolved(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h := newTHHost(t)
	def := h.nm().proxyhostAdd()
	if def.Handler.Resolved == nil {
		t.Fatal("the action has no resolver")
	}
	creation := func(state core.EffectState) core.Effect {
		return core.Effect{
			ChangeID: "c1", Seq: 2, Kind: core.EffectCreate, Resource: "npm.proxyhost:?a.example.com,b.example.com",
			Recovery: &core.RecoveryID{Kind: "name", Value: "a.example.com,b.example.com"}, State: state,
		}
	}
	resolve := func(state core.EffectState, resolution string) {
		t.Helper()
		err := h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error { return def.Handler.Resolved(ctx, tx, creation(state), resolution) })
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	resolve(core.EffectMade, "reconcile")
	resolve(core.EffectAccepted, "accept")
	if got, err := watched(ctx, h.db.Read(), "proxy_host"); err != nil || len(got) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("watched %v (%v) after a made and an accepted creation, want none", got, err)
	}
	resolve(core.EffectSkipped, "reconcile")
	got, err := watched(ctx, h.db.Read(), "proxy_host")
	if err != nil || !got["a.example.com,b.example.com"] {
		t.Errorf("watched %v (%v), want the skipped creation's domain set", got, err)
	}
}

func TestPaDefinition(t *testing.T) {
	_, n := paHost(t)
	def := n.proxyhostAdd()
	s := def.Spec
	if s.Name != "npm.proxyhost.add" || s.Version != 1 || s.Class != core.ClassCompensable || s.Side != core.SideAgent ||
		!slices.Equal(s.LockKeys, []string{"npm"}) {
		t.Errorf("spec %+v", s)
	}
	if def.Handler.Inverse == nil || def.Handler.Observe == nil || def.Handler.Resolved == nil {
		t.Errorf("inverse %v observe %v resolved %v, want all three", def.Handler.Inverse != nil, def.Handler.Observe != nil, def.Handler.Resolved != nil)
	}
	if _, err := core.NewRegistry(paModule{def}); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("the registry refuses the action: %v", err)
	}
	v := schema.NewValidator()
	valid := paJSON(t, paInput("a.example.com"))
	if err := v.ValidateInput(def, valid); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("a valid input was refused: %v", err)
	}
	var withExtra map[string]any
	if err := json.Unmarshal(valid, &withExtra); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	withExtra["x"] = 1
	err := v.ValidateInput(def, paJSON(t, withExtra))
	var coded *errcode.Error
	if !errors.As(err, &coded) || !slices.ContainsFunc(coded.Fields, func(f errcode.FieldError) bool { return f.Path == "/x" }) {
		t.Errorf("an unknown field: %v, want a refusal naming /x", err)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `internal/modules/npm/proxyhost_add.go` in package `npm`. Name every file-local identifier beyond the ones above with the prefix `proxyHost` or `proxyhostAdd`; Task 20 and Task 19 declare their own helpers in the same package.

1. `validateProxyHostInput(in)` returns nil or a `core.Definite` `*errcode.Error` of code `errcode.InvalidInput` whose `Detail` is the first problem and whose `Fields` hold the JSON-pointer path of each problem (`/domains`, `/domains/<i>`, `/forwardScheme`, `/forwardHost`, `/forwardPort`, `/sslForced`, `/hsts`, `/certificateId`, `/accessListId`). Rules: at least one domain; each domain is lowercase, at most 253 bytes, and either `*.` followed by or just dot-separated labels of letters, digits and `-` (a label starts and ends with a letter or digit, at most 63 bytes, at least two labels, the last starting with a letter); no duplicate (the path names the later one); `ForwardScheme` is `http` or `https`; `ForwardHost` is an IP literal or a name of letters, digits, `_`, `.` and `-` starting with a letter or digit, at most 253 bytes; `ForwardPort` is 1 to 65535; `CertificateID` and `AccessListID` are not negative; `SSLForced` needs a `CertificateID` above zero; `HSTS` needs `SSLForced`.
2. `proxyHostBody(in, parent)` returns the `npmapi.ProxyHostBody` NPM is sent: `DomainNames` the input's domains sorted ascending, the forward scheme, host and port, `CertificateID`, `AccessListID`, `SSLForced`, `HSTSEnabled` from `HSTS`, `AllowWebsocketUpgrade` from `Websockets`, no host-level `AdvancedConfig`, and one location `proxy.Location(scheme, host, port, parent == "panel.exposure.add")`. `proxyHostState(h)` returns the JSON object with exactly the keys `domains` (sorted), `forwardScheme`, `forwardHost`, `forwardPort`, `certificateId`, `accessListId`, `sslForced`, `hsts`, `websockets`, `locations` (an array, `[]` when there is none, of objects with `path`, `forwardScheme`, `forwardHost`, `forwardPort`, `advancedConfig`) and `advancedConfig`; the id and the times are not in it. `proxyHostCreatedOn(s)` accepts RFC 3339 (with or without fractional seconds) and `2006-01-02 15:04:05` as UTC and returns an error otherwise.
3. `func (n nm) checkUpstream(ctx, env, in)` calls `n.d.panel(ctx, env)`, adds to the result's `NPMAddrs` every IPv4 and IPv6 address of the NPM container's endpoints (`InspectContainer(ctx, containerName)`, all networks, `IPAddress`, `GlobalIPv6Address` and the static `IPAMConfig` addresses; a missing container adds none), and returns `core.Definite(proxy.CheckUpstream(r, in.ForwardHost, in.ForwardPort, env.Call.ParentAction))` when it errs. A failure of the Engine read is returned as is.
4. `func (n nm) proxyhostAdd() core.ActionDef` is `core.DefineMutating[ProxyHostInput, ProxyHostView]`: name `npm.proxyhost.add`, summary "Add a proxy host.", version 1, `core.ClassCompensable`, `core.SideAgent`, `LockKeys: []string{lockKey}`. The handler `proxyhostAddHandler{n nm}` implements Plan, Apply, Verify, Observe, Inverse and Resolved. Plan and Apply share one `check` that changes nothing and, on a refusal, returns a `core.Definite` error: `validateProxyHostInput`; `n.checkUpstream`; `n.d.api(ctx, env)` for a client; the list of proxy hosts, refusing with `errcode.InvalidInput` at `/domains/<i>` when any host already has domain `i` (plan and apply both); `Certificate(ctx, id)` and `AccessList(ctx, id)` for a non-zero id, a not-found answer being `errcode.InvalidInput` at `/certificateId` or `/accessListId`. Any NPM answer is mapped before it is returned, with a file-local helper: a 4xx `*npmapi.APIError` becomes `errcode.Wrap(errcode.InvalidInput, err, "Nginx Proxy Manager refused the request (HTTP <status>): <message>")`, any other failure `errcode.Wrap(errcode.NPMUnhealthy, err, "Nginx Proxy Manager did not answer")`; the wrapped error stays the cause, so `n.run` still sees `npmapi.IsClientError`. No detail holds an NPM response body.
5. Plan returns the diff `+` "proxy host <sorted domains joined by \", \">", ` ` "forward <scheme>://<host>:<port>", then, each only when set, ` ` "certificate <id>", ` ` "access list <id>", ` ` "force SSL", ` ` "HSTS", ` ` "websockets"; `Guards` is the empty non-nil slice; `StateHash` is the hex SHA-256 of a JSON document of the sorted domains, the forward target, whether the certificate and the access list exist (with their ids) and the parent action.
6. Apply runs `check`, then journals one creation built by `npmEffect(core.EffectCreate, resourceOf(nounProxyHost, "?"+<sorted domains joined by ",">), nullState().State, commitWait)` and runs it with `n.run(ctx, env, e, h.Observe, do)`; `do` calls `CreateProxyHost(ctx, proxyHostBody(in, env.Call.ParentAction))` and returns `npm.proxyhost:<id>` of the answer. When `env.Call.ParentAction` is `npm.admin.expose` or `panel.exposure.add`, one `env.DB.Write` transaction then calls `addReserved` for the proxy host (kind `proxy_host`) and, when `CertificateID` is not zero and `reservedOf` finds no row for it, for the certificate (kind `certificate`), with owner the parent action, exposure the first input domain and change id `env.ChangeID`. The output is the `ProxyHostView` of the created host as NPM reports it (domains sorted, `Reserved` the owner or `""`, `Unclaimed` false).
7. Verify, from `in` alone: a proxy host with the input's exact domain set exists and has the input's forward scheme, host and port, otherwise an error; when `out.ID` is not zero it is that host.
8. Observe switches on `identityOf(e.Resource)`. For a made object (`npm.proxyhost:<id>`): `ProxyHost(ctx, id)`; a not-found answer is `named(nullState().State)`; otherwise `named(proxyHostState(host))` with `Current.Version` the JSON object `{"createdOn", "modifiedOn"}` of the host. For a creation (`?` prefix): list the proxy hosts, keep those whose sorted domain set equals the identity's and whose `created_on` is not before the intent time (`e.CommitBy` minus `e.CommitWait`, truncated to the second); none gives `Current` the null state and `Matches` 0; one gives `Matches` 1, `Resource` `npm.proxyhost:<id>`, `Current` that host's state and version; several give `Matches` N, the candidates sorted by resource and `Current` the null state; `Exclusive` is true in every case. NPM errors go through the helper of step 4.
9. Inverse deletes the proxy host of `e.Resource` with `DeleteProxyHost`: a not-found answer is done, a 4xx answer is `core.Definite`, any other failure is returned as it is. After the delete, one `env.DB.Write` removes every `npm_reserved` row whose `change_id` is `e.ChangeID`.
10. Resolved(ctx, tx, e, resolution): when `e.State` is `core.EffectSkipped` and `e.Recovery` is set, call `addWatch(ctx, tx, "proxy_host", e.Recovery.Value, e.ChangeID, e.Seq)`; do nothing for any other state.

### Task 17: npm: accesslist.add and accesslist.remove

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/modules/npm/accesslist.go`
- Test: `internal/modules/npm/accesslist_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Contracts "NPM wire shapes" (`AccessListInput`, `AccessClientInput`, `AccessListRemoveInput`, `AccessListView`); "NPM API client" (`npmapi.AccessList`, `AccessListBody`, `AccessClient`, `APIError`, `IsNotFound`, `IsClientError`; the `npmAPI` methods `AccessLists`, `AccessList`, `CreateAccessList`, `DeleteAccessList`, `ProxyHosts`); "NPM deps" (`nm`, `deps.api`); "NPM shared internals" (`lockKey`, `commitWait`, `nounAccessList`, `resourceOf`, `identityOf`, `npmEffect`, `nm.run`, `nullState`, `named`); "NPM store" (`loadInstance`, `reservedOf`, `addReserved`, `dropReservedExposure`, `addWatch`, `reservedRow`); "NPM identities" (the access-list creation identity `darkpanel-<change id>-<seq>`, `Exclusive: true`, the `npm_watch` write by a `Resolver`); "NPM test host".
- Produces: `func (n nm) accesslistAdd() core.ActionDef` and `func (n nm) accesslistRemove() core.ActionDef` (Contracts "NPM actions"; Task 27 lists them in `actions()`). Names this task declares in package `npm`, used by no other task: `accessListAddHandler`, `accessListRemoveHandler`, `func (n nm) observeAccessList`, `accessListState`, `accessListView`, `accessListClient`, `accessListRules`, `accessListInvalid`, `accessListName`, `accessListNextSeq`, `accessListUsers`, `accessListReservedBy`, `accessListRemovalOf`. The access-list state document is `State` `{"name": <string>, "satisfyAny": <bool>, "clients": [{"address", "directive"}...]}` (client order kept: nginx takes the first matching rule) and `Version` `{"id": <int>, "createdOn": <string>}`, so a list recreated by an undo is State-equal to the original.

**Items:** 30

**Depends on:** Task 11
**Implementer:** dforge:impl-opus-high
**Evaluation:** files 1 - spec 2 - coupling 1 - risk 2 = 6

Decisions this task embodies (spec 6 "Management", "Recovery identities", "Reserved resources"):
- `npm.accesslist.add` takes IP client rules only and no name. The module names the list `darkpanel-<change id>-<seq>`, where `<seq>` is the sequence number the engine will give the creation effect. The journal assigns `max(seq)+1` for the change (`lifecycle/effects.go`, `Intend`), and the identity must be journaled with the intent, so `accessListNextSeq` reads `env.Effects.List` and returns the highest `Seq` plus one (1 when the list is empty). The test pins the prediction against the journal.
- Both actions are compensable. The add's undo deletes the list it created. The remove's undo recreates the list from the `Before` state in the journal; the recreated list has a new id, which `Inverse` returns, and the effect declares `InverseRecovery` (kind `name`, the list's name) so an inverse cut off before it returned can still be observed.
- Beyond the spec, both the remove and the add's undo refuse a list that a proxy host still uses (`request.invalid_input`, naming the proxy hosts): deleting it would silently lift the protection of those hosts.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `internal/modules/npm/accesslist_test.go`:

```go
package npm

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"reflect"
	"slices"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/npm/npmapi"
)

// alAPI is the module's NPM client with CreateAccessList replaced.
type alAPI struct {
	npmAPI
	create func(ctx context.Context, b npmapi.AccessListBody) (npmapi.AccessList, error)
}

func (a alAPI) CreateAccessList(ctx context.Context, b npmapi.AccessListBody) (npmapi.AccessList, error) {
	return a.create(ctx, b)
}

// alReplaceCreate returns n with its NPM client answering CreateAccessList
// through create, which also receives the live client.
func alReplaceCreate(n nm, create func(ctx context.Context, live npmAPI, b npmapi.AccessListBody) (npmapi.AccessList, error)) nm {
	inner := n.d.api
	n.d.api = func(ctx context.Context, env core.Env) (npmAPI, error) {
		live, err := inner(ctx, env)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		return alAPI{npmAPI: live, create: func(ctx context.Context, b npmapi.AccessListBody) (npmapi.AccessList, error) {
			return create(ctx, live, b)
		}}, nil
	}
	return n
}

func alSetup(t *testing.T) (*thHost, nm) {
	t.Helper()
	h := newTHHost(t)
	h.deployed()
	return h, h.nm()
}

func alInput() AccessListInput {
	return AccessListInput{Clients: []AccessClientInput{
		{Address: "10.0.0.0/8", Directive: "allow"},
		{Address: "10.9.0.5", Directive: "deny"},
	}}
}

func alErr(t *testing.T, err error) *errcode.Error {
	t.Helper()
	var ec *errcode.Error
	if !errors.As(err, &ec) {
		t.Fatalf("error = %v, want an *errcode.Error", err)
	}
	return ec
}

func alJSON(t *testing.T, raw []byte) any {
	t.Helper()
	var v any
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &v); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("decode %s: %v", raw, err)
	}
	return v
}

func alLists(t *testing.T, n nm, env core.Env) []npmapi.AccessList {
	t.Helper()
	api, err := n.d.api(context.Background(), env)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("open the NPM client: %v", err)
	}
	lists, err := api.AccessLists(context.Background())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("list access lists: %v", err)
	}
	return lists
}

func alReserve(t *testing.T, h *thHost, id int, owner string) {
	t.Helper()
	ctx := context.Background()
	err := h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		return addReserved(ctx, tx, reservedRow{Kind: "access_list", ObjectID: id, Owner: owner, Exposure: "npm.example.com", ChangeID: "c0"})
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("reserve access list %d: %v", id, err)
	}
}

func TestAlSpecs(t *testing.T) {
	n := newTHHost(t).nm()
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		def      core.ActionDef
		name     string
		resolver bool
	}{
		{n.accesslistAdd(), "npm.accesslist.add", true},
		{n.accesslistRemove(), "npm.accesslist.remove", false},
	} {
		s := tc.def.Spec
		if s.Name != tc.name || s.Version != 1 || s.Class != core.ClassCompensable || s.Side != core.SideAgent {
			t.Errorf("%s: spec = %+v", tc.name, s)
		}
		if !slices.Equal(s.LockKeys, []string{"npm"}) {
			t.Errorf("%s: LockKeys = %v, want [npm]", tc.name, s.LockKeys)
		}
		if core.RequiredScope(s) != core.ScopeOperate {
			t.Errorf("%s: scope = %s, want operate", tc.name, core.RequiredScope(s))
		}
		h := tc.def.Handler
		if h.Plan == nil || h.Apply == nil || h.Verify == nil || h.Observe == nil || h.Inverse == nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: a handler member is missing: %+v", tc.name, h)
		}
		if (h.Resolved != nil) != tc.resolver {
			t.Errorf("%s: Resolved set = %v, want %v", tc.name, h.Resolved != nil, tc.resolver)
		}
	}
}

func TestAlAddPlan(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n := alSetup(t)
	handler := accessListAddHandler{n: n}
	out, err := handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), alInput())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Plan: %v", err)
	}
	wantDiff := []core.DiffLine{
		{Op: "+", Text: "access list (named when applied)"},
		{Op: " ", Text: "satisfy all"},
		{Op: "+", Text: "allow 10.0.0.0/8"},
		{Op: "+", Text: "deny 10.9.0.5"},
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(out.Diff, wantDiff) {
		t.Errorf("diff = %+v, want %+v", out.Diff, wantDiff)
	}
	again, err := handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), alInput())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Plan again: %v", err)
	}
	if out.StateHash == "" || again.StateHash != out.StateHash {
		t.Errorf("state hashes %q and %q, want equal and non-empty", out.StateHash, again.StateHash)
	}
	loose := alInput()
	loose.SatisfyAny = true
	other, err := handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), loose)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Plan satisfy any: %v", err)
	}
	if other.StateHash == out.StateHash || other.Diff[1].Text != "satisfy any" {
		t.Errorf("satisfy any plan = %+v, want another hash and the line %q", other, "satisfy any")
	}
}

func TestAlAddPlanRefusesInvalidInput(t *testing.T) {
	h, n := alSetup(t)
	handler := accessListAddHandler{n: n}
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name string
		in   AccessListInput
		path string
	}{
		{"no clients", AccessListInput{}, "/clients"},
		{"not an address", AccessListInput{Clients: []AccessClientInput{{Address: "ten", Directive: "allow"}}}, "/clients/0/address"},
		{"unmasked prefix", AccessListInput{Clients: []AccessClientInput{{Address: "10.0.0.5/8", Directive: "allow"}}}, "/clients/0/address"},
		{"unknown directive", AccessListInput{Clients: []AccessClientInput{{Address: "10.0.0.1", Directive: "allow"}, {Address: "10.0.0.2", Directive: "permit"}}}, "/clients/1/directive"},
	} {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			_, err := handler.Plan(context.Background(), h.env(""), tc.in)
			if !core.IsDefinite(err) {
				t.Fatalf("error = %v, want a Definite refusal", err)
			}
			ec := alErr(t, err)
			if ec.Code != errcode.InvalidInput || len(ec.Fields) == 0 || ec.Fields[0].Path != tc.path {
				t.Errorf("error = %+v, want request.invalid_input at %s", ec, tc.path)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestAlAddNeedsAnAnsweringInstance(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	bare := newTHHost(t)
	bare.addChange("c1", "npm.accesslist.add")
	handler := accessListAddHandler{n: bare.nm()}
	for name, run := range map[string]func() error{
		"plan":  func() error { _, err := handler.Plan(ctx, bare.env(""), alInput()); return err },
		"apply": func() error { _, err := handler.Apply(ctx, bare.env("c1"), alInput()); return err },
	} {
		err := run()
		if !core.IsDefinite(err) || alErr(t, err).Code != errcode.NotFound {
			t.Errorf("%s without an instance: error = %v, want a Definite not_found", name, err)
		}
	}
	if got := bare.journal.effects(); len(got) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("journaled %d effects without an instance, want none", len(got))
	}

	h, n := alSetup(t)
	n.d.api = func(context.Context, core.Env) (npmAPI, error) { return nil, errors.New("npm: connection refused") }
	_, err := accessListAddHandler{n: n}.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), alInput())
	if !core.IsDefinite(err) || alErr(t, err).Code != errcode.NPMUnhealthy {
		t.Errorf("plan with NPM down: error = %v, want a Definite npm.unhealthy", err)
	}
}

func TestAlAddApplyJournalsTheNamedIdentity(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n := alSetup(t)
	h.addChange("c1", "npm.accesslist.add")
	env := h.env("c1")
	handler := accessListAddHandler{n: n}
	inputs := []AccessListInput{
		alInput(),
		{SatisfyAny: true, Clients: []AccessClientInput{{Address: "2001:db8::/32", Directive: "allow"}}},
	}
	var views []AccessListView
	for i, in := range inputs {
		v, err := handler.Apply(ctx, env, in)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("Apply %d: %v", i+1, err)
		}
		views = append(views, v)
	}
	effects := h.journal.effects()
	if len(effects) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("journaled %d effects, want 2", len(effects))
	}
	for i, e := range effects {
		name := fmt.Sprintf("darkpanel-c1-%d", i+1)
		v := views[i]
		if v.Name != name || v.SatisfyAny != inputs[i].SatisfyAny || !reflect.DeepEqual(v.Clients, inputs[i].Clients) || v.Reserved != "" || v.Unclaimed {
			t.Errorf("view %d = %+v, want name %s and the input's rules", i+1, v, name)
		}
		if e.Seq != i+1 || e.Kind != core.EffectCreate || e.State != core.EffectMade || e.Local || e.CommitWait != 2*time.Minute ||
			e.Resource != "npm.accesslist:"+strconv.Itoa(v.ID) || !reflect.DeepEqual(e.Recovery, &core.RecoveryID{Kind: "name", Value: name}) || e.Post == nil {
			t.Errorf("effect %d = %+v", i+1, e)
		}
	}
	wantState := alJSON(t, []byte(`{"name":"darkpanel-c1-1","satisfyAny":false,"clients":[{"address":"10.0.0.0/8","directive":"allow"},{"address":"10.9.0.5","directive":"deny"}]}`))
	if got := alJSON(t, effects[0].Post.State); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, wantState) {
		t.Errorf("post state = %v, want %v", got, wantState)
	}
	version, _ := alJSON(t, effects[0].Post.Version).(map[string]any)
	if version["id"] != float64(views[0].ID) {
		t.Errorf("post version = %v, want the list id %d", version, views[0].ID)
	}
	if lists := alLists(t, n, env); len(lists) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("NPM holds %d access lists, want 2", len(lists))
	}
	if err := handler.Verify(ctx, env, inputs[0], views[0]); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("Verify: %v", err)
	}
	api, err := n.d.api(ctx, env)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := api.DeleteAccessList(ctx, views[1].ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := handler.Verify(ctx, env, inputs[1], views[1]); err == nil {
		t.Error("Verify passed for a list NPM no longer holds")
	}
}

func TestAlAddVerifyFindsTheListFromTheChangeAlone(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()

	h.npm.SeedAccessList(npmapi.AccessList{Name: "darkpanel-c1-9", Clients: []npmapi.AccessClient{{Address: "10.1.1.1", Directive: "allow"}}})
	if err := handler.Verify(ctx, env, alInput(), AccessListView{}); err == nil {
		t.Error("Verify with the zero output passed although two lists carry the change id")
	}
}

func TestAlAddRefusedByNPMIsDefiniteAndSkipped(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n := alSetup(t)
	h.addChange("c1", "npm.accesslist.add")

	})

func TestAlAddLostResponseLeavesTheEffectObservable(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n := alSetup(t)
	h.addChange("c1", "npm.accesslist.add")
	env := h.env("c1")
	n = alReplaceCreate(n, func(ctx context.Context, live npmAPI, b npmapi.AccessListBody) (npmapi.AccessList, error) {
		if _, err := live.CreateAccessList(ctx, b); err != nil {
			return npmapi.AccessList{}, err
		}
		return npmapi.AccessList{}, errors.New("npm: connection reset by peer")
	})
	handler := accessListAddHandler{n: n}
	_, err := handler.Apply(ctx, env, alInput())
	if err == nil || core.IsDefinite(err) {
		t.Fatalf("error = %v, want a failure that is not Definite", err)
	}
	effects := h.journal.effects()
	if len(effects) != 1 || effects[0].State != core.EffectIntended || effects[0].Resource != "npm.accesslist:?darkpanel-c1-1" {
		t.Fatalf("effects = %+v, want one intended creation of npm.accesslist:?darkpanel-c1-1", effects)
	}
	lists := alLists(t, n, env)
	if len(lists) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("NPM holds %d access lists, want the one the lost response created", len(lists))
	}
	obs, err := handler.Observe(ctx, env, effects[0])
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Observe: %v", err)
	}
	if obs.Matches != 1 || !obs.Exclusive || obs.Resource != "npm.accesslist:"+strconv.Itoa(lists[0].ID) {
		t.Errorf("observation = %+v, want one exclusive match of list %d", obs, lists[0].ID)
	}
	if got, _ := alJSON(t, obs.Current.State).(map[string]any); got["name"] != "darkpanel-c1-1" {
		t.Errorf("observed state = %s, want the list's state", obs.Current.State)
	}
}

func TestAlObserveCreationCountsMatchesByName(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n := alSetup(t)
	handler := accessListAddHandler{n: n}
	e := core.Effect{
		ChangeID: "c1", Seq: 1, Kind: core.EffectCreate, Resource: "npm.accesslist:?darkpanel-c1-1",
		Recovery: &core.RecoveryID{Kind: "name", Value: "darkpanel-c1-1"}, State: core.EffectIntended,
	}
	none, err := handler.Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), e)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if none.Matches != 0 || string(none.Current.State) != "null" {
		t.Errorf("no list: observation = %+v, want Matches 0 and state null", none)
	}
	for range 2 {
		h.npm.SeedAccessList(npmapi.AccessList{Name: "darkpanel-c1-1", Clients: []npmapi.AccessClient{{Address: "10.1.1.1", Directive: "allow"}}})
	}
	h.npm.SeedAccessList(npmapi.AccessList{Name: "darkpanel-c1-12"})
	two, err := handler.Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), e)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if two.Matches != 2 || len(two.Candidates) != 2 || string(two.Current.State) != "null" {
		t.Fatalf("two lists: observation = %+v, want Matches 2, two candidates and state null", two)
	}
	if !slices.IsSortedFunc(two.Candidates, func(a, b core.Candidate) int { return strings.Compare(a.Resource, b.Resource) }) {
		t.Errorf("candidates %+v are not sorted by resource", two.Candidates)
	}
}

func TestAlAddUndoDeletesTheListAndRefusesOneInUse(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n := alSetup(t)
	h.addChange("c1", "npm.accesslist.add")
	env := h.env("c1")
	handler := accessListAddHandler{n: n}
	view, err := handler.Apply(ctx, env, alInput())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	made := h.journal.effects()[0]

	h.npm.SeedProxyHost(npmapi.ProxyHost{DomainNames: []string{"app.example.com"}, ForwardScheme: "http", ForwardHost: "app", ForwardPort: 8080, AccessListID: view.ID})
	_, err = handler.Inverse(ctx, env, made)
	if !core.IsDefinite(err) || alErr(t, err).Code != errcode.InvalidInput || !strings.Contains(alErr(t, err).Detail, "app.example.com") {
		t.Fatalf("undo of a list in use: error = %v, want a Definite invalid_input naming the proxy host", err)
	}
	if len(alLists(t, n, env)) != 1 {
		t.Fatal("the list in use was deleted")
	}

	api, err := n.d.api(ctx, env)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	hosts, err := api.ProxyHosts(ctx)
	if err != nil || len(hosts) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("proxy hosts = %v, %v", hosts, err)
	}
	if err := api.DeleteProxyHost(ctx, hosts[0].ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	res, err := handler.Inverse(ctx, env, made)
	if err != nil || res.Resource != "" {
		t.Fatalf("Inverse = %+v, %v, want an empty result", res, err)
	}
	if len(alLists(t, n, env)) != 0 {
		t.Error("the list is still there after the undo")
	}
	if _, err := handler.Inverse(ctx, env, made); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("undoing a list that is already gone: %v", err)
	}
	obs, err := handler.Observe(ctx, env, made)
	if err != nil || string(obs.Current.State) != "null" {
		t.Errorf("Observe after the undo = %+v, %v, want state null", obs, err)
	}
}

func TestAlResolvedWatchesOnlyASkippedCreation(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n := alSetup(t)
	handler := accessListAddHandler{n: n}
	created := func(seq int, state core.EffectState) core.Effect {
		name := fmt.Sprintf("darkpanel-c1-%d", seq)
		return core.Effect{
			ChangeID: "c1", Seq: seq, Kind: core.EffectCreate, Resource: "npm.accesslist:?" + name,
			Recovery: &core.RecoveryID{Kind: "name", Value: name}, State: state,
		}
	}
	for _, e := range []core.Effect{created(3, core.EffectSkipped), created(4, core.EffectAccepted)} {
		err := h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error { return handler.Resolved(ctx, tx, e, "reconcile") })
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("Resolved(%s): %v", e.State, err)
		}
	}
	watching, err := watched(ctx, h.db.Read(), "access_list")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !watching["darkpanel-c1-3"] || watching["darkpanel-c1-4"] || len(watching) != 1 {
		t.Errorf("watched access lists = %v, want only darkpanel-c1-3", watching)
	}
}

func TestAlRemovePlanApplyAndVerify(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n := alSetup(t)
	seeded := h.npm.SeedAccessList(npmapi.AccessList{
		Name: "office", SatisfyAny: true,
		Clients: []npmapi.AccessClient{{Address: "203.0.113.0/24", Directive: "allow"}},
	})
	handler := accessListRemoveHandler{n: n}
	in := AccessListRemoveInput{ID: seeded.ID}
	plan, err := handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Plan: %v", err)
	}
	wantDiff := []core.DiffLine{
		{Op: "-", Text: fmt.Sprintf("access list office (id %d)", seeded.ID)},
		{Op: " ", Text: "satisfy any"},
		{Op: "-", Text: "allow 203.0.113.0/24"},
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(plan.Diff, wantDiff) || plan.StateHash == "" || plan.Confirmation != "" {
		t.Errorf("plan = %+v, want diff %+v, a state hash and no confirmation", plan, wantDiff)
	}
	if err := handler.Verify(ctx, h.env(""), in, AccessListView{}); err == nil {
		t.Error("Verify passed while the list still exists")
	}

	h.addChange("c2", "npm.accesslist.remove")
	env := h.env("c2")
	view, err := handler.Apply(ctx, env, in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Apply: %v", err)
	}
	wantView := AccessListView{ID: seeded.ID, Name: "office", SatisfyAny: true, Clients: []AccessClientInput{{Address: "203.0.113.0/24", Directive: "allow"}}}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(view, wantView) {
		t.Errorf("view = %+v, want %+v", view, wantView)
	}
	if len(alLists(t, n, env)) != 0 {
		t.Error("the list is still in NPM")
	}
	effects := h.journal.effects()
	if len(effects) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("journaled %d effects, want 1", len(effects))
	}
	e := effects[0]
	if e.Kind != core.EffectDelete || e.State != core.EffectMade || e.Local || e.CommitWait != 2*time.Minute ||
		e.Resource != "npm.accesslist:"+strconv.Itoa(seeded.ID) || e.Recovery != nil ||
		!reflect.DeepEqual(e.InverseRecovery, &core.RecoveryID{Kind: "name", Value: "office"}) {
		t.Errorf("effect = %+v", e)
	}
	wantBefore := alJSON(t, []byte(`{"name":"office","satisfyAny":true,"clients":[{"address":"203.0.113.0/24","directive":"allow"}]}`))
	if got := alJSON(t, e.Before.State); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, wantBefore) {
		t.Errorf("before state = %v, want %v", got, wantBefore)
	}
	if err := handler.Verify(ctx, env, in, view); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("Verify after the removal: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestAlRemoveRefusals(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n := alSetup(t)
	handler := accessListRemoveHandler{n: n}
	used := h.npm.SeedAccessList(npmapi.AccessList{Name: "used", Clients: []npmapi.AccessClient{{Address: "10.1.1.1", Directive: "allow"}}})
	h.npm.SeedProxyHost(npmapi.ProxyHost{DomainNames: []string{"app.example.com"}, ForwardScheme: "http", ForwardHost: "app", ForwardPort: 8080, AccessListID: used.ID})
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name   string
		id     int
		code   errcode.Code
		detail string
	}{
		{"zero id", 0, errcode.InvalidInput, ""},
		{"unknown list", 4242, errcode.NotFound, ""},
		{"list in use", used.ID, errcode.InvalidInput, "app.example.com"},
	} {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			_, err := handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), AccessListRemoveInput{ID: tc.id})
			if !core.IsDefinite(err) {
				t.Fatalf("error = %v, want a Definite refusal", err)
			}
			if ec := alErr(t, err); ec.Code != tc.code || !strings.Contains(ec.Detail, tc.detail) {
				t.Errorf("error = %+v, want %s containing %q", ec, tc.code, tc.detail)
			}
		})
	}
	if len(alLists(t, n, h.env(""))) != 1 {
		t.Error("a refused removal changed NPM")
	}
}

func TestAlRemoveOfAReservedListNeedsTheOwnersRemoval(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n := alSetup(t)
	handler := accessListRemoveHandler{n: n}
	list := h.npm.SeedAccessList(npmapi.AccessList{Name: "admin-only", Clients: []npmapi.AccessClient{{Address: "203.0.113.7", Directive: "allow"}}})
	alReserve(t, h, list.ID, "npm.admin.expose")
	in := AccessListRemoveInput{ID: list.ID}
	h.addChange("c3", "npm.accesslist.remove")
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name   string
		env    core.Env
		refuse bool
	}{
		{"operator", h.env("c3"), true},
		{"the owner's own composite", h.child("c3", "npm.admin.expose"), true},
		{"another owner's removal", h.child("c3", "panel.exposure.remove"), true},
		{"the owner's removal", h.child("c3", "npm.admin.unexpose"), false},
	} {
		_, err := handler.Plan(ctx, tc.env, in)
		if tc.refuse {
			if !core.IsDefinite(err) || alErr(t, err).Code != errcode.NPMReserved {
				t.Errorf("%s: error = %v, want a Definite npm.reserved", tc.name, err)
			}
		} else if err != nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: Plan: %v", tc.name, err)
		}
	}
	if _, err := handler.Apply(ctx, h.env("c3"), in); alErr(t, err).Code != errcode.NPMReserved {
		t.Errorf("Apply as the operator: error = %v, want npm.reserved", err)
	}
	if len(alLists(t, n, h.env(""))) != 1 {
		t.Fatal("a refused removal deleted the list")
	}
	if _, err := handler.Apply(ctx, h.child("c3", "npm.admin.unexpose"), in); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Apply as the owner's removal: %v", err)
	}
	if len(alLists(t, n, h.env(""))) != 0 {
		t.Error("the owner's removal left the list in NPM")
	}
	if _, found, err := reservedOf(ctx, h.db.Read(), "access_list", list.ID); err != nil || found {
		t.Errorf("reservedOf after the removal = %v, %v, want no row", found, err)
	}
}

func TestAlRemoveUndoRecreatesTheList(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n := alSetup(t)
	seeded := h.npm.SeedAccessList(npmapi.AccessList{
		Name: "office", SatisfyAny: true,
		Clients: []npmapi.AccessClient{{Address: "203.0.113.0/24", Directive: "allow"}, {Address: "203.0.113.9", Directive: "deny"}},
	})
	h.addChange("c2", "npm.accesslist.remove")
	env := h.env("c2")
	handler := accessListRemoveHandler{n: n}
	if _, err := handler.Apply(ctx, env, AccessListRemoveInput{ID: seeded.ID}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	made := h.journal.effects()[0]
	res, err := handler.Inverse(ctx, env, made)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Inverse: %v", err)
	}
	lists := alLists(t, n, env)
	if len(lists) != 1 || res.Resource != "npm.accesslist:"+strconv.Itoa(lists[0].ID) {
		t.Fatalf("Inverse result %+v with lists %+v, want the recreated list's resource", res, lists)
	}
	if got := lists[0]; got.Name != "office" || !got.SatisfyAny || !reflect.DeepEqual(got.Clients, seeded.Clients) {
		t.Errorf("recreated list = %+v, want the original's name, mode and rules", got)
	}

	undone := made
	undone.State = core.EffectUndone
	undone.UndoResource = res.Resource
	obs, err := handler.Observe(ctx, env, undone)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Observe after the undo: %v", err)
	}
	if obs.Matches != 1 || !reflect.DeepEqual(alJSON(t, obs.Current.State), alJSON(t, made.Before.State)) {
		t.Errorf("observation = %+v, want one match State-equal to the original", obs)
	}

	cut := made
	cut.State = core.EffectUndoing
	obs, err = handler.Observe(ctx, env, cut)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Observe of an inverse cut off before it returned: %v", err)
	}
	if obs.Matches != 1 || obs.Resource != res.Resource || obs.Exclusive {
		t.Errorf("observation = %+v, want the recreated list found by name, not exclusive", obs)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run test to verify it fails**

Run: `mkdir -p /root/.cache/w6a-tmp/17 && export TMPDIR=/root/.cache/w6a-tmp/17 GOTOOLCHAIN=local && timeout 300 go test -count=1 -run 'TestAl' ./internal/modules/npm/`
Expected: FAIL with a build error naming the first undefined identifier (`n.accesslistAdd undefined`, `undefined: accessListAddHandler`). Quote it in the report.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `internal/modules/npm/accesslist.go` (package `npm`, doc comment on each type and non-trivial function, comments only for a non-obvious why):

1. **Constructors.** `func (n nm) accesslistAdd() core.ActionDef` is `core.DefineMutating[AccessListInput, AccessListView]` with `ActionSpec{Name: ID + ".accesslist.add", Summary: "Create an IP access list in Nginx Proxy Manager.", Version: 1, Class: core.ClassCompensable, Side: core.SideAgent, LockKeys: []string{lockKey}}` and the handler `accessListAddHandler{n nm}`. `func (n nm) accesslistRemove() core.ActionDef` is the same with `AccessListRemoveInput`, `Name: ID + ".accesslist.remove"`, Summary "Delete an access list from Nginx Proxy Manager." and `accessListRemoveHandler{n nm}`. Neither sets `ApplyTimeout` or `LongRunning`.
2. **Client and refusals.** `func (n nm) accessListClient(ctx, env) (npmAPI, error)` reads `loadInstance(ctx, env.DB.Read())`: no row gives `errcode.New(errcode.NotFound, "no Nginx Proxy Manager instance is deployed on this host")`; then `n.d.api(ctx, env)`: an error gives `errcode.Wrap(errcode.NPMUnhealthy, err, "the Nginx Proxy Manager API is not available")`. Every error it returns is wrapped `core.Definite` (it only reads). `accessListInvalid(path, msg string) error` is a `core.Definite` `*errcode.Error` with code `request.invalid_input`, `Detail` msg and one `FieldError{Path, Message}`.
3. **Rules.** `accessListRules(in AccessListInput) ([]npmapi.AccessClient, error)` refuses with `accessListInvalid`: no clients (path `/clients`); an address that is neither `netip.ParseAddr` nor `netip.ParsePrefix` valid, or a prefix that differs from `p.Masked()` (path `/clients/<i>/address`); a directive other than exactly `allow` or `deny` (path `/clients/<i>/directive`). It keeps the order and the strings as given and returns `[]npmapi.AccessClient`.
4. **State and view.** `accessListState(a npmapi.AccessList) (core.StateDoc, error)` renders the document in Interfaces (`clients` an empty array, never null). `accessListView(a npmapi.AccessList) AccessListView` copies ID, Name, SatisfyAny and the clients as `AccessClientInput` (empty slice, not nil) and leaves `Reserved` empty and `Unclaimed` false. `accessListName(changeID string, seq int) string` is `darkpanel-<change id>-<seq>`; `accessListNextSeq(ctx, env) (int, error)` is described in the decisions above.
5. **Users of a list.** `accessListUsers(ctx, api npmAPI, id int) ([]string, error)` lists the proxy hosts whose `AccessListID` equals id and returns the first domain of each, sorted. A non-empty result makes the remove's target check and the add's `Inverse` fail with `accessListInvalid("/id", "access list <id> is used by proxy hosts: <domains joined by \", \">")`.
6. **Reservation.** `accessListRemovalOf(owner string) string` maps `npm.admin.expose` to `npm.admin.unexpose` and `panel.exposure.add` to `panel.exposure.remove`, and anything else to the empty string. `accessListReservedBy(ctx, env, id int) (reservedRow, bool, error)` reads `reservedOf(ctx, env.DB.Read(), "access_list", id)` and, when a row exists and `env.Call.ParentAction` is not `accessListRemovalOf(row.Owner)`, returns `core.Definite(errcode.New(errcode.NPMReserved, "access list <id> belongs to the exposure <row.Exposure>"))`; it returns the row (found true) when the removal is allowed.
7. **`accessListAddHandler`.**
   - `Plan`: `accessListRules`, then `accessListClient` (the plan proves the instance answers); `PlanOutcome.Diff` is exactly the four-line shape the test pins: `{"+", "access list (named when applied)"}`, `{" ", "satisfy any"}` or `{" ", "satisfy all"}`, then one `{"+", "<directive> <address>"}` per client in order; `StateHash` is the hex SHA-256 of `json.Marshal` of the input.
   - `Apply`: validate, `accessListClient`, `accessListNextSeq`, build `npmapi.AccessListBody{Name: accessListName(env.ChangeID, seq), SatisfyAny, PassAuth: false, Clients}`. The effect is `npmEffect(core.EffectCreate, resourceOf(nounAccessList, "?"+name), json.RawMessage("null"), commitWait)`. Call `n.run(ctx, env, e, h.n.observeAccessList, do)`, where `do` calls `CreateAccessList` and returns `resourceOf(nounAccessList, strconv.Itoa(created.ID))`; it returns NPM's error unchanged so `run` can tell a 4xx (skipped, Definite) from a lost response (left intended). Return `accessListView(created)`.
   - `Verify`: with `out.ID != 0` read that list (`AccessList(ctx, id)`), otherwise (recovery passes the zero output) list the lists whose name starts with `darkpanel-<env.ChangeID>-` and require exactly one. Require the list's `SatisfyAny` and clients to equal `in`'s; any difference or a missing list is an error. Read-only.
   - `Observe`: `h.n.observeAccessList`.
   - `Inverse`: `accessListClient`; the id is `identityOf(e.Resource)` parsed as an integer (anything else is an error); `AccessList(ctx, id)`: `npmapi.IsNotFound` returns an empty `core.InverseResult` (already gone); read `accessListUsers` and refuse a list in use; `DeleteAccessList`: success or `IsNotFound` returns the empty result, `npmapi.IsClientError` returns `core.Definite(err)`, any other error is returned as is.
   - `Resolved(ctx, tx, e, resolution)`: when `e.State == core.EffectSkipped` and `e.Recovery != nil`, call `addWatch(ctx, tx, "access_list", e.Recovery.Value, e.ChangeID, e.Seq)`; every other state returns nil.
8. **`accessListRemoveHandler`.**
   - A private `target(ctx, env, in) (npmAPI, npmapi.AccessList, reservedRow, bool, error)`: refuse `in.ID <= 0` with `accessListInvalid("/id", "an access list id is required")`; `accessListClient`; `AccessList(ctx, in.ID)` with `npmapi.IsNotFound` giving `core.Definite(errcode.New(errcode.NotFound, "access list <id> not found"))` and any other read error `core.Definite(err)`; `accessListReservedBy`; then `accessListUsers`. The checks run in this order.
   - `Plan`: `target`; `Diff` exactly as the test pins (`- access list <name> (id <id>)`, the satisfy line, one `{"-", "<directive> <address>"}` per client); `StateHash` the hex SHA-256 of `json.Marshal` of the `StateDoc` from `accessListState`; no `Confirmation` (the action is compensable).
   - `Apply`: `target` again (the engine re-plans, and the object may have changed); the effect is `npmEffect(core.EffectDelete, resourceOf(nounAccessList, strconv.Itoa(id)), <State>, commitWait)` with `e.Before.Version` set to the document's `Version` and `e.InverseRecovery = &core.RecoveryID{Kind: "name", Value: <name>}`; `n.run` with `h.n.observeAccessList` and a `do` that calls `DeleteAccessList` and returns the resource. When `target` returned a reserved row, drop its exposure's rows afterwards with `env.DB.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error { return dropReservedExposure(ctx, tx, row.Exposure) })`; a failure there is returned wrapped (`npm: ...`), not Definite. Return `accessListView` of the list as removed, with `Reserved` set to the row's owner when there was one.
   - `Verify`: id is `out.ID`, or `in.ID` when `out.ID == 0`; the list must be gone (`IsNotFound`), an existing list is an error. Read-only.
   - `Observe`: `h.n.observeAccessList`.
   - `Inverse`: `accessListClient`; require `e.Kind == core.EffectDelete`; decode `e.Before.State` into the state document's fields (a `null` or undecodable state is an error); `CreateAccessList` with that name, `SatisfyAny`, `PassAuth: false` and the clients; `npmapi.IsClientError` returns `core.Definite(err)`; success returns `core.InverseResult{Resource: resourceOf(nounAccessList, <new id>)}`.
9. **`func (n nm) observeAccessList(ctx, env, e core.Effect) (core.Observation, error)`** reads only. It takes `identity` from `identityOf(e.Resource)` and picks one case:
   - identity starts with `?` (a creation): list the access lists and keep those whose `Name` equals the identity without the `?`; 
   - `e.Kind == core.EffectDelete` and `e.State` is `undoing` or `undone`: when `e.UndoResource` is set read that list by id, otherwise (the inverse was cut off) find the lists named `e.InverseRecovery.Value` (an effect without `InverseRecovery` is an error);
   - otherwise: read the list by the id in identity.
   Reading by id gives `named(<state JSON>)` for a list that exists and `named(nullState().State)` for `npmapi.IsNotFound`. Finding by name gives, sorted by resource string: no list `core.Observation{Current: nullState()}`; one list `{Current: its state, Resource: its resource, Matches: 1, Exclusive: <true for a creation, false for the inverse-recovery name>}`; several `{Current: nullState(), Matches: len, Candidates: every candidate with its state}`. A resource that is not `npm.accesslist:` or names no id is an error.
10. Touch no other file. `Manifest()` is unchanged (Task 27 registers the actions).
