**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Contracts "NPM wire shapes" (`CertificateInput`, `CertificateRemoveInput`, `CertificateView`); "NPM API client" (`npmapi.Certificate`, `CertificateBody`, `CertificateMeta`, `APIError`, `IsNotFound`, `IsClientError`; the `npmAPI` methods `Certificates`, `Certificate`, `CreateCertificate`, `DeleteCertificate`, `ProxyHosts`); "NPM deps"; "NPM shared internals" (`lockKey`, `certCommitWait`, `nounCertificate`, `resourceOf`, `identityOf`, `npmEffect`, `nm.run`, `nullState`, `named`); "NPM store" (`loadInstance` and `instanceRow.AdminEmail`, which is the stored `acmeEmail`; `reservedOf`, `addReserved`, `dropReservedExposure`, `addWatch`, `reservedRow`); "NPM identities" (the certificate creation identity: `npm.certificate:?<sorted domains joined by ",">`, recovery `name`, `Exclusive: false`; the module sends no `nice_name`); "NPM test host".
- Produces: `func (n nm) certificateRequest() core.ActionDef` and `func (n nm) certificateRemove() core.ActionDef` (Contracts "NPM actions"). Names this task declares in package `npm`, used by no other task: `certificateRequestHandler`, `certificateRemoveHandler`, `func (n nm) observeCertificate`, `certificateState`, `certificateView`, `certificateClient`, `certificateDomains`, `certificateInvalid`, `certificateIdentity`, `certificateCreatedOn`, `certificateUsers`, `certificateReservedBy`, `certificateRemovalOf`, `certificateLabel`. The certificate state document is `State` `{"name": <the nice_name NPM reports>, "provider": <string>, "domains": [<sorted>]}` and `Version` `{"id": <int>, "createdOn": <string>, "expiresOn": <string>}`. `certificate.request` returns `CertificateView` whose JSON `id` the composites read as `${steps.cert.output.id}`.

**Items:** 30

**Depends on:** Task 11
**Implementer:** dforge:impl-opus-high
**Evaluation:** files 1 - spec 2 - coupling 1 - risk 2 = 6

Decisions this task embodies (spec 6 "Management", "Recovery identities", "Reserved resources", "Admin UI"):
- `npm.certificate.request` sends `provider: "letsencrypt"`, `meta.letsencrypt_email` = the stored `acmeEmail` (`instanceRow.AdminEmail`) and `meta.letsencrypt_agree: true`. It is `LongRunning` with `ApplyTimeout` 10 minutes, and its effect waits `certCommitWait` (10 minutes).
- The identity of a creation is `npm.certificate:?<sorted domains joined by ",">` with recovery `name` of that value and `CommitWait` `certCommitWait`. The module sends no `nice_name`, because NPM overwrites a Let's Encrypt certificate's name with its domains, so the domain set is the only identity there is. `Observe` finds a creation by the exact domain set and a `created_on` not before the intent, and is never exclusive: another certificate for the same domains, made by the operator or by an earlier request, cannot be told from this change's. A creation with a match therefore stays for the operator's `reconcile`, and the undo deletes only the id its effect recorded (NPM's answer, or the candidate the operator picked), never an object found by its domains.
- The domains are validated for HTTP-01: lowercase, sorted, unique, each a DNS name with at least two labels whose last label starts with a letter (no IP address, no single label), no wildcard (HTTP-01 cannot validate one), at most 100 names, each at most 253 characters, labels of 1 to 63 characters made of letters, digits and inner hyphens.
- `npm.certificate.remove` is irreversible with a typed confirmation, refuses a certificate a proxy host still uses (`request.invalid_input`) and a reserved certificate unless the change is a child of the owning exposure's removal. The certificate `npm.admin.expose` left behind is removable once `npm.admin.unexpose` dropped its `npm_reserved` rows.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `internal/modules/npm/certificate_test.go`:

```go
package npm

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"net/http"
	"reflect"
	"slices"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/npm/npmapi"
)

// ceAPI is the module's NPM client with CreateCertificate replaced.
type ceAPI struct {
	npmAPI
	create func(ctx context.Context, b npmapi.CertificateBody) (npmapi.Certificate, error)
}

func (a ceAPI) CreateCertificate(ctx context.Context, b npmapi.CertificateBody) (npmapi.Certificate, error) {
	return a.create(ctx, b)
}

// ceReplaceCreate returns n with its NPM client answering CreateCertificate
// through create, which also receives the live client.
func ceReplaceCreate(n nm, create func(ctx context.Context, live npmAPI, b npmapi.CertificateBody) (npmapi.Certificate, error)) nm {
	inner := n.d.api
	n.d.api = func(ctx context.Context, env core.Env) (npmAPI, error) {
		live, err := inner(ctx, env)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		return ceAPI{npmAPI: live, create: func(ctx context.Context, b npmapi.CertificateBody) (npmapi.Certificate, error) {
			return create(ctx, live, b)
		}}, nil
	}
	return n
}

// ceRecordBodies returns n with every CreateCertificate body appended to
// bodies before the live client handles it.
func ceRecordBodies(n nm, bodies *[]npmapi.CertificateBody) nm {
	return ceReplaceCreate(n, func(ctx context.Context, live npmAPI, b npmapi.CertificateBody) (npmapi.Certificate, error) {
		*bodies = append(*bodies, b)
		return live.CreateCertificate(ctx, b)
	})
}

func ceSetup(t *testing.T) (*thHost, nm, thInstance) {
	t.Helper()
	h := newTHHost(t)
	inst := h.deployed()
	return h, h.nm(), inst
}

func ceErr(t *testing.T, err error) *errcode.Error {
	t.Helper()
	var ec *errcode.Error
	if !errors.As(err, &ec) {
		t.Fatalf("error = %v, want an *errcode.Error", err)
	}
	return ec
}

func ceJSON(t *testing.T, raw []byte) any {
	t.Helper()
	var v any
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &v); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("decode %s: %v", raw, err)
	}
	return v
}

func ceCerts(t *testing.T, n nm, env core.Env) []npmapi.Certificate {
	t.Helper()
	api, err := n.d.api(context.Background(), env)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("open the NPM client: %v", err)
	}
	certs, err := api.Certificates(context.Background())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("list certificates: %v", err)
	}
	return certs
}

func ceReserve(t *testing.T, h *thHost, id int, owner string) {
	t.Helper()
	ctx := context.Background()
	err := h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		return addReserved(ctx, tx, reservedRow{Kind: "certificate", ObjectID: id, Owner: owner, Exposure: "npm.example.com", ChangeID: "c0"})
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("reserve certificate %d: %v", id, err)
	}
}

func TestCeSpecs(t *testing.T) {
	n := newTHHost(t).nm()
	req, rem := n.certificateRequest().Spec, n.certificateRemove().Spec
	if req.Name != "npm.certificate.request" || req.Version != 1 || req.Class != core.ClassCompensable || req.Side != core.SideAgent ||
		!req.LongRunning || req.ApplyTimeout != 10*time.Minute || !slices.Equal(req.LockKeys, []string{"npm"}) {
		t.Errorf("request spec = %+v", req)
	}
	if rem.Name != "npm.certificate.remove" || rem.Version != 1 || rem.Class != core.ClassIrreversible || rem.Side != core.SideAgent ||
		rem.LongRunning || !slices.Equal(rem.LockKeys, []string{"npm"}) {
		t.Errorf("remove spec = %+v", rem)
	}
	for _, s := range []core.ActionSpec{req, rem} {
		if core.RequiredScope(s) != core.ScopeOperate {
			t.Errorf("%s: scope = %s, want operate", s.Name, core.RequiredScope(s))
		}
	}
	rh, mh := n.certificateRequest().Handler, n.certificateRemove().Handler
	if rh.Observe == nil || rh.Inverse == nil || rh.Resolved == nil {
		t.Errorf("request handler misses Observe, Inverse or Resolved: %+v", rh)
	}
	if mh.Observe == nil || mh.Inverse != nil {
		t.Errorf("remove handler: Observe set = %v, Inverse set = %v, want true and false", mh.Observe != nil, mh.Inverse != nil)
	}
}

func TestCeRequestPlan(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n, inst := ceSetup(t)
	handler := certificateRequestHandler{n: n}
	out, err := handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), CertificateInput{Domains: []string{"B.example.com", "a.example.com"}})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Plan: %v", err)
	}
	wantDiff := []core.DiffLine{
		{Op: "+", Text: "certificate for a.example.com, b.example.com"},
		{Op: " ", Text: "issued by Let's Encrypt through NPM, validated over HTTP on port 80"},
		{Op: " ", Text: "contact " + inst.Email},
		{Op: " ", Text: "the request waits up to 10 minutes for the ACME order"},
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(out.Diff, wantDiff) || out.StateHash == "" {
		t.Errorf("plan = %+v, want diff %+v and a state hash", out, wantDiff)
	}
	same, err := handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), CertificateInput{Domains: []string{"a.example.com", "b.example.com"}})
	if err != nil || same.StateHash != out.StateHash {
		t.Errorf("the same names in another order: hash %q, error %v, want %q", same.StateHash, err, out.StateHash)
	}
	other, err := handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), CertificateInput{Domains: []string{"a.example.com"}})
	if err != nil || other.StateHash == out.StateHash {
		t.Errorf("other names: hash %q, error %v, want a different hash", other.StateHash, err)
	}
}

func TestCeRequestPlanRefusesInvalidDomains(t *testing.T) {
	h, n, _ := ceSetup(t)
	handler := certificateRequestHandler{n: n}
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name    string
		domains []string
		path    string
	}{
		{"none", nil, "/domains"},
		{"wildcard", []string{"*.example.com"}, "/domains/0"},
		{"single label", []string{"localhost"}, "/domains/0"},
		{"ip address", []string{"192.0.2.1"}, "/domains/0"},
		{"underscore", []string{"ok.example.com", "my_host.example.com"}, "/domains/1"},
		{"leading hyphen", []string{"-a.example.com"}, "/domains/0"},
		{"duplicate after lowercasing", []string{"a.example.com", "A.example.com"}, "/domains/1"},
		{"label too long", []string{strings.Repeat("a", 64) + ".example.com"}, "/domains/0"},
	} {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			_, err := handler.Plan(context.Background(), h.env(""), CertificateInput{Domains: tc.domains})
			if !core.IsDefinite(err) {
				t.Fatalf("error = %v, want a Definite refusal", err)
			}
			ec := ceErr(t, err)
			if ec.Code != errcode.InvalidInput || len(ec.Fields) == 0 || ec.Fields[0].Path != tc.path {
				t.Errorf("error = %+v, want request.invalid_input at %s", ec, tc.path)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestCeRequestNeedsAnAnsweringInstance(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	bare := newTHHost(t)
	bare.addChange("c1", "npm.certificate.request")
	handler := certificateRequestHandler{n: bare.nm()}
	in := CertificateInput{Domains: []string{"a.example.com"}}
	for name, run := range map[string]func() error{
		"plan":  func() error { _, err := handler.Plan(ctx, bare.env(""), in); return err },
		"apply": func() error { _, err := handler.Apply(ctx, bare.env("c1"), in); return err },
	} {
		err := run()
		if !core.IsDefinite(err) || ceErr(t, err).Code != errcode.NotFound {
			t.Errorf("%s without an instance: error = %v, want a Definite not_found", name, err)
		}
	}
	if got := bare.journal.effects(); len(got) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("journaled %d effects without an instance, want none", len(got))
	}

	h, n, _ := ceSetup(t)
	n.d.api = func(context.Context, core.Env) (npmAPI, error) { return nil, errors.New("npm: connection refused") }
	_, err := certificateRequestHandler{n: n}.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), in)
	if !core.IsDefinite(err) || ceErr(t, err).Code != errcode.NPMUnhealthy {
		t.Errorf("plan with NPM down: error = %v, want a Definite npm.unhealthy", err)
	}
}

func TestCeRequestApplySendsTheLetsEncryptBodyAndJournalsTheDomainSet(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n, inst := ceSetup(t)
	h.addChange("c1", "npm.certificate.request")
	env := h.env("c1")
	var bodies []npmapi.CertificateBody
	handler := certificateRequestHandler{n: ceRecordBodies(n, &bodies)}
	inputs := []CertificateInput{
		{Domains: []string{"B.example.com", "a.example.com"}},
		{Domains: []string{"app.example.org"}},
	}
	var views []CertificateView
	for i, in := range inputs {
		v, err := handler.Apply(ctx, env, in)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("Apply %d: %v", i+1, err)
		}
		views = append(views, v)
	}
	wantDomains := [][]string{{"a.example.com", "b.example.com"}, {"app.example.org"}}
	effects := h.journal.effects()
	if len(bodies) != 2 || len(effects) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("%d bodies and %d effects, want 2 each", len(bodies), len(effects))
	}
	for i, e := range effects {
		identity := strings.Join(wantDomains[i], ",")
		wantBody := npmapi.CertificateBody{
			Provider: "letsencrypt", DomainNames: wantDomains[i],
			Meta: npmapi.CertificateMeta{LetsencryptEmail: inst.Email, LetsencryptAgree: true},
		}
		if !reflect.DeepEqual(bodies[i], wantBody) {
			t.Errorf("body %d = %+v, want %+v and no nice_name", i+1, bodies[i], wantBody)
		}
		v := views[i]
		if v.Name != strings.Join(wantDomains[i], ", ") || !reflect.DeepEqual(v.Domains, wantDomains[i]) || v.Reserved != "" || v.Unclaimed {
			t.Errorf("view %d = %+v, want the name NPM gave (the domains joined by \", \") and domains %v", i+1, v, wantDomains[i])
		}
		if e.Seq != i+1 || e.Kind != core.EffectCreate || e.State != core.EffectMade || e.Local || e.CommitWait != 10*time.Minute ||
			e.Resource != "npm.certificate:"+strconv.Itoa(v.ID) || !reflect.DeepEqual(e.Recovery, &core.RecoveryID{Kind: "name", Value: identity}) || e.Post == nil {
			t.Errorf("effect %d = %+v, want a made creation recovered by the name %q", i+1, e, identity)
		}
	}
	posts := 0
	for _, r := range h.npm.Requests() {
		if r.Method == http.MethodPost && r.Path == "/api/nginx/certificates" {
			posts++
			if strings.Contains(string(r.Body), "nice_name") {
				t.Errorf("the request body %s carries a nice_name", r.Body)
			}
		}
	}
	if posts != 2 {
		t.Errorf("NPM received %d certificate creations, want 2", posts)
	}
	wantState := ceJSON(t, []byte(`{"name":"a.example.com, b.example.com","provider":"letsencrypt","domains":["a.example.com","b.example.com"]}`))
	if got := ceJSON(t, effects[0].Post.State); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, wantState) {
		t.Errorf("post state = %v, want %v", got, wantState)
	}
	if err := handler.Verify(ctx, env, inputs[0], views[0]); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("Verify: %v", err)
	}
	api, err := n.d.api(ctx, env)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := api.DeleteCertificate(ctx, views[1].ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := handler.Verify(ctx, env, inputs[1], views[1]); err == nil {
		t.Error("Verify passed for a certificate NPM no longer holds")
	}
}

func TestCeRequestVerifyFindsTheCertificateFromTheChangeAlone(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n, _ := ceSetup(t)
	h.addChange("c1", "npm.certificate.request")
	env := h.env("c1")
	handler := certificateRequestHandler{n: n}
	in := CertificateInput{Domains: []string{"a.example.com"}}
	if _, err := handler.Apply(ctx, env, in); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := handler.Verify(ctx, env, in, CertificateView{}); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("Verify with the zero output: %v", err)
	}
	if err := handler.Verify(ctx, env, CertificateInput{Domains: []string{"other.example.com"}}, CertificateView{}); err == nil {
		t.Error("Verify with the zero output passed for other domains")
	}
}

func TestCeRequestRefusedByNPMIsDefiniteAndSkipped(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n, _ := ceSetup(t)
	h.addChange("c1", "npm.certificate.request")
	n = ceReplaceCreate(n, func(context.Context, npmAPI, npmapi.CertificateBody) (npmapi.Certificate, error) {
		return npmapi.Certificate{}, &npmapi.APIError{Status: 400, Message: "domain is invalid"}
	})
	_, err := certificateRequestHandler{n: n}.Apply(ctx, h.env("c1"), CertificateInput{Domains: []string{"a.example.com"}})
	if !core.IsDefinite(err) {
		t.Fatalf("error = %v, want a Definite refusal", err)
	}
	effects := h.journal.effects()
	if len(effects) != 1 || effects[0].State != core.EffectSkipped {
		t.Errorf("effects = %+v, want one skipped effect", effects)
	}
}

func TestCeRequestLostResponseLeavesTheEffectObservable(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n, _ := ceSetup(t)
	h.addChange("c1", "npm.certificate.request")
	env := h.env("c1")
	n = ceReplaceCreate(n, func(ctx context.Context, live npmAPI, b npmapi.CertificateBody) (npmapi.Certificate, error) {
		if _, err := live.CreateCertificate(ctx, b); err != nil {
			return npmapi.Certificate{}, err
		}
		return npmapi.Certificate{}, errors.New("npm: connection reset by peer")
	})
	handler := certificateRequestHandler{n: n}
	_, err := handler.Apply(ctx, env, CertificateInput{Domains: []string{"a.example.com"}})
	if err == nil || core.IsDefinite(err) {
		t.Fatalf("error = %v, want a failure that is not Definite", err)
	}
	effects := h.journal.effects()
	if len(effects) != 1 || effects[0].State != core.EffectIntended || effects[0].Resource != "npm.certificate:?a.example.com" {
		t.Fatalf("effects = %+v, want one intended creation of npm.certificate:?a.example.com", effects)
	}
	certs := ceCerts(t, n, env)
	if len(certs) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("NPM holds %d certificates, want the one the lost response created", len(certs))
	}
	created, err := certificateCreatedOn(certs[0].CreatedOn)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	e := effects[0]
	e.CommitBy = created.Add(-time.Second).Add(e.CommitWait)
	obs, err := handler.Observe(ctx, env, e)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Observe: %v", err)
	}
	if obs.Matches != 1 || obs.Exclusive || obs.Resource != "npm.certificate:"+strconv.Itoa(certs[0].ID) {
		t.Errorf("observation = %+v, want one match of certificate %d that is not exclusive", obs, certs[0].ID)
	}
}

// ceCreation is an intended creation of the certificate for domains whose
// intent time is intent.
func ceCreation(domains []string, intent time.Time) core.Effect {
	identity := certificateIdentity(domains)
	return core.Effect{
		ChangeID: "c1", Seq: 1, Kind: core.EffectCreate, Resource: "npm.certificate:?" + identity,
		Recovery: &core.RecoveryID{Kind: "name", Value: identity}, State: core.EffectIntended,
		CommitWait: certCommitWait, CommitBy: intent.Add(certCommitWait),
	}
}

func TestCeCreatedOn(t *testing.T) {
	want := time.Date(2026, 11, 2, 10, 0, 1, 0, time.UTC)
	for _, s := range []string{"2026-11-02T10:00:01Z", "2026-11-02T10:00:01.000Z", "2026-11-02 10:00:01"} {
		if got, err := certificateCreatedOn(s); err != nil || !got.Equal(want) {
			t.Errorf("%q parses to %v (%v), want %v", s, got, err, want)
		}
	}
	if _, err := certificateCreatedOn("yesterday"); err == nil {
		t.Error("an unparseable time was accepted")
	}
}

func TestCeIdentityIsTheSortedDomainSet(t *testing.T) {
	in := []string{"b.example.com", "a.example.com"}
	if got := certificateIdentity(in); got != "a.example.com,b.example.com" {
		t.Errorf("identity = %q, want the sorted names joined by a comma", got)
	}
	if in[0] != "b.example.com" {
		t.Errorf("the identity sorted its argument in place: %v", in)
	}
}

func TestCeObserveCreationMatchesTheExactDomainSetNotBeforeTheIntent(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n, _ := ceSetup(t)
	handler := certificateRequestHandler{n: n}
	const before, after = "2026-11-02T09:00:00.000Z", "2026-11-02T10:00:05.000Z"
	seed := func(createdOn string, domains ...string) npmapi.Certificate {
		return h.npm.SeedCertificate(npmapi.Certificate{
			CreatedOn: createdOn, NiceName: strings.Join(domains, ", "), Provider: "letsencrypt", DomainNames: domains,
		})
	}
	wanted := []string{"b.example.com", "a.example.com"}
	e := ceCreation(wanted, time.Date(2026, 11, 2, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC))

	none, err := handler.Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), e)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if none.Matches != 0 || string(none.Current.State) != "null" || none.Exclusive || len(none.Candidates) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("no certificate: observation = %+v, want Matches 0, state null and not exclusive", none)
	}

	seed(before, "a.example.com", "b.example.com")
	seed(after, "a.example.com")
	seed(after, "a.example.com", "b.example.com", "c.example.com")
	seed(after, "a.example.com", "x.example.com")
	none, err = handler.Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), e)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if none.Matches != 0 || string(none.Current.State) != "null" || none.Exclusive {
		t.Errorf("same domains created before the intent, a subset, a superset and another set: observation = %+v, want Matches 0 and state null", none)
	}

	one := seed(after, "b.example.com", "a.example.com")
	wantState, err := certificateState(one)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for name, intent := range map[string]time.Time{
		"an intent just before the creation":          time.Date(2026, 11, 2, 10, 0, 4, 0, time.UTC),
		"an intent within the second of the creation": time.Date(2026, 11, 2, 10, 0, 5, 700_000_000, time.UTC),
		"an intent long before the creation":          time.Date(2026, 11, 2, 9, 30, 0, 0, time.UTC),
	} {
		obs, err := handler.Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), ceCreation(wanted, intent))
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("%s: %v", name, err)
		}
		if obs.Matches != 1 || obs.Exclusive || obs.Resource != "npm.certificate:"+strconv.Itoa(one.ID) || string(obs.Current.State) != string(wantState.State) {
			t.Errorf("%s: observation = %+v, want one match of certificate %d, not exclusive, in state %s", name, obs, one.ID, wantState.State)
		}
	}
	late, err := handler.Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), ceCreation(wanted, time.Date(2026, 11, 2, 10, 0, 6, 0, time.UTC)))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if late.Matches != 0 || string(late.Current.State) != "null" {
		t.Errorf("an intent after the creation: observation = %+v, want Matches 0 and state null", late)
	}

	seed(after, "a.example.com", "b.example.com")
	two, err := handler.Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), e)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if two.Matches != 2 || len(two.Candidates) != 2 || string(two.Current.State) != "null" || two.Exclusive {
		t.Fatalf("two certificates: observation = %+v, want Matches 2, two candidates, state null and not exclusive", two)
	}
	if !slices.IsSortedFunc(two.Candidates, func(a, b core.Candidate) int { return strings.Compare(a.Resource, b.Resource) }) {
		t.Errorf("candidates %+v are not sorted by resource", two.Candidates)
	}
}

func TestCeRequestUndoDeletesTheCertificateAndRefusesOneInUse(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n, _ := ceSetup(t)
	h.addChange("c1", "npm.certificate.request")
	env := h.env("c1")
	handler := certificateRequestHandler{n: n}
	view, err := handler.Apply(ctx, env, CertificateInput{Domains: []string{"app.example.com"}})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	made := h.journal.effects()[0]

	h.npm.SeedProxyHost(npmapi.ProxyHost{DomainNames: []string{"app.example.com"}, ForwardScheme: "http", ForwardHost: "app", ForwardPort: 8080, CertificateID: view.ID})
	_, err = handler.Inverse(ctx, env, made)
	if !core.IsDefinite(err) || ceErr(t, err).Code != errcode.InvalidInput || !strings.Contains(ceErr(t, err).Detail, "app.example.com") {
		t.Fatalf("undo of a certificate in use: error = %v, want a Definite invalid_input naming the proxy host", err)
	}
	if len(ceCerts(t, n, env)) != 1 {
		t.Fatal("the certificate in use was deleted")
	}

	api, err := n.d.api(ctx, env)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	hosts, err := api.ProxyHosts(ctx)
	if err != nil || len(hosts) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("proxy hosts = %v, %v", hosts, err)
	}
	if err := api.DeleteProxyHost(ctx, hosts[0].ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if res, err := handler.Inverse(ctx, env, made); err != nil || res.Resource != "" {
		t.Fatalf("Inverse = %+v, %v, want an empty result", res, err)
	}
	if len(ceCerts(t, n, env)) != 0 {
		t.Error("the certificate is still there after the undo")
	}
	if _, err := handler.Inverse(ctx, env, made); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("undoing a certificate that is already gone: %v", err)
	}
	if obs, err := handler.Observe(ctx, env, made); err != nil || string(obs.Current.State) != "null" {
		t.Errorf("Observe after the undo = %+v, %v, want state null", obs, err)
	}
}

func TestCeResolvedWatchesOnlyASkippedCreation(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n, _ := ceSetup(t)
	handler := certificateRequestHandler{n: n}
	created := func(seq int, domains []string, state core.EffectState) core.Effect {
		identity := certificateIdentity(domains)
		return core.Effect{
			ChangeID: "c1", Seq: seq, Kind: core.EffectCreate, Resource: "npm.certificate:?" + identity,
			Recovery: &core.RecoveryID{Kind: "name", Value: identity}, State: state,
		}
	}
	skipped := created(3, []string{"b.example.com", "a.example.com"}, core.EffectSkipped)
	accepted := created(4, []string{"c.example.com"}, core.EffectAccepted)
	for _, e := range []core.Effect{skipped, accepted} {
		err := h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error { return handler.Resolved(ctx, tx, e, "reconcile") })
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("Resolved(%s): %v", e.State, err)
		}
	}
	watching, err := watched(ctx, h.db.Read(), "certificate")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !watching["a.example.com,b.example.com"] || watching["c.example.com"] || len(watching) != 1 {
		t.Errorf("watched certificates = %v, want only the sorted domain set a.example.com,b.example.com", watching)
	}
}

func TestCeRemovePlanApplyAndVerify(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n, _ := ceSetup(t)
	seeded := h.npm.SeedCertificate(npmapi.Certificate{
		NiceName: "old.example.com", Provider: "letsencrypt",
		DomainNames: []string{"old.example.com", "www.old.example.com"}, ExpiresOn: "2027-03-04 05:06:07",
	})
	handler := certificateRemoveHandler{n: n}
	in := CertificateRemoveInput{ID: seeded.ID}
	plan, err := handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Plan: %v", err)
	}
	wantDiff := []core.DiffLine{
		{Op: "-", Text: fmt.Sprintf("certificate old.example.com (id %d)", seeded.ID)},
		{Op: " ", Text: "domains old.example.com, www.old.example.com"},
		{Op: " ", Text: "expires 2027-03-04 05:06:07"},
		{Op: " ", Text: "the certificate cannot be recovered"},
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(plan.Diff, wantDiff) || plan.StateHash == "" || plan.Confirmation != "old.example.com" {
		t.Errorf("plan = %+v, want diff %+v, a state hash and the confirmation old.example.com", plan, wantDiff)
	}
	if err := handler.Verify(ctx, h.env(""), in, CertificateView{}); err == nil {
		t.Error("Verify passed while the certificate still exists")
	}

	h.addChange("c2", "npm.certificate.remove")
	env := h.env("c2")
	view, err := handler.Apply(ctx, env, in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Apply: %v", err)
	}
	wantView := CertificateView{ID: seeded.ID, Name: "old.example.com", Domains: []string{"old.example.com", "www.old.example.com"}, ExpiresOn: "2027-03-04 05:06:07"}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(view, wantView) {
		t.Errorf("view = %+v, want %+v", view, wantView)
	}
	if len(ceCerts(t, n, env)) != 0 {
		t.Error("the certificate is still in NPM")
	}
	effects := h.journal.effects()
	if len(effects) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("journaled %d effects, want 1", len(effects))
	}
	e := effects[0]
	if e.Kind != core.EffectDelete || e.State != core.EffectMade || e.Local || e.CommitWait != 10*time.Minute ||
		e.Resource != "npm.certificate:"+strconv.Itoa(seeded.ID) || e.Recovery != nil {
		t.Errorf("effect = %+v", e)
	}
	wantBefore := ceJSON(t, []byte(`{"name":"old.example.com","provider":"letsencrypt","domains":["old.example.com","www.old.example.com"]}`))
	if got := ceJSON(t, e.Before.State); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, wantBefore) {
		t.Errorf("before state = %v, want %v", got, wantBefore)
	}
	if err := handler.Verify(ctx, env, in, view); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("Verify after the removal: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestCeRemoveRefusals(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n, _ := ceSetup(t)
	handler := certificateRemoveHandler{n: n}
	used := h.npm.SeedCertificate(npmapi.Certificate{NiceName: "app.example.com", Provider: "letsencrypt", DomainNames: []string{"app.example.com"}})
	h.npm.SeedProxyHost(npmapi.ProxyHost{DomainNames: []string{"app.example.com"}, ForwardScheme: "http", ForwardHost: "app", ForwardPort: 8080, CertificateID: used.ID})
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name   string
		id     int
		code   errcode.Code
		detail string
	}{
		{"zero id", 0, errcode.InvalidInput, ""},
		{"unknown certificate", 4242, errcode.NotFound, ""},
		{"certificate in use", used.ID, errcode.InvalidInput, "app.example.com"},
	} {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			_, err := handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), CertificateRemoveInput{ID: tc.id})
			if !core.IsDefinite(err) {
				t.Fatalf("error = %v, want a Definite refusal", err)
			}
			if ec := ceErr(t, err); ec.Code != tc.code || !strings.Contains(ec.Detail, tc.detail) {
				t.Errorf("error = %+v, want %s containing %q", ec, tc.code, tc.detail)
			}
		})
	}
	if len(ceCerts(t, n, h.env(""))) != 1 {
		t.Error("a refused removal changed NPM")
	}
}

func TestCeRemoveOfAReservedCertificateNeedsTheOwnersRemoval(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name   string
		owner  string
		parent string
		refuse bool
	}{
		{"operator", "npm.admin.expose", "", true},
		{"the owner's own composite", "npm.admin.expose", "npm.admin.expose", true},
		{"another owner's removal", "npm.admin.expose", "panel.exposure.remove", true},
		{"the admin exposure's removal", "npm.admin.expose", "npm.admin.unexpose", false},
		{"the panel exposure's removal", "panel.exposure.add", "panel.exposure.remove", false},
	} {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h, n, _ := ceSetup(t)
			handler := certificateRemoveHandler{n: n}
			cert := h.npm.SeedCertificate(npmapi.Certificate{NiceName: "admin.example.com", Provider: "letsencrypt", DomainNames: []string{"admin.example.com"}})
			ceReserve(t, h, cert.ID, tc.owner)
			in := CertificateRemoveInput{ID: cert.ID}
			h.addChange("c3", "npm.certificate.remove")
			env := h.env("c3")
			if tc.parent != "" {
				env = h.child("c3", tc.parent)
			}
			_, err := handler.Plan(ctx, env, in)
			if tc.refuse {
				if !core.IsDefinite(err) || ceErr(t, err).Code != errcode.NPMReserved {
					t.Fatalf("Plan: error = %v, want a Definite npm.reserved", err)
				}
				if _, err := handler.Apply(ctx, env, in); err == nil || ceErr(t, err).Code != errcode.NPMReserved {
					t.Errorf("Apply: error = %v, want npm.reserved", err)
				}
				if len(ceCerts(t, n, h.env(""))) != 1 {
					t.Error("a refused removal deleted the certificate")
				}
				return
			}
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("Plan: %v", err)
			}
			if _, err := handler.Apply(ctx, env, in); err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("Apply: %v", err)
			}
			if len(ceCerts(t, n, h.env(""))) != 0 {
				t.Error("the owner's removal left the certificate in NPM")
			}
			if _, found, err := reservedOf(ctx, h.db.Read(), "certificate", cert.ID); err != nil || found {
				t.Errorf("reservedOf after the removal = %v, %v, want no row", found, err)
			}
		})
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `internal/modules/npm/certificate.go` (package `npm`):

1. **Constructors.** `func (n nm) certificateRequest() core.ActionDef` is `core.DefineMutating[CertificateInput, CertificateView]` with `ActionSpec{Name: ID + ".certificate.request", Summary: "Request a Let's Encrypt certificate through Nginx Proxy Manager.", Version: 1, Class: core.ClassCompensable, Side: core.SideAgent, LockKeys: []string{lockKey}, LongRunning: true, ApplyTimeout: 10 * time.Minute}` and the handler `certificateRequestHandler{n nm}`. `func (n nm) certificateRemove() core.ActionDef` is `core.DefineMutating[CertificateRemoveInput, CertificateView]` with `Name: ID + ".certificate.remove"`, Summary "Delete a certificate from Nginx Proxy Manager.", `Class: core.ClassIrreversible`, the same side and lock, no `LongRunning`, and the handler `certificateRemoveHandler{n nm}`.
2. **Client and refusals.** `func (n nm) certificateClient(ctx, env) (npmAPI, instanceRow, error)` does what Task 17's `accessListClient` does (no row: `errcode.NotFound` "no Nginx Proxy Manager instance is deployed on this host"; an `n.d.api` error: `errcode.Wrap(errcode.NPMUnhealthy, err, "the Nginx Proxy Manager API is not available")`; both `core.Definite`) and also returns the `instanceRow`. `certificateInvalid(path, msg string) error` is a `core.Definite` `request.invalid_input` `*errcode.Error` with one field error.
3. **Domains.** `certificateDomains(in []string) ([]string, error)` lowercases each name, then refuses with `certificateInvalid`: none (path `/domains`); more than 100; a name that does not match the DNS rule of the decisions above (path `/domains/<i>`, the index in the input); a name equal to an earlier one after lowercasing (path of the later one). The result is sorted.
4. **State and view.** `certificateState(c npmapi.Certificate) (core.StateDoc, error)` renders the document in Interfaces (`domains` sorted, never null). `certificateView(c npmapi.Certificate) CertificateView` copies ID, `Name` from `NiceName`, `Domains` (a non-nil copy in NPM's order) and `ExpiresOn`; `Reserved` empty, `Unclaimed` false. `certificateLabel(c npmapi.Certificate) string` is `NiceName`, or the decimal id when `NiceName` is empty. `certificateIdentity(domains []string) string` is a sorted copy of `domains` joined by `,` (it does not sort its argument in place, and changes no case); it is the creation identity and the `Recovery.Value`. `certificateCreatedOn(s string) (time.Time, error)` accepts RFC 3339 (with or without fractional seconds) and `2006-01-02 15:04:05` as UTC and returns an error otherwise. There is no per-change name and no sequence prediction for a certificate.
5. **Users, reservation.** `certificateUsers(ctx, api, id) ([]string, error)` returns the sorted first domains of the proxy hosts whose `CertificateID` equals id; a non-empty result refuses with `certificateInvalid("/id", "certificate <id> is used by proxy hosts: <domains joined by \", \">")`. `certificateRemovalOf(owner)` and `certificateReservedBy(ctx, env, id)` do what Task 17's `accessListRemovalOf` and `accessListReservedBy` do for kind `certificate` (error `npm.reserved`, detail "certificate <id> belongs to the exposure <exposure>").
6. **`certificateRequestHandler`.**
   - `Plan`: `certificateDomains`, `certificateClient`; `Diff` exactly the four lines the test pins (names joined by `", "`, the contact is `row.AdminEmail`); `StateHash` the hex SHA-256 of the sorted names joined by `,` plus `|` plus the email.
   - `Apply`: validate, `certificateClient`. The effect is `npmEffect(core.EffectCreate, resourceOf(nounCertificate, "?"+certificateIdentity(<sorted domains>)), json.RawMessage("null"), certCommitWait)`. `n.run` with `h.n.observeCertificate`; `do` calls `CreateCertificate(ctx, npmapi.CertificateBody{Provider: "letsencrypt", DomainNames: <sorted domains>, Meta: npmapi.CertificateMeta{LetsencryptEmail: row.AdminEmail, LetsencryptAgree: true}})` (no `NiceName`: NPM names the certificate itself) and returns `resourceOf(nounCertificate, <id>)`, handing NPM's error back unchanged. The call runs under the engine's 10-minute apply context; set no other timeout. Return `certificateView(created)`.
   - `Verify`: with `out.ID != 0` read that certificate and require its sorted `DomainNames` to equal the validated `in.Domains`; otherwise (recovery passes the zero output) require that at least one certificate has exactly that sorted domain set. A missing certificate or a different set is an error. Read-only.
   - `Observe`: `h.n.observeCertificate`.
   - `Inverse`: as Task 17's add undo for certificates: `certificateClient`, parse the id, `Certificate(ctx, id)` (`IsNotFound` gives the empty result), refuse a certificate in use, `DeleteCertificate` (success or `IsNotFound` gives the empty result, `IsClientError` gives `core.Definite(err)`). It does not check `npm_reserved`: the composite's own unwind undoes the certificate its child created.
   - `Resolved`: as Task 17's, with kind `certificate`: the watched identity is `e.Recovery.Value`, which is the sorted domain set joined by `,`.
7. **`certificateRemoveHandler`.**
   - A private `target(ctx, env, in) (npmAPI, npmapi.Certificate, reservedRow, bool, error)`: refuse `in.ID <= 0` (`certificateInvalid("/id", "a certificate id is required")`); `certificateClient`; `Certificate(ctx, in.ID)` (`IsNotFound` gives `core.Definite(errcode.New(errcode.NotFound, "certificate <id> not found"))`); `certificateReservedBy`; `certificateUsers`, in this order.
   - `Plan`: `target`; `Diff` exactly as the test pins (the expires line only when `ExpiresOn` is not empty; the last line always); `StateHash` the hex SHA-256 of `json.Marshal` of the `StateDoc`; `Confirmation` is `certificateLabel`.
   - `Apply`: `target` again; the effect is `npmEffect(core.EffectDelete, resourceOf(nounCertificate, strconv.Itoa(id)), <State>, certCommitWait)` with `e.Before.Version` set; `n.run` with `h.n.observeCertificate` and a `do` that calls `DeleteCertificate`. When `target` returned a reserved row, drop its exposure's rows afterwards in one `env.DB.Write` with `dropReservedExposure` (a failure is returned wrapped, not Definite). Return `certificateView` of the certificate as removed, `Reserved` set to the row's owner when there was one.
   - `Verify`: the certificate is gone (`IsNotFound`) for `out.ID`, or `in.ID` when the output is zero; an existing certificate is an error.
   - `Observe`: `h.n.observeCertificate`. The handler has no `Inverse` (the action is irreversible) and no `Resolved`.
8. **`func (n nm) observeCertificate(ctx, env, e core.Effect) (core.Observation, error)`** reads only. A creation identity (starts with `?`) lists the certificates and keeps those whose sorted `DomainNames`, joined by `,`, equal the identity without the `?` and whose `created_on` is not before the intent time, which is `e.CommitBy` minus `e.CommitWait` truncated to the second (NPM's `created_on` is whole seconds); a `created_on` that `certificateCreatedOn` cannot read is an error. It answers like Task 17's `observeAccessList` for a creation, except that `Exclusive` is false in every case: none gives `Observation{Current: nullState()}`; one gives `{Current: its state, Resource: its resource, Matches: 1}`; several give `{Current: nullState(), Matches: len, Candidates: every match with its state}` sorted by resource string. Nothing about the answer tells the operator's `reconcile` that the match is this change's, so an intended creation with a match is resolved by the operator and never by Darkpanel. Any other identity is an id: `Certificate(ctx, id)` gives `named(<state JSON>)`, and `npmapi.IsNotFound` gives `named(nullState().State)`. A resource that is not `npm.certificate:` or names no id is an error.
9. Touch no other file. `Manifest()` is unchanged.

### Task 19: npm: list reads and unclaimed objects

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/modules/npm/lists.go`
- Test: `internal/modules/npm/lists_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Contracts "NPM wire shapes" (`ProxyHostListInput`, `AccessListListInput`, `CertificateListInput`, `ProxyHostList`, `AccessListList`, `CertificateList`, `ProxyHostView`, `AccessListView`, `CertificateView`, `AccessClientInput`); "NPM API client" (`ProxyHosts`, `AccessLists`, `Certificates`, `npmapi.ProxyHost`, `AccessList`, `Certificate`); "NPM deps" (`nm`, `deps.api`); "NPM store" (`loadInstance`, `reservedOf`, `watched`, `addWatch`, `addReserved`); "NPM identities" (the `npm_watch` identities: a proxy host's and a certificate's sorted domains joined by `,`, an access list's name); "NPM test host".
- Produces: `func (n nm) proxyhostList() core.ActionDef`, `func (n nm) accesslistList() core.ActionDef`, `func (n nm) certificateList() core.ActionDef` (Contracts "NPM actions"; Task 27 registers them; the web tabs of Task 9 read them as `npm.proxyhost.list`, `npm.accesslist.list`, `npm.certificate.list`). Names this task declares in package `npm`, used by no other task: `listProxyHostReader`, `listAccessListReader`, `listCertificateReader`, `listClient`, `listProxyHostItems`, `listAccessListItems`, `listCertificateItems`, `listMarks`.

**Depends on:** Task 11
**Implementer:** dforge:impl-sonnet-medium
**Evaluation:** files 1 - spec 2 - coupling 1 - risk 0 = 4

Decisions this task embodies (spec 6 "Reserved resources" and "Settlement"; Contracts "NPM identities"):
- Each read lists NPM's objects ordered by id and marks each item. `Reserved` is the owning action from `npm_reserved` (kind `proxy_host`, `access_list` or `certificate`), or empty. `Unclaimed` is true when the object's identity is in `npm_watch` for its kind: a proxy host by its sorted domain names joined by `,` (the same value the creation effect used as `Recovery.Value`), an access list by its name, a certificate by its sorted domain names joined by `,` (the same value its creation effect used as `Recovery.Value`; NPM sets a Let's Encrypt certificate's `nice_name` itself, so the name is not an identity). Such an object appeared after the operator resolved its creation `skipped`; the page shows it as an unclaimed Darkpanel object and nothing removes it automatically.
- The reads take no lock, need only scope `read`, and never write. A host without an instance answers `not_found`, an instance NPM does not answer `npm.unhealthy`.
- Every list is an empty array, never `null`, when NPM holds no object.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `internal/modules/npm/lists_test.go`:

```go
package npm

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"reflect"
	"testing"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/npm/npmapi"
)

func lsSetup(t *testing.T) (*thHost, nm) {
	t.Helper()
	h := newTHHost(t)
	h.deployed()
	return h, h.nm()
}

func lsReserve(t *testing.T, h *thHost, kind string, id int, owner string) {
	t.Helper()
	ctx := context.Background()
	err := h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		return addReserved(ctx, tx, reservedRow{Kind: kind, ObjectID: id, Owner: owner, Exposure: "admin.example.com", ChangeID: "c0"})
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("reserve %s %d: %v", kind, id, err)
	}
}

func lsWatch(t *testing.T, h *thHost, kind, identity string) {
	t.Helper()
	ctx := context.Background()
	err := h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error { return addWatch(ctx, tx, kind, identity, "c9", 1) })
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("watch %s %q: %v", kind, identity, err)
	}
}

func lsCode(t *testing.T, err error) errcode.Code {
	t.Helper()
	var ec *errcode.Error
	if !errors.As(err, &ec) {
		t.Fatalf("error = %v, want an *errcode.Error", err)
	}
	return ec.Code
}

func TestLsSpecs(t *testing.T) {
	n := newTHHost(t).nm()
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		def  core.ActionDef
		name string
	}{
		{n.proxyhostList(), "npm.proxyhost.list"},
		{n.accesslistList(), "npm.accesslist.list"},
		{n.certificateList(), "npm.certificate.list"},
	} {
		s := tc.def.Spec
		if s.Name != tc.name || s.Version != 1 || s.Class != core.ClassRead || s.Side != core.SideAgent || len(s.LockKeys) != 0 {
			t.Errorf("%s: spec = %+v", tc.name, s)
		}
		if core.RequiredScope(s) != core.ScopeRead {
			t.Errorf("%s: scope = %s, want read", tc.name, core.RequiredScope(s))
		}
		if tc.def.Handler.Read == nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: no Read handler", tc.name)
		}
	}
}

func TestLsProxyHostsAreMarkedReservedAndUnclaimed(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n := lsSetup(t)
	list := h.npm.SeedAccessList(npmapi.AccessList{Name: "office", Clients: []npmapi.AccessClient{{Address: "10.1.1.1", Directive: "allow"}}})
	cert := h.npm.SeedCertificate(npmapi.Certificate{NiceName: "web.example.com", Provider: "letsencrypt", DomainNames: []string{"web.example.com"}})
	plain := h.npm.SeedProxyHost(npmapi.ProxyHost{
		DomainNames: []string{"web.example.com"}, ForwardScheme: "https", ForwardHost: "web", ForwardPort: 8443,
		CertificateID: cert.ID, AccessListID: list.ID, SSLForced: true, HSTSEnabled: true, AllowWebsocketUpgrade: true,
	})
	reserved := h.npm.SeedProxyHost(npmapi.ProxyHost{DomainNames: []string{"admin.example.com"}, ForwardScheme: "http", ForwardHost: "127.0.0.1", ForwardPort: 81})
	lost := h.npm.SeedProxyHost(npmapi.ProxyHost{DomainNames: []string{"z.example.com", "y.example.com"}, ForwardScheme: "http", ForwardHost: "app", ForwardPort: 80})
	lsReserve(t, h, "proxy_host", reserved.ID, "npm.admin.expose")
	lsWatch(t, h, "proxy_host", "y.example.com,z.example.com")
	lsWatch(t, h, "access_list", "web.example.com")

	out, err := listProxyHostReader{n: n}.Read(ctx, h.env(""), ProxyHostListInput{})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Read: %v", err)
	}
	want := []ProxyHostView{
		{
			ID: plain.ID, Domains: []string{"web.example.com"}, ForwardScheme: "https", ForwardHost: "web", ForwardPort: 8443,
			CertificateID: cert.ID, AccessListID: list.ID, SSLForced: true, HSTS: true, Websockets: true,
		},
		{ID: reserved.ID, Domains: []string{"admin.example.com"}, ForwardScheme: "http", ForwardHost: "127.0.0.1", ForwardPort: 81, Reserved: "npm.admin.expose"},
		{ID: lost.ID, Domains: []string{"z.example.com", "y.example.com"}, ForwardScheme: "http", ForwardHost: "app", ForwardPort: 80, Unclaimed: true},
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(out.Items, want) {
		t.Errorf("items = %+v\nwant   %+v", out.Items, want)
	}
}

func TestLsAccessListsAreMarkedReservedAndUnclaimed(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n := lsSetup(t)
	plain := h.npm.SeedAccessList(npmapi.AccessList{
		Name: "office", SatisfyAny: true,
		Clients: []npmapi.AccessClient{{Address: "203.0.113.0/24", Directive: "allow"}, {Address: "203.0.113.9", Directive: "deny"}},
	})
	reserved := h.npm.SeedAccessList(npmapi.AccessList{Name: "admin-only", Clients: []npmapi.AccessClient{{Address: "198.51.100.1", Directive: "allow"}}})
	lost := h.npm.SeedAccessList(npmapi.AccessList{Name: "darkpanel-c9-1", Clients: []npmapi.AccessClient{{Address: "192.0.2.1", Directive: "allow"}}})
	lsReserve(t, h, "access_list", reserved.ID, "panel.exposure.add")
	lsWatch(t, h, "access_list", "darkpanel-c9-1")
	lsWatch(t, h, "certificate", "office")

	out, err := listAccessListReader{n: n}.Read(ctx, h.env(""), AccessListListInput{})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Read: %v", err)
	}
	want := []AccessListView{
		{ID: plain.ID, Name: "office", SatisfyAny: true, Clients: []AccessClientInput{{Address: "203.0.113.0/24", Directive: "allow"}, {Address: "203.0.113.9", Directive: "deny"}}},
		{ID: reserved.ID, Name: "admin-only", Clients: []AccessClientInput{{Address: "198.51.100.1", Directive: "allow"}}, Reserved: "panel.exposure.add"},
		{ID: lost.ID, Name: "darkpanel-c9-1", Clients: []AccessClientInput{{Address: "192.0.2.1", Directive: "allow"}}, Unclaimed: true},
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(out.Items, want) {
		t.Errorf("items = %+v\nwant   %+v", out.Items, want)
	}
}

func TestLsCertificatesAreMarkedReservedAndUnclaimed(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n := lsSetup(t)
	plain := h.npm.SeedCertificate(npmapi.Certificate{
		NiceName: "web.example.com", Provider: "letsencrypt", DomainNames: []string{"web.example.com", "www.web.example.com"}, ExpiresOn: "2027-03-04 05:06:07",
	})
	reserved := h.npm.SeedCertificate(npmapi.Certificate{NiceName: "admin.example.com", Provider: "letsencrypt", DomainNames: []string{"admin.example.com"}})
	lost := h.npm.SeedCertificate(npmapi.Certificate{
		NiceName: "late.example.com, a.late.example.com", Provider: "letsencrypt", DomainNames: []string{"late.example.com", "a.late.example.com"},
	})
	subset := h.npm.SeedCertificate(npmapi.Certificate{NiceName: "late.example.com", Provider: "letsencrypt", DomainNames: []string{"late.example.com"}})
	lsReserve(t, h, "certificate", reserved.ID, "npm.admin.expose")
	lsWatch(t, h, "certificate", "a.late.example.com,late.example.com")
	lsWatch(t, h, "certificate", "web.example.com")
	lsWatch(t, h, "proxy_host", "web.example.com,www.web.example.com")

	out, err := listCertificateReader{n: n}.Read(ctx, h.env(""), CertificateListInput{})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Read: %v", err)
	}
	want := []CertificateView{
		{ID: plain.ID, Name: "web.example.com", Domains: []string{"web.example.com", "www.web.example.com"}, ExpiresOn: "2027-03-04 05:06:07"},
		{ID: reserved.ID, Name: "admin.example.com", Domains: []string{"admin.example.com"}, Reserved: "npm.admin.expose"},
		{ID: lost.ID, Name: "late.example.com, a.late.example.com", Domains: []string{"late.example.com", "a.late.example.com"}, Unclaimed: true},
		{ID: subset.ID, Name: "late.example.com", Domains: []string{"late.example.com"}},
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(out.Items, want) {
		t.Errorf("items = %+v\nwant   %+v", out.Items, want)
	}
}

func TestLsEmptyListsEncodeAsEmptyArrays(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, n := lsSetup(t)
	env := h.env("")
	proxy, err := listProxyHostReader{n: n}.Read(ctx, env, ProxyHostListInput{})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	access, err := listAccessListReader{n: n}.Read(ctx, env, AccessListListInput{})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	certs, err := listCertificateReader{n: n}.Read(ctx, env, CertificateListInput{})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for name, v := range map[string]any{"proxy hosts": proxy, "access lists": access, "certificates": certs} {
		b, err := json.Marshal(v)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if string(b) != `{"items":[]}` {
			t.Errorf("%s encode as %s, want {\"items\":[]}", name, b)
		}
	}
}

func TestLsReadsRefuseWithoutAnAnsweringInstance(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	reads := func(n nm, env core.Env) map[string]func() error {
		return map[string]func() error{
			"proxy hosts":  func() error { _, err := listProxyHostReader{n: n}.Read(ctx, env, ProxyHostListInput{}); return err },
			"access lists": func() error { _, err := listAccessListReader{n: n}.Read(ctx, env, AccessListListInput{}); return err },
			"certificates": func() error { _, err := listCertificateReader{n: n}.Read(ctx, env, CertificateListInput{}); return err },
		}
	}
	bare := newTHHost(t)
	for name, read := range reads(bare.nm(), bare.env("")) {
		if err := read(); lsCode(t, err) != errcode.NotFound {
			t.Errorf("%s without an instance: error = %v, want not_found", name, err)
		}
	}
	h, n := lsSetup(t)
	n.d.api = func(context.Context, core.Env) (npmAPI, error) { return nil, errors.New("npm: connection refused") }
	for name, read := range reads(n, h.env("")) {
		if err := read(); lsCode(t, err) != errcode.NPMUnhealthy {
			t.Errorf("%s with NPM down: error = %v, want npm.unhealthy", name, err)
		}
	}
}
```

Expected: FAIL with a build error naming the first undefined identifier (`n.proxyhostList undefined`, `undefined: listProxyHostReader`). Quote it in the report.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `internal/modules/npm/lists.go` (package `npm`):

1. **Constructors.** `func (n nm) proxyhostList() core.ActionDef` is `core.DefineRead[ProxyHostListInput, ProxyHostList]` with `ActionSpec{Name: ID + ".proxyhost.list", Summary: "List the proxy hosts of Nginx Proxy Manager.", Version: 1, Side: core.SideAgent}` (no lock keys; `DefineRead` sets the class) and the reader `listProxyHostReader{n nm}`. `accesslistList` (`ID + ".accesslist.list"`, Summary "List the access lists of Nginx Proxy Manager.", `AccessListListInput`, `AccessListList`, `listAccessListReader`) and `certificateList` (`ID + ".certificate.list"`, Summary "List the certificates of Nginx Proxy Manager.", `CertificateListInput`, `CertificateList`, `listCertificateReader`) follow the same shape. Each reader has `Read(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in <its input>) (<its list>, error)`.
2. **Client.** `func (n nm) listClient(ctx, env) (npmAPI, error)` reads `loadInstance(ctx, env.DB.Read())`: an error is returned wrapped as `npm: load the instance: ...`; no row gives `errcode.New(errcode.NotFound, "no Nginx Proxy Manager instance is deployed on this host")`; then `n.d.api(ctx, env)`, whose error becomes `errcode.Wrap(errcode.NPMUnhealthy, err, "the Nginx Proxy Manager API is not available")`.
3. **Marks.** `listMarks` is a small struct holding the reserved lookup and the watched set of one kind: `func (n nm) listMarks(ctx, env, kind string) (listMarks, error)` reads `watched(ctx, env.DB.Read(), kind)` once and keeps `env` for per-object `reservedOf(ctx, env.DB.Read(), kind, id)` lookups; its method `of(ctx context.Context, id int, identity string) (reserved string, unclaimed bool, err error)` returns the row's `Owner` (empty when there is no row) and whether `identity` is watched. Store errors are returned wrapped (`npm: read the reservations: ...`).
4. **Items.**
   - `listProxyHostItems(ctx, env, api) ([]ProxyHostView, error)`: `api.ProxyHosts(ctx)`, sorted by `ID` ascending; each view copies the Contracts fields (`Websockets` from `AllowWebsocketUpgrade`, `HSTS` from `HSTSEnabled`, `Domains` a non-nil copy in NPM's order); its identity is a sorted copy of `DomainNames` joined by `,` (no case change: the creation effect used the domains as sent).
   - `listAccessListItems`: `api.AccessLists(ctx)`, sorted by `ID`; `Clients` as `[]AccessClientInput` (empty slice, not nil); identity is `Name`.
   - `listCertificateItems`: `api.Certificates(ctx)`, sorted by `ID`; `Name` from `NiceName`, `Domains` a non-nil copy in NPM's order, `ExpiresOn`; identity is a sorted copy of `DomainNames` joined by `,`, exactly as for a proxy host (the creation effect's `Recovery.Value`; the exact set, so a certificate for a subset or superset of a watched set is not marked).
   - Each returns a non-nil slice, so an empty NPM encodes as `{"items":[]}`. An NPM read error is returned as `errcode.Wrap(errcode.NPMUnhealthy, err, "the Nginx Proxy Manager API is not available")`.
   - Every reader is `listClient`, then its items, then `{Items: items}`. Nothing writes to NPM, to the journal or to a table.
5. Touch no other file. `Manifest()` is unchanged.
