```go
package npm

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"reflect"
	"slices"
	"strconv"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/npm/npmapi"
)

const puDomain = "app.example.com"

// puInput is a valid proxy host input for puDomain; each change edits it.
func puInput(change ...func(*ProxyHostInput)) ProxyHostInput {
	in := ProxyHostInput{Domains: []string{puDomain}, ForwardScheme: "http", ForwardHost: "app", ForwardPort: 8080}
	for _, c := range change {
		c(&in)
	}
	return in
}

// puHostOf is the proxy host NPM holds for body.
func puHostOf(b npmapi.ProxyHostBody) npmapi.ProxyHost {
	return npmapi.ProxyHost{
		DomainNames: b.DomainNames, ForwardScheme: b.ForwardScheme, ForwardHost: b.ForwardHost, ForwardPort: b.ForwardPort,
		CertificateID: b.CertificateID, AccessListID: b.AccessListID, SSLForced: b.SSLForced, HSTSEnabled: b.HSTSEnabled,
		AllowWebsocketUpgrade: b.AllowWebsocketUpgrade, Locations: b.Locations, AdvancedConfig: b.AdvancedConfig,
	}
}

// puBodyOf is the body members of h.
func puBodyOf(h npmapi.ProxyHost) npmapi.ProxyHostBody {
	return npmapi.ProxyHostBody{
		DomainNames: h.DomainNames, ForwardScheme: h.ForwardScheme, ForwardHost: h.ForwardHost, ForwardPort: h.ForwardPort,
		CertificateID: h.CertificateID, AccessListID: h.AccessListID, SSLForced: h.SSLForced, HSTSEnabled: h.HSTSEnabled,
		AllowWebsocketUpgrade: h.AllowWebsocketUpgrade, Locations: h.Locations, AdvancedConfig: h.AdvancedConfig,
	}
}

// puSeed is a host with a deployed instance and one proxy host made from in.
func puSeed(t *testing.T, in ProxyHostInput) (*thHost, npmapi.ProxyHost) {
	t.Helper()
	h := newTHHost(t)
	h.deployed()
	h.addChange("c0", "npm.admin.expose")
	return h, h.npm.SeedProxyHost(puHostOf(proxyHostBody(in, "")))
}

// puHosts lists the proxy hosts NPM holds, read the way the module reads them.
func puHosts(t *testing.T, h *thHost) []npmapi.ProxyHost {
	t.Helper()
	api, err := h.nm().d.api(context.Background(), h.env(""))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	hosts, err := api.ProxyHosts(context.Background())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return hosts
}

func puJSON(t *testing.T, v any) json.RawMessage {
	t.Helper()
	b, err := json.Marshal(v)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return b
}

func puCoded(t *testing.T, err error, code errcode.Code) {
	t.Helper()
	var coded *errcode.Error
	if !errors.As(err, &coded) || coded.Code != code {
		t.Fatalf("error %v, want code %s", err, code)
	}
}

// puStateBody decodes the State of a proxy host effect document.
func puStateBody(t *testing.T, state json.RawMessage) npmapi.ProxyHostBody {
	t.Helper()
	var b npmapi.ProxyHostBody
	if err := json.Unmarshal(state, &b); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("state %s is not a proxy host body: %v", state, err)
	}
	return b
}

func puSameJSON(a, b json.RawMessage) bool {
	var x, y any
	if json.Unmarshal(a, &x) != nil || json.Unmarshal(b, &y) != nil {
		return false
	}
	return reflect.DeepEqual(x, y)
}

func puEffects(t *testing.T, h *thHost) []core.Effect {
	t.Helper()
	effects, err := h.journal.List(context.Background())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return effects
}

// puReserve records that owner reserved the object of kind and id for the exposure of hostname.
func puReserve(t *testing.T, h *thHost, kind string, id int, owner, hostname string) {
	t.Helper()
	ctx := context.Background()
	err := h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		return addReserved(ctx, tx, reservedRow{Kind: kind, ObjectID: id, Owner: owner, Exposure: hostname, ChangeID: "c0"})
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

func puReserved(t *testing.T, h *thHost, kind string, id int) bool {
	t.Helper()
	_, ok, err := reservedOf(context.Background(), h.db.Read(), kind, id)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return ok
}

// puFault is the failure a fault-injecting NPM client returns; commit makes the call take effect first, as a lost
// response does.
type puFault struct {
	err    error
	commit bool
}

type puFaults struct{ update, create, remove puFault }

// puAPI wraps the module's NPM client with the faults of f.
type puAPI struct {
	npmAPI
	f *puFaults
}

func (a puAPI) UpdateProxyHost(ctx context.Context, id int, b npmapi.ProxyHostBody) (npmapi.ProxyHost, error) {
	return puCall(a.f.update, func() (npmapi.ProxyHost, error) { return a.npmAPI.UpdateProxyHost(ctx, id, b) })
}

func (a puAPI) CreateProxyHost(ctx context.Context, b npmapi.ProxyHostBody) (npmapi.ProxyHost, error) {
	return puCall(a.f.create, func() (npmapi.ProxyHost, error) { return a.npmAPI.CreateProxyHost(ctx, b) })
}

func (a puAPI) DeleteProxyHost(ctx context.Context, id int) error {
	_, err := puCall(a.f.remove, func() (npmapi.ProxyHost, error) { return npmapi.ProxyHost{}, a.npmAPI.DeleteProxyHost(ctx, id) })
	return err
}

func puCall(f puFault, call func() (npmapi.ProxyHost, error)) (npmapi.ProxyHost, error) {
	if f.err == nil {
		return call()
	}
	if f.commit {
		if _, err := call(); err != nil {
			return npmapi.ProxyHost{}, err
		}
	}
	return npmapi.ProxyHost{}, f.err
}

// puFaulty is the module over h whose NPM client fails as f says.
func puFaulty(h *thHost, f *puFaults) nm {
	n := h.nm()
	inner := n.d.api
	n.d.api = func(ctx context.Context, env core.Env) (npmAPI, error) {
		a, err := inner(ctx, env)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		return puAPI{npmAPI: a, f: f}, nil
	}
	return n
}

func TestPuDeclarations(t *testing.T) {
	n := newTHHost(t).nm()
	tests := []struct {
		def       core.ActionDef
		name      string
		converges bool
		in        reflect.Type
	}{
		{n.proxyhostUpdate(), "npm.proxyhost.update", false, reflect.TypeFor[ProxyHostUpdateInput]()},
		{n.proxyhostRemove(), "npm.proxyhost.remove", true, reflect.TypeFor[ProxyHostRemoveInput]()},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		s, hd := tc.def.Spec, tc.def.Handler
		if s.Name != tc.name || s.Version != 1 || s.Class != core.ClassCompensable || s.Side != core.SideAgent ||
			!slices.Equal(s.LockKeys, []string{"npm"}) || s.Converges != tc.converges || s.LongRunning || s.Task || s.Internal ||
			s.Reveal || s.MinScope != "" || s.Available != nil || len(s.Guards) != 0 || s.CompensatedBy != "" || s.ForwardOnly ||
			s.ApplyTimeout != 0 || s.Summary == "" {
			t.Errorf("%s: spec %+v, want a compensable agent action locking npm with the default timeout (converges %v)", tc.name, s, tc.converges)
		}
		if tc.def.InputType != tc.in || tc.def.OutputType != reflect.TypeFor[ProxyHostView]() {
			t.Errorf("%s: input %v and output %v", tc.name, tc.def.InputType, tc.def.OutputType)
		}
		if hd.Plan == nil || hd.Apply == nil || hd.Verify == nil || hd.Observe == nil || hd.Inverse == nil || hd.Read != nil || hd.Task != nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: handler has Plan %v Apply %v Verify %v Observe %v Inverse %v Read %v Task %v", tc.name,
				hd.Plan != nil, hd.Apply != nil, hd.Verify != nil, hd.Observe != nil, hd.Inverse != nil, hd.Read != nil, hd.Task != nil)
		}
	}
}

func TestPuUpdateAppliesTheInput(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, host := puSeed(t, puInput())
	h.addChange("c1", "npm.proxyhost.update")
	next := puInput(func(in *ProxyHostInput) {
		in.ForwardPort = 9090
		in.Websockets = true
	})
	def := h.nm().proxyhostUpdate()
	raw := puJSON(t, ProxyHostUpdateInput{ID: host.ID, ProxyHostInput: next})

	plan, err := def.Handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), raw)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	wantDiff := []core.DiffLine{
		{Op: "-", Text: "forward: http://app:8080"}, {Op: "+", Text: "forward: http://app:9090"},
		{Op: "-", Text: "websockets: off"}, {Op: "+", Text: "websockets: on"},
	}
	if !slices.Equal(plan.Diff, wantDiff) || len(plan.StateHash) != 64 || plan.Confirmation != "" || len(plan.Guards) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("plan %+v, want diff %+v, a 64-digit hash, no confirmation and no guard", plan, wantDiff)
	}
	if got := puHosts(t, h); len(got) != 1 || got[0].ForwardPort != 8080 || len(puEffects(t, h)) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("plan changed the host: %+v, effects %+v", got, puEffects(t, h))
	}
	again, err := def.Handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), raw)
	if err != nil || again.StateHash != plan.StateHash {
		t.Errorf("a second plan hash %q (error %v), want %q", again.StateHash, err, plan.StateHash)
	}

	out, err := def.Handler.Apply(ctx, h.env("c1"), raw)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var view ProxyHostView
	if err := json.Unmarshal(out, &view); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	wantView := ProxyHostView{ID: host.ID, Domains: []string{puDomain}, ForwardScheme: "http", ForwardHost: "app", ForwardPort: 9090, Websockets: true}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(view, wantView) {
		t.Errorf("view %+v, want %+v", view, wantView)
	}
	hosts := puHosts(t, h)
	wantBody := proxyHostBody(next, "")
	if len(hosts) != 1 || hosts[0].ID != host.ID || !reflect.DeepEqual(puBodyOf(hosts[0]), wantBody) {
		t.Errorf("NPM holds %+v, want one proxy host %d with the body %+v", hosts, host.ID, wantBody)
	}

	effects := puEffects(t, h)
	if len(effects) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("journal %+v, want one effect", effects)
	}
	e := effects[0]
	if e.Kind != core.EffectModify || e.Resource != "npm.proxyhost:"+strconv.Itoa(host.ID) || e.State != core.EffectMade || e.Local ||
		e.CommitWait != 2*time.Minute || e.Recovery != nil || e.After == nil {
		t.Errorf("effect %+v, want a made non-local modify of npm.proxyhost:%d with a 2 minute commit wait", e, host.ID)
	}
	if got := puStateBody(t, e.Before.State); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, puBodyOf(host)) {
		t.Errorf("before %+v, want the body the host had: %+v", got, puBodyOf(host))
	}
	if got := puStateBody(t, e.After.State); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, wantBody) {
		t.Errorf("after %+v, want the requested body %+v", got, wantBody)
	}
}

func TestPuUpdateRefusals(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	tests := []struct {
		name  string
		setup func(t *testing.T, h *thHost, host npmapi.ProxyHost) (parent string, in ProxyHostUpdateInput)
		code  errcode.Code
	}{
		{"an id NPM does not hold", func(_ *testing.T, _ *thHost, host npmapi.ProxyHost) (string, ProxyHostUpdateInput) {
			return "", ProxyHostUpdateInput{ID: host.ID + 100, ProxyHostInput: puInput()}
		}, errcode.NotFound},
		{"no id", func(_ *testing.T, _ *thHost, _ npmapi.ProxyHost) (string, ProxyHostUpdateInput) {
			return "", ProxyHostUpdateInput{ProxyHostInput: puInput()}
		}, errcode.InvalidInput},
		{"an input without a domain", func(_ *testing.T, _ *thHost, host npmapi.ProxyHost) (string, ProxyHostUpdateInput) {
			return "", ProxyHostUpdateInput{ID: host.ID, ProxyHostInput: puInput(func(in *ProxyHostInput) { in.Domains = nil })}
		}, errcode.InvalidInput},
		{"a host reserved by an exposure", func(t *testing.T, h *thHost, host npmapi.ProxyHost) (string, ProxyHostUpdateInput) {
			puReserve(t, h, "proxy_host", host.ID, "npm.admin.expose", puDomain)
			return "", ProxyHostUpdateInput{ID: host.ID, ProxyHostInput: puInput()}
		}, errcode.NPMReserved},
		{"a host reserved by an exposure, under another exposure's removal", func(t *testing.T, h *thHost, host npmapi.ProxyHost) (string, ProxyHostUpdateInput) {
			puReserve(t, h, "proxy_host", host.ID, "npm.admin.expose", puDomain)
			return "panel.exposure.remove", ProxyHostUpdateInput{ID: host.ID, ProxyHostInput: puInput()}
		}, errcode.NPMReserved},
		{"the admin port of NPM as upstream", func(_ *testing.T, _ *thHost, host npmapi.ProxyHost) (string, ProxyHostUpdateInput) {
			in := puInput(func(in *ProxyHostInput) {
				in.ForwardHost = "127.0.0.1"
				in.ForwardPort = 81
			})
			return "", ProxyHostUpdateInput{ID: host.ID, ProxyHostInput: in}
		}, errcode.NPMReserved},
		{"a domain another proxy host serves", func(_ *testing.T, h *thHost, host npmapi.ProxyHost) (string, ProxyHostUpdateInput) {
			other := puInput(func(in *ProxyHostInput) { in.Domains = []string{"other.example.com"} })
			h.npm.SeedProxyHost(puHostOf(proxyHostBody(other, "")))
			in := puInput(func(in *ProxyHostInput) { in.Domains = []string{puDomain, "other.example.com"} })
			return "", ProxyHostUpdateInput{ID: host.ID, ProxyHostInput: in}
		}, errcode.InvalidInput},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h, host := puSeed(t, puInput())
			h.addChange("c1", "npm.proxyhost.update")
			parent, in := tc.setup(t, h, host)
			raw := puJSON(t, in)
			def := h.nm().proxyhostUpdate()
			before := puHosts(t, h)

			_, err := def.Handler.Plan(ctx, h.child("", parent), raw)
			puCoded(t, err, tc.code)
			_, err = def.Handler.Apply(ctx, h.child("c1", parent), raw)
			puCoded(t, err, tc.code)
			if !core.IsDefinite(err) {
				t.Errorf("apply error %v is not definite, though nothing was committed", err)
			}
			if got := puHosts(t, h); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, before) || len(puEffects(t, h)) != 0 {
				t.Errorf("a refusal changed NPM to %+v or journaled %+v", got, puEffects(t, h))
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestPuUpdateTheOutcomeOfAFailedCall(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	tests := []struct {
		name     string
		fault    puFault
		state    core.EffectState
		definite bool
		wantPort int
	}{
		{"refused by NPM", puFault{err: &npmapi.APIError{Status: 400, Message: "forward_port must be valid"}}, core.EffectSkipped, true, 8080},
		{"the response is lost after NPM committed", puFault{err: errors.New("npmapi: connection reset"), commit: true}, core.EffectIntended, false, 9090},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h, host := puSeed(t, puInput())
			h.addChange("c1", "npm.proxyhost.update")
			n := puFaulty(h, &puFaults{update: tc.fault})
			next := puInput(func(in *ProxyHostInput) { in.ForwardPort = 9090 })
			_, err := n.proxyhostUpdate().Handler.Apply(ctx, h.env("c1"), puJSON(t, ProxyHostUpdateInput{ID: host.ID, ProxyHostInput: next}))
			if err == nil || core.IsDefinite(err) != tc.definite {
				t.Fatalf("error %v (definite %v), want an error with definite %v", err, core.IsDefinite(err), tc.definite)
			}
			effects := puEffects(t, h)
			if len(effects) != 1 || effects[0].State != tc.state {
				t.Errorf("journal %+v, want one effect in state %s", effects, tc.state)
			}
			if got := puHosts(t, h); len(got) != 1 || got[0].ForwardPort != tc.wantPort {
				t.Errorf("NPM holds %+v, want the forward port %d", got, tc.wantPort)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestPuUpdateInverseRestoresTheBody(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, host := puSeed(t, puInput(func(in *ProxyHostInput) { in.Domains = []string{puDomain, "www.example.com"} }))
	h.addChange("c1", "npm.proxyhost.update")
	def := h.nm().proxyhostUpdate()
	next := puInput(func(in *ProxyHostInput) {
		in.ForwardHost = "other"
		in.ForwardPort = 9090
	})
	if _, err := def.Handler.Apply(ctx, h.env("c1"), puJSON(t, ProxyHostUpdateInput{ID: host.ID, ProxyHostInput: next})); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	e := puEffects(t, h)[0]
	e.UndoTo = &core.StateDoc{State: e.Before.State}

	res, err := def.Handler.Inverse(ctx, h.env("c1"), e)
	if err != nil || res.Resource != "" {
		t.Fatalf("inverse = %+v, %v; want no resource and no error", res, err)
	}
	hosts := puHosts(t, h)
	if len(hosts) != 1 || hosts[0].ID != host.ID || !reflect.DeepEqual(puBodyOf(hosts[0]), puBodyOf(host)) {
		t.Errorf("NPM holds %+v, want proxy host %d back at %+v", hosts, host.ID, puBodyOf(host))
	}
	obs, err := def.Handler.Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), e)
	if err != nil || obs.Matches != 1 || !obs.Exclusive || obs.Resource != e.Resource || !puSameJSON(obs.Current.State, e.UndoTo.State) {
		t.Errorf("observation %+v (%v), want the restored state of %s", obs, err, e.Resource)
	}

	// A host that NPM no longer holds cannot be restored, and the inverse says it changed nothing.
	api, err := h.nm().d.api(ctx, h.env(""))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := api.DeleteProxyHost(ctx, host.ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	_, err = def.Handler.Inverse(ctx, h.env("c1"), e)
	puCoded(t, err, errcode.NotFound)
	if !core.IsDefinite(err) || len(puHosts(t, h)) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("inverse of a vanished host: error %v (definite %v), hosts %+v; want a definite refusal that created nothing",
			err, core.IsDefinite(err), puHosts(t, h))
	}
}

func TestPuObserveReadsTheHostById(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, host := puSeed(t, puInput())
	def := h.nm().proxyhostUpdate()
	e := core.Effect{ChangeID: "c1", Seq: 1, Kind: core.EffectModify, Resource: "npm.proxyhost:" + strconv.Itoa(host.ID), State: core.EffectMade}

	obs, err := def.Handler.Observe(ctx, h.env(""), e)
	if err != nil || obs.Matches != 1 || !obs.Exclusive || obs.Resource != e.Resource || !reflect.DeepEqual(puStateBody(t, obs.Current.State), puBodyOf(host)) {
		t.Errorf("observation %+v (%v), want the body of host %d", obs, err, host.ID)
	}
	e.Resource = "npm.proxyhost:" + strconv.Itoa(host.ID+50)
	obs, err = def.Handler.Observe(ctx, h.env(""), e)
	if err != nil || obs.Matches != 1 || string(obs.Current.State) != "null" {
		t.Errorf("observation of a missing host %+v (%v), want one match whose state is null", obs, err)
	}
	e.Resource = "npm.proxyhost:?" + puDomain
	if _, err := def.Handler.Observe(ctx, h.env(""), e); err == nil {
		t.Error("observing a creation succeeded, want an error: this action creates nothing")
	}
}

func TestPuRemoveDeletesTheHost(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	tests := []struct {
		name  string
		input func(host npmapi.ProxyHost) ProxyHostRemoveInput
	}{
		{"by id", func(host npmapi.ProxyHost) ProxyHostRemoveInput { return ProxyHostRemoveInput{ID: host.ID} }},
		{"by one of its domains", func(npmapi.ProxyHost) ProxyHostRemoveInput { return ProxyHostRemoveInput{Domain: "www.example.com"} }},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			in := puInput(func(in *ProxyHostInput) { in.Domains = []string{puDomain, "www.example.com"} })
			h, host := puSeed(t, in)
			other := puInput(func(in *ProxyHostInput) { in.Domains = []string{"keep.example.com"} })
			keep := h.npm.SeedProxyHost(puHostOf(proxyHostBody(other, "")))
			h.addChange("c1", "npm.proxyhost.remove")
			def := h.nm().proxyhostRemove()
			raw := puJSON(t, tc.input(host))

			plan, err := def.Handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), raw)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			wantDiff := []core.DiffLine{{Op: "-", Text: "proxy host app.example.com, www.example.com -> http://app:8080"}}
			if !slices.Equal(plan.Diff, wantDiff) || len(plan.StateHash) != 64 || len(puHosts(t, h)) != 2 {
				t.Errorf("plan %+v, want diff %+v, a 64-digit hash and both hosts still held", plan, wantDiff)
			}

			out, err := def.Handler.Apply(ctx, h.env("c1"), raw)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			var view ProxyHostView
			if err := json.Unmarshal(out, &view); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if view.ID != host.ID || !slices.Equal(view.Domains, []string{puDomain, "www.example.com"}) || view.ForwardHost != "app" || view.ForwardPort != 8080 {
				t.Errorf("view %+v, want the host as removed", view)
			}
			if got := puHosts(t, h); len(got) != 1 || got[0].ID != keep.ID {
				t.Errorf("NPM holds %+v, want only proxy host %d", got, keep.ID)
			}
			effects := puEffects(t, h)
			if len(effects) != 1 {
				t.Fatalf("journal %+v, want one effect", effects)
			}
			e := effects[0]
			wantRecovery := &core.RecoveryID{Kind: "name", Value: "app.example.com,www.example.com"}
			if e.Kind != core.EffectDelete || e.Resource != "npm.proxyhost:"+strconv.Itoa(host.ID) || e.State != core.EffectMade || e.Local ||
				e.CommitWait != 2*time.Minute || e.Recovery != nil || !reflect.DeepEqual(e.InverseRecovery, wantRecovery) ||
				e.After == nil || string(e.After.State) != "null" {
				t.Errorf("effect %+v, want a made non-local delete of the host, ending null, whose inverse is found by %+v", e, wantRecovery)
			}
			if got := puStateBody(t, e.Before.State); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, puBodyOf(host)) {
				t.Errorf("before %+v, want the body the host had: %+v", got, puBodyOf(host))
			}
			if err := def.Handler.Verify(ctx, h.env("c1"), raw, out); err != nil {
				t.Errorf("verify after the removal: %v", err)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestPuRemoveOfAnAbsentHostConverges(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	tests := []struct {
		name string
		in   ProxyHostRemoveInput
		view ProxyHostView
	}{
		{"by id", ProxyHostRemoveInput{ID: 4242}, ProxyHostView{ID: 4242, Domains: []string{}}},
		{"by domain", ProxyHostRemoveInput{Domain: "gone.example.com"}, ProxyHostView{Domains: []string{"gone.example.com"}}},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h, host := puSeed(t, puInput())
			h.addChange("c1", "npm.proxyhost.remove")
			def := h.nm().proxyhostRemove()
			raw := puJSON(t, tc.in)

			plan, err := def.Handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), raw)
			if err != nil || len(plan.Diff) != 0 {
				t.Fatalf("plan %+v (%v), want an empty diff for an absent host", plan, err)
			}
			out, err := def.Handler.Apply(ctx, h.env("c1"), raw)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			var view ProxyHostView
			if err := json.Unmarshal(out, &view); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if !reflect.DeepEqual(view, tc.view) {
				t.Errorf("view %+v, want %+v", view, tc.view)
			}
			if got := puHosts(t, h); len(got) != 1 || got[0].ID != host.ID || len(puEffects(t, h)) != 0 {
				t.Errorf("NPM holds %+v and the journal %+v, want the other host untouched and no effect", got, puEffects(t, h))
			}
			if err := def.Handler.Verify(ctx, h.env("c1"), raw, out); err != nil {
				t.Errorf("verify of an absent host: %v", err)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestPuRemoveRefusesAnAmbiguousTarget(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, host := puSeed(t, puInput())
	h.addChange("c1", "npm.proxyhost.remove")
	def := h.nm().proxyhostRemove()
	for name, in := range map[string]ProxyHostRemoveInput{
		"an id and a domain": {ID: host.ID, Domain: puDomain},
		"neither":            {},
		"a negative id":      {ID: -1},
	} {
		raw := puJSON(t, in)
		_, err := def.Handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), raw)
		puCoded(t, err, errcode.InvalidInput)
		_, err = def.Handler.Apply(ctx, h.env("c1"), raw)
		puCoded(t, err, errcode.InvalidInput)
		if !core.IsDefinite(err) || len(puHosts(t, h)) != 1 || len(puEffects(t, h)) != 0 {
			t.Errorf("%s: error %v (definite %v), hosts %+v, effects %+v", name, err, core.IsDefinite(err), puHosts(t, h), puEffects(t, h))
		}
	}
}

func TestPuRemoveOfAReservedHost(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	const admin = "admin.example.com"
	seed := func(t *testing.T) (*thHost, npmapi.ProxyHost) {
		h, host := puSeed(t, puInput(func(in *ProxyHostInput) { in.Domains = []string{admin} }))
		h.addChange("c1", "npm.proxyhost.remove")
		puReserve(t, h, "proxy_host", host.ID, "npm.admin.expose", admin)
		puReserve(t, h, "certificate", 77, "npm.admin.expose", admin)
		puReserve(t, h, "certificate", 88, "npm.admin.expose", "other.example.com")
		return h, host
	}

	for _, parent := range []string{"", "npm.admin.expose", "panel.exposure.remove"} {
		t.Run("refused under "+parent, func(t *testing.T) {
			h, host := seed(t)
			raw := puJSON(t, ProxyHostRemoveInput{ID: host.ID})
			def := h.nm().proxyhostRemove()
			_, err := def.Handler.Plan(ctx, h.child("", parent), raw)
			puCoded(t, err, errcode.NPMReserved)
			_, err = def.Handler.Apply(ctx, h.child("c1", parent), raw)
			puCoded(t, err, errcode.NPMReserved)
			if !core.IsDefinite(err) || len(puHosts(t, h)) != 1 || len(puEffects(t, h)) != 0 || !puReserved(t, h, "proxy_host", host.ID) {
				t.Errorf("a refusal left error %v (definite %v), hosts %+v, effects %+v", err, core.IsDefinite(err), puHosts(t, h), puEffects(t, h))
			}
		})
	}

	t.Run("removed by the owning exposure's removal", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, host := seed(t)
		raw := puJSON(t, ProxyHostRemoveInput{Domain: admin})
		def := h.nm().proxyhostRemove()
		out, err := def.Handler.Apply(ctx, h.child("c1", "npm.admin.unexpose"), raw)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		var view ProxyHostView
		if err := json.Unmarshal(out, &view); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if view.ID != host.ID || view.Reserved != "npm.admin.expose" || len(puHosts(t, h)) != 0 {
			t.Errorf("view %+v, hosts %+v; want the reserved host removed and reported as reserved by npm.admin.expose", view, puHosts(t, h))
		}
		if puReserved(t, h, "proxy_host", host.ID) || puReserved(t, h, "certificate", 77) {
			t.Error("the exposure's reservation records are still held, want the proxy host's and the certificate's dropped")
		}
		if !puReserved(t, h, "certificate", 88) {
			t.Error("the reservation record of another exposure was dropped")
		}
	})

	t.Run("a repeated removal drops what a cut-off one left", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, _ := seed(t)
		api, err := h.nm().d.api(ctx, h.env(""))
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		hosts := puHosts(t, h)
		if err := api.DeleteProxyHost(ctx, hosts[0].ID); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		raw := puJSON(t, ProxyHostRemoveInput{Domain: admin})
		def := h.nm().proxyhostRemove()

		if _, err := def.Handler.Apply(ctx, h.env("c1"), raw); err != nil || !puReserved(t, h, "certificate", 77) {
			t.Fatalf("a removal outside an exposure's removal: error %v, certificate record kept %v; want it left alone", err, puReserved(t, h, "certificate", 77))
		}
		if _, err := def.Handler.Apply(ctx, h.child("c1", "npm.admin.unexpose"), raw); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if puReserved(t, h, "certificate", 77) || !puReserved(t, h, "certificate", 88) {
			t.Error("the repeated removal did not drop exactly the records of the exposure")
		}
	})
}

func TestPuRemoveInverseRecreatesTheHost(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	in := puInput(func(in *ProxyHostInput) { in.Domains = []string{puDomain, "www.example.com"} })
	h, host := puSeed(t, in)
	h.addChange("c1", "npm.proxyhost.remove")
	def := h.nm().proxyhostRemove()
	if _, err := def.Handler.Apply(ctx, h.env("c1"), puJSON(t, ProxyHostRemoveInput{ID: host.ID})); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	e := puEffects(t, h)[0]
	e.UndoTo = &core.StateDoc{State: e.Before.State}

	made, err := def.Handler.Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), e)
	if err != nil || made.Matches != 1 || string(made.Current.State) != "null" {
		t.Fatalf("observation of the removed host %+v (%v), want one match whose state is null", made, err)
	}
	res, err := def.Handler.Inverse(ctx, h.env("c1"), e)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	hosts := puHosts(t, h)
	if len(hosts) != 1 || !reflect.DeepEqual(puBodyOf(hosts[0]), puBodyOf(host)) || res.Resource != "npm.proxyhost:"+strconv.Itoa(hosts[0].ID) {
		t.Fatalf("inverse = %+v; NPM holds %+v, want one host with the removed body whose resource is returned", res, hosts)
	}

	e.State = core.EffectUndoing
	for name, undoResource := range map[string]string{"by the object the inverse returned": res.Resource, "by the inverse's recovery identity": ""} {
		e.UndoResource = undoResource
		obs, err := def.Handler.Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), e)
		if err != nil || obs.Matches != 1 || !obs.Exclusive || obs.Resource != res.Resource || !puSameJSON(obs.Current.State, e.UndoTo.State) {
			t.Errorf("%s: observation %+v (%v), want one match, %s, in the restored state", name, obs, err, res.Resource)
		}
	}
}

func TestPuRemoveTheOutcomeOfAFailedCall(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	tests := []struct {
		name     string
		fault    puFault
		state    core.EffectState
		definite bool
		held     int
	}{
		{"refused by NPM", puFault{err: &npmapi.APIError{Status: 400, Message: "in use"}}, core.EffectSkipped, true, 1},
		{"the response is lost after NPM committed", puFault{err: errors.New("npmapi: connection reset"), commit: true}, core.EffectIntended, false, 0},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h, host := puSeed(t, puInput())
			h.addChange("c1", "npm.proxyhost.remove")
			n := puFaulty(h, &puFaults{remove: tc.fault})
			_, err := n.proxyhostRemove().Handler.Apply(ctx, h.env("c1"), puJSON(t, ProxyHostRemoveInput{ID: host.ID}))
			if err == nil || core.IsDefinite(err) != tc.definite {
				t.Fatalf("error %v (definite %v), want an error with definite %v", err, core.IsDefinite(err), tc.definite)
			}
			effects := puEffects(t, h)
			if len(effects) != 1 || effects[0].State != tc.state || len(puHosts(t, h)) != tc.held {
				t.Errorf("journal %+v, hosts %+v; want one effect in state %s and %d host(s) held", effects, puHosts(t, h), tc.state, tc.held)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestPuVerifyChecksTheUpdatedHost(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, host := puSeed(t, puInput())
	h.addChange("c1", "npm.proxyhost.update")
	def := h.nm().proxyhostUpdate()
	next := puInput(func(in *ProxyHostInput) { in.ForwardPort = 9090 })
	raw := puJSON(t, ProxyHostUpdateInput{ID: host.ID, ProxyHostInput: next})

	if err := def.Handler.Verify(ctx, h.env("c1"), raw, nil); err == nil {
		t.Error("verify before the update succeeded, want an error: the host still forwards to port 8080")
	}
	out, err := def.Handler.Apply(ctx, h.env("c1"), raw)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := def.Handler.Verify(ctx, h.env("c1"), raw, out); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("verify after the update: %v", err)
	}
	if err := def.Handler.Verify(ctx, h.env("c1"), raw, nil); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("verify without an output, as recovery calls it: %v", err)
	}
	api, err := h.nm().d.api(ctx, h.env(""))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := api.DeleteProxyHost(ctx, host.ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := def.Handler.Verify(ctx, h.env("c1"), raw, out); err == nil {
		t.Error("verify of a vanished host succeeded, want an error")
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `internal/modules/npm/proxyhost_edit.go`**

Both actions are compensable agent actions that lock `npm` (`lockKey`) and keep the default apply timeout. `npm.proxyhost.update` is `core.DefineMutating[ProxyHostUpdateInput, ProxyHostView]`; `npm.proxyhost.remove` is `core.DefineMutating[ProxyHostRemoveInput, ProxyHostView]` with `Converges: true`. Each has a one-sentence `Summary`, no guard, no `Available`, the default scopes. The constructors are `func (n nm) proxyhostUpdate() core.ActionDef` and `func (n nm) proxyhostRemove() core.ActionDef`; the handlers are `proxyHostUpdateHandler` and `proxyHostRemoveHandler`, which both embed one `proxyHostEdit struct{ n nm }` that holds everything they share, so `Observe` is declared once and found on both by `findHandlers`.

*Shared pieces (`proxyHostEdit`).*
1. `proxyHostEditBody(h npmapi.ProxyHost) npmapi.ProxyHostBody` copies the body members of a proxy host. `proxyHostEditDoc(h) core.StateDoc` is the effect document of a host: `State` is the JSON of its body with `DomainNames` as a sorted copy, `Version` is `{"createdOn": ..., "modifiedOn": ...}`. The effect documents are this file's own and not `proxyHostState`: the inverses decode a document back into an `npmapi.ProxyHostBody`, and Contracts do not state that `proxyHostState` allows it. `proxyHostEditKey(domains []string) string` is the sorted domains joined by `,`, the recovery value of a recreated host. `proxyHostEditView(h npmapi.ProxyHost, reserved string) ProxyHostView` maps a host to its view (`Domains` in NPM's order and never null, `Reserved` the owning action or `""`, `Unclaimed` false).
2. `client(ctx, env)` returns `n.d.api(ctx, env)`; its error is returned wrapped with `core.Definite`, because nothing was sent. `fetch(ctx, api, id)` reads one host with `api.ProxyHost`: `npmapi.IsNotFound` gives (zero, false, nil); any other error is `core.Definite(errcode.Wrap(errcode.NPMUnhealthy, err, "Nginx Proxy Manager did not answer"))`, because a read commits nothing.
3. `refuseReserved(ctx, env, id)` looks the host up with `reservedOf(ctx, env.DB.Read(), "proxy_host", id)`. No row: no restriction. A row whose owner's removal is `env.Call.ParentAction` (the map `proxyHostEditRemoval`: `npm.admin.expose` is removed by `npm.admin.unexpose`, `panel.exposure.add` by `panel.exposure.remove`) is returned to the caller. Any other caller, including a child of the other owner's removal, is refused with `core.Definite(errcode.New(errcode.NPMReserved, ...))` whose detail names the host id and `row.Exposure`.
4. `Observe(ctx, env, e)` accepts only a resource `npm.proxyhost:<id>` with a numeric identity and refuses a creation (`?` identity) with an error: neither action creates through its own effect. For a delete effect whose state is `undoing` or `undone` it looks for the recreated host: the id of `e.UndoResource` when set, otherwise every host whose sorted domains equal the value of `e.InverseRecovery`; the observation carries `Matches` (0, 1 or more; with more, `Candidates` sorted by resource), `Resource` of a single match, `Current` the host's `proxyHostEditDoc` (`nullState()` when nothing matches) and `Exclusive: true`. For every other effect it reads the host `e.Resource` names: not found is `Matches: 1` with `nullState()`, a host is `Matches: 1`, `Resource: e.Resource`, `Exclusive: true` and its `proxyHostEditDoc`.

*`npm.proxyhost.update`.*
5. Checks, run by `Plan` and again by `Apply` (their refusals are `core.Definite`), in this order: `in.ID` above 0 (`request.invalid_input`, field `/id`); `validateProxyHostInput(in.ProxyHostInput)` (returned as it is); the host exists (`not_found`, "proxy host N does not exist"); `refuseReserved`; `n.checkUpstream(ctx, env, in.ProxyHostInput)`; no other host (a different id) holds any of `in.Domains` (`request.invalid_input`, field `/domains`, "domain D is already served by proxy host N"). The new body is `proxyHostBody(in.ProxyHostInput, env.Call.ParentAction)`.
6. `Plan` returns the diff and a `StateHash`, the hex SHA-256 of the current host's state JSON and the new body's state JSON; it calls nothing that changes NPM and journals nothing. The diff has, in this order and only for a member that changes, a `-` line with the old value then a `+` line with the new one: `domains: a, b` (sorted, joined by `, `), `forward: scheme://host:port`, `certificate: N` (`none` for 0), `access list: N` (`none` for 0), `force SSL: on|off`, `HSTS: on|off`, `websockets: on|off`. An input that changes nothing has an empty diff.
7. `Apply` repeats the checks, then journals one effect: `npmEffect(core.EffectModify, resourceOf(nounProxyHost, <id>), <state of the current body>, commitWait)` with `After` set to the document of the new body, and runs it through `n.run` (the handler's `Observe` is its observer), whose `do` calls `api.UpdateProxyHost(ctx, id, body)` and returns the effect's resource. It returns `proxyHostEditView` of the host NPM answered with, `Reserved` empty. A 4xx from NPM therefore leaves the effect `skipped` with a `core.Definite` error, and any other failure leaves it `intended`, as `n.run` does for every action.
8. `Inverse(ctx, env, e)` decodes `e.UndoTo.State` (`e.Before.State` when `UndoTo` is nil) into an `npmapi.ProxyHostBody` and sends it with `UpdateProxyHost` for the id of `e.Resource`. NPM not finding the host is `core.Definite(errcode.New(errcode.NotFound, ...))`; any other 4xx is `core.Definite`; a failure to obtain the client or to decode the document is `core.Definite`; every other error is returned as it is. It returns an empty `core.InverseResult`.
9. `Verify(ctx, env, in, out)` reads the host `in.ID` and fails unless its view matches the input: the same domains as a set, forward scheme, host and port, certificate, access list, forced SSL, HSTS and websockets. It reads only the host and works with an empty `out`.

*`npm.proxyhost.remove`.*
10. Input rule, in `Plan` and `Apply`: exactly one of `ID` (above 0) and `Domain` (not empty) is set, otherwise `core.Definite` `request.invalid_input` with a field error on `/id` and `/domain`; a negative `ID` is refused too. The target is the host with that id, or the host that lists `Domain` among its domains (the whole host is removed, as a composite exposes one host for all its domains). `Plan`/`Apply` run `refuseReserved` for a host that exists.
11. A host that does not exist converges. `Plan` returns an empty diff and a `StateHash` of the input; `Apply` journals nothing and returns `ProxyHostView{ID: in.ID, Domains: []string{in.Domain} or an empty slice}`. When `env.Call.ParentAction` is `npm.admin.unexpose` or `panel.exposure.remove` and `in.Domain` is set, it also drops the reservation rows of that exposure with `dropReservedExposure(ctx, tx, in.Domain)` inside `env.DB.Write`, so a removal cut off between the NPM call and the bookkeeping is completed by running it again; a caller that is not such a removal drops nothing.
12. For an existing host `Plan` shows one `-` line `proxy host <domains sorted, joined by ", "> -> scheme://host:port`. `Apply` journals `npmEffect(core.EffectDelete, resource, <state of the current body>, commitWait)` with `After` `nullState()` and `InverseRecovery` `core.RecoveryID{Kind: "name", Value: proxyHostEditKey(domains)}`, and runs `api.DeleteProxyHost` through `n.run` the same way. After success, when the host was reserved (and so `refuseReserved` let a removal through), it drops that exposure's rows with `dropReservedExposure(ctx, tx, row.Exposure)` inside `env.DB.Write`; a failure there is returned (not definite). The output is `proxyHostEditView` of the host as it was, `Reserved` the row's owner.
13. `Inverse` decodes `e.UndoTo.State` (or `e.Before.State`) into a body and recreates the host with `api.CreateProxyHost`; it returns `core.InverseResult{Resource: resourceOf(nounProxyHost, <new id>)}`. A 4xx or a decode failure is `core.Definite`. It does not restore reservation rows: a removal that dropped them is a step of a forward composite, and the engine undoes a step only through its composite (`undo.go`), which a forward composite never does.
14. `Verify` passes when no host has the id (or, for a Domain input, lists the domain).

No comment names a task or a plan. Error strings start with `npm:`. No detail carries an NPM response body.

```go
package npm

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"reflect"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/docker"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/docker/logfilter"
)

// rvReveal runs the action's reveal handler the way the agent's Engine.Reveal does.
func rvReveal(h *thHost) (identity string, raw json.RawMessage, err error) {
	return h.nm().adminRevealPassword().Handler.Reveal(context.Background(), h.env(""), json.RawMessage(`{}`))
}

// rvCorrupt replaces the sealed password of the stored instance with bytes the sealer cannot open.
func rvCorrupt(t *testing.T, h *thHost) {
	t.Helper()
	ctx := context.Background()
	row, ok, err := loadInstance(ctx, h.db.Read())
	if err != nil || !ok {
		t.Fatalf("instance row: found %v, error %v", ok, err)
	}
	row.PasswordCT = []byte("not a sealed value")
	if err := h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error { return saveInstance(ctx, tx, row) }); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

func TestRvDeclaration(t *testing.T) {
	def := newTHHost(t).nm().adminRevealPassword()
	s := def.Spec
	if s.Name != "npm.admin.reveal-password" || s.Version != 1 || s.Class != core.ClassRead || s.Side != core.SideAgent ||
		!s.Reveal || s.Internal || s.MinScope != core.ScopeAdmin || len(s.LockKeys) != 0 || len(s.Guards) != 0 ||
		s.Available != nil || s.Summary == "" {
		t.Errorf("spec %+v, want the admin-scope reveal read npm.admin.reveal-password v1 on the agent with no lock or guard", s)
	}
	if def.InputType != reflect.TypeFor[RevealInput]() || def.OutputType != reflect.TypeFor[AdminPassword]() {
		t.Errorf("types %v and %v, want RevealInput and AdminPassword", def.InputType, def.OutputType)
	}
	if def.Handler.Reveal == nil || def.Handler.Read != nil || def.Handler.Plan != nil || def.Handler.Apply != nil {
		t.Errorf("handler %+v, want the reveal handler only", def.Handler)
	}
	field, ok := reflect.TypeFor[AdminPassword]().FieldByName("Password")
	if !ok || field.Tag.Get("secret") != "true" {
		t.Errorf("AdminPassword.Password tag %q, want secret:\"true\"", field.Tag)
	}
}

// TestRvRevealReturnsTheStoredAdmin: the answer is the stored email and the unsealed password, the identity names the
// host and not the value, and the read touches neither NPM nor Docker.
func TestRvRevealReturnsTheStoredAdmin(t *testing.T) {
	h := newTHHost(t)
	inst := h.deployed()
	requests, calls := len(h.npm.Requests()), len(h.docker.calls())

	identity, raw, err := rvReveal(h)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if identity != "npm admin password, host h1" {
		t.Errorf("identity = %q, want %q", identity, "npm admin password, host h1")
	}
	var members map[string]string
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &members); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := map[string]string{"email": inst.Email, "password": inst.Password}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(members, want) {
		t.Errorf("value members %v, want exactly email and password: %v", members, want)
	}
	if strings.Contains(identity, inst.Password) {
		t.Error("the identity carries the password")
	}
	if got := len(h.npm.Requests()); got != requests {
		t.Errorf("the reveal sent %d requests to NPM, want none", got-requests)
	}
	if got := len(h.docker.calls()); got != calls {
		t.Errorf("the reveal made %d Docker calls, want none", got-calls)
	}
}

func TestRvRevealRefusals(t *testing.T) {
	t.Run("no instance is deployed", func(t *testing.T) {
		_, _, err := rvReveal(newTHHost(t))
		var coded *errcode.Error
		if !errors.As(err, &coded) || coded.Code != errcode.NotFound {
			t.Errorf("error %v, want %s", err, errcode.NotFound)
		}
	})
	t.Run("the sealed password cannot be opened", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newTHHost(t)
		inst := h.deployed()
		rvCorrupt(t, h)
		_, raw, err := rvReveal(h)
		if err == nil || len(raw) != 0 {
			t.Errorf("value %s, error %v; want an error and no value", raw, err)
		}
		if err != nil && strings.Contains(err.Error(), inst.Password) {
			t.Error("the error carries the password")
		}
	})
}

// TestRvLogSecretsAreThePassword: the source registered for the module answers the stored password for any
// container carrying the module's label, whichever container id it is asked about, and the docker log filter built
// from it masks the password wherever a log line holds it.
func TestRvLogSecretsAreThePassword(t *testing.T) {
	h := newTHHost(t)
	inst := h.deployed()

	for _, id := range []string{inst.ContainerID, "an-upgraded-container-the-row-does-not-hold-yet"} {
		secrets, err := revealLogSecrets(context.Background(), h.env(""), id)
		if err != nil || len(secrets) != 1 || string(secrets[0]) != inst.Password {
			t.Fatalf("secrets for %q = %q, %v; want exactly the stored password", id, secrets, err)
		}
		f := logfilter.New(secrets)
		out := string(f.Push("stdout", []byte("created user with secret "+inst.Password+" ok\n"))) + string(f.Flush("stdout"))
		if strings.Contains(out, inst.Password) || !strings.Contains(out, logfilter.Mask) {
			t.Errorf("filtered line %q, want the password replaced by %q", out, logfilter.Mask)
		}
	}
}

func TestRvLogSecretsWithoutAnInstance(t *testing.T) {
	secrets, err := revealLogSecrets(context.Background(), newTHHost(t).env(""), "c1")
	if secrets != nil || err != nil {
		t.Errorf("secrets = %q, %v; want none and no error before an instance exists", secrets, err)
	}
}

// TestRvLogSecretsFailClosed: a stored password that cannot be opened is an error, which makes docker refuse the log
// read rather than show the logs of the container unmasked.
func TestRvLogSecretsFailClosed(t *testing.T) {
	h := newTHHost(t)
	inst := h.deployed()
	rvCorrupt(t, h)
	secrets, err := revealLogSecrets(context.Background(), h.env(""), inst.ContainerID)
	if err == nil || secrets != nil {
		t.Errorf("secrets = %q, %v; want an error and no secret", secrets, err)
	}
}

// TestRvLogSecretsAreRegistered: the package registered its source with the docker module when it was imported, so
// a second registration for the module id panics.
func TestRvLogSecretsAreRegistered(t *testing.T) {
	defer func() {
		if recover() == nil {
			t.Error("registering a second source for npm did not panic, so the package registered none")
		}
	}()
	docker.RegisterLogSecrets(ID, func(context.Context, core.Env, string) ([][]byte, error) { return nil, nil })
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `internal/modules/npm/reveal.go`**

1. `func (n nm) adminRevealPassword() core.ActionDef` is `core.DefineReveal[RevealInput, AdminPassword]` with `ActionSpec{Name: ID + ".admin.reveal-password", Summary: <one sentence>, Version: 1, Side: core.SideAgent, MinScope: core.ScopeAdmin}` as `docker.container.reveal-env` declares its own spec; the constructor fills in the class `read` and the `Reveal` flag. It takes no lock, no guard and has no `Available`.
2. One unexported function opens the stored admin password for both entry points: it loads the instance row with `loadInstance(ctx, env.DB.Read())` and opens `PasswordCT` with `env.Sealer.Open("npm_instances.password_ct.1", row.PasswordCT)`. It returns the row's email, the password bytes and whether an instance exists. It never calls NPM or Docker. An unsealing error is returned wrapped as `npm: open the admin password: ...` and carries neither the ciphertext nor any value.
3. The reveal function returns `core.Revealed[AdminPassword]{Identity: "npm admin password, host " + env.HostID, Value: AdminPassword{Email: <stored email>, Password: <password>}}`. Without an instance it returns `errcode.New(errcode.NotFound, "no Nginx Proxy Manager instance is deployed on this host")`. The reveal path journals the audit entry itself; this function writes nothing and logs nothing.
4. `revealLogSecrets(ctx, env, containerID)` is the log-secret source: it returns the stored password as the single secret, `nil, nil` when no instance exists, and the unsealing error when the stored password cannot be opened, so that the docker module refuses the read instead of showing the logs unmasked. It ignores `containerID` on purpose, with a comment saying why: docker asks only for containers labelled `darkpanel.module=npm`, and the container of an upgrade in progress has an id the instance row does not hold yet.
5. An `init` function calls `docker.RegisterLogSecrets(ID, revealLogSecrets)`. It is the package's only `init`; registration happens nowhere else.

No comment names a task or a plan. The password appears in no error, log line, identity or diff.

```go
package npm

)

const upAppNet = "app-net"

var (
	upFromImage = "jc21/nginx-proxy-manager:2.13.0@sha256:" + strings.Repeat("a", 64)
	upAppIP     = netip.MustParseAddr("172.30.0.9")
	// upTars is what the fake Engine serves for the archive of each mount, keyed by the copied path.
	upTars = map[string][]byte{"/data": []byte("tar-of-data"), "/etc/letsencrypt": []byte("tar-of-letsencrypt")}
)

func upSum(b []byte) string {
	s := sha256.Sum256(b)
	return hex.EncodeToString(s[:])
}

type upConnect struct {
	Network, Container string
	Endpoint           *dockerapi.EndpointSettings
}

type upPut struct {
	Container, Path string
	Data            []byte
}

// upDocker wraps the host's fake Engine. It serves the archives of upTars, records what an upgrade creates,
// connects and copies back, traces the calls that change the host and lets a test fail a call: fail receives the
// call, with its argument where it has one, and how often that call has been made.
type upDocker struct {
	dockerAPI
	trace    func(call string)
	fail     func(call string, n int) error
	counts   map[string]int
	created  []dockerapi.ContainerCreate
	ids      []string
	connects []upConnect
	puts     []upPut
	missing  map[string]bool
}

func (d *upDocker) note(call string) error {
	d.counts[call]++
	if method, _, found := strings.Cut(call, " "); found {
		d.counts[method]++
	}
	if d.trace != nil {
		d.trace(call)
	}
	if d.fail != nil {
		return d.fail(call, d.counts[call])
	}
	return nil
}

func (d *upDocker) InspectImage(ctx context.Context, ref string) (dockerapi.ImageInspect, error) {
	if d.missing[ref] {
		return dockerapi.ImageInspect{}, &dockerapi.APIError{StatusCode: 404, Message: "no such image: " + ref}
	}
	return d.dockerAPI.InspectImage(ctx, ref)
}

func (d *upDocker) PullImage(ctx context.Context, ref string) (pullStream, error) {
	if err := d.note("PullImage"); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return d.dockerAPI.PullImage(ctx, ref)
}

func (d *upDocker) StopContainer(ctx context.Context, id string, timeout time.Duration) (bool, error) {
	if err := d.note("StopContainer"); err != nil {
		return false, err
	}
	return d.dockerAPI.StopContainer(ctx, id, timeout)
}

func (d *upDocker) RemoveContainer(ctx context.Context, id string, opts dockerapi.RemoveContainerOptions) error {
	if err := d.note("RemoveContainer"); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return d.dockerAPI.RemoveContainer(ctx, id, opts)
}

func (d *upDocker) CreateContainer(ctx context.Context, cfg dockerapi.ContainerCreate) (string, error) {
	if err := d.note("CreateContainer"); err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	id, err := d.dockerAPI.CreateContainer(ctx, cfg)
	if err == nil {
		d.created = append(d.created, cfg)
		d.ids = append(d.ids, id)
	}
	return id, err
}

func (d *upDocker) StartContainer(ctx context.Context, id string) (bool, error) {
	if err := d.note("StartContainer"); err != nil {
		return false, err
	}
	return d.dockerAPI.StartContainer(ctx, id)
}

func (d *upDocker) ConnectNetwork(ctx context.Context, network, container string, endpoint *dockerapi.EndpointSettings) error {
	if err := d.note("ConnectNetwork " + network); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	d.connects = append(d.connects, upConnect{network, container, endpoint})
	return d.dockerAPI.ConnectNetwork(ctx, network, container, endpoint)
}

func (d *upDocker) RemoveVolume(ctx context.Context, name string, force bool) error {
	if err := d.note("RemoveVolume " + name); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return d.dockerAPI.RemoveVolume(ctx, name, force)
}

func (d *upDocker) CreateVolume(ctx context.Context, v dockerapi.VolumeCreate) (dockerapi.Volume, error) {
	if err := d.note("CreateVolume " + v.Name); err != nil {
		return dockerapi.Volume{}, err
	}
	return d.dockerAPI.CreateVolume(ctx, v)
}

func (d *upDocker) CopyFromContainer(_ context.Context, _, path string) (io.ReadCloser, error) {
	if err := d.note("CopyFromContainer " + path); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	data, ok := upTars[path]
	if !ok {
		return nil, errors.New("upDocker: no archive is served for " + path)
	}
	return io.NopCloser(bytes.NewReader(data)), nil
}

func (d *upDocker) CopyToContainer(_ context.Context, id, path string, archive io.Reader) error {
	if err := d.note("CopyToContainer " + path); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	data, err := io.ReadAll(archive)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	d.puts = append(d.puts, upPut{id, path, data})
	return nil
}

// upFiles wraps the environment's file writer: it traces and can fail a managed write, and records the managed
// removals without requiring the file to exist.
type upFiles struct {
	core.FileWriter
	trace     func(call string)
	failWrite func(name string) error
	removed   []string
}

func (f *upFiles) WriteManaged(ctx context.Context, module, dir, name string, data []byte, mode fs.FileMode, uid, gid int) error {
	if f.trace != nil {
		f.trace("WriteManaged " + name)
	}
	if f.failWrite != nil {
		if err := f.failWrite(name); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return f.FileWriter.WriteManaged(ctx, module, dir, name, data, mode, uid, gid)
}

func (f *upFiles) RemoveManaged(ctx context.Context, module, dir, name string) error {
	f.removed = append(f.removed, name)
	_ = f.FileWriter.RemoveManaged(ctx, module, dir, name)
	return nil
}

// upDown is an NPM client whose API does not answer.
type upDown struct{ npmAPI }

func (upDown) Health(context.Context) (npmapi.Health, error) {
	return npmapi.Health{}, errors.New("npmapi: connection refused")
}

// upHealthTrace calls note on every Health call.
type upHealthTrace struct {
	npmAPI
	note func()
}

func (a upHealthTrace) Health(ctx context.Context) (npmapi.Health, error) {
	a.note()
	return a.npmAPI.Health(ctx)
}

// upSetup is a host whose instance runs from, with an extra network attached at a static address, and change c1's
// environment, whose Engine and file writer are the wrappers above.
type upSetup struct {
	t     *testing.T
	h     *thHost
	inst  thInstance
	orig  dockerapi.ContainerJSON
	n     nm
	dock  *upDocker
	files *upFiles
	env   core.Env
}

// upNewSetup puts the pinned image on the host; unless pinned is set, the Engine answers that it has no such image.
func upNewSetup(t *testing.T, from string, pinned bool) *upSetup {
	t.Helper()
	ctx := context.Background()
	h := newTHHost(t)
	inst := h.deployed()
	h.addChange("c1", "npm.instance.upgrade")
	c, ok := h.docker.container(inst.ContainerID)
	if !ok {
		t.Fatalf("the deployed container %s is not in the fake Engine", inst.ContainerID)
	}
	c.Config.Image = from
	c.Config.Env = append(slices.Clone(c.Config.Env), "HARNESS_ONLY=1")
	if c.NetworkSettings.Networks == nil {
		c.NetworkSettings.Networks = map[string]*dockerapi.EndpointSettings{}
	}
	c.NetworkSettings.Networks[upAppNet] = &dockerapi.EndpointSettings{
		IPAMConfig: &dockerapi.EndpointIPAMConfig{IPv4Address: upAppIP}, Aliases: []string{"web"}, NetworkID: "net-app",
		EndpointID: "ep-app", IPAddress: upAppIP, MacAddress: "02:42:ac:1e:00:09",
	}
	h.docker.putContainer(c)
	h.docker.putNetwork(thNetwork(upAppNet))
	h.docker.putVolume(dockerapi.Volume{Name: dataVolume})
	h.docker.putVolume(dockerapi.Volume{Name: certVolume})
	h.docker.putImage(Image)
	row, ok, err := loadInstance(ctx, h.db.Read())
	if err != nil || !ok {
		t.Fatalf("instance row: found %v, error %v", ok, err)
	}
	row.Image, row.Public = from, "confirmed"
	if err := h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error { return saveInstance(ctx, tx, row) }); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	s := &upSetup{t: t, h: h, inst: inst, orig: c, n: h.nm()}
	s.dock = &upDocker{dockerAPI: s.n.d.docker, counts: map[string]int{}, missing: map[string]bool{Image: !pinned}}
	s.n.d.docker = s.dock
	s.env = h.env("c1")
	s.files = &upFiles{FileWriter: s.env.Files}
	s.env.Files = s.files
	return s
}

// apiDown makes the API of every NPM the module starts unreachable.
func (s *upSetup) apiDown() {
	inner := s.n.d.anon
	s.n.d.anon = func() npmAPI { return upDown{npmAPI: inner()} }
}

func (s *upSetup) phase() string {
	u, ok, err := loadUpgrade(context.Background(), s.h.db.Read(), "c1")
	if err != nil || !ok {
		return "no row"
	}
	return u.Phase
}

func (s *upSetup) upgrade() upgradeRow {
	s.t.Helper()
	u, ok, err := loadUpgrade(context.Background(), s.h.db.Read(), "c1")
	if err != nil || !ok {
		s.t.Fatalf("upgrade row of c1: found %v, error %v", ok, err)
	}
	return u
}

func (s *upSetup) instanceRow() instanceRow {
	s.t.Helper()
	row, ok, err := loadInstance(context.Background(), s.h.db.Read())
	if err != nil || !ok {
		s.t.Fatalf("instance row: found %v, error %v", ok, err)
	}
	return row
}

func (s *upSetup) effects() []core.Effect {
	s.t.Helper()
	effects, err := s.h.journal.List(context.Background())
	if err != nil {
		s.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return effects
}

// apply runs the action's Apply while advancing the host's clock by one second whenever it waits.
func (s *upSetup) apply() (json.RawMessage, error) {
	s.t.Helper()
	var out json.RawMessage
	var err error
	upDrive(s.t, s.h, func() {
		out, err = s.n.instanceUpgrade().Handler.Apply(context.Background(), s.env, json.RawMessage(`{}`))
	})
	return out, err
}

func upDrive(t *testing.T, h *thHost, call func()) {
	t.Helper()
	done := make(chan struct{})
	go func() {
		defer close(done)
		call()
	}()
	for deadline := time.Now().Add(30 * time.Second); ; {
		select {
		case <-done:
			return
		default:
		}
		if h.clock.Pending() > 0 {
			h.clock.Advance(time.Second)
		} else {
			time.Sleep(time.Millisecond)
		}
		if time.Now().After(deadline) {
			t.Fatal("the call did not finish")
		}
	}
}

// wantRestored checks that the original instance runs again, recreated from its journaled configuration with its
// volumes restored from the archives, and that the upgrade ended rolled back with its effect skipped.
func (s *upSetup) wantRestored() {
	s.t.Helper()
	t := s.t
	if got := s.phase(); got != "rolled_back" {
		t.Errorf("phase %q, want rolled_back", got)
	}
	c, ok := s.h.docker.container(containerName)
	row := s.instanceRow()
	if !ok || c.ID == s.inst.ContainerID || c.ID != row.ContainerID || c.Config.Image != upFromImage || !c.State.Running {
		t.Fatalf("container %+v (found %v) with the instance row at %s %s; want a new running container of %s that the row names",
			c, ok, row.ContainerID, row.Image, upFromImage)
	}
	if row.Image != upFromImage || row.Public != "confirmed" {
		t.Errorf("instance row image %q, public %q; want %q and confirmed", row.Image, row.Public, upFromImage)
	}
	if len(s.dock.created) == 0 {
		t.Fatal("no container was recreated")
	}
	created := s.dock.created[len(s.dock.created)-1]
	wantConfig := s.orig.Config
	wantConfig.Hostname = ""
	if created.Name != containerName || !reflect.DeepEqual(created.ContainerConfig, wantConfig) || created.HostConfig == nil ||
		!reflect.DeepEqual(*created.HostConfig, s.orig.HostConfig) {
		t.Errorf("the original was recreated as %+v, want its configuration %+v and host configuration %+v", created, wantConfig, s.orig.HostConfig)
	}
	if created.NetworkingConfig == nil || len(created.NetworkingConfig.EndpointsConfig) != 1 || created.NetworkingConfig.EndpointsConfig[networkName] == nil {
		t.Errorf("networking configuration %+v, want only the %s endpoint", created.NetworkingConfig, networkName)
	}
	var puts []upPut
	for _, p := range s.dock.puts {
		if p.Container == c.ID {
			puts = append(puts, p)
		}
	}
	wantPuts := []upPut{{c.ID, "/", upTars["/data"]}, {c.ID, "/etc", upTars["/etc/letsencrypt"]}}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(puts, wantPuts) {
		t.Errorf("archives copied back to the recreated container: %+v, want %+v", puts, wantPuts)
	}
	reconnected := false
	for _, cn := range s.dock.connects {
		reconnected = reconnected || (cn.Network == upAppNet && cn.Container == c.ID)
	}
	if !reconnected {
		t.Errorf("connects %+v, want %s connected to the recreated container", s.dock.connects, upAppNet)
	}
	for _, v := range []string{dataVolume, certVolume} {
		if _, ok := s.h.docker.volume(v); !ok || s.dock.counts["RemoveVolume "+v] != 1 || s.dock.counts["CreateVolume "+v] != 1 {
			t.Errorf("volume %s: present %v, removed %d, created %d; want it removed once and created once", v, ok,
				s.dock.counts["RemoveVolume "+v], s.dock.counts["CreateVolume "+v])
		}
	}
	for _, id := range s.dock.ids[:len(s.dock.ids)-1] {
		if _, ok := s.h.docker.container(id); ok {
			t.Errorf("container %s of the failed upgrade is still there", id)
		}
	}
	if effects := s.effects(); len(effects) != 1 || effects[0].State != core.EffectSkipped {
		t.Errorf("journal %+v, want one skipped effect", effects)
	}
}

func upImageOf(t *testing.T, doc core.StateDoc) string {
	t.Helper()
	if string(doc.State) == "null" {
		return ""
	}
	var s struct {
		Image string `json:"image"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(doc.State, &s); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("state %s: %v", doc.State, err)
	}
	return s.Image
}

func TestUpDeclaration(t *testing.T) {
	def := newTHHost(t).nm().instanceUpgrade()
	s, hd := def.Spec, def.Handler
	if s.Name != "npm.instance.upgrade" || s.Version != 1 || s.Class != core.ClassCompensable || s.Side != core.SideAgent ||
		!slices.Equal(s.LockKeys, []string{"npm"}) || s.ApplyTimeout != 20*time.Minute || !s.LongRunning || s.Task || s.Converges ||
		s.Internal || s.Reveal || s.MinScope != "" || s.Available != nil || len(s.Guards) != 0 || s.Summary == "" {
		t.Errorf("spec %+v, want a long-running compensable agent action locking npm for 20 minutes", s)
	}
	if def.InputType != reflect.TypeFor[UpgradeInput]() || def.OutputType != reflect.TypeFor[Upgraded]() {
		t.Errorf("types %v and %v, want UpgradeInput and Upgraded", def.InputType, def.OutputType)
	}
	if hd.Plan == nil || hd.Apply == nil || hd.Verify == nil || hd.Observe == nil || hd.Read != nil || hd.Task != nil {
		t.Errorf("handler has Plan %v Apply %v Verify %v Observe %v Read %v Task %v", hd.Plan != nil, hd.Apply != nil,
			hd.Verify != nil, hd.Observe != nil, hd.Read != nil, hd.Task != nil)
	}
}

func TestUpPlan(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	s := upNewSetup(t, upFromImage, true)
	s.dock.fail = func(call string, _ int) error { return errors.New("a plan must not call " + call) }
	h := s.n.instanceUpgrade().Handler

	out, err := h.Plan(ctx, s.h.env(""), json.RawMessage(`{}`))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	line := func(op, text string) core.DiffLine { return core.DiffLine{Op: op, Text: text} }
	want := []core.DiffLine{
		line("-", "image "+upFromImage),
		line("+", "image "+Image),
		line(" ", "both volumes ("+dataVolume+", "+certVolume+") are archived to "+backupDir+" first"),
		line(" ", "the original container and volumes are restored if the new container's API does not answer within 120 s"),
	}
	if !slices.Equal(out.Diff, want) || out.Confirmation != "" || len(out.Guards) != 0 || len(out.StateHash) != 64 {
		t.Errorf("plan %+v, want diff %+v, no confirmation, no guard and a 64-digit hash", out, want)
	}
	if len(s.effects()) != 0 || s.phase() != "no row" {
		t.Errorf("plan journaled %+v or recorded phase %q, want nothing", s.effects(), s.phase())
	}
	again, err := h.Plan(ctx, s.h.env(""), json.RawMessage(`{}`))
	if err != nil || again.StateHash != out.StateHash {
		t.Errorf("a second plan hash %q (error %v), want %q", again.StateHash, err, out.StateHash)
	}
	row := s.instanceRow()
	row.Image = "jc21/nginx-proxy-manager:2.12.0@sha256:" + strings.Repeat("b", 64)
	if err := s.h.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error { return saveInstance(ctx, tx, row) }); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if other, err := h.Plan(ctx, s.h.env(""), json.RawMessage(`{}`)); err != nil || other.StateHash == out.StateHash {
		t.Errorf("plan over another installed image: hash %q (error %v), want one different from %q", other.StateHash, err, out.StateHash)
	}
}

func TestUpRefusals(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	tests := []struct {
		name  string
		setup func(t *testing.T) (*thHost, nm)
		code  errcode.Code
	}{
		{"no instance is deployed", func(t *testing.T) (*thHost, nm) {
			h := newTHHost(t)
			return h, h.nm()
		}, errcode.NotFound},
		{"the instance's container is gone", func(t *testing.T) (*thHost, nm) {
			s := upNewSetup(t, upFromImage, true)
			if err := s.h.docker.RemoveContainer(ctx, s.inst.ContainerID, dockerapi.RemoveContainerOptions{Force: true}); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			return s.h, s.h.nm()
		}, errcode.NPMUnhealthy},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h, n := tc.setup(t)
			h.addChange("c2", "npm.instance.upgrade")
			def := n.instanceUpgrade()
			var coded *errcode.Error
			_, err := def.Handler.Plan(ctx, h.env(""), json.RawMessage(`{}`))
			if !errors.As(err, &coded) || coded.Code != tc.code {
				t.Errorf("plan error %v, want code %s", err, tc.code)
			}
			_, err = def.Handler.Apply(ctx, h.env("c2"), json.RawMessage(`{}`))
			if !errors.As(err, &coded) || coded.Code != tc.code || !core.IsDefinite(err) {
				t.Errorf("apply error %v (definite %v), want a definite %s", err, core.IsDefinite(err), tc.code)
			}
			effects, lerr := h.journal.List(ctx)
			if lerr != nil || len(effects) != 0 {
				t.Errorf("a refusal journaled %+v (%v)", effects, lerr)
			}
			if _, ok, _ := loadUpgrade(ctx, h.db.Read(), "c2"); ok {
				t.Error("a refusal recorded an upgrade")
			}
		})
	}
}

// TestUpApplyUpgradesTheInstance covers the whole successful path: the archives, the recreated container and the
// bookkeeping.
func TestUpApplyUpgradesTheInstance(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	s := upNewSetup(t, upFromImage, true)
	out, err := s.apply()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var got Upgraded
	if err := json.Unmarshal(out, &got); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got != (Upgraded{FromImage: upFromImage, ToImage: Image}) {
		t.Errorf("output %+v, want from %s to %s", got, upFromImage, Image)
	}

	c, ok := s.h.docker.container(containerName)
	if !ok || c.ID == s.inst.ContainerID || c.Config.Image != Image || !c.State.Running {
		t.Fatalf("container %+v (found %v), want a new running container of %s", c, ok, Image)
	}
	if _, ok := s.h.docker.container(s.inst.ContainerID); ok {
		t.Error("the original container still exists")
	}
	if len(s.dock.created) != 1 || !reflect.DeepEqual(s.dock.created[0], instanceCreate(Image)) {
		t.Errorf("created %+v, want one container made from the deploy configuration of %s", s.dock.created, Image)
	}
	wantEndpoint := &dockerapi.EndpointSettings{IPAMConfig: &dockerapi.EndpointIPAMConfig{IPv4Address: upAppIP}}
	if len(s.dock.connects) != 1 || s.dock.connects[0].Network != upAppNet || s.dock.connects[0].Container != c.ID ||
		!reflect.DeepEqual(s.dock.connects[0].Endpoint, wantEndpoint) {
		t.Errorf("connects %+v, want %s connected to the new container with only its static address", s.dock.connects, upAppNet)
	}
	if len(s.dock.puts) != 0 || s.dock.counts["RemoveVolume"] != 0 {
		t.Errorf("a successful upgrade restored archives %+v or removed %d volumes", s.dock.puts, s.dock.counts["RemoveVolume"])
	}

	row := s.instanceRow()
	if row.ContainerID != c.ID || row.Image != Image || row.Public != "confirmed" {
		t.Errorf("instance row %+v, want container %s, image %s and the public mark kept", row, c.ID, Image)
	}

	u := s.upgrade()
	wantArchives := []archiveRef{
		{Volume: dataVolume, File: "c1-data.tar", SHA256: upSum(upTars["/data"])},
		{Volume: certVolume, File: "c1-letsencrypt.tar", SHA256: upSum(upTars["/etc/letsencrypt"])},
	}
	if u.Phase != "done" || u.FromImage != upFromImage || u.ToImage != Image || !reflect.DeepEqual(u.Archives, wantArchives) {
		t.Errorf("upgrade row %+v, want phase done from %s to %s with archives %+v", u, upFromImage, Image, wantArchives)
	}
	wantFiles := map[string][]byte{"c1-data.tar": upTars["/data"], "c1-letsencrypt.tar": upTars["/etc/letsencrypt"]}
	for name, data := range wantFiles {
		if !bytes.Equal(s.h.managed[backupDir+"/"+name], data) {
			t.Errorf("managed file %s holds %q, want %q", name, s.h.managed[backupDir+"/"+name], data)
		}
	}
	if u.Config.Name != containerName || u.Config.Create.Image != upFromImage || !slices.Contains(u.Config.Create.Env, "HARNESS_ONLY=1") {
		t.Errorf("journaled configuration %+v, want the original container's", u.Config)
	}
	if len(u.Config.Networks) != 1 || u.Config.Networks[upAppNet] == nil || !reflect.DeepEqual(u.Config.Networks[upAppNet], wantEndpoint) {
		t.Errorf("journaled networks %+v, want only %s with its static address", u.Config.Networks, upAppNet)
	}

	effects := s.effects()
	if len(effects) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("journal %+v, want one effect", effects)
	}
	e := effects[0]
	if e.Kind != core.EffectModify || e.Resource != "npm.instance:image" || !e.Local || e.InverseLocal || e.State != core.EffectMade ||
		upImageOf(t, e.Before) != upFromImage || e.After == nil || upImageOf(t, *e.After) != Image || e.Post == nil || upImageOf(t, *e.Post) != Image {
		t.Errorf("effect %+v, want a made local modify of npm.instance:image from %s to %s", e, upFromImage, Image)
	}

	def := s.n.instanceUpgrade()
	if err := def.Handler.Verify(ctx, s.env, json.RawMessage(`{}`), out); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("verify after the upgrade: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestUpSameImageStillRecreatesTheInstance(t *testing.T) {
	s := upNewSetup(t, Image, true)
	out, err := s.apply()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var got Upgraded
	if err := json.Unmarshal(out, &got); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	c, ok := s.h.docker.container(containerName)
	if got != (Upgraded{FromImage: Image, ToImage: Image}) || !ok || c.ID == s.inst.ContainerID || !c.State.Running || s.phase() != "done" {
		t.Errorf("output %+v, container %+v (found %v), phase %q; want a new running container of the same image and phase done", got, c, ok, s.phase())
	}
}

// TestUpPhasesAreWrittenBeforeTheirChange: at each call that changes the host, the upgrade's row already names the
// phase the call belongs to, so a crash leaves the row naming the last phase that may be half done.
func TestUpPhasesAreWrittenBeforeTheirChange(t *testing.T) {
	s := upNewSetup(t, upFromImage, true)
	var calls []string
	note := func(call string) { calls = append(calls, call+"@"+s.phase()) }
	s.dock.trace = func(call string) {
		if slices.Contains([]string{"StopContainer", "RemoveContainer", "CreateContainer", "StartContainer"}, call) {
			note(call)
		}
	}
	s.files.trace = note
	inner := s.n.d.anon
	s.n.d.anon = func() npmAPI { return upHealthTrace{npmAPI: inner(), note: func() { note("Health") }} }
	if _, err := s.apply(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := []string{
		"StopContainer@stopped",
		"WriteManaged c1-data.tar@archiving",
		"WriteManaged c1-letsencrypt.tar@archiving",
		"RemoveContainer@recreating",
		"CreateContainer@recreating",
		"StartContainer@recreating",
		"Health@waiting",
	}
	if !slices.Equal(calls, want) {
		t.Errorf("calls and the phase recorded at each:\n%q\nwant\n%q", calls, want)
	}
}

func TestUpPullFailureChangesNothing(t *testing.T) {
	s := upNewSetup(t, upFromImage, false)
	s.dock.fail = func(call string, _ int) error {
		if call == "PullImage" {
			return errors.New("pull access denied")
		}
		return nil
	}
	_, err := s.apply()
	if err == nil || !core.IsDefinite(err) {
		t.Fatalf("error %v (definite %v), want a definite refusal", err, core.IsDefinite(err))
	}
	c, ok := s.h.docker.container(s.inst.ContainerID)
	if !ok || !c.State.Running || s.dock.counts["StopContainer"] != 0 || len(s.effects()) != 0 || s.phase() != "no row" {
		t.Errorf("container %+v (found %v), stops %d, effects %+v, phase %q; want the instance untouched and nothing recorded",
			c, ok, s.dock.counts["StopContainer"], s.effects(), s.phase())
	}
}

// TestUpFailureBeforeTheRecreateStartsTheOriginalAgain: until the original container is removed, a failure only has
// to start it again and take back the archives.
func TestUpFailureBeforeTheRecreateStartsTheOriginalAgain(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name    string
		partial bool
		setup   func(s *upSetup)
	}{
		{"the container does not stop", false, func(s *upSetup) {
			s.dock.fail = func(call string, _ int) error {
				if call == "StopContainer" {
					return errors.New("docker: stop timed out")
				}
				return nil
			}
		}},
		{"an archive cannot be written", true, func(s *upSetup) {
			s.files.failWrite = func(name string) error {
				if name == "c1-letsencrypt.tar" {
					return errors.New("disk full")
				}
				return nil
			}
		}},
		{"an archive reads back differently", true, func(s *upSetup) {
			inner := s.n.d.readFile
			s.n.d.readFile = func(name string) ([]byte, error) {
				b, err := inner(name)
				if err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
				return append(slices.Clone(b), 'x'), nil
			}
		}},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			s := upNewSetup(t, upFromImage, true)
			tc.setup(s)
			_, err := s.apply()
			if err == nil || core.IsDefinite(err) {
				t.Fatalf("error %v (definite %v), want an error that is not definite: the host changed", err, core.IsDefinite(err))
			}
			c, ok := s.h.docker.container(s.inst.ContainerID)
			if !ok || !c.State.Running || s.dock.counts["CreateContainer"] != 0 || s.dock.counts["RemoveContainer"] != 0 {
				t.Errorf("original %+v (found %v), creates %d, removals %d; want it running again and nothing recreated",
					c, ok, s.dock.counts["CreateContainer"], s.dock.counts["RemoveContainer"])
			}
			for _, name := range []string{"c1-data.tar", "c1-letsencrypt.tar"} {
				if slices.Contains(s.files.removed, name) != tc.partial {
					t.Errorf("removed archives %v, want %s removed: %v (a partial archive is deleted)", s.files.removed, name, tc.partial)
				}
			}
			if row := s.instanceRow(); row.ContainerID != s.inst.ContainerID || row.Image != upFromImage {
				t.Errorf("instance row %+v, want it unchanged", row)
			}
			if s.phase() != "rolled_back" {
				t.Errorf("phase %q, want rolled_back", s.phase())
			}
			if effects := s.effects(); len(effects) != 1 || effects[0].State != core.EffectSkipped {
				t.Errorf("journal %+v, want one skipped effect", effects)
			}
		})
	}
}

// TestUpRollbackRestoresTheOriginal covers a failure after the original container is gone: the new container is
// removed, the volumes are recreated from the verified archives and the original is recreated and started.
func TestUpRollbackRestoresTheOriginal(t *testing.T) {
	t.Run("the new container cannot be created", func(t *testing.T) {
		s := upNewSetup(t, upFromImage, true)
		s.dock.fail = func(call string, n int) error {
			if call == "CreateContainer" && n == 1 {
				return errors.New("docker: no space left on device")
			}
			return nil
		}
		_, err := s.apply()
		if err == nil || core.IsDefinite(err) {
			t.Fatalf("error %v (definite %v), want an error that is not definite", err, core.IsDefinite(err))
		}
		s.wantRestored()
	})

	t.Run("the new container's API does not answer within 120 seconds", func(t *testing.T) {
		s := upNewSetup(t, upFromImage, true)
		s.apiDown()
		start := s.h.clock.Now()
		_, err := s.apply()
		var coded *errcode.Error
		if !errors.As(err, &coded) || coded.Code != errcode.NPMUnhealthy || core.IsDefinite(err) {
			t.Fatalf("error %v (definite %v), want an npm.unhealthy error that is not definite", err, core.IsDefinite(err))
		}
		if waited := s.h.clock.Now().Sub(start); waited < 120*time.Second || waited >= 126*time.Second {
			t.Errorf("the upgrade waited %v for the API, want 120 s", waited)
		}
		if len(s.dock.ids) != 2 {
			t.Fatalf("created containers %v, want the new one and the recreated original", s.dock.ids)
		}
		s.wantRestored()
	})
}

func TestUpRollbackFailureIsRecorded(t *testing.T) {
	s := upNewSetup(t, upFromImage, true)
	s.apiDown()
	s.dock.fail = func(call string, _ int) error {
		if call == "CopyToContainer /etc" {
			return errors.New("docker: no space left on device")
		}
		return nil
	}
	_, err := s.apply()
	if err == nil || core.IsDefinite(err) {
		t.Fatalf("error %v (definite %v), want an error that is not definite", err, core.IsDefinite(err))
	}
	if u := s.upgrade(); u.Phase != "failed" || u.Result == "" {
		t.Errorf("upgrade row %+v, want phase failed with the reason", u)
	}
	if effects := s.effects(); len(effects) != 1 || effects[0].State != core.EffectIntended {
		t.Errorf("journal %+v, want the effect left intended for the engine to settle", effects)
	}
}

// TestUpRollbackVerifiesTheArchivesBeforeRemovingAnything: an archive that no longer matches its checksum stops the
// restore before the new container or the volumes are touched.
func TestUpRollbackVerifiesTheArchivesBeforeRemovingAnything(t *testing.T) {
	s := upNewSetup(t, upFromImage, true)
	s.apiDown()
	inner := s.n.d.readFile
	s.n.d.readFile = func(name string) ([]byte, error) {
		b, err := inner(name)
		if err != nil || s.phase() != "waiting" {
			return b, err
		}
		return append(slices.Clone(b), 'x'), nil
	}
	if _, err := s.apply(); err == nil || core.IsDefinite(err) {
		t.Fatalf("error %v (definite %v), want an error that is not definite", err, core.IsDefinite(err))
	}
	if _, ok := s.h.docker.container(s.dock.ids[0]); !ok || s.dock.counts["RemoveContainer"] != 1 || s.dock.counts["RemoveVolume"] != 0 {
		t.Errorf("new container present %v, container removals %d, volume removals %d; want only the original's removal",
			ok, s.dock.counts["RemoveContainer"], s.dock.counts["RemoveVolume"])
	}
	if u := s.upgrade(); u.Phase != "failed" || u.Result == "" {
		t.Errorf("upgrade row %+v, want phase failed with the reason", u)
	}
}

// TestUpPrunesOldArchiveSets: after a completed upgrade the archive sets of the three newest changes are kept, and so
// is any set a transaction that is not finished refers to.
func TestUpPrunesOldArchiveSets(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	s := upNewSetup(t, upFromImage, true)
	listing := fstest.MapFS{"readme.txt": {}}
	for _, id := range []string{"a1", "a2", "a3", "a4", "c1"} {
		listing[id+"-data.tar"] = &fstest.MapFile{}
		listing[id+"-letsencrypt.tar"] = &fstest.MapFile{}
	}
	s.n.d.readDir = func(name string) ([]os.DirEntry, error) {
		if name != backupDir {
			return nil, fs.ErrNotExist
		}
		return fs.ReadDir(listing, ".")
	}
	s.h.addChange("a1", "npm.instance.upgrade")
	err := insertUpgrade(ctx, s.h.db, upgradeRow{
		ChangeID: "a1", FromImage: upFromImage, ToImage: Image, Phase: "waiting", UpdatedAt: s.h.clock.Now(),
		Archives: []archiveRef{{Volume: dataVolume, File: "a1-data.tar", SHA256: "x"}, {Volume: certVolume, File: "a1-letsencrypt.tar", SHA256: "x"}},
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := s.apply(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	removed := slices.Clone(s.files.removed)
	slices.Sort(removed)
	if want := []string{"a2-data.tar", "a2-letsencrypt.tar"}; !slices.Equal(removed, want) {
		t.Errorf("removed %v, want %v: the oldest set that no unfinished transaction refers to", removed, want)
	}
}

func TestUpObserveReadsTheRunningImage(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	s := upNewSetup(t, upFromImage, true)
	h := s.n.instanceUpgrade().Handler
	e := core.Effect{ChangeID: "c1", Seq: 1, Kind: core.EffectModify, Resource: "npm.instance:image", Local: true}

	obs, err := h.Observe(ctx, s.env, e)
	if err != nil || obs.Matches != 1 || !obs.Exclusive || upImageOf(t, obs.Current) != upFromImage {
		t.Errorf("observation %+v (%v), want one exclusive match in image %s", obs, err, upFromImage)
	}
	if err := s.h.docker.RemoveContainer(ctx, s.inst.ContainerID, dockerapi.RemoveContainerOptions{Force: true}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	obs, err = h.Observe(ctx, s.env, e)
	if err != nil || obs.Matches != 1 || string(obs.Current.State) != "null" {
		t.Errorf("observation without a container %+v (%v), want state null", obs, err)
	}
	e.Resource = "npm.instance:public"
	if _, err := h.Observe(ctx, s.env, e); err == nil {
		t.Error("observing a resource this action does not change succeeded, want an error")
	}
}

func TestUpVerifyChecksThePinnedImageRuns(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	s := upNewSetup(t, upFromImage, true)
	h := s.n.instanceUpgrade().Handler
	if err := h.Verify(ctx, s.env, json.RawMessage(`{}`), nil); err == nil {
		t.Error("verify before the upgrade succeeded, want an error: the instance runs the older image")
	}
	if _, err := s.apply(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := h.Verify(ctx, s.env, json.RawMessage(`{}`), nil); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("verify without an output, as recovery calls it: %v", err)
	}
	if err := s.h.docker.RemoveContainer(ctx, s.instanceRow().ContainerID, dockerapi.RemoveContainerOptions{Force: true}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := h.Verify(ctx, s.env, json.RawMessage(`{}`), nil); err == nil {
		t.Error("verify without a container succeeded, want an error")
	}
}
```
