```go
package npm

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"reflect"
	"regexp"
	"slices"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/schema"
)

// caHost is the NPM host of these tests; the test host's own id ("h1") is deliberately another.
const caHost = "h7"

// caSteps asks def for its steps the way the engine does, through the erased handler.
func caSteps(t *testing.T, def core.ActionDef, in any) ([]core.Step, error) {
	t.Helper()
	raw, err := json.Marshal(in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return def.Handler.Steps(context.Background(), core.Env{}, raw)
}

// caGeneric is v as decoded JSON.
func caGeneric(t *testing.T, v any) any {
	t.Helper()
	raw, err := json.Marshal(v)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var out any
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &out); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return out
}

var caRef = regexp.MustCompile(`\$\{([^}]*)\}`)

// caResolve resolves the references of a step template against the composite's input in and the outputs of earlier
// steps, as the engine does: a string that is exactly one reference becomes the referenced value with its JSON type,
// a reference inside a longer string is replaced by the value's text, and a value that was substituted is never read
// as a template again. A reference to a member that does not exist fails the test, as it fails the plan.
func caResolve(t *testing.T, tmpl json.RawMessage, in any, outputs map[string]any) json.RawMessage {
	t.Helper()
	input := caGeneric(t, in)
	steps := map[string]any{}
	for id, v := range outputs {
		steps[id] = map[string]any{"output": caGeneric(t, v)}
	}
	lookup := func(ref string) any {
		parts := strings.Split(ref, ".")
		var cur any
		switch parts[0] {
		case "input":
			cur = input
		case "steps":
			cur = steps
		default:
			t.Errorf("reference ${%s} is neither an input nor a step reference", ref)
			return nil
		}
		for _, p := range parts[1:] {
			switch c := cur.(type) {
			case map[string]any:
				next, ok := c[p]
				if !ok {
					t.Errorf("reference ${%s}: there is no member %q", ref, p)
					return nil
				}
				cur = next
			case []any:
				i, err := strconv.Atoi(p)
				if err != nil || i < 0 || i >= len(c) {
					t.Errorf("reference ${%s}: %q is not an index of the array", ref, p)
					return nil
				}
				cur = c[i]
			default:
				t.Errorf("reference ${%s}: %q is looked up in a value that has no members", ref, p)
				return nil
			}
		}
		return cur
	}
	var walk func(v any) any
	walk = func(v any) any {
		switch x := v.(type) {
		case string:
			if m := caRef.FindStringSubmatch(x); m != nil && m[0] == x {
				return lookup(m[1])
			}
			return caRef.ReplaceAllStringFunc(x, func(s string) string { return fmt.Sprint(lookup(s[2 : len(s)-1])) })
		case map[string]any:
			for k, e := range x {
				x[k] = walk(e)
			}
		case []any:
			for i, e := range x {
				x[i] = walk(e)
			}
		}
		return v
	}
	var tv any
	if err := json.Unmarshal(tmpl, &tv); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("template %s: %v", tmpl, err)
	}
	out, err := json.Marshal(walk(tv))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return out
}

// caStrict decodes raw into a T and refuses a member T does not have.
func caStrict[T any](t *testing.T, raw json.RawMessage) T {
	t.Helper()
	var v T
	dec := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(raw))
	dec.DisallowUnknownFields()
	if err := dec.Decode(&v); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("input %s: %v", raw, err)
	}
	return v
}

// caWantStep fails the test unless s is the step id on host running action after the steps after.
func caWantStep(t *testing.T, s core.Step, id, host, action string, after []string) {
	t.Helper()
	if s.ID != id || s.Host != host || s.Action.String() != action || !slices.Equal(s.After, after) {
		t.Errorf("step %+v, want %s: %s on host %q after %v", s, id, action, host, after)
	}
}

func caIDs(steps []core.Step) []string {
	var ids []string
	for _, s := range steps {
		ids = append(ids, s.ID)
	}
	return ids
}

// caRefs counts the step references in the template of s.
func caRefs(s core.Step) int { return strings.Count(string(s.Input), "${steps.") }

func caJSON(t *testing.T, v any) json.RawMessage {
	t.Helper()
	raw, err := json.Marshal(v)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return raw
}

// TestCaDeclarations: both are controller-side composites that take the module's coordination lock; the exposure
// unwinds in reverse, the removal forward.
func TestCaDeclarations(t *testing.T) {
	n := newTHHost(t).nm()
	tests := []struct {
		name   string
		def    core.ActionDef
		unwind core.Unwind
	}{
		{"npm.admin.expose", n.adminExpose(), core.UnwindReverse},
		{"npm.admin.unexpose", n.adminUnexpose(), core.UnwindForward},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		s := tc.def.Spec
		if s.Name != tc.name || s.Version != 1 || s.Summary == "" || s.Class != core.ClassComposite || s.Side != core.SideController ||
			s.Unwind != tc.unwind || !slices.Equal(s.LockKeys, []string{"composite:npm"}) {
			t.Errorf("%s: spec %+v, want version 1, a summary, a controller-side composite, unwind %v, lock composite:npm", tc.name, s, tc.unwind)
		}
		if tc.def.Handler.Steps == nil || tc.def.Handler.Output == nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: the handler lacks Steps or Output", tc.name)
		}
		if _, err := schema.ActionSnapshot(tc.def); err != nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: schema: %v", tc.name, err)
		}
	}
}

// TestCaAdminExposeSteps: a public instance is required first, the hostname gets a certificate, and the proxy host
// forwards it to NPM's own admin port over HTTP with forced SSL and HSTS, behind the access list when one is given.
func TestCaAdminExposeSteps(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name string
		in   AdminExposeInput
		want ProxyHostInput
	}{
		{"with an access list", AdminExposeInput{HostID: caHost, Hostname: "npm.example.com", AccessListID: 5},
			ProxyHostInput{Domains: []string{"npm.example.com"}, ForwardScheme: "http", ForwardHost: "127.0.0.1", ForwardPort: 81, CertificateID: 42, AccessListID: 5, SSLForced: true, HSTS: true}},
		{"without one", AdminExposeInput{HostID: caHost, Hostname: "admin.example.org"},
			ProxyHostInput{Domains: []string{"admin.example.org"}, ForwardScheme: "http", ForwardHost: "127.0.0.1", ForwardPort: 81, CertificateID: 42, SSLForced: true, HSTS: true}},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			steps, err := caSteps(t, newTHHost(t).nm().adminExpose(), tc.in)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if want := []string{"public", "cert", "proxy"}; !slices.Equal(caIDs(steps), want) {
				t.Fatalf("steps %v, want %v", caIDs(steps), want)
			}
			caWantStep(t, steps[0], "public", "", "npm.public.check/v1", nil)
			if got := caStrict[PublicInput](t, caResolve(t, steps[0].Input, tc.in, nil)); got != (PublicInput{HostID: caHost}) {
				t.Errorf("public input %+v, want host %s", got, caHost)
			}
			caWantStep(t, steps[1], "cert", caHost, "npm.certificate.request/v1", []string{"public"})
			if got := caStrict[CertificateInput](t, caResolve(t, steps[1].Input, tc.in, nil)); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, CertificateInput{Domains: []string{tc.in.Hostname}}) {
				t.Errorf("certificate input %+v, want the hostname alone", got)
			}
			caWantStep(t, steps[2], "proxy", caHost, "npm.proxyhost.add/v1", []string{"cert"})
			outputs := map[string]any{"cert": CertificateView{ID: 42}}
			if got := caStrict[ProxyHostInput](t, caResolve(t, steps[2].Input, tc.in, outputs)); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, tc.want) {
				t.Errorf("proxy input %+v, want %+v", got, tc.want)
			}
			if caRefs(steps[2]) != 1 || caRefs(steps[0])+caRefs(steps[1]) != 0 {
				t.Errorf("step references: public %d, cert %d, proxy %d; want only the proxy step's reference to the certificate",
					caRefs(steps[0]), caRefs(steps[1]), caRefs(steps[2]))
			}
		})
	}
}

// TestCaAdminExposeKeepsInputOutOfTheTemplate: the hostname is typed by the operator and reaches the steps only
// through references to the input.
func TestCaAdminExposeKeepsInputOutOfTheTemplate(t *testing.T) {
	in := AdminExposeInput{HostID: caHost, Hostname: "${steps.cert.output.id}.example.com"}
	steps, err := caSteps(t, newTHHost(t).nm().adminExpose(), in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	outputs := map[string]any{"cert": CertificateView{ID: 9}}
	if got := caStrict[CertificateInput](t, caResolve(t, steps[1].Input, in, outputs)); !reflect.DeepEqual(got.Domains, []string{in.Hostname}) {
		t.Errorf("certificate domains %v, want the typed hostname unchanged", got.Domains)
	}
	proxy := caStrict[ProxyHostInput](t, caResolve(t, steps[2].Input, in, outputs))
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(proxy.Domains, []string{in.Hostname}) || proxy.CertificateID != 9 {
		t.Errorf("proxy input %+v, want the typed hostname unchanged and certificate 9", proxy)
	}
}

func TestCaAdminExposeOutput(t *testing.T) {
	def := newTHHost(t).nm().adminExpose()
	in := caJSON(t, AdminExposeInput{HostID: caHost, Hostname: "npm.example.com"})
	steps := map[string]json.RawMessage{"cert": caJSON(t, CertificateView{ID: 7}), "proxy": caJSON(t, ProxyHostView{ID: 11})}
	out, err := def.Handler.Output(context.Background(), core.Env{}, in, steps)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := caStrict[AdminExposeOutput](t, out); got != (AdminExposeOutput{ProxyHostID: 11, CertificateID: 7}) {
		t.Errorf("output %+v, want proxy host 11 and certificate 7", got)
	}
	for _, missing := range []string{"proxy", "cert"} {
		partial := map[string]json.RawMessage{}
		for k, v := range steps {
			if k != missing {
				partial[k] = v
			}
		}
		if _, err := def.Handler.Output(context.Background(), core.Env{}, in, partial); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), missing) {
			t.Errorf("output without the %s step: %v, want an error naming it", missing, err)
		}
	}
}

// TestCaAdminUnexposeSteps: the one step removes the proxy host of the hostname; the certificate stays.
func TestCaAdminUnexposeSteps(t *testing.T) {
	in := AdminUnexposeInput{HostID: caHost, Hostname: "npm.example.com"}
	steps, err := caSteps(t, newTHHost(t).nm().adminUnexpose(), in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if want := []string{"proxy"}; !slices.Equal(caIDs(steps), want) {
		t.Fatalf("steps %v, want %v", caIDs(steps), want)
	}
	caWantStep(t, steps[0], "proxy", caHost, "npm.proxyhost.remove/v1", nil)
	if got := caStrict[ProxyHostRemoveInput](t, caResolve(t, steps[0].Input, in, nil)); got != (ProxyHostRemoveInput{Domain: "npm.example.com"}) {
		t.Errorf("remove input %+v, want the hostname as the domain and no id", got)
	}
	if caRefs(steps[0]) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("template %s holds a step reference, want references to the input only", steps[0].Input)
	}
	hostile := AdminUnexposeInput{HostID: caHost, Hostname: "${steps.proxy.output.id}"}
	steps, err = caSteps(t, newTHHost(t).nm().adminUnexpose(), hostile)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := caStrict[ProxyHostRemoveInput](t, caResolve(t, steps[0].Input, hostile, nil)); got.Domain != hostile.Hostname {
		t.Errorf("remove domain %q, want the typed hostname unchanged", got.Domain)
	}
}

func TestCaAdminUnexposeOutput(t *testing.T) {
	def := newTHHost(t).nm().adminUnexpose()
	for _, hostname := range []string{"npm.example.com", "admin.example.org"} {
		in := caJSON(t, AdminUnexposeInput{HostID: caHost, Hostname: hostname})
		out, err := def.Handler.Output(context.Background(), core.Env{}, in, map[string]json.RawMessage{"proxy": caJSON(t, ProxyHostView{ID: 11})})
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if got := caStrict[AdminUnexposeOutput](t, out); got != (AdminUnexposeOutput{Hostname: hostname}) {
			t.Errorf("output %+v, want hostname %s", got, hostname)
		}
	}
	in := caJSON(t, AdminUnexposeInput{HostID: caHost, Hostname: "npm.example.com"})
	if _, err := def.Handler.Output(context.Background(), core.Env{}, in, map[string]json.RawMessage{}); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "proxy") {
		t.Errorf("output without the proxy step: %v, want an error naming proxy", err)
	}
}
```

The tests decode every step input strictly into the wire type of the action it feeds, so the member names a template uses must be the `json` names of Task 11's `wire.go`; Contracts prescribes lowerCamelCase, and `wire.go` is the authority if a name differs.

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the tests to verify they fail**

Run: `mkdir -p /root/.cache/w6a-tmp/26 && export TMPDIR=/root/.cache/w6a-tmp/26 GOTOOLCHAIN=local && timeout 300 go test -count=1 -run 'TestCa' ./internal/modules/npm/`
Expected: FAIL to build, naming the first undefined identifier (`newTHHost(t).nm().adminExpose undefined`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

In `composite_admin.go`, each constructor is `core.DefineComposite[In, Out]` with `Version: 1`, a one-sentence summary and the `Unwind` named above. Summaries: admin.expose "Publish Nginx Proxy Manager's admin UI over HTTPS on a hostname, with a certificate, forced SSL and HSTS."; admin.unexpose "Remove the proxy host that publishes Nginx Proxy Manager's admin UI; its certificate stays." Each composer is a struct with `Steps` and `Output`.

Rules for every template (the engine reads each `${` of a template as a reference, so what an operator typed must never be spliced into a template): the host id and the hostname are passed only as whole-string references to the input, `${input.hostId}` and `${input.hostname}` (inside an array element for `domains`); integers, booleans and constants that cannot hold `${` are written as literals. A step's `Host` is `in.HostID` for agent actions and empty for the controller read. Build each input as a map marshalled with `encoding/json`, in a helper `adminStepInput`, with the member names of `wire.go`.

1. `npm.admin.expose`:
   - `public` (`npm.public.check`, no host, no `After`): `hostId` is `${input.hostId}`.
   - `cert` (`npm.certificate.request`, host `in.HostID`, `After: ["public"]`): `domains` is an array of the one element `${input.hostname}`.
   - `proxy` (`npm.proxyhost.add`, host `in.HostID`, `After: ["cert"]`): `domains` is the array of the one element `${input.hostname}`, `forwardScheme` `http`, `forwardHost` `127.0.0.1`, `forwardPort` the literal `81`, `certificateId` the whole-string reference `${steps.cert.output.id}`, `accessListId` the literal `in.AccessListID` (zero: none), `sslForced` and `hsts` `true`, `websockets` `false`. This is the only template with a step reference. The child runs with `ParentAction` `npm.admin.expose`, which is what lets `npm.proxyhost.add` accept the reserved upstream and record the reservation; the composite adds nothing for that.
   - `Output` reads the `id` of the `proxy` and `cert` outputs through `adminStepID(steps, id) (int, error)` (it decodes a struct whose one member `ID int` carries the JSON name `id`; an absent step is an error "npm: the admin exposure has no output of its <id> step") and returns `AdminExposeOutput{ProxyHostID, CertificateID}`.
2. `npm.admin.unexpose`:
   - `proxy` (`npm.proxyhost.remove`, host `in.HostID`, no `After`): `domain` is `${input.hostname}`; no `id`. As a child of `npm.admin.unexpose` it may remove the reserved proxy host and release the exposure's reservations; the certificate stays and is removed only by the explicitly confirmed `npm.certificate.remove`.
   - `Output` requires the `proxy` step's output (error "npm: the admin unexposure has no output of its proxy step" otherwise) and returns `AdminUnexposeOutput{Hostname: in.Hostname}`.
3. `Steps` reads only its input. Error strings start with `npm:`. No new error code.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

`internal/modules/npm/register_test.go` is the module's registration test. It checks the table of Contracts "NPM actions" row by row (class, side, locks, the scope `core.RequiredScope` computes, long-running flag and deadline, internal and reveal flags, converges, unwind, availability check, undo, recovery and resolver handlers, handler shape per class, input and output types), that each input type's schema accepts a fully populated sample marshaled from the Go value and refuses an unknown member, that the members the contracts make required or typed are refused when missing or mistyped, that only the revealed password is a secret, that the 26 snapshots are committed and no other `schemas/npm.*` directory exists, that the registry accepts the module, and that each composite's steps name registered actions and obey the engine's plan-time rules with inputs valid for the child's schema once references are filled in.

```go
package npm

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"io/fs"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"reflect"
	"regexp"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/schema"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/firewall"
)

// regAction is one row of the table of the actions spec 6 gives the module.
type regAction struct {
	name      string
	class     core.Class
	side      core.Side
	long      bool          // LongRunning
	apply     time.Duration // the ApplyTimeout of a long-running action
	in, out   reflect.Type  // out nil: not checked; in nil: the input type has no name in the contracts
	available bool          // declares its own availability check
	undoer    bool          // has an Inverse (and so an Observe) handler
	recoverer bool          // has a Recover handler
	resolver  bool          // has a Resolved handler
	converges bool
	internal  bool
	reveal    bool
	unwind    core.Unwind
}

func regType[T any]() reflect.Type { return reflect.TypeFor[T]() }

var regActions = []regAction{
	{name: "npm.instance.deploy", class: core.ClassCompensable, side: core.SideAgent, long: true, apply: 20 * time.Minute, available: true, undoer: true, in: regType[DeployInput](), out: regType[Deployed]()},
	{name: "npm.instance.status", class: core.ClassRead, side: core.SideAgent, in: regType[StatusInput](), out: regType[InstanceStatus]()},
	{name: "npm.instance.confirm-public", class: core.ClassCompensable, side: core.SideAgent, undoer: true, in: regType[ConfirmPublicInput](), out: regType[PublicMark]()},
	{name: "npm.instance.remove", class: core.ClassIrreversible, side: core.SideAgent, in: regType[RemoveInput](), out: regType[Removed]()},
	{name: "npm.instance.upgrade", class: core.ClassCompensable, side: core.SideAgent, long: true, apply: 20 * time.Minute, undoer: true, recoverer: true, in: regType[UpgradeInput](), out: regType[Upgraded]()},
	{name: "npm.network.attach", class: core.ClassCompensable, side: core.SideAgent, undoer: true, in: regType[AttachInput](), out: regType[Attachment]()},
	{name: "npm.network.detach", class: core.ClassCompensable, side: core.SideAgent, undoer: true, converges: true, in: regType[DetachInput]()},
	{name: "npm.proxyhost.add", class: core.ClassCompensable, side: core.SideAgent, undoer: true, resolver: true, in: regType[ProxyHostInput](), out: regType[ProxyHostView]()},
	{name: "npm.proxyhost.update", class: core.ClassCompensable, side: core.SideAgent, undoer: true, in: regType[ProxyHostUpdateInput](), out: regType[ProxyHostView]()},
	{name: "npm.proxyhost.remove", class: core.ClassCompensable, side: core.SideAgent, undoer: true, converges: true, in: regType[ProxyHostRemoveInput](), out: regType[ProxyHostView]()},
	{name: "npm.proxyhost.list", class: core.ClassRead, side: core.SideAgent, in: regType[ProxyHostListInput](), out: regType[ProxyHostList]()},

	{name: "npm.certificate.remove", class: core.ClassIrreversible, side: core.SideAgent, in: regType[CertificateRemoveInput](), out: regType[CertificateView]()},
	{name: "npm.certificate.list", class: core.ClassRead, side: core.SideAgent, in: regType[CertificateListInput](), out: regType[CertificateList]()},

func regModule(t *testing.T) module {
	t.Helper()
	return module{n: newTHHost(t).nm()}
}

func regDefs(t *testing.T, m core.Module) map[string]core.ActionDef {
	t.Helper()
	defs := map[string]core.ActionDef{}
	for _, a := range m.Manifest().Actions {
		if _, dup := defs[a.Spec.Name]; dup {
			t.Errorf("%s is declared twice", a.Spec.Name)
		}
		defs[a.Spec.Name] = a
	}
	return defs
}

// regScope is the scope an action needs: reads need read, a reveal admin, a composite admin and every
// other agent mutation operate.
func regScope(a regAction) core.Scope {
	switch {
	case a.reveal, a.class == core.ClassComposite:
		return core.ScopeAdmin
	case a.class == core.ClassRead:
		return core.ScopeRead
	default:
		return core.ScopeOperate
	}
}

// TestRegManifestDeclaresTheActions: the module declares the 26 actions of the table, all version 1,
// each on its side, the agent mutations under the npm lock, the composites under composite:npm and the reads under
// none, with the class, scope, handlers, availability check, deadline and unwind policy of the table.
func TestRegManifestDeclaresTheActions(t *testing.T) {
	m := regModule(t)
	man := m.Manifest()
	if man.ID != ID || man.Version != 1 || !man.DefaultEnabled {
		t.Errorf("manifest %q v%d default-enabled %v, want npm v1 enabled by default", man.ID, man.Version, man.DefaultEnabled)
	}
	got := regDefs(t, m)
	for _, want := range regActions {
		a, ok := got[want.name]
		if !ok {
			t.Errorf("%s is not declared", want.name)
			continue
		}
		delete(got, want.name)
		s := a.Spec
		wantLocks := []string{"npm"}
		switch want.class {
		case core.ClassRead:
			wantLocks = nil
		case core.ClassComposite:
			wantLocks = []string{"composite:npm"}
		}
		if s.Version != 1 || s.Class != want.class || s.Side != want.side || !slices.Equal(s.LockKeys, wantLocks) ||
			core.RequiredScope(s) != regScope(want) || s.LongRunning != want.long || s.Internal != want.internal || s.Reveal != want.reveal ||
			s.Converges != want.converges || s.Unwind != want.unwind || s.RepairedBy != "" || s.BreakGlass || len(s.Guards) != 0 {
			t.Errorf("%s: spec %+v; want class %s on the %s, locks %v, scope %s, long-running %v, internal %v, reveal %v, converges %v, unwind %q, no guard",
				want.name, s, want.class, want.side, wantLocks, regScope(want), want.long, want.internal, want.reveal, want.converges, want.unwind)
		}
		if want.long && s.ApplyTimeout != want.apply {
			t.Errorf("%s: apply timeout %s, want %s", want.name, s.ApplyTimeout, want.apply)
		}
		if (s.Available != nil) != want.available {
			t.Errorf("%s: availability check %v, want %v", want.name, s.Available != nil, want.available)
		}
		h := a.Handler
		if (h.Inverse != nil) != want.undoer || (want.undoer && h.Observe == nil) || (h.Recover != nil) != want.recoverer || (want.resolver && h.Resolved == nil) {
			t.Errorf("%s: undo handler %v, observer %v, recovery handler %v, resolver %v; want undo %v, recovery %v, resolver %v",
				want.name, h.Inverse != nil, h.Observe != nil, h.Recover != nil, h.Resolved != nil, want.undoer, want.recoverer, want.resolver)
		}
		var served bool
		switch {
		case want.internal || want.reveal:
			served = h.Reveal != nil && h.Read == nil
		case want.class == core.ClassRead:
			served = h.Read != nil && h.Reveal == nil
		case want.class == core.ClassComposite:
			served = h.Steps != nil && h.Output != nil && h.Plan == nil && h.Apply == nil
		default:
			served = h.Plan != nil && h.Verify != nil && (h.Apply != nil || h.Task != nil)
		}
		if !served {
			t.Errorf("%s: handlers do not match its class %s (internal %v, reveal %v)", want.name, want.class, want.internal, want.reveal)
		}
		if want.in != nil && a.InputType != want.in {
			t.Errorf("%s: input type %v, want %v", want.name, a.InputType, want.in)
		}
		if want.out != nil && a.OutputType != want.out {
			t.Errorf("%s: output type %v, want %v", want.name, a.OutputType, want.out)
		}
		if _, err := schema.ActionSnapshot(a); err != nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: %v", a.Ref(), err)
		}
	}
	for name := range got {
		t.Errorf("%s is declared but is not in the action table", name)
	}
}

// TestRegManifestDeclaresTheRest: no resource, no preflight check and no external unit, the one owned directory and
// the agent's migrations.
func TestRegManifestDeclaresTheRest(t *testing.T) {
	man := regModule(t).Manifest()
	if len(man.Resources) != 0 || len(man.Preflight) != 0 || len(man.ExternalUnits) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("manifest declares %d resources, %d preflight checks and %d external units, want none: availability is per action and the fleet read is npm.instance.list",
			len(man.Resources), len(man.Preflight), len(man.ExternalUnits))
	}
	if len(man.OwnedPaths) != 1 || man.OwnedPaths[0].Path != backupDir || man.OwnedPaths[0].Kind != core.OwnedManagedDir {
		t.Errorf("owned paths %+v, want the one managed directory %s", man.OwnedPaths, backupDir)
	}
	migrations, ok := man.Migrations[core.SideAgent]
	if !ok || len(man.Migrations) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("migrations %v, want the agent side only", man.Migrations)
	}
	found := false
	err := fs.WalkDir(migrations, ".", func(p string, d fs.DirEntry, err error) error {
		found = found || err == nil && !d.IsDir() && filepath.Base(p) == "0001_npm.sql"
		return err
	})
	if err != nil || !found {
		t.Errorf("agent migrations hold no 0001_npm.sql (walk error %v)", err)
	}
}

// regMembers marshals a sample into its JSON members.
func regMembers(t *testing.T, v any) map[string]any {
	t.Helper()
	raw, err := json.Marshal(v)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var m map[string]any
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &m); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return m
}

// regRefusal requires err to be request.invalid_input naming field.
func regRefusal(err error, field string) bool {
	var coded *errcode.Error
	return errors.As(err, &coded) && coded.Code == errcode.InvalidInput &&
		slices.ContainsFunc(coded.Fields, func(f errcode.FieldError) bool { return f.Path == field })
}

// TestRegInputsAcceptTheirSampleAndRefuseUnknownMembers: each action is registered with its own input type, so its
// schema accepts the input it takes, written with the type's own JSON names, and refuses a member it does not have.
func TestRegInputsAcceptTheirSampleAndRefuseUnknownMembers(t *testing.T) {
	defs := regDefs(t, regModule(t))
	v := schema.NewValidator()
	for _, want := range regActions {
		def, ok := defs[want.name]
		if !ok {
			t.Errorf("%s is not declared", want.name)
			continue
		}
		sample, ok := regSamples[want.name]
		if !ok {
			t.Errorf("%s has no sample input", want.name)
			continue
		}
		members := regMembers(t, sample)
		raw, _ := json.Marshal(members)
		if err := v.ValidateInput(def, raw); err != nil {
			t.Errorf("%s %s: %v, want it accepted", want.name, raw, err)
		}
		members["x"] = 1
		raw, _ = json.Marshal(members)
		if err := v.ValidateInput(def, raw); !regRefusal(err, "/x") {
			t.Errorf("%s %s: %v, want request.invalid_input naming /x", want.name, raw, err)
		}
	}
}

// TestRegInputsRefuseWhatTheWireTypesRequire: the members that spec 6 and the wire types make required or typed.
func TestRegInputsRefuseWhatTheWireTypesRequire(t *testing.T) {
	defs := regDefs(t, regModule(t))
	v := schema.NewValidator()
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name, action, field string
		mutate              func(map[string]any)
	}{
		{"deploy without the ACME email", "npm.instance.deploy", "/acmeEmail", func(m map[string]any) { delete(m, "acmeEmail") }},
		{"deploy-public without the host", "npm.instance.deploy-public", "/hostId", func(m map[string]any) { delete(m, "hostId") }},
		{"deploy-public with a text integration flag", "npm.instance.deploy-public", "/integration", func(m map[string]any) { m["integration"] = "yes" }},
		{"expose without the host", "npm.expose.container", "/hostId", func(m map[string]any) { delete(m, "hostId") }},
		{"expose with a text port", "npm.expose.container", "/port", func(m map[string]any) { m["port"] = "8080" }},
		{"proxy host with one domain as text", "npm.proxyhost.add", "/domains", func(m map[string]any) { m["domains"] = "app.example.com" }},
		{"access list removed by a text id", "npm.accesslist.remove", "/id", func(m map[string]any) { m["id"] = "2" }},
		{"attach with a numeric network", "npm.network.attach", "/network", func(m map[string]any) { m["network"] = 1 }},
	} {
		def, ok := defs[tc.action]
		if !ok {
			t.Errorf("%s: %s is not declared", tc.name, tc.action)
			continue
		}
		members := regMembers(t, regSamples[tc.action])
		tc.mutate(members)
		raw, _ := json.Marshal(members)
		if err := v.ValidateInput(def, raw); !regRefusal(err, tc.field) {
			t.Errorf("%s: %s %s: %v, want request.invalid_input naming %s", tc.name, tc.action, raw, err, tc.field)
		}
	}
}

// TestRegOnlyTheRevealedPasswordIsSecret: the admin password is the one secret value the module declares, in the
// output of the reveal action; no input and no other output is marked secret, because the password, an NPM token and
// a private key never enter an action's input or output.
func TestRegOnlyTheRevealedPasswordIsSecret(t *testing.T) {
	defs := regDefs(t, regModule(t))
	for _, want := range regActions {
		def, ok := defs[want.name]
		if !ok {
			t.Errorf("%s is not declared", want.name)
			continue
		}
		in, err := schema.Input(def.InputType)
		if err != nil {
			t.Errorf("%s input: %v", want.name, err)
		}
		out, err := schema.Output(def.OutputType)
		if err != nil {
			t.Errorf("%s output: %v", want.name, err)
		}
		if bytes.Contains(in, []byte("x-secret")) {
			t.Errorf("%s: its input schema marks a field secret", want.name)
		}
		if secret := bytes.Contains(out, []byte("x-secret")); secret != want.reveal {
			t.Errorf("%s: its output schema marks a field secret: %v, want %v", want.name, secret, want.reveal)
		}
	}
}

// TestRegSnapshotsAreCommitted: each action has its committed snapshot, and no npm snapshot exists for anything else
// (modules.TestSnapshotsCurrent checks that each file is current).
func TestRegSnapshotsAreCommitted(t *testing.T) {
	dir := filepath.Join("..", "..", "..", "schemas")
	for _, a := range regActions {
		if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(dir, a.name, "v1.json")); err != nil {
			t.Errorf("schemas/%s/v1.json: %v; run make schemas", a.name, err)
		}
	}
	dirs, err := filepath.Glob(filepath.Join(dir, "npm.*"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := map[string]bool{}
	for _, a := range regActions {
		want[a.name] = true
	}
	for _, d := range dirs {
		if name := filepath.Base(d); !want[name] {
			t.Errorf("schemas/%s belongs to no npm action; run make schemas", name)
		}
	}
}

func TestRegRegistryAcceptsTheModule(t *testing.T) {
	for _, m := range []core.Module{regModule(t), Module()} {
		reg, err := core.NewRegistry(m, firewall.Module())
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		for _, a := range regActions {
			if _, ok := reg.Action(core.ActionRef{Name: a.name, Version: 1}); !ok {
				t.Errorf("the registry has no %s", a.name)
			}
		}
	}
}

var (
	regStepRef  = regexp.MustCompile(`\$\{steps\.([a-z][a-z0-9]*)\.output`)
	regInputRef = regexp.MustCompile(`"\$\{input\.([A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9]*)\}"`)
	// regFill gives the id a step's output reference stands for a value of the right type.
	regFill = regexp.MustCompile(`"\$\{steps\.[a-z][a-z0-9]*\.output\.id\}"`)
)

// regComposites are the composites over the NPM host h1 with the step ids each returns.
var regComposites = []struct {
	name    string
	action  string
	input   any
	forward bool
	steps   []string
}{
	{"deploy without the integration", "npm.instance.deploy-public", DeployPublicInput{HostID: "h1", AcmeEmail: "ops@example.com"}, false, []string{"check", "deploy"}},
	{"deploy with the integration", "npm.instance.deploy-public", DeployPublicInput{HostID: "h1", AcmeEmail: "ops@example.com", Integration: true}, false, []string{"check", "deploy", "ports"}},
	{"expose a container", "npm.expose.container", ExposeInput{HostID: "h1", Container: "web", Port: 8080, Network: "appnet", Domains: []string{"app.example.com"}, Websockets: true, AccessListID: 2}, false, []string{"public", "network", "cert", "proxy"}},
	{"remove an exposure", "npm.expose.remove", ExposeRemoveInput{HostID: "h1", Domain: "app.example.com"}, true, []string{"proxy", "network"}},
	{"expose the admin UI", "npm.admin.expose", AdminExposeInput{HostID: "h1", Hostname: "npm.example.com", AccessListID: 2}, false, []string{"public", "cert", "proxy"}},
	{"unexpose the admin UI", "npm.admin.unexpose", AdminUnexposeInput{HostID: "h1", Hostname: "npm.example.com"}, true, []string{"proxy"}},
}

// TestRegStepsMatchTheRegistry: every step a composite returns names a registered action, and obeys the rules the
// engine checks at plan time: a controller action takes no host and an agent action the NPM host, no step is
// irreversible or a composite, at most one step is revertible and it is last, every compensable step of a reverse
// composite has an undo handler, every step of a forward composite that is not a read converges, each step follows
// only steps before it and references only steps it follows, and each step's input is valid for its action's schema
// once the references are filled in.
func TestRegStepsMatchTheRegistry(t *testing.T) {
	validator := schema.NewValidator()
	for _, c := range regComposites {
		t.Run(c.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h := newTHHost(t)
			reg, err := core.NewRegistry(module{n: h.nm()}, firewall.Module())
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			def, ok := reg.Action(core.ActionRef{Name: c.action, Version: 1})
			if !ok {
				t.Fatalf("%s is not registered", c.action)
			}
			if (def.Spec.Unwind == core.UnwindForward) != c.forward {
				t.Errorf("%s unwinds %q, want forward %v", c.action, def.Spec.Unwind, c.forward)
			}
			members := regMembers(t, c.input)
			raw, _ := json.Marshal(c.input)
			steps, err := def.Handler.Steps(context.Background(), h.ctlEnv(""), raw)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			ids := map[string]int{}
			revertible := -1
			var got []string
			for i, s := range steps {
				got = append(got, s.ID)
				if _, dup := ids[s.ID]; dup {
					t.Errorf("step id %s appears twice", s.ID)
				}
				ids[s.ID] = i
				child, ok := reg.Action(s.Action)
				if !ok {
					t.Errorf("step %s: %s is not registered", s.ID, s.Action)
					continue
				}
				if child.Spec.Side == core.SideController && s.Host != "" || child.Spec.Side == core.SideAgent && s.Host != "h1" {
					t.Errorf("step %s: %s is %s-side but the step names host %q", s.ID, s.Action, child.Spec.Side, s.Host)
				}
				switch child.Spec.Class {
				case core.ClassIrreversible, core.ClassComposite:
					t.Errorf("step %s: %s is %s, which a step never is", s.ID, s.Action, child.Spec.Class)
				case core.ClassRevertible:
					if revertible >= 0 {
						t.Errorf("step %s: a second revertible step", s.ID)
					}
					revertible = i
				case core.ClassCompensable:
					if !c.forward && child.Handler.Inverse == nil {
						t.Errorf("step %s: %s has no undo handler, which a reverse composite needs", s.ID, s.Action)
					}
				}
				if c.forward && child.Spec.Class != core.ClassRead && !child.Spec.Converges {
					t.Errorf("step %s: %s does not converge, which a forward composite needs of every step that is not a read", s.ID, s.Action)
				}
				for _, a := range s.After {
					if before, ok := ids[a]; !ok || before >= i {
						t.Errorf("step %s follows %s, which is not an earlier step", s.ID, a)
					}
				}
				if i > 0 && len(s.After) == 0 {
					t.Errorf("step %s follows no step", s.ID)
				}
				input := regInputRef.ReplaceAllStringFunc(string(s.Input), func(ref string) string {
					member := regInputRef.FindStringSubmatch(ref)[1]
					v, ok := members[member]
					if !ok {
						t.Errorf("step %s: %s names a member the composite's input does not hold", s.ID, ref)
						return ref
					}
					b, _ := json.Marshal(v)
					return string(b)
				})
				for _, m := range regStepRef.FindAllStringSubmatch(input, -1) {
					if !slices.Contains(s.After, m[1]) {
						t.Errorf("step %s references step %s but does not follow it", s.ID, m[1])
					}
				}
				input = regFill.ReplaceAllString(input, "1")
				if strings.Contains(input, "${") {
					t.Errorf("step %s: input %s holds a reference the test does not fill", s.ID, s.Input)
					continue
				}
				if err := validator.ValidateInput(child, json.RawMessage(input)); err != nil {
					t.Errorf("step %s: input %s is invalid for %s: %v", s.ID, input, s.Action, err)
				}
			}
			if revertible >= 0 && revertible != len(steps)-1 {
				t.Errorf("the revertible step %s is not last", steps[revertible].ID)
			}
			if !slices.Equal(got, c.steps) {
				t.Errorf("steps %v, want %v", got, c.steps)
			}
		})
	}
}

// TestRegDeployPublicPortsStep: the ports step of deploy-public allows both published ports of darkpanel-npm from
// every source, in one firewall.containers.apply.
func TestRegDeployPublicPortsStep(t *testing.T) {
	h := newTHHost(t)
	def, ok := regDefs(t, module{n: h.nm()})["npm.instance.deploy-public"]
	if !ok {
		t.Fatal("npm.instance.deploy-public is not declared")
	}
	raw, _ := json.Marshal(DeployPublicInput{HostID: "h1", AcmeEmail: "ops@example.com", Integration: true})
	steps, err := def.Handler.Steps(context.Background(), h.ctlEnv(""), raw)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	i := slices.IndexFunc(steps, func(s core.Step) bool { return s.ID == "ports" })
	if i < 0 {
		t.Fatalf("steps %+v have no ports step", steps)
	}
	if s := steps[i]; s.Action.Name != "firewall.containers.apply" || !slices.Equal(s.After, []string{"deploy"}) {
		t.Errorf("ports step is %s after %v, want firewall.containers.apply after [deploy]", s.Action.Name, s.After)
	}
	var in firewall.ContainersApplyInput
	if err := json.Unmarshal(steps[i].Input, &in); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var ports []uint16
	for _, op := range in.Ops {
		if op.Op != "allow" || op.Binding == nil || op.Binding.Selector != "darkpanel-npm" || op.Binding.Proto != "tcp" || op.From.IsValid() {
			t.Errorf("op %+v, want an allow of a tcp binding of darkpanel-npm from every source", op)
			continue
		}
		ports = append(ports, op.Binding.HostPort)
	}
	slices.Sort(ports)
	if !slices.Equal(ports, []uint16{80, 443}) {
		t.Errorf("ports %v, want 80 and 443", ports)
	}
}
```

### Task 28: harness: TestM6NPM against Pebble

**Files:**
- Create: `test/harness/m6_npm_test.go` (`//go:build harness`; `TestM6NPM`, its helpers and its unit tests, all in this one file)
- Read, not edited: `test/harness/m5_derp_test.go` (`m5dLab` and the step table), `m5_derp_helpers_test.go` (`m5dCapDiagnostic`), `m5_helpers_test.go`, `lab_helpers_test.go`, `m3_docker_helpers_test.go` (`m3dxImage`, `m3dxShell`, `m3dDocker`, `m3dWaitRunning`), `m4_helpers_test.go` (`m4Plan`, `m4ApplyAsync`, `m4Reveal`), `api.go` (`APIError`); `pins.go` and `images.json` stay as they are, because the NPM image is `npm.Image` and not a pin

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Contracts "Harness M6 NPM" (this task's half), "NPM image" (`npm.Image`), "NPM wire shapes" (`DeployPublicInput`, `DeployPublicOutput`, `InstanceStatus`, `InstancesOutput`, `HostInstance`, `StatusInput`, `ConfirmPublicInput`, `RevealInput`, `AdminPassword`, `ProxyHostInput`, `ProxyHostListInput`, `ProxyHostList`, `ProxyHostView`, `ExposeInput`, `ExposeOutput`, `ExposeRemoveInput`, `UpgradeInput`, `Upgraded`); the registered actions of Task 27, called by name through the controller API; `errcode.NPMUnsupportedTarget` and `errcode.NPMReserved`; the harness helpers named under Files; Task 1's record `docs/dforge/notes/v1-w6a-npm-spike.md` for the CA-bundle variable.
- Produces: `TestM6NPM`, the flag `-m6n` (`m6nFlag`) and `const m6nDeadline = 40*time.Minute`, which Task 29's workflow test reads; identifiers prefixed `m6n`; unit tests named `TestM6nUnit...`, which `go test -tags harness -run Unit ./test/harness/...` runs.

**Depends on:** Task 27
**Implementer:** dforge:impl-sonnet-high
**Evaluation:** files 0 - spec 2 - coupling 2 - risk 1 = 5

Host safety: nothing here runs Docker, Pebble or NPM on this host. The scenario drives the agent VM over SSH, and it cannot run here at all (this host has no `/dev/kvm`); the pure helpers are unit-tested and the scenario is checked by `go vet`, by lint and by running it without `-m6n`, where it skips. Its first real run is the plan's gate 7 on the pushed branch, after Task 29 adds the job. Do not run `gh workflow run` here.

What the scenario does, in order (each is a `t.Run`; a failed gate step ends the test, and any failed step logs the containers, networks, container logs, NPM's `/api` and `ufw status` of the agent):

| Step | Gate | What it proves |
|---|---|---|
| `prepare` | yes | Pebble's config (validation ports 80 and 443) and CA file are on the agent; the NPM, `pebble` and `pebble-challtestsrv` images are pulled with retries |
| `deploy` | yes | `npm.instance.deploy-public` with `Integration: false` completes (the lab leaves the firewall's Docker integration off, so no `ports` step) |
| `harness-settings` | yes | `darkpanel-npm` is recreated with the same name, labels, mounts, ports and network plus `LE_SERVER` and the CA-bundle variable, and its new id is written to `npm_instances.container_id` with `sqlite3` (installed on every harness VM, `provision.go:61`); the admin survives in the volume; challtestsrv answers every name with NPM's address; Pebble runs on the NPM network under the alias `pebble` |
| `admin` | no | `npm.instance.status` finds the recreated container by the id `harness-settings` wrote to the instance row; `npm.instance.list` shows it; `npm.admin.reveal-password` returns 32 random bytes in base64url and NPM accepts them for the admin's email |
| `public` | yes | ports 80 and 443 answer from the scanner; `npm.instance.confirm-public` marks the instance public |
| `unsupported-target` | no | a default-bridge-only container is refused in the preview with `npm.unsupported_target` and the reason |
| `reserved-upstreams` | no | `npm.proxyhost.add` alone is refused with `npm.reserved` for 127.0.0.1:81, localhost:81, NPM's own address on 81, the gateway of a network named `darkpanel-panel` and an address inside it; no proxy host exists afterwards |
| `expose` | yes | `npm.expose.container` for the Pebble image's HTTP listener and an echo server on one user-defined network: NPM joins it, a certificate comes from Pebble, and the scanner gets both over HTTPS with a chain that verifies under Pebble's root |
| `headers` | no | through the custom location the echo server receives one `Host` header and one `X-Darkpanel-Client` equal to the scanner's address, whether the request lacks the header, forges it or forges it twice |
| `expose-remove` | yes | `npm.expose.remove` of the first exposure removes its proxy host; NPM stays on the shared network (the other claim) and the other host still answers |
| `upgrade` | yes | `npm.instance.upgrade` to the image already running completes, leaves the instance ready and keeps the proxy host |
| `expose-remove-last` | no | the last `npm.expose.remove` works on the recreated NPM and detaches it from the network |

func TestM6nUnitInspectEnv(t *testing.T) {
	env, err := m6nInspectEnv([]byte(`[{"Config":{"Env":["PATH=/bin","DISABLE_IPV6=true"]}}]`))
	if err != nil || !slices.Equal(env, []string{"PATH=/bin", "DISABLE_IPV6=true"}) {
		t.Errorf("m6nInspectEnv = %v, %v, want the two variables", env, err)
	}
	for _, bad := range []string{`[]`, `[{},{}]`, `{`} {
		if _, err := m6nInspectEnv([]byte(bad)); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("m6nInspectEnv(%s) was accepted, want an error", bad)

	}

func TestM6nUnitPebbleConfig(t *testing.T) {
	var c struct {
		Pebble struct {
			ListenAddress string `json:"listenAddress"`
			HTTPPort      int    `json:"httpPort"`
			TLSPort       int    `json:"tlsPort"`
			Certificate   string `json:"certificate"`
			PrivateKey    string `json:"privateKey"`
		} `json:"pebble"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(m6nPebbleConfig), &c); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	p := c.Pebble
	if p.HTTPPort != 80 || p.TLSPort != 443 || p.ListenAddress != "0.0.0.0:14000" || p.Certificate == "" || p.PrivateKey == "" {
		t.Errorf("Pebble configuration %+v, want validation on 80 and 443, the directory on 14000 and a certificate", p)
	}
}

func TestM6nUnitRawHeaders(t *testing.T) {
	h, err := m6nRawHeaders([]byte(`["Host","a.test","host","b.test","X-Darkpanel-Client","198.18.0.20"]`))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := h.Values("Host"); !slices.Equal(got, []string{"a.test", "b.test"}) {
		t.Errorf("Host values %q, want both repetitions in order", got)
	}
	if got := h.Values("x-darkpanel-client"); !slices.Equal(got, []string{"198.18.0.20"}) {
		t.Errorf("X-Darkpanel-Client values %q, want the one", got)
	}
	for _, bad := range []string{`["Host"]`, `{"host":"a"}`, ``, `[1,2]`} {
		if _, err := m6nRawHeaders([]byte(bad)); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("m6nRawHeaders(%q) was accepted, want an error", bad)
		}
	}
}

// m6nUnitCA returns a self-signed CA certificate with its key.
func m6nUnitCA(t *testing.T, name string, notAfter time.Time) (*x509.Certificate, *ecdsa.PrivateKey) {
	t.Helper()
	key, err := ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P256(), rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	tmpl := &x509.Certificate{
		SerialNumber: big.NewInt(1), Subject: pkix.Name{CommonName: name},
		NotBefore: time.Now().Add(-time.Hour), NotAfter: notAfter, IsCA: true, BasicConstraintsValid: true,
		KeyUsage: x509.KeyUsageCertSign,
	}
	der, err := x509.CreateCertificate(rand.Reader, tmpl, tmpl, &key.PublicKey, key)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cert, err := x509.ParseCertificate(der)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return cert, key
}

// m6nUnitLeaf returns a leaf for host signed by ca.
func m6nUnitLeaf(t *testing.T, ca *x509.Certificate, caKey *ecdsa.PrivateKey, host string, notAfter time.Time) *x509.Certificate {
	t.Helper()
	key, err := ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P256(), rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	tmpl := &x509.Certificate{
		SerialNumber: big.NewInt(2), Subject: pkix.Name{CommonName: host}, DNSNames: []string{host},
		NotBefore: time.Now().Add(-time.Hour), NotAfter: notAfter, KeyUsage: x509.KeyUsageDigitalSignature,
		ExtKeyUsage: []x509.ExtKeyUsage{x509.ExtKeyUsageServerAuth},
	}
	der, err := x509.CreateCertificate(rand.Reader, tmpl, ca, &key.PublicKey, caKey)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cert, err := x509.ParseCertificate(der)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return cert
}

func TestM6nUnitCheckChain(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Now()
	root, rootKey := m6nUnitCA(t, "Pebble Root", now.Add(24*time.Hour))
	other, _ := m6nUnitCA(t, "Other Root", now.Add(24*time.Hour))
	rootPEM := pem.EncodeToMemory(&pem.Block{Type: "CERTIFICATE", Bytes: root.Raw})
	otherPEM := pem.EncodeToMemory(&pem.Block{Type: "CERTIFICATE", Bytes: other.Raw})
	leaf := m6nUnitLeaf(t, root, rootKey, "app.example.test", now.Add(time.Hour))
	expired := m6nUnitLeaf(t, root, rootKey, "app.example.test", now.Add(-time.Minute))
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name  string
		chain []*x509.Certificate
		root  []byte
		host  string
		want  string // "" when accepted
	}{
		{"valid", []*x509.Certificate{leaf}, rootPEM, "app.example.test", ""},
		{"other host", []*x509.Certificate{leaf}, rootPEM, "other.example.test", "not other.example.test"},
		{"expired", []*x509.Certificate{expired}, rootPEM, "app.example.test", "expired"},
		{"unknown root", []*x509.Certificate{leaf}, otherPEM, "app.example.test", "unknown authority"},
		{"no chain", nil, rootPEM, "app.example.test", "no certificate"},
		{"root not PEM", []*x509.Certificate{leaf}, []byte("x"), "app.example.test", "not a PEM"},
	} {
		err := m6nCheckChain(tc.chain, tc.root, tc.host, now)
		switch {
		case tc.want == "" && err != nil:
			t.Errorf("%s: %v, want it accepted", tc.name, err)
		case tc.want != "" && (err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), tc.want)):
			t.Errorf("%s: %v, want an error containing %q", tc.name, err, tc.want)
		}
	}
}

func TestM6nUnitIsApp(t *testing.T) {
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name string
		r    m6nResponse
		want bool
	}{
		{"the target's 404", m6nResponse{Status: 404, Body: []byte("404 page not found\n")}, true},
		{"NPM's 404 page", m6nResponse{Status: 404, Body: []byte("<html>404 Not Found nginx</html>")}, false},
		{"bad gateway", m6nResponse{Status: 502, Body: []byte("404 page not found")}, false},
	} {
		if got := m6nIsApp(tc.r); got != tc.want {
			t.Errorf("%s: m6nIsApp = %v, want %v", tc.name, got, tc.want)
		}
	}
}
```

```go
const (
	m6nAcmeEmail = "ops@darkpanel.dev"
	// m6nCABundleVar is the variable certbot reads for the CA bundle of the ACME server.
	m6nCABundleVar = "REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE"
	// m6nDir holds the files the scenario puts on the agent VM.

	// m6nNPMName is both the container and the network spec 6 names.
	m6nNPMName      = "darkpanel-npm"
	m6nPebbleName   = "m6n-pebble"
	m6nChallName    = "m6n-challtestsrv"
	m6nAppName      = "m6n-app"
	m6nEchoName     = "m6n-echo"
	m6nBridgeName   = "m6n-bridge"
	m6nAppNet       = "m6n-net"
	m6nPanelNet     = "darkpanel-panel"
	m6nPanelSubnet  = "10.99.77.0/24"
	m6nPanelGateway = "10.99.77.1"
	// m6nDirectory is Pebble's ACME directory as the NPM container reaches
	// it: the alias pebble is on the SAN list of Pebble's own certificate.
	m6nDirectory = "https://pebble:14000/dir"
	// m6nCAInNPM is where the NPM container finds the CA file that signed
	// Pebble's own certificate.
	m6nCAInNPM  = "/m6n/pebble.minica.pem"
	m6nAppHost  = "m6n-app.example.test"
	m6nEchoHost = "m6n-echo.example.test"
	m6nAppPort  = 8055
	m6nEchoPort = 8080
	// m6nAppBody is what the management server of pebble-challtestsrv answers
	// for a path it does not serve: Go's own 404, which NPM's pages are not.
	m6nAppBody = "404 page not found"
	// m6nForged is a client header value the scanner sends and NPM must overwrite.
	m6nForged = "203.0.113.99"
	// m6nUnsupportedReason is the reason spec 6 gives for a refused target.
	m6nUnsupportedReason = "connect the container to a user-defined network"
	// m6nEchoScript answers every request with its raw header lines, in
	// order, as a JSON array of alternating names and values.
	m6nEchoScript = `require("http").createServer((q, r) => r.end(JSON.stringify(q.rawHeaders))).listen(8080)`
	// m6nPebbleConfig is Pebble's own test configuration with its validation
	// ports moved to 80 and 443, where NPM listens.
	m6nPebbleConfig = `{
  "pebble": {
    "listenAddress": "0.0.0.0:14000",
    "managementListenAddress": "0.0.0.0:15000",
    "certificate": "test/certs/localhost/cert.pem",
    "privateKey": "test/certs/localhost/key.pem",
    "httpPort": 80,
    "tlsPort": 443,
    "ocspResponderURL": "",
    "externalAccountBindingRequired": false
  }
}
`
)```

`m6nCABundleVar` must be the variable Task 1's record names for the CA bundle that NPM's certbot reads (the record is `docs/dforge/notes/v1-w6a-npm-spike.md`; certbot honours `REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE`, which is the value above). Open the record, compare, and use the record's name if it differs; if the record names none, stop and report. `LE_SERVER` is the variable spec 6 names.

Then these pure functions and types, each exactly as described (the unit tests pin them):

1. `type m6nRunSpec struct`: string fields `Name`, `Image`, `Network`, `Alias`, `Entrypoint`, `Restart`; string-slice fields `Labels`, `Publish`, `Env`, `Volumes`, `Args`.
2. `func m6nRunCmd(s m6nRunSpec) string`: the command line, built with `m3dxShell` over the words `sudo docker run -d --name <Name>`, then, each only when set and in this order, `--restart <Restart>`, `--network <Network>`, `--network-alias <Alias>`, one `--label <l>` per Labels, one `-p <p>` per Publish, one `-e <e>` per Env, one `-v <v>` per Volumes, `--entrypoint <Entrypoint>`; then `<Image>` and every Args word, an empty word included (`m3dxShell` quotes it as `''`).
3. `func m6nNPMSpec(image string, env []string, caFile string) m6nRunSpec`: the `darkpanel-npm` container as Global Constraints "Values copied from the spec" give it and Task 12 creates it: `Name` and `Network` `m6nNPMName`, `Restart` `unless-stopped`, `Labels` `darkpanel.module=npm`, `Publish` `80:80`, `443:443` and `127.0.0.1:81:81`, `Env` as given, `Volumes` `darkpanel-npm-data:/data`, `darkpanel-npm-letsencrypt:/etc/letsencrypt` and `<caFile>:<m6nCAInNPM>:ro`.
4. `func m6nInspectEnv(inspect []byte) ([]string, error)`: the `Config.Env` of the one container in the JSON array `docker inspect` prints; an error naming `docker inspect` for malformed JSON and for an array that does not hold exactly one container.
5. `func m6nRawHeaders(body []byte) (http.Header, error)`: the echo server's answer, a JSON array of alternating header names and values, as an `http.Header` whose values keep every repetition in order; an error for malformed JSON and for an odd number of words.
6. `type m6nResponse struct`: `Status int`, `Body []byte`, `Chain []*x509.Certificate`.
7. `func m6nIsApp(r m6nResponse) bool`: true exactly when `Status` is 404 and `Body`, trimmed, is `m6nAppBody`.
8. `func m6nCheckChain(chain []*x509.Certificate, rootPEM []byte, host string, now time.Time) error`: verifies `chain[0]` for the DNS name `host` at `now` against a pool built from `rootPEM`, with `chain[1:]` as intermediates. Errors: `harness: the server presented no certificate` for an empty chain, `harness: Pebble's root is not a PEM certificate` when the pool takes nothing from `rootPEM`, and `harness: the served certificate: %w` around the x509 error otherwise.

import (

```go
var m6nFlag = flag.Bool("m6n", false, "run TestM6NPM; needs -os and -m2, and is a go test run of its own")

// m6nDeadline bounds TestM6NPM. The workflow step that runs it must keep its
// -timeout at least m6nDeadline plus 10 minutes, for the cleanups that stop the VMs.
const m6nDeadline = 40 * time.Minute

// m6nGet GETs https://host/path through the network of from, which reaches
// the agent's port 443 whatever host names, with header added. The server
// name goes out in the handshake so that NPM picks the proxy host.
func m6nGet(ctx context.Context, from *vm.VM, host, path string, header http.Header) (m6nResponse, error) {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 15*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	u := url.URL{Scheme: "https", Host: host, Path: path}
	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodGet, u.String(), nil)
	if err != nil {
		return m6nResponse{}, fmt.Errorf("harness: %w", err)
	}
	for k, vs := range header {
		req.Header[k] = vs
	}
	tr := &http.Transport{
		DialContext: func(ctx context.Context, network, _ string) (net.Conn, error) {
			return from.DialContext(ctx, network, net.JoinHostPort(agentIPv4, "443"))
		},
		DisableKeepAlives: true,
		TLSClientConfig:   &tls.Config{InsecureSkipVerify: true, ServerName: host}, //nolint:gosec // G402: the chain is checked by m6nCheckChain
	}
	defer tr.CloseIdleConnections()
	resp, err := (&http.Client{Transport: tr}).Do(req)
	if err != nil {
		return m6nResponse{}, fmt.Errorf("harness: %w", err)
	}
	defer func() { _ = resp.Body.Close() }()
	body, err := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(resp.Body, 1<<16))
	if err != nil {
		return m6nResponse{}, fmt.Errorf("harness: GET %s: %w", u.String(), err)
	}
	out := m6nResponse{Status: resp.StatusCode, Body: body}
	if resp.TLS != nil {
		out.Chain = resp.TLS.PeerCertificates
	}
	return out, nil
}

// m6nPull pulls ref on v, retrying for ten minutes, because the registries
// refuse anonymous pulls now and then.
func m6nPull(ctx context.Context, t *testing.T, v *vm.VM, ref string) {
	t.Helper()
	err := eventually(ctx, 10*time.Minute, func(ctx context.Context) error {
		out, err := v.Run(ctx, "sudo docker pull "+m3dxShell(ref))
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("harness: %w\n%s", err, out)
		}
		return nil
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("docker pull %s: %v", ref, err)
	}
}

// m6nContainerIP returns the address of container on network.
func m6nContainerIP(ctx context.Context, t *testing.T, v *vm.VM, container, network string) string {
	t.Helper()
	format := `{{(index .NetworkSettings.Networks "` + network + `").IPAddress}}`
	out := strings.TrimSpace(m3Run(ctx, t, v, "sudo docker inspect -f "+m3Quote(format)+" "+m3dxShell(container)))
	if _, err := netip.ParseAddr(out); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("container %s has no address on %s: %q", container, network, out)
	}
	return out
}

// m6nNetworkMembers returns the names of the containers attached to network.
func m6nNetworkMembers(ctx context.Context, t *testing.T, v *vm.VM, network string) []string {
	t.Helper()
	format := `{{range .Containers}}{{.Name}} {{end}}`
	return strings.Fields(m3Run(ctx, t, v, "sudo docker network inspect -f "+m3Quote(format)+" "+m3dxShell(network)))
}

// m6nDiagnostic is one command that a failed step logs.
type m6nDiagnostic struct{ Label, Command string }

// m6nDiagnostics are what a failed step logs from the agent: the containers,
// the networks, the end of each container's log and NPM's own answer.
func m6nDiagnostics() []m6nDiagnostic {
	ds := []m6nDiagnostic{
		{"containers", `sudo docker ps -a --format 'table {{.Names}}\t{{.Image}}\t{{.Status}}'`},
		{"networks", "sudo docker network ls"},
		{"NPM API", "curl -sS --max-time 10 http://127.0.0.1:81/api"},
		{"ufw status", "sudo ufw status verbose"},
	}
	for _, name := range []string{m6nNPMName, m6nPebbleName, m6nChallName, m6nAppName, m6nEchoName} {
		ds = append(ds, m6nDiagnostic{name + " log", "sudo docker logs --tail 40 " + name + " 2>&1"})
	}
	return ds
}

// m6nRun is what the steps of TestM6NPM share.
type m6nRun struct {
	ctx   context.Context
	f     *fleet
	npmIP string // darkpanel-npm's address on its network, set by harnessSettings
	root  []byte // Pebble's root certificate, PEM, set by harnessSettings
}

// TestM6NPM is the NPM half of M6 (spec 6, Verification): a real NPM,
// deployed through npm.instance.deploy-public, issues a certificate from Pebble
// for a container it exposes, forwards an ordinary request with one Host header
// and an overwritten X-Darkpanel-Client, refuses a default-bridge-only target
// in the preview and the reserved upstreams outside their composites, removes
// an exposure by claim, and survives npm.instance.upgrade. The workflow selects
// it with -run, -os, -m2 and -m6n.
func TestM6NPM(t *testing.T) {
	if *osFlag == "" || *m2Flag == "" || !*m6nFlag {
		t.Skip("no -os, -m2 or -m6n given: the VM tests run in .github/workflows/harness.yml")
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), m6nDeadline)
	t.Cleanup(cancel)
	r := &m6nRun{ctx: ctx, f: m5dLab(ctx, t)}
	steps := []struct {
		name string
		gate bool // later steps need this one
		run  func(t *testing.T)
	}{
		{"prepare", true, r.prepare},
		{"deploy", true, r.deploy},
		{"harness-settings", true, r.harnessSettings},
		{"admin", false, r.admin},
		{"public", true, r.public},
		{"unsupported-target", false, r.unsupportedTarget},
		{"reserved-upstreams", false, r.reservedUpstreams},
		{"expose", true, r.expose},
		{"headers", false, r.headers},
		{"expose-remove", true, r.exposeRemove},
		{"upgrade", true, r.upgrade},
		{"expose-remove-last", false, r.exposeRemoveLast},
	}
	for _, s := range steps {
		if !t.Run(s.name, s.run) {
			r.logDiagnostics(t)
			if s.gate {
				return
			}
		}
	}
}

// image returns the pinned reference of the container image name.
func (r *m6nRun) image(t *testing.T, name string) string {
	t.Helper()
	ref, err := m3dxImage(name)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return ref
}

// prepare puts the files and images the scenario needs on the agent: Pebble's
// configuration and CA file, and the three images pulled once, with retries.
func (r *m6nRun) prepare(t *testing.T) {
	v := r.f.agent
	m3Run(r.ctx, t, v, "sudo install -d -m 0755 "+m6nDir)
	conf := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "pebble-config.json")
	if err := os.WriteFile(conf, []byte(m6nPebbleConfig), 0o600); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := v.CopyFile(r.ctx, conf, m6nDir+"/pebble-config.json", 0o644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	pebble, chall := r.image(t, "pebble"), r.image(t, "pebble-challtestsrv")
	for _, ref := range []string{npm.Image, pebble, chall} {
		m6nPull(r.ctx, t, v, ref)
	}
	m3Run(r.ctx, t, v, "sudo docker rm -f m6n-ca >/dev/null 2>&1 || true")
	m3dDocker(r.ctx, t, v, "create", "--name", "m6n-ca", pebble)
	m3dDocker(r.ctx, t, v, "cp", "m6n-ca:/test/certs/pebble.minica.pem", m6nDir+"/pebble.minica.pem")
	m3dDocker(r.ctx, t, v, "rm", "m6n-ca")
}

// deploy deploys NPM through npm.instance.deploy-public. The lab leaves the
// Docker integration of the firewall off, so the composite has no ports step
// and the instance is public only after npm.instance.confirm-public.
func (r *m6nRun) deploy(t *testing.T) {
	var fd struct {
		Enabled   bool `json:"enabled"`
		Supported bool `json:"supported"`
	}
	if err := r.f.api.Resource(r.ctx, r.f.agentID, "firewall.docker", &fd); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if fd.Enabled {
		t.Fatal("the lab's Docker integration is enabled, which this scenario does not expect")
	}
	ch := m4ApplyAsync(r.ctx, t, r.f.api, "", "npm.instance.deploy-public",
		npm.DeployPublicInput{HostID: r.f.agentID, AcmeEmail: m6nAcmeEmail, Integration: false})
	var out npm.DeployPublicOutput
	if err := json.Unmarshal(ch.Output, &out); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("output of npm.instance.deploy-public: %v", err)
	}
	if out.ContainerID == "" || out.PortsOpened {
		t.Errorf("deploy-public output %+v, want a container id and no ports step", out)
	}
}

// harnessSettings is what only a harness deployment has (spec 6, Verification):
// NPM's certbot talks to Pebble. The image takes the ACME directory and the CA
// file from its environment, which a deployed container cannot be given, so the
// container is recreated over SSH with the same name, labels, mounts, ports
// and network plus the variables; the volumes, and with them the admin, stay.
// The module finds its container by the id in its instance row, so the new id
// is written there; the agent database is SQLite and takes the write while the
// agent runs.
// Pebble and its DNS server then join the NPM network, and the DNS server
// answers every name with NPM's address.
func (r *m6nRun) harnessSettings(t *testing.T) {
	v := r.f.agent
	inspect := m3dDocker(r.ctx, t, v, "inspect", m6nNPMName)
	env, err := m6nInspectEnv([]byte(inspect))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	env = append(env, "LE_SERVER="+m6nDirectory, m6nCABundleVar+"="+m6nCAInNPM)
	m3dDocker(r.ctx, t, v, "rm", "-f", m6nNPMName)
	m3Run(r.ctx, t, v, m6nRunCmd(m6nNPMSpec(npm.Image, env, m6nDir+"/pebble.minica.pem")))
	id := strings.TrimSpace(m3dDocker(r.ctx, t, v, "inspect", "-f", "{{.Id}}", m6nNPMName))
	if len(id) != 64 || strings.Trim(id, "0123456789abcdef") != "" {
		t.Fatalf("harness: the recreated container's id is %q", id)
	}
	m3Run(r.ctx, t, v, "sudo sqlite3 -cmd '.timeout 10000' "+paths.Production().AgentDB()+" \"UPDATE npm_instances SET container_id = '"+id+"' WHERE id = 1\"")
	err = eventually(r.ctx, 3*time.Minute, func(ctx context.Context) error {
		out, err := v.Run(ctx, "curl -fsS --max-time 10 http://127.0.0.1:81/api")
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		var health struct {
			Setup bool `json:"setup"`
		}
		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(out), &health); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if !health.Setup {
			return errors.New("harness: GET /api reports setup false: the admin did not survive the recreation")
		}
		return nil
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("NPM after the recreation: %v", err)
	}
	r.npmIP = m6nContainerIP(r.ctx, t, v, m6nNPMName, m6nNPMName)

	m3Run(r.ctx, t, v, m6nRunCmd(m6nRunSpec{
		Name: m6nChallName, Image: r.image(t, "pebble-challtestsrv"), Network: m6nNPMName, Entrypoint: "pebble-challtestsrv",
		Args: []string{"-defaultIPv4", r.npmIP, "-defaultIPv6", "", "-http01", "", "-https01", "", "-tlsalpn01", ""},
	}))
	m3dWaitRunning(r.ctx, t, v, m6nChallName)
	dns := m6nContainerIP(r.ctx, t, v, m6nChallName, m6nNPMName) + ":8053"
	m3Run(r.ctx, t, v, m6nRunCmd(m6nRunSpec{
		Name: m6nPebbleName, Image: r.image(t, "pebble"), Network: m6nNPMName, Alias: "pebble", Entrypoint: "pebble",
		Publish: []string{"127.0.0.1:15000:15000"},
		Env:     []string{"PEBBLE_VA_NOSLEEP=1", "PEBBLE_WFE_NONCEREJECT=0"},
		Volumes: []string{m6nDir + "/pebble-config.json:/m6n/pebble-config.json:ro"},
		Args:    []string{"-config", "/m6n/pebble-config.json", "-dnsserver", dns},
	}))
	err = eventually(r.ctx, time.Minute, func(ctx context.Context) error {
		out, err := v.Run(ctx, "curl -fsSk --max-time 10 https://127.0.0.1:15000/roots/0")
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if !strings.Contains(out, "BEGIN CERTIFICATE") {
			return fmt.Errorf("harness: Pebble answered %.80q for its root", out)
		}
		r.root = []byte(out)
		return nil
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Pebble: %v", err)
	}
}

// admin: npm.instance.status shows the instance running with its API ready
// after the recreation, npm.instance.list shows it on the fleet read,
// npm.admin.reveal-password returns a 32-byte base64url password, and NPM
// accepts that password for the admin's email. The password is sent on
// standard input, never named in a command line, a log or a message.
func (r *m6nRun) admin(t *testing.T) {
	var st npm.InstanceStatus
	if err := r.f.api.Read(r.ctx, r.f.agentID, "npm.instance.status/v1", npm.StatusInput{}, &st); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !st.Deployed || !st.Running || !st.APIReady || st.Image != npm.Image || st.PinnedImage != npm.Image || st.AdminEmail != m6nAcmeEmail || st.Public != "no" {
		t.Errorf("npm.instance.status %+v, want a running, ready instance of %s with admin %s that is not public yet", st, npm.Image, m6nAcmeEmail)
	}
	var fleet npm.InstancesOutput
	if err := r.f.api.Read(r.ctx, "", "npm.instance.list/v1", struct{}{}, &fleet); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !slices.ContainsFunc(fleet.Instances, func(i npm.HostInstance) bool { return i.HostID == r.f.agentID && i.Status.Deployed }) {
		t.Errorf("npm.instance.list %+v, want the agent's host with a deployed instance", fleet.Instances)
	}
	var pw npm.AdminPassword
	if err := m4Reveal(r.ctx, r.f.api, r.f.agentID, "npm.admin.reveal-password", npm.RevealInput{}, &pw); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	raw, err := base64.RawURLEncoding.DecodeString(pw.Password)
	if pw.Email != m6nAcmeEmail || err != nil || len(raw) != 32 {
		t.Fatalf("npm.admin.reveal-password gave email %q and a password of %d characters (decode error %v), want %s and 32 random bytes in base64url", pw.Email, len(pw.Password), err, m6nAcmeEmail)
	}
	body, err := json.Marshal(map[string]string{"identity": pw.Email, "secret": pw.Password})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cmd := "curl -fsS --max-time 20 -o /dev/null -X POST -H 'Content-Type: application/json' --data-binary @- http://127.0.0.1:81/api/tokens"
	if _, err := r.f.agent.RunInput(r.ctx, cmd, bytes.NewReader(body)); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("POST /api/tokens with the revealed password: %v", err)
	}
}

// public: ports 80 and 443 answer from the scanner, then the operator's
// confirmation marks the instance public, which npm.expose.container requires.
func (r *m6nRun) public(t *testing.T) {
	for _, port := range []string{"80", "443"} {
		err := eventually(r.ctx, time.Minute, func(ctx context.Context) error {
			c, err := r.f.scanner.DialContext(ctx, "tcp", net.JoinHostPort(agentIPv4, port))
			if err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return c.Close()
		})
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("%s:%s from the scanner: %v", agentIPv4, port, err)
		}
	}
	m4ApplyAsync(r.ctx, t, r.f.api, r.f.agentID, "npm.instance.confirm-public", npm.ConfirmPublicInput{})
	var st npm.InstanceStatus
	if err := r.f.api.Read(r.ctx, r.f.agentID, "npm.instance.status/v1", npm.StatusInput{}, &st); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if st.Public != "confirmed" {
		t.Errorf("npm.instance.status public %q after npm.instance.confirm-public, want confirmed", st.Public)
	}
}

// m6nRefused requires that err is an API refusal with code and, when reason is
// not empty, a detail naming it.
func m6nRefused(t *testing.T, what string, err error, code errcode.Code, reason string) {
	t.Helper()
	var apiErr *APIError
	switch {
	case err == nil:
		t.Errorf("%s was planned, want it refused with %s", what, code)
	case !errors.As(err, &apiErr) || apiErr.Code != string(code):
		t.Errorf("%s: %v, want an API refusal with code %s", what, err, code)
	case reason != "" && !strings.Contains(apiErr.Detail, reason):
		t.Errorf("%s: detail %q, want it to name %q", what, apiErr.Detail, reason)
	}
}

// listProxyHosts reads npm.proxyhost.list.
func (r *m6nRun) listProxyHosts(t *testing.T) []npm.ProxyHostView {
	t.Helper()
	var l npm.ProxyHostList
	if err := r.f.api.Read(r.ctx, r.f.agentID, "npm.proxyhost.list/v1", npm.ProxyHostListInput{}, &l); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return l.Items
}

// m6nHasDomain reports whether a proxy host of hosts serves domain.
func m6nHasDomain(hosts []npm.ProxyHostView, domain string) bool {
	return slices.ContainsFunc(hosts, func(h npm.ProxyHostView) bool { return slices.Contains(h.Domains, domain) })
}

// unsupportedTarget: a container on the default bridge only cannot be reached
// by name, and the exposure is refused in its preview.
func (r *m6nRun) unsupportedTarget(t *testing.T) {
	v := r.f.agent
	m3Run(r.ctx, t, v, m6nRunCmd(m6nRunSpec{
		Name: m6nBridgeName, Image: r.image(t, "pebble-challtestsrv"), Entrypoint: "pebble-challtestsrv",
		Args: []string{"-dns01", "", "-http01", "", "-https01", "", "-tlsalpn01", ""},
	}))
	m3dWaitRunning(r.ctx, t, v, m6nBridgeName)
	_, err := m4Plan(r.ctx, r.f.api, "", "npm.expose.container", npm.ExposeInput{
		HostID: r.f.agentID, Container: m6nBridgeName, Port: m6nAppPort, Network: "bridge", Domains: []string{"m6n-bridge.example.test"},
	})
	m6nRefused(t, "the exposure of a default-bridge-only container", err, errcode.NPMUnsupportedTarget, m6nUnsupportedReason)
}

// reservedUpstreams: npm.proxyhost.add, called on its own, refuses the
// upstreams that belong to the admin and panel exposures, whose composites
// are the only parents allowed to use them. A network named darkpanel-panel
// stands in for the panel's.
func (r *m6nRun) reservedUpstreams(t *testing.T) {
	v := r.f.agent
	m3dDocker(r.ctx, t, v, "network", "create", "--subnet", m6nPanelSubnet, "--gateway", m6nPanelGateway, m6nPanelNet)
	t.Cleanup(func() {
		ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), time.Minute)
		defer cancel()
		_, _ = v.Run(ctx, "sudo docker network rm "+m6nPanelNet)
	})
	for i, c := range []struct {
		name, host string
		port       int
	}{
		{"the admin port on 127.0.0.1", "127.0.0.1", 81},
		{"the admin port on localhost", "localhost", 81},
		{"the admin port on NPM's own address", r.npmIP, 81},
		{"the panel gateway", m6nPanelGateway, 8080},
		{"an address of the panel network", "10.99.77.2", 8080},
	} {
		_, err := m4Plan(r.ctx, r.f.api, r.f.agentID, "npm.proxyhost.add", npm.ProxyHostInput{
			Domains: []string{fmt.Sprintf("m6n-reserved-%d.example.test", i)}, ForwardScheme: "http", ForwardHost: c.host, ForwardPort: c.port,
		})
		m6nRefused(t, "npm.proxyhost.add to "+c.name, err, errcode.NPMReserved, "")
	}
	for _, h := range r.listProxyHosts(t) {
		t.Errorf("proxy host %v exists after the refused plans, want none", h.Domains)
	}
}

// expose exposes the Pebble image's HTTP listener and an echo server on one
// user-defined network through npm.expose.container: NPM joins the network, a
// certificate comes from Pebble through the real ACME flow, and the scanner
// reaches both targets over HTTPS with a chain that verifies under Pebble's root.
func (r *m6nRun) expose(t *testing.T) {
	v := r.f.agent
	m3dDocker(r.ctx, t, v, "network", "create", m6nAppNet)
	m3Run(r.ctx, t, v, m6nRunCmd(m6nRunSpec{
		Name: m6nAppName, Image: r.image(t, "pebble-challtestsrv"), Network: m6nAppNet, Entrypoint: "pebble-challtestsrv",
		Args: []string{"-dns01", "", "-http01", "", "-https01", "", "-tlsalpn01", ""},
	}))
	m3Run(r.ctx, t, v, m6nRunCmd(m6nRunSpec{
		Name: m6nEchoName, Image: npm.Image, Network: m6nAppNet, Entrypoint: "node", Args: []string{"-e", m6nEchoScript},
	}))
	m3dWaitRunning(r.ctx, t, v, m6nAppName)
	m3dWaitRunning(r.ctx, t, v, m6nEchoName)
	for _, e := range []struct {
		container, host string
		port            int
	}{{m6nAppName, m6nAppHost, m6nAppPort}, {m6nEchoName, m6nEchoHost, m6nEchoPort}} {
		ch := m4ApplyAsync(r.ctx, t, r.f.api, "", "npm.expose.container", npm.ExposeInput{
			HostID: r.f.agentID, Container: e.container, Port: e.port, Network: m6nAppNet, Domains: []string{e.host},
		})
		var out npm.ExposeOutput
		if err := json.Unmarshal(ch.Output, &out); err != nil || out.ProxyHostID == 0 || out.CertificateID == 0 {
			t.Fatalf("output of npm.expose.container for %s: %s (%v), want a proxy host and a certificate id", e.host, ch.Output, err)
		}
	}
	if members := m6nNetworkMembers(r.ctx, t, v, m6nAppNet); !slices.Contains(members, m6nNPMName) {
		t.Errorf("network %s holds %v, want %s attached by the exposure", m6nAppNet, members, m6nNPMName)
	}
	for _, host := range []string{m6nAppHost, m6nEchoHost} {
		err := eventually(r.ctx, 2*time.Minute, func(ctx context.Context) error {
			resp, err := m6nGet(ctx, r.f.scanner, host, "/", nil)
			if err != nil {
				return err
			}
			if err := m6nCheckChain(resp.Chain, r.root, host, time.Now()); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			switch {
			case host == m6nAppHost && !m6nIsApp(resp):
				return fmt.Errorf("harness: GET / answered %d %.80q, want the target's own 404", resp.Status, resp.Body)
			case host == m6nEchoHost && resp.Status != http.StatusOK:
				return fmt.Errorf("harness: GET / answered %d %.80q, want 200", resp.Status, resp.Body)
			}
			return nil
		})
		if err != nil {
			t.Errorf("GET https://%s/ from the scanner: %v", host, err)
		}
	}
}

// headers: a request through the custom location arrives with exactly one Host
// header, and X-Darkpanel-Client arrives once with the scanner's address
// whether the request lacks it, forges it, or forges it twice.
func (r *m6nRun) headers(t *testing.T) {
	for _, c := range []struct {
		name   string
		header http.Header
	}{
		{"ordinary request", nil},
		{"forged client header", http.Header{"X-Darkpanel-Client": {m6nForged}}},
		{"client header forged twice", http.Header{"X-Darkpanel-Client": {m6nForged, "198.51.100.7"}}},
	} {
		resp, err := m6nGet(r.ctx, r.f.scanner, m6nEchoHost, "/", c.header)
		if err != nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: %v", c.name, err)
			continue
		}
		h, err := m6nRawHeaders(resp.Body)
		if err != nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: %v", c.name, err)
			continue
		}
		if got := h.Values("Host"); !slices.Equal(got, []string{m6nEchoHost}) {
			t.Errorf("%s: the target received Host %q, want exactly %q", c.name, got, m6nEchoHost)
		}
		if got := h.Values("X-Darkpanel-Client"); !slices.Equal(got, []string{scannerIPv4}) {
			t.Errorf("%s: the target received X-Darkpanel-Client %q, want exactly %q", c.name, got, scannerIPv4)
		}
	}
}

// exposeRemove removes the first exposure by npm.expose.remove: its proxy host
// is gone and its host no longer reaches the target, while the other
// exposure still works and NPM stays on the shared network, because the claim
// of the other exposure remains.
func (r *m6nRun) exposeRemove(t *testing.T) {
	m4ApplyAsync(r.ctx, t, r.f.api, "", "npm.expose.remove", npm.ExposeRemoveInput{HostID: r.f.agentID, Domain: m6nAppHost})
	hosts := r.listProxyHosts(t)
	if m6nHasDomain(hosts, m6nAppHost) || !m6nHasDomain(hosts, m6nEchoHost) {
		t.Errorf("proxy hosts %+v after removing %s, want only the one for %s", hosts, m6nAppHost, m6nEchoHost)
	}
	err := eventually(r.ctx, 30*time.Second, func(ctx context.Context) error {
		if resp, err := m6nGet(ctx, r.f.scanner, m6nAppHost, "/", nil); err == nil && m6nIsApp(resp) {
			return errors.New("harness: the host still reaches the removed target")
		}
		return nil
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Errorf("GET https://%s/ after the removal: %v", m6nAppHost, err)
	}
	if members := m6nNetworkMembers(r.ctx, t, r.f.agent, m6nAppNet); !slices.Contains(members, m6nNPMName) {
		t.Errorf("network %s holds %v, want %s kept for the exposure that remains", m6nAppNet, members, m6nNPMName)
	}
	resp, err := m6nGet(r.ctx, r.f.scanner, m6nEchoHost, "/", nil)
	if err != nil || resp.Status != http.StatusOK {
		t.Errorf("GET https://%s/ after the removal: %v %d, want 200", m6nEchoHost, err, resp.Status)
	}
}

// upgrade: npm.instance.upgrade to the pinned image, which the instance
// already runs, completes, leaves the instance ready and keeps the proxy host.
func (r *m6nRun) upgrade(t *testing.T) {
	before := r.listProxyHosts(t)
	ch := m4ApplyAsync(r.ctx, t, r.f.api, r.f.agentID, "npm.instance.upgrade", npm.UpgradeInput{})
	var out npm.Upgraded
	if err := json.Unmarshal(ch.Output, &out); err != nil || out.ToImage != npm.Image {
		t.Errorf("output of npm.instance.upgrade: %s (%v), want ToImage %s", ch.Output, err, npm.Image)
	}
	var st npm.InstanceStatus
	if err := r.f.api.Read(r.ctx, r.f.agentID, "npm.instance.status/v1", npm.StatusInput{}, &st); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !st.Running || !st.APIReady || st.Image != npm.Image {
		t.Errorf("npm.instance.status %+v after the upgrade, want a running, ready instance of %s", st, npm.Image)
	}
	after := r.listProxyHosts(t)
	if len(after) != len(before) || !m6nHasDomain(after, m6nEchoHost) {
		t.Errorf("proxy hosts %+v after the upgrade, want the %d from before", after, len(before))
	}
	err := eventually(r.ctx, 2*time.Minute, func(ctx context.Context) error {
		resp, err := m6nGet(ctx, r.f.scanner, m6nEchoHost, "/", nil)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if resp.Status != http.StatusOK {
			return fmt.Errorf("harness: GET / answered %d", resp.Status)
		}
		return nil
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Errorf("GET https://%s/ after the upgrade: %v", m6nEchoHost, err)
	}
}

// exposeRemoveLast removes the last exposure, after the upgrade: NPM, now
// recreated, still manages its objects, and no claim is left, so the network
// is detached.
func (r *m6nRun) exposeRemoveLast(t *testing.T) {
	m4ApplyAsync(r.ctx, t, r.f.api, "", "npm.expose.remove", npm.ExposeRemoveInput{HostID: r.f.agentID, Domain: m6nEchoHost})
	if hosts := r.listProxyHosts(t); len(hosts) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("proxy hosts %+v after the last removal, want none", hosts)
	}
	if members := m6nNetworkMembers(r.ctx, t, r.f.agent, m6nAppNet); slices.Contains(members, m6nNPMName) {
		t.Errorf("network %s holds %v, want %s detached once no exposure claims it", m6nAppNet, members, m6nNPMName)
	}
}

// m6nDiagnosticTimeout bounds each command of logDiagnostics.
const m6nDiagnosticTimeout = 15 * time.Second

// logDiagnostics logs what m6nDiagnostics collects. A command that fails is
// logged with its error and never fails the test.
func (r *m6nRun) logDiagnostics(t *testing.T) {
	for _, d := range m6nDiagnostics() {
		ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(r.ctx, m6nDiagnosticTimeout)
		out, err := r.f.agent.Run(ctx, d.Command)
		cancel()
		out = m5dCapDiagnostic(out, strings.Contains(d.Command, "logs"))
		if err != nil {
			t.Logf("diagnostic %s\n$ %s\n%s\nerror: %v", d.Label, d.Command, out, err)
			continue
		}
		t.Logf("diagnostic %s\n$ %s\n%s", d.Label, d.Command, out)
	}
}```
